# The Roman Missal — English (England and Wales), on the anchors of the Missale Romanum

## Proper of Time

#### Sunday in the First Week of Advent {#misal.dominica-i-adventus}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-i-adventus.ant-ad-introitum}

To you, I lift up my soul, O my God. In you, I have trusted; let me not be put to shame. Nor let not my enemies exult over me; and let none who hope in you be put to shame.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-i-adventus.collecta}

Grant your faithful, we pray, almighty God, the resolve to run forth to meet your Christ with righteous deeds at his coming, so that, gathered at his right hand, they may be worthy to possess the heavenly kingdom.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-i-adventus.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, these offerings we make, gathered from among your gifts to us, and may what you grant us to celebrate devoutly here below, gain for us the prize of eternal redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-i-adventus.ant-ad-communionem}

The Lord will bestow his bounty, and our earth shall yield its increase.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-i-adventus.post-communionem}

May these mysteries, O Lord, in which we have participated, profit us, we pray, for even now, as we walk amid passing things, you teach us by them to love the things of heaven and hold fast to what endures.

#### Monday in the First Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

Hear the word of the Lord, O nations; declare it to the distant lands: Behold, our Saviour will come; you need no longer fear.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-ii.collecta}

Keep us alert, we pray, O Lord our God, as we await the advent of Christ your Son, so that when he comes and knocks he may find us watchful in prayer and exultant in his praise.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-ii.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, these offerings we make, gathered from among your gifts to us, and may what you grant us to celebrate devoutly here below, gain for us the prize of eternal redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

Come, O Lord, visit us in peace, that we may rejoice before you with a blameless heart.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-ii.post-communionem}

May these mysteries, O Lord, in which we have participated, profit us, we pray, for even now, as we walk amid you teach us by them to love the things of heaven and hold fast to what endures.

#### Tuesday in the First Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold, the Lord will come, and all his holy ones with him; and on that day there will be a great light.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iii.collecta}

Look with favour, Lord God, on our petitions, and in our trials grant us your compassionate help, that, consoled by the presence of your Son, whose coming we now await, we may be tainted no longer by the corruption of former ways.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, with our humble prayers and offerings, and since we have no merits to plead our cause, come, we pray, to our rescue with the protection of your mercy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

The Just Judge will bestow a crown of righteousness on those who eagerly await his coming.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iii.post-communionem}

Replenished by the food of spiritual nourishment, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, through our partaking in this mystery, you may teach us to judge wisely the things of earth and hold firm to the things of heaven.

#### Wednesday in the First Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord will come and he will not delay. He will illumine what is hidden in darkness, and reveal himself to all the nations.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iv.collecta}

Prepare our hearts, we pray, O Lord our God, by your divine power, so that at the coming of Christ your Son we may be found worthy of the banquet of eternal life and merit to receive heavenly nourishment from his hands.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iv.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice of our worship, Lord, we pray, be offered to you unceasingly, to complete what was begun in sacred mystery and powerfully accomplish for us your saving work.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold our Lord will come with power, and will enlighten the eyes of his servants.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-iv.post-communionem}

We implore your mercy, Lord, that this divine sustenance may cleanse us of our faults and prepare us for the coming feasts.

#### Thursday in the First Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

You, O Lord, are close, and all your ways are truth. From of old I have known of your decrees, for you are eternal.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-v.collecta}

Stir up your power, O Lord, and come to our help with mighty strength, that what our sins impede the grace of your mercy may hasten.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-v.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, these offerings we make, gathered from among your gifts to us, and may what you grant us to celebrate devoutly here below, gain for us the prize of eternal redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

Let us live justly and devoutly in this age, as we await the blessed hope and the coming of the glory of our great God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-v.post-communionem}

May these mysteries in which we have participated profit us, we pray, O Lord, for even now, as we journey through this passing world, you teach us by them to love the things of heaven and hold fast to what will endure.

#### Friday in the First Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold, the Lord will come descending with splendour to visit his people with peace, and he will bestow on them eternal life.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-vi.collecta}

Stir up your power, we pray, O Lord, and come, that with you to protect us we may find rescue from the pressing dangers of our sins, and with you to set us free, we may be found worthy of salvation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, with our humble prayers and offerings, and since we have no merits to plead our cause, come, we pray, to our rescue with the protection of your mercy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

We await a saviour, the Lord Jesus Christ, who will change our mortal bodies, to conform with his glorified body.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-feria-vi.post-communionem}

Replenished by the food of spiritual nourishment, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, through our partaking in this mystery, you may teach us to judge wisely the things of earth and hold firm to the things of heaven.

#### Saturday in the First Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-sabbato}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

Come and show us your face, O Lord, who are seated upon the cherubim, and we will be saved.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-sabbato.collecta}

O God, who sent your Only Begotten Son into this world to free the human race from its ancient bondage, bestow on those who devoutly await him the grace of your compassion from on high, that we may attain the prize of true freedom.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-sabbato.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice of our worship, Lord, we pray, be offered to you unceasingly, to complete what was begun in sacred mystery and powerfully accomplish for us your saving work.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, I am coming soon and my recompense is with me, to bestow a reward according to the deeds of each.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-adventus-sabbato.post-communionem}

We implore your mercy, Lord, that this divine sustenance may cleanse us of our faults and prepare us for the coming feasts.

*SECOND WEEK OF ADVENT*

#### Sunday in the Second Week of Advent {#misal.dominica-ii-adventus}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ii-adventus.ant-ad-introitum}

O people of Sion, behold, the Lord will come to save the nations, and the Lord will make the glory of his voice heard in the joy of your heart.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-ii-adventus.collecta}

Almighty and merciful God, may no earthly undertaking hinder those who set out in haste to meet your Son, but may our learning of heavenly wisdom gain us admittance to his company.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-ii-adventus.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, with our humble prayers and offerings, and since we have no merits to plead our cause, come, we pray, to our rescue with the protection of your mercy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ii-adventus.ant-ad-communionem}

Jerusalem, arise and stand upon the heights, and behold the joy which comes to you from God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-ii-adventus.post-communionem}

Replenished by the food of spiritual nourishment, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, through our partaking in this mystery, you may teach us to judge wisely the things of earth and hold firm to the things of heaven.

#### Monday in the Second Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

Hear the word of the Lord, O nations; declare it to the distant lands: Behold, our Saviour will come; you need no longer fear.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-ii.collecta}

May our prayer of petition rise before you, we pray, O Lord, that, with purity unblemished, your servants may come, as they desire, to celebrate the great mystery of the Incarnation of your Only Begotten Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-ii.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, these offerings we make, gathered from among your gifts to us, and may what you grant us to celebrate devoutly here below, gain for us the prize of eternal redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

Come, O Lord, visit us in peace, that we may rejoice before you with a blameless heart.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-ii.post-communionem}

May these mysteries, O Lord, in which we have participated, profit us, we pray, for even now, as we walk amid passing things, you teach us by them to love the things of heaven and hold fast to what endures.

#### Tuesday in the Second Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold, the Lord will come, and all his holy ones with him; and on that day there will be a great light.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iii.collecta}

O God, who have shown forth your salvation to all the ends of the earth, grant, we pray that we may look forward in joy to the glorious Nativity of Christ.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, with our humble prayers and offerings, and since we have no merits to plead our cause, come we pray, to our rescue with the protection of your mercy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

The Just Judge will bestow a crown of righteousness on those who eagerly await his coming.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iii.post-communionem}

Replenished by the food of spiritual nourishment, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, through our partaking in this mystery, you may teach us to judge wisely the things of earth and hold firm to the things of heaven.

#### Wednesday in the Second Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord will come and he will not delay. He will illumine what is hidden in darkness, and reveal himself to all the nations.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iv.collecta}

Almighty God, who command us to prepare the way for Christ the Lord, grant in your kindness, we pray, that no infirmity may weary us as we long for the comforting presence of our heavenly physician.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iv.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice of our worship, Lord, we pray, be offered to you unceasingly, to complete what was begun in sacred mystery and powerfully accomplish for us your saving work.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold our Lord will come with power, and will enlighten the eyes of his servants.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-iv.post-communionem}

We implore your mercy, Lord, that this divine sustenance may cleanse us of our faults and prepare us for the coming feasts.

#### Thursday in the Second Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

You, O Lord, are close, and all your ways are truth. From of old I have known of your decrees, for you are eternal.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-v.collecta}

Stir up our hearts, O Lord, to make ready the paths of your Only Begotten Son, that through his coming we may be found worthy to serve you with minds made pure.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-v.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, these offerings we make, gathered from among your gifts to us, and may what you grant us to celebrate devoutly here below, gain for us the prize of eternal redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

Let us live justly and devoutly in this age, as we await the blessed hope and the coming of the glory of our great God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-v.post-communionem}

May these mysteries, O Lord, in which we have participated, profit us, we pray, for even now, as we walk amid passing things, you teach us by them to love the things of heaven and hold fast to what endures.

#### Friday in the Second Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold, the Lord will come descending with splendour to visit his people with peace, and he will bestow on them eternal life.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-vi.collecta}

Grant that your people, we pray, almighty God, may be ever watchful for the coming of your Only Begotten Son, that as the author of our salvation himself has taught us, we may hasten alert and with lighted lamps to meet him when he comes.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, with our humble prayers and offerings, and since we have no merits to plead our cause, come, we pray, to our rescue with the protection of your mercy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

We await a saviour, the Lord Jesus Christ, who will change our mortal bodies, to conform with his glorified body.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-feria-vi.post-communionem}

Replenished by the food of spiritual nourishment, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, through our partaking in this mystery, you may teach us to judge wisely the things of earth and hold firm to the things of heaven.

#### Saturday in the Second Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-sabbato}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

Come and show us your face, O Lord, who are seated upon the Cherubim, and we will be saved.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-sabbato.collecta}

May the splendour of your glory dawn in our hearts, we pray, almighty God, that all shadows of the night may be scattered and we may be shown to be children of light by the advent of your Only Begotten Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-sabbato.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice of our worship, Lord, we pray, be offered to you unceasingly, to complete what was begun in sacred mystery and powerfully accomplish for us your saving work.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, I am coming soon and my recompense is with me, says the Lord, to bestow a reward according to the deeds of each.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-adventus-sabbato.post-communionem}

We implore your mercy, Lord, that this divine sustenance may cleanse us of our faults and prepare us for the coming feasts.

*THIRD WEEK OF ADVENT*

#### Sunday in the Third Week of Advent {#misal.dominica-iii-adventus}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iii-adventus.ant-ad-introitum}

Rejoice in the Lord always; again I say, rejoice. Indeed, the Lord is near.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-iii-adventus.collecta}

O God, who see how your people faithfully await the feast of the Lord's Nativity, enable us, we pray, to attain the joys of so great a salvation, and to celebrate them always with solemn worship and glad rejoicing.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-iii-adventus.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice of our worship, Lord, we pray, be offered to you unceasingly, to complete what was begun in sacred mystery and powerfully accomplish for us your saving work.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iii-adventus.ant-ad-communionem}

Say to the faint of heart: Be strong and do not fear. Behold, our God will come, and he will save us.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-iii-adventus.post-communionem}

We implore your mercy, Lord, that this divine sustenance may cleanse us of our faults and prepare us for the coming feasts.

#### Monday in the Third Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

Hear the word of the Lord, O nations; declare it to the distant lands: Behold, our Saviour will come; you need no longer fear.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-ii.collecta}

Incline a merciful ear to our cry, we pray, O Lord, and casting light on the darkness of our hearts, visit us with the grace of your Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-ii.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, these offerings we make, gathered from among your gifts to us, and may what you grant us to celebrate devoutly here below, gain for us the prize of eternal redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

Come, O Lord, visit us in peace, that we may rejoice before you with a blameless heart.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-ii.post-communionem}

May these mysteries, O Lord, in which we have participated, profit us, we pray, for even now, as we walk amid passing things, you teach us by them to love the things of heaven and hold fast to what endures.

#### Tuesday in the Third Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold, the Lord will come, and all his holy ones with him; and on that day there will be a great light.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iii.collecta}

O God, who through your Only Begotten Son have made us a new creation, look kindly, we pray, on the handiwork of your mercy, and at your Son's coming cleanse us from every stain of the old way of life.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, with our humble prayers and offerings, and since we have no merits to plead our cause, come, we pray, to our rescue with the protection of your mercy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

The Just Judge will bestow a crown of righteousness on those who eagerly await his coming.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iii.post-communionem}

Replenished by the food of spiritual nourishment, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, through our partaking in this mystery, you may teach us to judge wisely the things of earth and hold firm to the things of heaven.

#### Wednesday in the Third Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord will come and he will not delay. He will illumine what is hidden in darkness, and reveal himself to all the nations.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iv.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that the coming solemnity of your Son may bestow healing upon us in this present life and bring us the rewards of life eternal.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iv.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice of our worship, Lord, we pray, be offered to you unceasingly, to complete what was begun in sacred mystery and powerfully accomplish for us your saving work.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold our Lord will come with power, and will enlighten the eyes of his servants.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-iv.post-communionem}

We implore your mercy, Lord, that this divine sustenance may cleanse us of our faults and prepare us for the coming feasts.

#### Thursday in the Third Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

You, O Lord, are close, and all your ways are truth. From of old I have known of your decrees, for you are eternal.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-v.collecta}

Unworthy servants that we are, O Lord, grieved by the guilt of our deeds, we pray that you may gladden us by the saving advent of your Only Begotten Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-v.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, these offerings we make, gathered from among your gifts to us, and may what you grant us to celebrate devoutly here below, gain for us the prize of eternal redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

Let us live justly and devoutly in this age, as we await the blessed hope and the coming of the glory of our great God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-v.post-communionem}

May these mysteries, O Lord, in which we have participated, profit us, we pray, for even now, as we walk amid passing things, you teach us by them to love the things of heaven and hold fast to what endures.

#### Friday in the Third Week of Advent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold, the Lord will come descending with splendour to visit his people with peace, and he will bestow on them eternal life.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-vi.collecta}

May your grace, almighty God, always go before us and follow after, so that we who await with heartfelt desire the coming of your Only Begotten Son, may receive your help both now and in the life to come.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Be pleased with our humble prayers and offerings, O Lord, we pray, and since we have no merits to plead our cause, come to our rescue with the protection of your mercy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

We await a saviour, the Lord Jesus Christ, who will change our mortal bodies, to conform with his glorified body.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-adventus-feria-vi.post-communionem}

Replenished by the food of spiritual nourishment, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, through our partaking in this mystery, you may teach us to judge wisely the things of earth and hold firm to the things of heaven.

*FOURTH WEEK OF ADVENT*

#### Fourth Sunday of Advent {#misal.dominica-iv-adventus}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iv-adventus.ant-ad-introitum}

Drop down dew from above, you heavens, and let the clouds rain down the Just One; let the earth be opened and bring forth a Saviour.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-iv-adventus.collecta}

Pour forth, we beseech you, O Lord, your grace into our hearts, that we, to whom the Incarnation of Christ your Son was made known by the message of an Angel, may by his Passion and Cross be brought to the glory of his Resurrection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-iv-adventus.super-oblata}

May the Holy Spirit, O Lord, sanctify these gifts laid upon your altar, just as he filled with his power the womb of the blessed Virgin Mary.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iv-adventus.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold a Virgin shall conceive, and shall bear a son; and his name will be called Emmanuel.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-iv-adventus.post-communionem}

Having received this pledge of eternal redemption, we pray, almighty God, that as the feast day of our salvation draws ever nearer, so we may press forward all the more eagerly to the worthy celebration of the mystery of your Son's Nativity.

*SPECIAL MASSES FOR DECEMBER 17 to 24*

#### December 17 {#misal.die-17-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-17-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

Rejoice, O heavens, and exult, O earth, for our Lord will come to show mercy to his poor.

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-decembris.collecta}

O God, Creator and Redeemer of human nature, who willed that your Word should take flesh in an ever-virgin womb, look with favour on our prayers, that your Only Begotten Son, having taken to himself our humanity, may be pleased to grant us a share in his divinity.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-17-decembris.super-oblata}

Sanctify these gifts of your Church, O Lord, and grant that through these venerable mysteries we may be nourished with the bread of heaven.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-17-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, the Desired of all the nations will come, and the house of the Lord will be filled with glory.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-17-decembris.post-communionem}

Nourished by these divine gifts, almighty God, we ask you to grant our desire: that, aflame with your Spirit, we may shine like bright torches before your Christ when he comes.

#### December 18 {#misal.die-18-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-18-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

Christ our King is coming, he is the Lamb foretold by John.

##### Collect {#misal.die-18-decembris.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that we who are weighed down from of old by slavery beneath the yoke of sin may be set free by the newness of the long-awaited Nativity of your Only Begotten Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-18-decembris.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice to be offered to you, O Lord, make us acceptable to your Name, that we may merit for all eternity to be the companions of Christ, by whose Death our own mortality was healed.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-18-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

His name will be called Emmanuel, which means God-with-us.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-18-decembris.post-communionem}

May we receive your mercy in the midst of your temple, O Lord, and show fitting honour to the coming solemnities of our redemption.

#### December 19 {#misal.die-19-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-19-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

He who is to come will come and will not delay, and now there will be no fear within our land, for he is our Saviour.

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-decembris.collecta}

O God, who through the child bearing of the holy Virgin graciously revealed the radiance of your glory to the world, grant, we pray, that we may venerate with integrity of faith the mystery of so wondrous an Incarnation and always celebrate it with due reverence.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-19-decembris.super-oblata}

Look with favour, we pray, O Lord, on the offerings we lay upon your altars, that what we bring despite our weakness may be sanctified by your power.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-19-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

The Dawn from on high will visit us, guiding our feet in the way of peace.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-19-decembris.post-communionem}

As we give thanks, almighty God, for these gifts you have bestowed, graciously arouse in us, we pray, the desire for those yet to come, that we may welcome the Nativity of our Saviour and honour it with minds made pure.

#### December 20 {#misal.die-20-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-20-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

A branch shall sprout from the root of Jesse, and the glory of the Lord will fill the whole earth, and all flesh will see the salvation of God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-20-decembris.collecta}

O God, eternal majesty, whose ineffable Word the immaculate Virgin received through the message of an Angel, and so became the dwelling-place of divinity, filled with the light of the Holy Spirit, grant, we pray, that by her example, we may in humility hold fast to your will.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-20-decembris.super-oblata}

Look, O Lord, we pray, upon the one sacrifice of your Son, that by participating in this mystery we may possess at last the gifts we have awaited and for which our faith bids us hope.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-20-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

The Angel said to Mary: Behold, you will conceive and bear a son, and you shall name him Jesus.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-20-decembris.post-communionem}

Grant divine protection, O Lord, to those you renew with this heavenly gift, that to those who delight in your mysteries, you may give the joy of true peace.

#### December 21 {#misal.die-21-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-21-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord and Ruler will be coming soon, and his name will be Emmanuel, because he will be God with us.

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-decembris.collecta}

Hear in kindness, O Lord, the prayers of your people, that those who rejoice at the coming of your Only Begotten Son in our flesh, may, when at last he comes in glory, gain the reward of eternal life.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-21-decembris.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, to accept the offerings of your Church, for in your mercy you have given them to be offered, and by your power you transform them into the mystery of our salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-21-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are you who have believed, that what was spoken to you by the Lord would be fulfilled.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-21-decembris.post-communionem}

Lord, may participation in this divine mystery provide enduring protection for your people, so that, being subject to your glorious majesty in dedicated service, they may know abundant health in mind and body.

#### December 22 {#misal.die-22-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-22-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

O gates, lift high your heads; grow higher, ancient doors. Let him enter, the king of glory!

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-decembris.collecta}

O God, who, seeing the human race fallen into death, willed to redeem it by the coming of your Only Begotten Son, grant, we pray, that those who confess his Incarnation with humble fervour may merit his company as their Redeemer.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-22-decembris.super-oblata}

Trusting in your compassion, O Lord, we come eagerly with our offerings to your sacred altar, that, by the purifying action of your grace, we may be cleansed through the very mysteries we serve.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-22-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

My soul proclaims the greatness of the Lord, for the Almighty has done great things for me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-22-decembris.post-communionem}

May reception of your Sacrament strengthen us, O Lord, so that we may go out to meet our Saviour with worthy deeds when he comes and merit the rewards of the blessed.

#### December 23 {#misal.die-23-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-23-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

A child shall be born for us, and he will be called God, the Almighty; every tribe of the earth shall be blest in him.

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-decembris.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, as we see how the Nativity of your Son according to the flesh draws near, we pray that to us, your unworthy servants, mercy may flow from your Word, who chose to become flesh of the Virgin Mary and establish among us his dwelling, Jesus Christ our Lord.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-23-decembris.super-oblata}

May this oblation, by which divine worship in its fullness has been inaugurated for us, be our perfect reconciliation with you, O Lord, that we may celebrate with minds made pure the Nativity of our Redeemer.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-23-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, I stand at the door and knock: If anyone hears my voice, and opens the door to me, I will enter his house and dine with him, and he with me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-23-decembris.post-communionem}

Grant your peace, O Lord, to those you have nourished with these heavenly gifts, that we may be ready, with lighted lamps, to meet your dearly beloved Son at his coming.

#### December 24 {#misal.die-24-decembris-ad-missam-matutinam}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-24-decembris-ad-missam-matutinam.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold, when the fullness of time had come, God sent his Son into the world.

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-decembris-ad-missam-matutinam.collecta}

Come quickly, we pray, Lord Jesus, and do not delay, that those who trust in your compassion may find solace and relief in your coming.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-24-decembris-ad-missam-matutinam.super-oblata}

Graciously make your own, O Lord, the offerings which we bring, that partaking of them we may be cleansed of our sins and merit to stand ready with pure hearts for the coming in glory of your Son.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-24-decembris-ad-missam-matutinam.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed be the Lord, the God of Israel! He has visited his people and redeemed them.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-24-decembris-ad-missam-matutinam.post-communionem}

Grant to us who find new vigour, O Lord, in these your wondrous gifts, that as we prepare to celebrate in adoration the festivities of your Son's Nativity, so may we possess in gladness his everlasting rewards.\
Season of Christmas SEASON OF CHRISTMAS

### DECEMBER 25 — NATIVITY OF OUR LORD

#### DECEMBER 25 — NATIVITY OF OUR LORD — VIGIL MASS {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-introitum}

Today you will know that the Lord will come, and he will save us and in the morning you will see his glory.

##### Collect {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.collecta}

O God, who gladden us year by year as we wait in hope for our redemption, grant that just as we joyfully welcome your Only Begotten Son as our Redeemer we may also merit to look upon him confidently when he comes again as our Judge.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.super-oblata}

As we look forward, O Lord, to the coming festivities, may we serve you all the more eagerly for knowing that in them you make manifest the beginnings of our redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-communionem}

The glory of the Lord will be revealed, and all flesh will see the salvation of our God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.post-communionem}

Grant, O Lord, we pray, to draw new vigour from celebrating the Nativity of your Only Begotten Son, by whose heavenly mystery we receive both food and drink. Who lives and reigns for ever and ever.

#### DECEMBER 25 — NATIVITY OF OUR LORD — MIDNIGHT MASS {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-nocte}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-nocte.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord said to me: You are my Son. It is I who have begotten you this day.

*Or:*

Let us all rejoice in the Lord, for our Saviour has been born in the world. Today true peace has come down to us from heaven.

##### Collect {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-nocte.collecta}

O God, who have made this most sacred night radiant with the splendour of the true Light, grant, we pray, that we who have known the mysteries of his light on earth may also delight in his gladness in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-nocte.super-oblata}

May the oblation of this day's feast be pleasing to you, O Lord, we pray, that through this most holy exchange we may be found in the likeness of Christ, in whom our nature is united to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-nocte.ant-ad-communionem}

The Word became flesh, and we have seen his glory.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-nocte.post-communionem}

Grant us, we pray, O Lord our God, that we who are gladdened by participation in the feast of our Redeemer's Nativity may through an honourable way of life become worthy of union with him.

#### DECEMBER 25 — NATIVITY OF OUR LORD — MASS AT DAWN {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-aurora}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-aurora.ant-ad-introitum}

Today a light will shine upon us, for the Lord is born for us; and he will be called Wondrous God, Prince of peace, Father of future ages: his reign will be without end.

##### Collect {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-aurora.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, as we are bathed in the new radiance of your incarnate Word, the light of faith which illumines our minds may also shine through in our deeds.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-aurora.super-oblata}

May our offerings be worthy, we pray, O Lord, of the mysteries of the Nativity this day, that, just as Christ was born a man yet also shone forth as God, so may these earthly gifts confer on us what is divine.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-aurora.ant-ad-communionem}

Rejoice, O Daughter Sion; lift up praise, Daughter Jerusalem: Behold, your King will come, the Holy One and Saviour of the world.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-aurora.post-communionem}

Grant us, Lord, as we honour with joyful devotion the Nativity of your Son, that we may come to know with fullness of faith, the hidden depths of this mystery and to love them ever more and more.

#### DECEMBER 25 — NATIVITY OF OUR LORD — MASS DURING THE DAY {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-die}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-introitum}

A child is born for us, and a son is given to us; his sceptre of power rests upon his shoulder, and his name will be called Messenger of great counsel.

##### Collect {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-die.collecta}

O God, who wonderfully created the dignity of human nature and still more wonderfully restored it, grant, we pray, that we may share in the divinity of Christ who humbled himself to share in our humanity.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-die.super-oblata}

Make acceptable, O Lord, this solemn day's oblation by which our reconciliation has come forth, gaining your infinite favour, and inaugurating for us the fullness of divine worship.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-communionem}

All the ends of the earth have seen the salvation of our God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-nativitate-domini-ad-missam-in-die.post-communionem}

Grant, O merciful God, that just as the Saviour of the world, born this day, is the author of divine generation for us, so may he be the giver even of immortality.

#### DECEMBER 29 — FIFTH DAY OF THE OCTAVE {#misal.die-29-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-29-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

God so loved the world that he gave his Only Begotten Son, so that all who believe in him may not perish, but may have eternal life.

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-decembris.collecta}

Almighty and invisible God, who scattered the darkness of this world by the coming of your light, look, we pray, with serene countenance upon us, that we may acclaim with fitting praise the greatness of the Nativity of your Only Begotten Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-29-decembris.super-oblata}

Receive our oblation, O Lord, by which is brought about a glorious exchange, that by offering what you have given we may merit to receive your very self.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-29-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

Through the tender mercy of our God, the Dawn from on high will visit us.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-29-decembris.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, by the power of these holy mysteries, our life may be constantly sustained.

#### DECEMBER 30 — SIXTH DAY OF THE OCTAVE {#misal.die-30-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-30-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

When a profound silence covered all things, and night was in the middle of its course, your all-powerful Word, O Lord, bounded from heaven's royal throne.

##### Collect {#misal.die-30-decembris.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that the newness of the Nativity in the flesh of your Only Begotten Son may set us free, for ancient servitude holds us bound beneath the yoke of sin.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-30-decembris.super-oblata}

Receive with favour, O Lord, we pray, the offerings of your people, that what they profess with dutiful faith may be theirs through these heavenly mysteries.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-30-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

From his fullness we have all received grace upon grace.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-30-decembris.post-communionem}

O God, who touch us through our partaking of your Sacrament, work, we pray, the effects of its power in our hearts, that we may be made fit to receive your gift through this very gift itself.

#### DECEMBER 31 — SEVENTH DAY OF THE OCTAVE {#misal.die-31-decembris}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-31-decembris.ant-ad-introitum}

A child is born for us, and a son is given to us; his sceptre of power rests upon his shoulder, and his name will be called Messenger of great counsel.

##### Collect {#misal.die-31-decembris.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who in the Nativity of your Son established the beginning and fulfilment of all religion, grant, we pray, that we may be numbered among those who belong to him in whom is the fullness of human salvation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-31-decembris.super-oblata}

O God, who give us the gift of true prayer and of peace, graciously grant that through this offering we may do fitting homage to your divine majesty and by partaking of the sacred mystery may be faithfully united in mind and heart.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-31-decembris.ant-ad-communionem}

God sent his Only Begotten Son into the world, so that we might have life through him.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-31-decembris.post-communionem}

May your people, O Lord, whom you guide and sustain in many ways, experience, both now and in the future, the remedies which you bestow, that with the needed solace of things that pass away, they may strive with ever deepened trust for things eternal.

#### THE HOLY FAMILY OF JESUS, MARY AND JOSEPH \[Feast\] {#misal.s-familiae-iesu-mariae-et-ioseph}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.s-familiae-iesu-mariae-et-ioseph.ant-ad-introitum}

The shepherds went in haste, and found Mary and Joseph and the Infant lying in a manger.

##### Collect {#misal.s-familiae-iesu-mariae-et-ioseph.collecta}

O God, who were pleased to give us the shining example of the Holy Family, graciously grant that we may imitate them in practising the virtues of family life and in the bonds of charity, and so, in the joy of your house, delight one day in eternal rewards.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.s-familiae-iesu-mariae-et-ioseph.super-oblata}

We offer you, Lord, the sacrifice of conciliation, humbly asking that, through the intercession of the Virgin Mother of God and Saint Joseph, you may establish our families firmly in your grace and your peace.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.s-familiae-iesu-mariae-et-ioseph.ant-ad-communionem}

Our God has appeared on the earth, and lived among us.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.s-familiae-iesu-mariae-et-ioseph.post-communionem}

Bring those you refresh with this heavenly sacrament, most merciful Father, to imitate constantly the example of the Holy Family, so that, after the trials of this world, we may share their company for ever.

#### JANUARY 1 — SOLEMNITY OF MARY, MOTHER OF GOD {#misal.sollemnitas-sanctae-dei-genetricis-mariae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.sollemnitas-sanctae-dei-genetricis-mariae.ant-ad-introitum}

Hail, Holy Mother, who gave birth to the King, who rules heaven and earth forever.

*Or:*

Today a light will shine upon us, for the Lord is born for us; and he will be called Wondrous God, Prince of peace, Father of future ages: and his reign will be without end.

##### Collect {#misal.sollemnitas-sanctae-dei-genetricis-mariae.collecta}

O God, who through the fruitful virginity of Blessed Mary bestowed on the human race the grace of eternal salvation, grant, we pray, that we may experience the intercession of her, through whom we were found worthy to receive the author of life, our Lord Jesus Christ, your Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.sollemnitas-sanctae-dei-genetricis-mariae.super-oblata}

O God, who in your kindness begin all good things and bring them to fulfilment, grant to us who find joy in the Solemnity of the holy Mother of God, that, just as we glory in the beginnings of your grace, so one day we may rejoice in its completion.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.sollemnitas-sanctae-dei-genetricis-mariae.ant-ad-communionem}

Jesus Christ is the same yesterday, today, and for ever.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.sollemnitas-sanctae-dei-genetricis-mariae.post-communionem}

We have received this heavenly Sacrament with joy, O Lord: grant, we pray, that it may lead us to ever-living life, for we rejoice to proclaim the blessed ever-Virgin Mary Mother of your Son and Mother of the Church.

#### SECOND SUNDAY AFTER CHRISTMAS {#misal.dominica-ii-post-nativitatem}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ii-post-nativitatem.ant-ad-introitum}

When a profound silence covered all things and night was in the middle of its course, your all-powerful Word, O Lord, bounded from heaven's royal throne.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-ii-post-nativitatem.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, splendour of faithful souls, graciously be pleased to fill the world with your glory and show yourself to all peoples by the radiance of your light.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-ii-post-nativitatem.super-oblata}

Sanctify, O Lord, the offerings we make on the Nativity of your Only Begotten Son, for by it you show us the way of truth and promise the life of the heavenly Kingdom.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ii-post-nativitatem.ant-ad-communionem}

To all who would accept him, he gave the power to become children of God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-ii-post-nativitatem.post-communionem}

Lord our God, we humbly ask you that through the working of this mystery our offences may be cleansed and our just desires fulfilled.

### WEEKDAYS OF THE CHRISTMAS SEASON

#### WEEKDAYS OF THE CHRISTMAS SEASON — MONDAY {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

A holy day has dawned upon us: Come, you nations, and adore the Lord, for a great light has come down upon the earth.

*COLLECT (before Epiphany)*

Grant your people, O Lord, we pray, unshakable strength of faith, so that all who profess that your Only Begotten Son is with you for ever in your glory, and was born of the Virgin Mary in a body truly like our own, may be freed from present trials and given a place in abiding gladness.

*COLLECT (after Epiphany)*

O God, whose eternal Word adorns the face of the heavens yet accepted from the Virgin Mary the frailty of our flesh, grant, we pray, that he who appeared among us as the splendour of truth may go forth in the fullness of power for the redemption of the world.\
\[» \]\
\[» \]

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-ii.super-oblata}

Receive our oblation, O Lord, by which is brought about a glorious exchange, that by offering what you have given, we may merit to receive your very self.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

We have seen his glory, the glory of an only Son coming from the Father, filled with grace and truth.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-ii.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that by the power of the holy mysteries our life may be constantly sustained.

#### WEEKDAYS OF THE CHRISTMAS SEASON — TUESDAY {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed is he who comes in the name of the Lord: The Lord is God and has given us light.

*COLLECT (before Epiphany)*

O God, who in the blessed childbearing of the holy Virgin Mary kept the flesh of your Son free from the sentence incurred by the human race, grant, we pray, that we who have been taken up into this new creation may be freed from the ancient taint of sin.

*COLLECT (after Epiphany)*

O God, whose Only Begotten Son has appeared in our very flesh, grant, we pray, that we may be inwardly transformed through him whom we recognize as outwardly like us.\
\[» \]\
\[» \]

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Receive with favour, Lord, we pray, the offerings of your people, that what they profess with devotion and faith may be theirs through these heavenly mysteries.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

Because of that great love of his with which God loved us, he sent his Son in the likeness of sinful flesh.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iii.post-communionem}

O God, who touch us through our partaking of your Sacrament, work, we pray, the effects of its power in our hearts, that we may be made fit to receive your gift through this very gift itself.

#### WEEKDAYS OF THE CHRISTMAS SEASON — WEDNESDAY {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

A people who walked in darkness have seen a great light; for those dwelling in a land of deep gloom, a light has shone.

*COLLECT (before Epiphany)*

Grant us, almighty God, that the bringer of your salvation, who for the world's redemption came forth with newness of heavenly light, may dawn afresh in our hearts and bring us constant renewal.

*COLLECT (after Epiphany)*

O God, who bestow light on all the nations, grant your peoples the gladness of lasting peace, and pour into our hearts that brilliant light by which you purified the minds of our fathers in faith.\
\[» \]\
\[» \]

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iv.super-oblata}

O God, who give us the gift of true prayer and of peace, graciously grant that through this offering we may do fitting homage to your divine majesty and, by partaking of the sacred mystery may be faithfully united in mind and heart.\
or

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

That life which was with the Father became visible, and has appeared to us.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-iv.post-communionem}

May your people, O Lord, whom you guide and sustain in many ways, experience, both now and in the future, the remedies which you bestow, that with the needed solace of things that pass away, they may strive with ever deepened trust for things eternal.

#### WEEKDAYS OF THE CHRISTMAS SEASON — THURSDAY {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

In the beginning and before all ages, the Word was God and he humbled himself to be born the Saviour of the world.

*COLLECT (before Epiphany)*

O God, who by the Nativity of your Only Begotten Son wondrously began for your people the work of redemption, grant, we pray, to your servants such firmness of faith that by his guidance they may attain the glorious prize you have promised.

*COLLECT (after Epiphany)*

O God, who through your Son raised up your eternal light for all nations, grant that your people may come to acknowledge the full splendour of their Redeemer, and that, bathed ever more in his radiance, they may reach ever-living glory.\
\[» \]\
\[» \]

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-v.super-oblata}

Receive our oblation, O Lord, by which is brought about a glorious exchange, that by offering what you have given, we may merit to receive your very self.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

God so loved the world that he gave his Only Begotten Son, so that all who believe in him may not perish, but may have eternal life.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-v.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that by the power of the holy mysteries our life may be constantly sustained.

#### WEEKDAYS OF THE CHRISTMAS SEASON — FRIDAY {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

A light has risen in the darkness for the upright of heart; the Lord is generous, merciful and just.

*COLLECT (before Epiphany)*

Cast your kindly light upon your faithful, Lord, we pray, and with the splendour of your glory, set their hearts ever aflame, that they may never cease to acknowledge their Saviour and may truly hold fast to him.

*COLLECT (after Epiphany)*

Grant, we ask, almighty God, that the Nativity of the Saviour of the world, made known by the guidance of a star, may be revealed ever more fully to our minds.\
\[» \]\
\[» \]

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Receive with favour, O Lord, we pray, the offerings of your people, that what they profess with devotion and faith may be theirs through these heavenly mysteries.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

By this the love of God was revealed to us: God sent his Only Begotten Son into the world, so that we might have life through him.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-feria-vi.post-communionem}

O God, who touch us through our partaking of your Sacrament, work, we pray, the effects of its power in our hearts, that we may be made fit to receive your gift through this very gift itself.

#### WEEKDAYS OF THE CHRISTMAS SEASON — SATURDAY {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-sabbato}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

God sent his Son, born of a woman, so that we might receive adoption as children.

*COLLECT (before Epiphany)*

Almighty ever-living God, who were pleased to shine forth with new light through the coming of your Only Begotten Son, grant, we pray, that, just as he was pleased to share our bodily form through the childbearing of the Virgin Mary, so we, too, may one day merit to become companions in his kingdom of grace.

*COLLECT (after Epiphany)*

Almighty ever-living God, who through your Only Begotten Son have made us a new creation for yourself, grant, we pray, that by your grace we may be found in the likeness of him, in whom our nature is united to you.\
\[» \]\
\[» \]

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-sabbato.super-oblata}

O God, who give us the gift of true prayer and of peace, graciously grant that, through this offering, we may do fitting homage to your divine majesty and, by partaking of the sacred mystery, we may be faithfully united in mind and heart.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

From his fullness we have all received, grace upon grace.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-feriis-temporis-nativitatis-sabbato.post-communionem}

May your people, O Lord, whom you guide and sustain in many ways, experience, both now and in the future, the remedies which you bestow, that, with the needed solace of things that pass away, they may strive with ever deepened trust for things eternal.

#### THE EPIPHANY OF THE LORD \[Solemnity\] {#misal.in-epiphania-domini-ad-missam-in-die}

*\[Sunday between January 2 and 8, or January 6\]*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-epiphania-domini-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold, the Lord, the Mighty One, has come; and the kingship is in his grasp, and power and dominion.

##### Collect {#misal.in-epiphania-domini-ad-missam-in-die.collecta}

O God, who on this day revealed your Only Begotten Son to the nations by the guidance of a star, grant in your mercy that we who know you already by faith may be brought to behold the beauty of your sublime glory.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-epiphania-domini-ad-missam-in-die.super-oblata}

Look with favour, Lord, we pray, on these gifts of your Church, in which are offered now not gold or frankincense or myrrh but he who by them is proclaimed, sacrificed and received, Jesus Christ.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-epiphania-domini-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-communionem}

We have seen his star in the East, and have come with gifts to adore the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-epiphania-domini-ad-missam-in-die.post-communionem}

Go before us, O Lord, always and everywhere, that we may perceive with clear sight and revere with true affection the mystery in which you have willed us to participate.

#### THE BAPTISM OF THE LORD \[Feast\] — First Sunday in Ordinary Time {#misal.in-baptismate-domini}

*\[Sunday after January 6\]*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-baptismate-domini.ant-ad-introitum}

After the Lord was baptized, the heavens were opened, and the Spirit descended upon him like a dove, and the voice of the Father thundered: "This is my beloved Son, with whom I am well pleased."

##### Collect {#misal.in-baptismate-domini.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who, when Christ had been baptized in the River Jordan and as the Holy Spirit descended upon him, solemnly declared him your beloved Son, grant that your children by adoption, reborn of water and the Holy Spirit, may always be well pleasing to you.

*Or:*

O God, whose Only Begotten Son has appeared in our very flesh, grant, we pray, that we may be inwardly transformed through him whom we recognize as outwardly like ourselves.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-baptismate-domini.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, the offerings we have brought to honour the revealing of your beloved Son, so that the oblation of your faithful may be transformed into the sacrifice of him who willed in his compassion to wash away the sins of the world. Who lives and reigns for ever and ever.

##### Preface {#misal.in-baptismate-domini.praefatio}

Consecration and mission of Christ\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God.\
For in the waters of the Jordan you revealed with signs and wonders a new Baptism, so that through the voice that came down from heaven we might come to believe in your Word dwelling among us, and by the Spirit’s descending in the likeness of a dove we might know that Christ your Servant has been anointed with the oil of gladness and sent to bring the good news to the poor.\
And so, with the Powers of heaven, we worship you constantly on earth, and before your majesty without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-baptismate-domini.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold the One of whom John said: 'I have seen and testified that this is the Son of God.'

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-baptismate-domini.post-communionem}

Nourished with these sacred gifts, we humbly entreat your mercy, O Lord, that, faithfully listening to your Only Begotten Son, we may be your children in name and in truth.\
Season of Lent SEASON OF LENT\
First Week of Lent\
Second Week of Lent\
Third Week of Lent

*Fourth Week of Lent*

*Fifth Week of Lent*

#### ASH WEDNESDAY {#misal.feria-quarta-cinerum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.feria-quarta-cinerum.ant-ad-introitum}

You are merciful to all, O Lord, and despise nothing that you have made. You overlook people's sins, to bring them to repentance, and you spare them, for you are the Lord our God.

*The penitential rite and the Gloria are not said.*

##### Collect {#misal.feria-quarta-cinerum.collecta}

Grant, O Lord, that we may begin with holy fasting this campaign of Christian service, so that as we take up battle against spiritual evils, we may be armed with weapons of self-restraint.

*BLESSING AND IMPOSITION OF THE ASHES*

*After the Homily, the Priest, standing with hands joined, says:*

Dear brothers and sisters, let us humbly ask God our Father that he be pleased to bless with the abundance of his grace these ashes which we will put on our heads in penitence.

*After a brief prayer in silence, and, with hands extended, he continues:*

O God, who are moved by acts of humility and respond with forgiveness to works of penance, lend your merciful ear to our prayers, and in your kindness pour out the grace of your + blessing on your servants who are marked with these ashes, that as they follow the Lenten observances, they may be worthy to come with minds made pure to celebrate the paschal mystery of your Son. Through Christ our Lord.\
Amen.

*Or:*

O God, who desire not the death of sinners but their conversion, mercifully hear our prayers, and in your kindness be pleased to bless + these ashes, which we intend to receive upon our heads, that we, who acknowledge we are but ashes and shall return to dust, may, through a steadfast observance of Lent, gain pardon for sins and newness of life after the likeness of your Risen Son. Who lives and reigns for ever and ever.\
Amen.

*Then the Priest places ashes on all present, who come to him, and says to each one:*

Repent, and believe in the Gospel.

*Or:*

Remember that you are dust, and to dust you shall return.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.feria-quarta-cinerum.super-oblata}

As we solemnly offer the annual sacrifice for the beginning of Lent we entreat you, O Lord, that through works of penance and charity we may turn away from harmful pleasures, and cleansed from our sins, may become worthy to celebrate devoutly the Passion of your Son.

*\[Preface of Lent or \]*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.feria-quarta-cinerum.ant-ad-communionem}

He who ponders the law of the Lord day and night will yield fruit in due season.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.feria-quarta-cinerum.post-communionem}

May the Sacrament we have received sustain us, O Lord, that our Lenten fast may be pleasing to you and be for us a healing remedy.

*For the dismissal, the Priest says this prayer:*

##### Prayer Over The People {#misal.feria-quarta-cinerum.oratio-super-populum}

Pour out a spirit of compunction, O God, on those who bow before your majesty, and by your mercy may they merit the rewards you promise to those who do penance.

#### THURSDAY AFTER ASH WEDNESDAY {#misal.feria-quinta-post-cineres}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.feria-quinta-post-cineres.ant-ad-introitum}

When I cried to the Lord, he heard my voice; he rescued me from those who attack me. Entrust your cares to the Lord, and he will support you.

##### Collect {#misal.feria-quinta-post-cineres.collecta}

Prompt our actions with your inspiration, we pray, O Lord, and further them with your constant help, that all we do may always begin from you and by you be brought to completion.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.feria-quinta-post-cineres.super-oblata}

Regard with favour, O Lord, we pray, the offerings we set upon this sacred altar, that, bestowing on us your pardon, our oblations may give honour to your Name.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.feria-quinta-post-cineres.ant-ad-communionem}

Create a pure heart for me, O God; renew a steadfast spirit within me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.feria-quinta-post-cineres.post-communionem}

Having received the blessing of your heavenly gifts, we humbly beseech you, almighty God, that they may always be for us a source both of pardon and of salvation.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Almighty God, who have made known to your people the pathways to eternal life, lead it along them, we pray, to you, the unfading light.

#### FRIDAY AFTER ASH WEDNESDAY {#misal.feria-sexta-post-cineres}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.feria-sexta-post-cineres.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord heard and had mercy on me; the Lord became my helper.

##### Collect {#misal.feria-sexta-post-cineres.collecta}

Show gracious favour, O Lord, we pray, to the works of penance we have begun, that we may have strength to accomplish with sincerity the bodily observances we undertake.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.feria-sexta-post-cineres.super-oblata}

We offer, O Lord, the sacrifice of our Lenten observance, praying that it may make our intentions acceptable to you and add to our powers of self-restraint.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.feria-sexta-post-cineres.ant-ad-communionem}

O Lord, make me know your ways, teach me your paths.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.feria-sexta-post-cineres.post-communionem}

We pray, almighty God, that through partaking of this mystery, we may be cleansed of all our misdeeds, and so be suited for the remedies of your compassion.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

For your mighty deeds, O God of mercy, may your people offer endless thanks, and, by observing the age-old disciplines along its pilgrim journey, may it merit to come and behold you for ever.

#### SATURDAY AFTER ASH WEDNESDAY {#misal.sabbato-post-cineres}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.sabbato-post-cineres.ant-ad-introitum}

Answer us, Lord, for your mercy is kind; in the abundance of your mercies, look upon us.

##### Collect {#misal.sabbato-post-cineres.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, look with compassion on our weakness and ensure us your protection by stretching forth the right hand of your majesty.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.sabbato-post-cineres.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, the sacrifice of conciliation and praise, and grant that cleansed by its working, we may offer minds well pleasing to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.sabbato-post-cineres.ant-ad-communionem}

I desire mercy, not sacrifice, says the Lord, for I came to call not the just but sinners.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.sabbato-post-cineres.post-communionem}

Nourished with the gift of heavenly life, we pray, O Lord, that what remains for us a mystery in this present life may be for us a help to reach eternity.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Abide graciously, O Lord, with your people who have touched the sacred mysteries, that no dangers may bring affliction to those who trust in you, their protector.\
FIRST WEEK OF LENT

#### First Sunday of Lent {#misal.dominica-i-in-quadragesima}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-i-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-introitum}

When he calls on me, I will answer him; I will deliver him and give him glory, I will grant him length of days.

*The Gloria is not said.*

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-i-in-quadragesima.collecta}

Grant, almighty God, through the yearly observances of holy Lent, that we may grow in understanding of the riches hidden in Christ and by worthy conduct pursue their effects.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-i-in-quadragesima.super-oblata}

Give us the right dispositions, O Lord, we pray, to make these offerings, for with them we celebrate the beginning of this venerable and sacred time.

##### Preface {#misal.dominica-i-in-quadragesima.praefatio}

The temptation of the Lord\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God, through Christ our Lord.\
By abstaining forty long days from earthly food he consecrated by his fast the pattern of our Lenten observance, and by overturning all the snares of the ancient serpent taught us to cast out the leaven of malice, so that, celebrating worthily the paschal mystery, we might pass over at last to the eternal paschal feast.\
And so, with the company of Angels and Saints, we sing the hymn of your praise, as without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-i-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-communionem}

One does not live by bread alone, but by every word that comes forth from the mouth of God.

*Or:*

The Lord will conceal you with his pinions, and under his wings you will trust.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-i-in-quadragesima.post-communionem}

Renewed now with heavenly bread by which faith is nourished, hope increased, and and charity strengthened, we pray, O Lord, that we may learn to hunger for Christ, the true and living Bread and strive to live by every word which proceeds from your mouth.

##### Prayer Over The People {#misal.dominica-i-in-quadragesima.oratio-super-populum}

May bountiful blessing, O Lord, we pray, come down upon your people, that hope may grow in tribulation, virtue be strengthened in temptation, and eternal redemption be assured.

#### Monday in the First Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

Like the eyes of slaves on the hand of their lords, so our eyes are on the Lord our God, till he show us his mercy. Have mercy on us, Lord, have mercy.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.collecta}

Convert us, O God our Saviour, and instruct our minds by heavenly teaching that we may benefit from the works of Lent.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.super-oblata}

May this devout oblation be acceptable to you, O Lord, that by your power it may sanctify our manner of life and gain for us your conciliation and pardon.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

Amen, I say to you, whatever you did for one of the least of my brethren, you did for me, says the Lord. Come, you blessed of my Father, receive the kingdom prepared for you from the foundation of the world.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.post-communionem}

We pray, O Lord, that in receiving your Sacrament we may experience help in mind and body so that, kept safe in both, we may glory in the fullness of heavenly healing.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Enlighten the minds of your people, Lord, we pray, with the light of your glory, that they may see what must be done and have the strength to do what is right.

#### Tuesday in the First Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

O Lord, you have been our refuge, from generation to generation; from age to age, you are.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.collecta}

Look upon your family, Lord, that through the chastening effects of bodily discipline, our minds may be radiant in your presence with the strength of our yearning for you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Receive, O Creator, almighty God, what we bring from your bountiful goodness, and be pleased to transform this temporal sustenance you have given us, that it may bring us eternal life.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

When I called, the God of justice gave me answer; from anguish you released me, have mercy, O Lord, and hear my prayer!

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.post-communionem}

Grant us through these mysteries, Lord, that by tempering earthly desires we may learn to love the things of heaven.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

May your faithful be strengthened, O God, by your blessing: in grief, may you be their consolation, in tribulation, their power to endure, and in peril, their protection.

#### Wednesday in the First Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

Remember your compassion, O Lord, and your merciful love, for they are from of old. Let not our enemies exult over us. Redeem us, O God of Israel, from all our distress.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.collecta}

Look kindly, Lord, we pray, on the devotion of your people, that those who by self-denial are restrained in body may by the fruit of good works be renewed in mind.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.super-oblata}

We offer to you, O Lord, what you have given to be dedicated to your name that just as for our benefit you make these gifts a Sacrament, so let them become for us a eternal remedy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

All who take refuge in you shall be glad, O Lord, and ever cry out their joy, and you shall dwell among them.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.post-communionem}

O God, who never cease to nourish us by your Sacrament, grant that the refreshment you give us through it may bring us unending life.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Watch over your people, Lord, and in your kindness, cleanse them from all sins, for if evil has no dominion over them no trial can do them harm.

#### Thursday in the First Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

To my words give ear, O Lord; give heed to my sighs. Attend to the sound of my cry, my King and my God.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-v.collecta}

Bestow on us, we pray, O Lord, a spirit of always pondering on what is right and of hastening to carry it out, and since without you we cannot exist may we be enabled to live according to your will.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-v.super-oblata}

Be merciful, O Lord, to those who approach you in supplication, and, accepting the oblations and prayers of your people, turn the hearts of us all towards you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

Everyone who asks, receives; and the one who seeks, finds; and to the one who knocks, the door will be opened.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-v.post-communionem}

We pray, O Lord our God, that, as you have given these most sacred mysteries to be the safeguard of our salvation so you may make them a healing remedy for us both now and in time to come.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

May the mercy they have hoped for, O Lord, come to those who make supplication to you, and may the riches of heaven be given them, that they may know what it is right to ask and receive what they have sought.

#### Friday in the First Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

Set me free from my distress, O Lord. See my lowliness and suffering, and take away all my sins.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.collecta}

Grant that your faithful, O Lord, we pray, may be so conformed to the paschal observances, that the bodily discipline now solemnly begun may bear fruit in the souls of all.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Accept the sacrificial offerings, O Lord, by which in your power and kindness you willed us to be reconciled to yourself and our salvation to be restored.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

As I live, says the Lord, I do not desire the death of a sinner, but rather that he turn back and live.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.post-communionem}

May the holy refreshment of your Sacrament restore us anew, O Lord, and, cleansing us of old ways, take us up into the mystery of salvation.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Look with favour on your people, O Lord, that what their observance outwardly declares it may inwardly bring about.

#### Saturday in the First Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-sabbato}
