##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

The law of the Lord is perfect; it revives the soul. The decrees of the Lord are steadfast; they give wisdom to the simple.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-sabbato.collecta}

Turn our hearts to you, eternal Father, and grant that, seeking always the one thing necessary and carrying out works of charity, we may be dedicated to your worship.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-sabbato.super-oblata}

May these blessed mysteries by which we are restored, O Lord, we pray, make us worthy of the gift they bestow.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

Be perfect, as your heavenly Father is perfect, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-in-quadragesima-sabbato.post-communionem}

Show unceasing favour, O Lord, to those you refresh with this divine mystery, and accompany with salutary consolations those you have imbued with heavenly teaching.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

May the blessing for which they have longed strengthen your faithful, O God, so that, never straying from your will, they may always rejoice in your benefits.\
SECOND WEEK OF LENT

#### Second Sunday of Lent {#misal.dominica-ii-in-quadragesima}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ii-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-introitum}

Of you my heart has spoken: Seek his face. It is your face, O Lord, that I seek; hide not your face from me.

*Or:*

Remember your compassion, O Lord, and your merciful love, for they are from of old. Let not our enemies exult over us. Redeem us, O God of Israel, from all our distress.

*The Gloria is not said.*

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-ii-in-quadragesima.collecta}

O God, who have commanded us to listen to your beloved Son, be pleased, we pray, to nourish us inwardly by your word, that with spiritual sight made pure, we may rejoice to behold your glory.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-ii-in-quadragesima.super-oblata}

May this sacrifice, O Lord, we pray, cleanse us of our faults and sanctify your faithful in body and mind for the celebration of the paschal festivities.

##### Preface {#misal.dominica-ii-in-quadragesima.praefatio}

Transfiguration\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God, through Christ our Lord.\
For after he had told the disciples of his coming Death, on the holy mountain he manifested to them his glory, to show, even by the testimony of the law and the prophets, that the Passion leads to the glory of the Resurrection.\
And so, with the Powers of heaven, we worship you constantly on earth, and before your majesty without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ii-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-communionem}

This is my beloved Son with whom I am well pleased; listen to him.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-ii-in-quadragesima.post-communionem}

As we receive these glorious mysteries, we make thanksgiving to you, O Lord, for allowing us while still on earth to be partakers even now in the things of heaven.

##### Prayer Over The People {#misal.dominica-ii-in-quadragesima.oratio-super-populum}

Bless your faithful, we pray, O Lord, with a blessing that endures for ever, and keep them faithful to the Gospel of your Only Begotten Son, so that they may always desire and at last attain that glory whose beauty he showed in his own Body, to the amazement of his Apostles.

#### Monday in the Second Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

Redeem me, O Lord, and have mercy on me. My foot stands on level ground: I will bless the Lord in the assembly.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.collecta}

O God, who have taught us to chasten our bodies for the healing of our souls, enable us, we pray, to abstain from all sins and strengthen our hearts to carry out your loving commands.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.super-oblata}

Accept in your goodness these our prayers, O Lord, and set free from worldly attractions those you allow to serve the heavenly mysteries.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

Be merciful as your Father is merciful, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.post-communionem}

May this Communion, O Lord, cleanse us of wrongdoing and make us heirs to the joy of heaven.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Confirm the hearts of your faithful, O Lord, we pray, and strengthen them by the power of your grace, that they may be constant in making supplication to you and sincere in mutual love.

#### Tuesday in the Second Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

Give light to my eyes lest I fall asleep in death; lest my enemy say, I have overcome him.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.collecta}

Guard your Church, we pray, O Lord, in your unceasing mercy, and, since without you mortal humanity is sure to fall, may we be kept by your constant helps from all harm and directed to all that brings salvation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Be pleased to work your sanctification within us, by means of these mysteries, O Lord, for by it we are cleansed of earthly faults and led to the gifts of heaven.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

I will recount all your wonders. I will rejoice in you and be glad, and sing psalms to your name, O Most High.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.post-communionem}

May the refreshment of this sacred table, O Lord, we pray, bring us an increase in devoutness of life and the constant help of your work of conciliation.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Graciously hear the cries of your faithful, O Lord, and relieve the weariness of their souls, that having received your forgiveness, they may ever rejoice in your blessing.

#### Wednesday in the Second Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

Forsake me not, O Lord! My God, be not far from me! Make haste and come to my help, O Lord, my strong salvation!

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.collecta}

Keep your family, O Lord, schooled always in good works, and so comfort them with your protection here as to lead them graciously to gifts on high.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.super-oblata}

Look on with favour, Lord, the sacrificial gifts we offer you, and by this holy exchange undo the bonds of our sins.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

The Son of Man did not come to be served but to serve, and to give his life as a ransom for many.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, O Lord our God, that what you have given us as the pledge of immortality may work for our eternal salvation.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Bestow upon your servants, Lord, abundance of grace and protection, grant health of mind and body, grant fullness of fraternal charity, and make them always devoted to you.

#### Thursday in the Second Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

Test me, O God, and know my thoughts. See that my path is not wicked, and lead me in the way everlasting.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.collecta}

O God, who delight in innocence and restore it, direct the hearts of your servants to yourself, that, caught up in the fire of your Spirit, we may be found steadfast in faith and effective in works.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.super-oblata}

By this present sacrifice, we pray, O Lord, sanctify our observance, that what Lenten discipline outwardly declares it may inwardly bring about.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are those whose way is blameless, who walk in the law of the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.post-communionem}

May this sacrifice, O God, remain active in its effects and work ever more strongly within us.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Abide with your servants, O Lord, who implore the help of your grace, that they may receive from you the support and guidance of your protection.

#### Friday in the Second Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

In you, O Lord, I put my trust, let me never be put to shame. Release me from the snare they have hidden for me, for you indeed are my refuge.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, purifying us by the sacred practice of penance, you may lead us in sincerity of heart to attain the holy things to come.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.super-oblata}

May your merciful grace prepare your servants, O God, for the worthy celebration of these mysteries, and lead them to it by a devout way of life.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

God loved us, and sent his Son as expiation for our sins.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.post-communionem}

Having received this pledge of eternal salvation, we pray, O Lord, that we may set our course so well as to attain the redemption you promise.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Grant your people, O Lord, we pray, health of mind and body, that by constancy in good deeds it may always merit the defence of your protection.

#### Saturday in the Second Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-sabbato}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord is kind and full of compassion, slow to anger, abounding in mercy. How good is the Lord to all, compassionate to all his creatures.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.collecta}

O God, who grant us by glorious healing remedies while still on earth, to be partakers of the things of heaven, guide us, we pray, through this present life, and bring us to that light in which you dwell.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.super-oblata}

Through these sacred gifts, we pray, O Lord, may our redemption yield its fruits, restraining us from unruly desires and leading us onward to the gifts of salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

You must rejoice, my son, for your brother was dead and has come to life; he was lost and is found.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-ii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.post-communionem}

May your divine Sacrament, O Lord, which we have received, fill the inner depths of our heart and by its working mightily within us make us partakers of its grace.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

May the ears of your mercy be open, O Lord, to the prayers of those who call upon you and that you may grant what they desire, have them ask what is pleasing to you.\
THIRD WEEK OF LENT

#### Third Sunday of Lent {#misal.dominica-iii-in-quadragesima}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iii-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-introitum}

My eyes are always on the Lord, for he rescues my feet from the snare. Turn to me and have mercy on me, for I am alone and poor.

*Or:*

When I prove my holiness among you, I will gather you from all the foreign lands; and I will pour clean water upon you and cleanse you from all your impurities, and I will give you a new spirit, says the Lord.

*The Gloria is not said.*

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-iii-in-quadragesima.collecta}

O God, author of every mercy and of all goodness, who in fasting, prayer and almsgiving have shown us a remedy for sin, look graciously on this confession of our lowliness, that we who are bowed down by our conscience may always be lifted up by your mercy.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-iii-in-quadragesima.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, with these sacrificial offerings, and grant that we who beseech pardon for our own sins may take care to forgive our neighbour.

*or:*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iii-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-communionem}

*When the gospel of the Samaritan woman is read:*

For anyone who drinks it, says the Lord, the water I shall give will become in him a spring welling up to eternal life.

*When other gospels are read:*

The sparrow finds a home, and the swallow a nest where she sets her young: by your altars, O Lord of hosts, my King and my God. Blessed are they who dwell in your house, for ever singing your praise.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-iii-in-quadragesima.post-communionem}

As we receive the pledge of things yet hidden in heaven, and are nourished while still on earth with the Bread that comes from on high, we humbly entreat you, O Lord, that what is being brought about in us in mystery may come to true completion.

##### Prayer Over The People {#misal.dominica-iii-in-quadragesima.oratio-super-populum}

Direct, O Lord, we pray, the hearts of your faithful, and in your kindness grant your servants this grace: that, abiding in the love of you and their neighbour, they may fulfil the whole of your commands.

#### Monday in the Third Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

My soul is longing and yearning for the courts of the Lord. My heart and my flesh cry out to the living God.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.collecta}

May your unfailing compassion, O Lord, cleanse and protect your Church, and since without you she cannot stand secure, may she be always governed by your grace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.super-oblata}

May what we offer you, O Lord, in token of our service, be transformed by you into the sacrament of salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

O praise the Lord, all you nations, for his merciful love towards us is great.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.post-communionem}

May communion in this your Sacrament, we pray, O Lord, bring with it purification and the unity that is your gift.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

May your right hand, we ask, O Lord, protect this people that makes entreaty to you: graciously purify them and give them instruction, that finding solace in this life, they may reach the good things to come.

#### Tuesday in the Third Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

To you I call, for you will surely heed me, O God; turn your ear to me; hear my words. Guard me as the apple of your eye; in the shadow of your wings protect me.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.collecta}

May your grace not forsake us, O Lord, we pray, but make us dedicated to your holy service and obtain us your help at all times.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Grant us, O Lord, we pray, that this saving sacrifice may cleanse us of our faults and become an oblation pleasing to your almighty power.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

Lord, who may abide in your tent, and dwell on your holy mountain? Whoever walks without fault and does what is just.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.post-communionem}

May the holy partaking of this mystery give us life, O Lord, we pray, and grant us both pardon and protection.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

O God, founder and ruler of your people, drive away the sins that assail them, that they may always be pleasing to you and ever safe under your protection.

#### Wednesday in the Third Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

Let my steps be guided by your promise; may evil never rule me.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.collecta}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that, schooled through Lenten observance and nourished by your word, through holy restraint we may be devoted to you with all our heart and be ever united in prayer.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, we pray, the prayers of your people along with these sacrificial offerings, and defend those who celebrate your mysteries from every kind of danger.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

You will show me the path of life, the fullness of joy in your presence, O Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.post-communionem}

May the heavenly banquet at which we have been fed, sanctify us, O Lord, and cleansing us of all errors, make us worthy of your promises from on high.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Give to your people, our God, a resolve that is pleasing to you, for by conforming them to your teachings, you bestow on them every favour.

#### Thursday in the Third Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

I am the salvation of the people, says the Lord. Should they cry to me in any distress, I will hear them, and I will be their Lord for ever.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.collecta}

We implore your majesty most humbly, O Lord, that as the feast day of our salvation draws ever closer, so we may press forward all the more eagerly towards the worthy celebration of the paschal mystery.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.super-oblata}

Cleanse your people, Lord, we pray, from every taint of wickedness, that their gifts may be pleasing to you, and do not let them cling to false joys, for you promise the rewards of your truth.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

You have laid down your precepts to be carefully kept; may my ways be firm in keeping your statutes.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-v.post-communionem}

Graciously raise up, O Lord, those you renew with this Sacrament, that we may come to possess your salvation both in mystery and in the manner of our life.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

We call on your loving kindness and trust in your mercy, O Lord, that since we have from you all that we are, through your grace we may seek what is right and have strength to do the good we desire.

#### Friday in the Third Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

Among the gods there is none like you, O Lord, for you are great and do marvellous deeds; you alone are God.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.collecta}

Pour your grace into our hearts, we pray, O Lord, that we may be constantly drawn away from unruly desires and obey by your own gift the heavenly teaching you give us.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Look with favour, we pray, Lord, on the offerings we dedicate, that they may be pleasing in your sight and always be salutary for us.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

To love God with all your heart, and your neighbour as yourself, is worth more than any sacrifice.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.post-communionem}

May your strength be at work in us, O Lord, pervading our minds and bodies, that what we have partaken of here we may possess in the fullness of redemption.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Look graciously, O Lord, upon the faithful who implore your mercy, that trusting in your kindness they may spread far and wide the gifts your charity has bestowed.

#### Saturday in the Third Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-sabbato}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

Bless the Lord, O my soul, and never forget all his benefits; it is he who forgives all your sins.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.collecta}

Rejoicing in this annual celebration of our Lenten observance, we pray, O Lord, that, with our hearts set on the paschal mysteries, we may be gladdened by their full effects.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.super-oblata}

O God, by whose grace it comes to pass that we may approach your mysteries with minds made pure, grant, we pray, that in reverently handing them on we may offer you homage that is fitting.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

The tax collector stood at a distance, beating his breast and saying: O God, be merciful to me, a sinner.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iii-in-quadragesima-sabbato.post-communionem}

May we truly revere, O merciful God, these holy gifts by which you ceaselessly nourish us, and may we always partake of them with abundant faith in our heart.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Hold out to your faithful people, Lord, the right hand of heavenly assistance, that they may seek you with all their heart and merit the granting of what they ask.\
FOURTH WEEK OF LENT

#### Fourth Sunday of Lent {#misal.dominica-iv-in-quadragesima}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iv-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-introitum}

Rejoice, Jerusalem, and all who love her. Be joyful, all who were in mourning; exult and be satisfied at her consoling breast.

*The Gloria is not said.*

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-iv-in-quadragesima.collecta}

O God, who through your Word reconcile the human race to yourself in a wonderful way, grant, we pray, that with prompt devotion and eager faith the Christian people may hasten toward the solemn celebrations to come.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-iv-in-quadragesima.super-oblata}

We place before you with joy these offerings, which bring eternal remedy, O Lord, praying that we may both faithfully revere them and present them to you, as is fitting, for the salvation of all the world.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iv-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-communionem}

*Year A:*

The Lord anointed my eyes: I went away and I washed, I saw and I believed in God.

*Year B:*

Jerusalem is built as a city bonded as one together. It is there that the tribes go up, the tribes of the Lord, to praise the name of the Lord.

*Year C:*

You must rejoice, my son, for your brother was dead and has come to life; he was lost and is found.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-iv-in-quadragesima.post-communionem}

O God, who enlighten everyone who comes into this world, illuminate our hearts, we pray, with the splendour of your grace, that we may always ponder what is worthy and pleasing to your majesty and love you in all sincerity.

##### Prayer Over The People {#misal.dominica-iv-in-quadragesima.oratio-super-populum}

Look upon those who call to you, O Lord, and sustain the weak; give life by your unfailing light to those who walk in the shadow of death, and bring those rescued by your mercy from every evil to reach the highest good.

#### Monday in the Fourth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

As for me, I trust in the Lord. Let me be glad and rejoice in your mercy, for you have seen my affliction.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.collecta}

O God, who renew the world through mysteries beyond all telling, grant, we pray, that your Church may be guided by your eternal design and not be deprived of your help in this present age.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.super-oblata}

May we receive O Lord, we pray, the effects of this offering dedicated to you, so that, we may be cleansed from old earthly ways, and be renewed by growth in heavenly life.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

I will place my spirit within you and make you walk according to my laws; and my judgments you shall keep and observe, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.post-communionem}

May your holy gifts, O Lord, we pray, give us life by making us new and by sanctifying us lead us to things eternal.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Renew your people within and without, O Lord, and since it is your will that they be unhindered by bodily delights give them, we pray, perseverance in their spiritual intent.

#### Tuesday in the Fourth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

All who are thirsty, come to the waters, says the Lord. Though you have no money, come and drink with joy.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.collecta}

May the venerable exercises of holy devotion shape the hearts of your faithful, O Lord, to welcome worthily the paschal mystery and proclaim the praises of your salvation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.super-oblata}

We offer to you, O Lord, these gifts you yourself have bestowed; may they attest to your care as creator for this our mortal life, and effect in us the healing that brings us immortality.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

The Lord is my shepherd; there is nothing I shall want. Fresh and green are the pastures where he gives me repose, near restful waters he leads me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.post-communionem}

Purify our minds, O Lord, we pray, and renew them with this heavenly Sacrament, that we may find help for our bodies now and likewise in times to come.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Grant, O merciful God, that your people may remain always devoted to you and may constantly receive from your kindness whatever is for their good.

#### Wednesday in the Fourth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

I pray to you, O Lord, for a time of your favour. In your great mercy, answer me, O God, with your salvation that never fails.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.collecta}

O God, who reward the merits of the just and offer pardon to sinners who do penance, have mercy, we pray, on those who call upon you, that the confession of our guilt may serve to obtain your pardon for our sins.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.super-oblata}

May the power of this sacrifice, O Lord, we pray, mercifully wipe away what is old in us, and increase in us grace of salvation and newness of life.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

God did not send his Son into the world to judge the world, but that the world might be saved through him.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.post-communionem}

May your heavenly gifts, O Lord, we pray, which you bestow as a heavenly remedy on your people, not bring judgment to those who receive them.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

May your servants be shielded, O Lord, by the protection of your loving-kindness, that, doing what is good in this world, they may reach you, their highest good.

#### Thursday in the Fourth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

Let the hearts that seek the Lord rejoice. Turn to the Lord and his strength; constantly seek his face.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-v.collecta}

We invoke your mercy in humble prayer, O Lord, that you may cause us, your servants, corrected by penance and schooled by good works, to persevere sincerely in your commands and come safely to the paschal festivities.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-v.super-oblata}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that what we offer in sacrifice may cleanse us in our frailty from every evil, and always grant us your protection.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

I will place my law within them, and I will write it upon their hearts; and I will be their God, and they shall be my people, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-v.post-communionem}

May this Sacrament we have received purify us, we pray, O Lord; and grant your servants freedom from all blame, that those bound by a guilty conscience may glory in fullness of heavenly remedy.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

O God, protector of all who hope in you, bless your people, keep them safe, defend them, prepare them, that, free from sin and safe from the enemy, they may persevere always in your love.

#### Friday in the Fourth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

O God, save me by your name; by your power, defend my cause. O God, hear my prayer; give ear to the words of my mouth.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.collecta}

O God, who have prepared fitting helps for us in our weakness, grant, we pray, that we may receive their healing effects with joy, and reflect them in a holy way of life.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.super-oblata}

May this sacrifice, almighty God, cleanse us by its mighty power, and lead us to approach its source with ever greater purity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

In Christ, we have redemption by his blood, and forgiveness of our sins, in accord with the riches of his grace.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that as we pass from old to new so, with former ways left behind, we may be made new in holiness of mind.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Look upon your servants, O Lord, and in your goodness protect with heavenly assistance those who trust in your mercy.

#### Saturday in the Fourth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-sabbato}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

The waves of death rose about me; the pains of the netherworld surrounded me. In my anguish I called to the Lord, and from his holy temple he heard my voice.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-sabbato.collecta}

May the working of your mercy, O Lord, we pray, direct our hearts aright, for without your grace we cannot find favour in your sight.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-sabbato.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, we pray, with these oblations you receive from our hands, and even when our wills are defiant constrain them mercifully to turn to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

By the precious blood of Christ, the blood of a spotless and unblemished Lamb, we have been redeemed.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-iv-in-quadragesima-sabbato.post-communionem}

May your holy gifts purify us, O Lord, we pray, and by their action render us fully pleasing to you.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Look upon your people, O Lord, and as they draw near to the coming festivities, bestow upon them abundance of heavenly grace that, helped by tangible consolations, they may be impelled more readily towards the good things they cannot see.\
FIFTH WEEK OF LENT

#### Fifth Sunday of Lent {#misal.dominica-v-in-quadragesima}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-v-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-introitum}

Give me justice, O God, and plead my cause against a nation that is faithless. From the deceitful and cunning rescue me, for you, O God, are my strength.

*The Gloria is not said.*

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-v-in-quadragesima.collecta}

By your help, we beseech you, Lord our God, may we walk eagerly in that same charity with which, out of love for the world, your Son handed himself over to death.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-v-in-quadragesima.super-oblata}

Hear us, almighty God, and having instilled in your servants the teachings of the Christian faith, graciously purify them by the working of this sacrifice.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-v-in-quadragesima.ant-ad-communionem}

*When the gospel of Lazarus is read (Year A):*

Everyone who lives and believes in me will not die for ever, says the Lord.

*When the gospel of the adulteress is read (Year C):*

Has no one condemned you, woman? No one, Lord. Neither shall I condemn you. From now on, sin no more.

*When other gospels are read:*

Amen, amen I say to you: Unless a grain of wheat falls to the ground and dies, it remains a single grain. But if it dies, it bears much fruit.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-v-in-quadragesima.post-communionem}

We pray, almighty God, that we may always be counted among the members of Christ, in whose Body and Blood we have communion.

##### Prayer Over The People {#misal.dominica-v-in-quadragesima.oratio-super-populum}

Bless, O Lord, your people who long for the gift of your mercy, and grant that what, at your prompting, they desire they may receive by your generous gift.

#### Monday in the Fifth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

Have mercy on me, O God, for people assail me; they fight me all day long and oppress me.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.collecta}

O God, by whose wondrous grace we are enriched with every blessing, grant us so to pass from former ways to newness of life that we may be made ready for the glory of the heavenly Kingdom.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.super-oblata}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that preparing to celebrate the holy mysteries we may bring before you as the fruit of bodily penance a joyful purity of heart.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

*When the gospel of the adulteress is read:*

Has no one condemned you, woman? No one, Lord. Neither shall I condemn you. From now on, sin no more.

*When other gospels are read:*

I am the light of the world, says the Lord; whoever follows me will not walk in the darkness, but will have the light of life.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-ii.post-communionem}

Strengthened by the blessing of your Sacraments, we pray, O Lord, that through them we may constantly be cleansed of our faults and by following Christ, hasten our steps upward towards you.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Set free from their sins, O Lord, we pray, the people that calls upon you that, living a holy way of life, they may be kept safe from every trial.

#### Tuesday in the Fifth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

Wait for the Lord; be strong; be stout-hearted, and wait for the Lord!

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.collecta}

Grant us, we pray, O Lord, perseverance in obeying your will, that in our days the people dedicated to your service may grow in both merit and number.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.super-oblata}

We offer you, O Lord, the sacrifice of conciliation, that, being moved to compassion, you may both absolve our offences and direct our wavering hearts.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

When I am lifted up from the earth, I will draw all to myself, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iii.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that ever seeking what is divine, we may always be worthy to approach these heavenly gifts.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

O God, who choose to show mercy not anger to those who hope in you, grant that your faithful may weep, as they should, for the evil they have done, and so merit the grace of your consolation.

#### Wednesday in the Fifth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

My deliverer from angry nations, you set me above my assailants; you saved me from the violent man, O Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.collecta}

Enlighten, O God of compassion, the hearts of your children, sanctified by penance, and in your kindness grant those you stir to a sense of devotion a gracious hearing when they cry out to you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.super-oblata}

Receive back, O Lord, these sacrificial offerings which you have given to be offered to the honour of your Name, and grant that they become remedies for our healing.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

God has brought us to the kingdom of his beloved Son, in whom we have redemption through his Blood, the forgiveness of sins.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-iv.post-communionem}

May the mysteries we have received, O Lord, bring us heavenly medicine, that they may purge all evil from our heart and strengthen us with eternal protection.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Attend, almighty God, to the prayers of your people, and, as you endow them with confident hope in your compassion, let them feel as ever the effects of your mercy.

#### Thursday in the Fifth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

Christ is mediator of a New Covenant, so that by means of his death, those who are called may receive the promise of an eternal inheritance.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-v.collecta}

Be near, O Lord, to those who plead before you, and look kindly on those who place their hope in your mercy, that, cleansed from the stain of their sins, they may persevere in holy living and be made full heirs of your promise.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-v.super-oblata}

Look with favour, Lord, we pray, on these sacrificial offerings, that they may profit our conversion and the salvation of all the world.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

God did not spare his own Son, but handed him over for us all; with him, he has given us all things.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-v.post-communionem}

Nourished by your saving gifts, we beseech your mercy, Lord, that by this same Sacrament with which you feed us in the present age you may make us partakers of life eternal.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Be gracious to your people, Lord, we pray, that, as from day to day they reject what does not please you, they may be filled instead with delight at your commands.

#### Friday in the Fifth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

Have mercy on me, O Lord, for I am in distress. Deliver me from the hands of my enemies and those who pursue me. O Lord, let me never be put to shame, for I call on you.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.collecta}

Pardon the offences of your peoples, we pray, O Lord, and in your goodness set us free from the bonds of the sins committed in our weakness.

*Or:*

O God, who in this season give your Church the grace devoutly to imitate the Blessed Virgin Mary in contemplating the Passion of Christ, grant, we pray, through her intercession, that we may cling more firmly each day to your Only Begotten Son and come at last to the fullness of his grace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Grant, O merciful God, that we may be worthy to serve ever fittingly at your altars, and there to be saved by constant participation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

Jesus bore our sins in his own body on the cross, so that dead to sin, we might live for righteousness. By his wounds we have been healed.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-feria-vi.post-communionem}

May the unfailing protection of the sacrifice we have received never leave us, O Lord, and may it always drive far from us all that would do us harm.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that your servants who seek the grace of your protection may be free from every evil and serve you in peace of mind.

#### Saturday in the Fifth Week of Lent {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-sabbato}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

O Lord, do not stay afar off; my strength, make haste to help me! For I am a worm and no man, scorned by everyone, despised by the people.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-sabbato.collecta}

O God, who have made all those reborn in Christ a chosen race and a royal priesthood, grant us, we pray, the grace to will and to do what you command, that the people called to eternal life may be one in the faith of their hearts and in the homage of their deeds.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-sabbato.super-oblata}

May the gifts we offer from our fasting be acceptable to you, O Lord, we pray, and that, as an expiation for our sins, they may make us worthy of your grace, and lead us to what you promise for eternity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

Christ was handed over to gather into one the scattered children of God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-v-in-quadragesima-sabbato.post-communionem}

We entreat your majesty most humbly, O Lord, that, as you feed us with the nourishment which comes from the most holy Body and Blood of your Son, so you may make us sharers of his divine nature.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Have mercy, Lord, on your Church, as she brings you her supplications, and be attentive to those who incline their hearts before you: do not allow, we pray, those you have redeemed by the Death of your Only Begotten Son, to be harmed by their sins or weighed down by their trials.\
HOLY WEEK

#### PALM SUNDAY — Commemoration of the Lord's Entrance into Jerusalem {#misal.dominica-in-palmis-de-passione-domini}

FIRST FORM: THE PROCESSION

*At the scheduled time, the congregation assembles in a secondary church or chapel or in some other suitable place distinct from the church to which the procession will move. The faithful carry palm branches. The priest and ministers put on red vestments for Mass and go to the place where the people have assembled. Meanwhile, the following antiphon or any appropriate song is sung.*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-in-palmis-de-passione-domini.ant-ad-introitum}

Hosanna to the Son of David, blessed is he who comes in the name of the Lord, the King of Israel. Hosanna in the highest.

*The priest then greets the people in the usual way and gives a brief introduction, inviting them to take a full part in the celebration. He may use these or similar words.*

Dear brothers and sisters, since the beginning of Lent until now we have prepared our hearts by penance and charitable works. Today we gather together to herald with the whole Church the beginning of the celebration of our Lord's paschal mystery, that is to say, of his Passion and Resurrection. For it was to accomplish this mystery that he entered his own city of Jerusalem. Therefore, with all faith and devotion, let us commemorate the Lord's entry into the city for our salvation, following in his footsteps, so that being made by his grace partakers of the Cross, we may have a share also in his resurrection and in his life.

*Afterwards the priest, with hands joined, says one of the following prayers:*

Almighty ever-living God, sanctify + these branches with your blessing, that we who follow Christ the King in exultation may reach the eternal Jerusalem through him.

*Or:*

Increase the faith of those who place their hope in you, O God, and graciously hear the prayers of those who call on you: bless + those who today hold high these branches to hail Christ in his triumph, that we may bear fruit by good works in him.

*The priest sprinkles the branches with holy water in silence. Then the account of the Lord's entrance into Jerusalem is proclaimed from one of the four gospels. This is done in the usual way or, if there is no deacon, by the priest. Gospel A, B or C is read according to the Cycle for the Year.*

*After the gospel a brief homily may be given. Before the procession begins, the celebrant or other suitable minister may address the people in these or similar words:*

Dear brethren (brothers and sisters), like the crowds who acclaimed Jesus in Jerusalem, let us go forth in peace.

*Or:*

Let us go forth in peace.\
In the name of Christ. Amen.

*The procession to the church where mass will be celebrated then begins. During the procession, the choir and people sing the following or other appropriate songs:*

Antiphon 1

*This antiphon may be repeated between the verses of Psalm 23*

The children of the Hebrews, carrying olive branches, went to meet the Lord, crying out and saying: Hosanna in the highest.\
Psalm 23\
The Lord's is the earth and its fullness,\
the world, and those who dwell in it.\
It is he who set it on the seas;\
on the rivers he made it firm.\
The children of the Hebrews, carrying olive branches, went to meet the Lord, crying out and saying: Hosanna in the highest.\
Who shall climb the mountain of the Lord?\
The clean of hands and pure of heart,\
whose soul is not set on vain things,\
who has not sworn deceitful words.\
The children of the Hebrews, carrying olive branches, went to meet the Lord, crying out and saying: Hosanna in the highest.\
Blessings from the Lord shall he receive,\
and right reward from the God who saves him.\
Such are the people who seek him,\
who seek the face of the God of Jacob.\
The children of the Hebrews, carrying olive branches, went to meet the Lord, crying out and saying: Hosanna in the highest.\
O gates, lift high your heads;\
grow higher, ancient doors.\
Let him enter, the king of glory!\
who is this king of glory?\
The Lord, the mighty, the valiant;\
the Lord, the valiant in war.\
The children of the Hebrews, carrying olive branches, went to meet the Lord, crying out and saying: Hosanna in the highest.\
O gates, lift high your heads;\
grow higher, ancient doors.\
Let him enter, the King of glory!\
who is this king of glory?\
He, the Lord of hosts,\
he is the king of glory.\
The children of the Hebrews, carrying olive branches, went to meet the Lord, crying out and saying: Hosanna in the highest.\
Antiphon 2

*This antiphon may be repeated between the verses of Psalm 46*

The children of the Hebrews spread their garments on the road, crying out and saying: Hosanna to the Son of David; blest is he who comes in the name of the Lord.\
Psalm 46\
All peoples, clap your hands, cry to God with shouts of joy! For the Lord, the Most High, we must fear, great king over all the earth.\
The children of the Hebrews spread their garments on the road, crying out and saying: Hosanna to the Son of David; blest is he who comes in the name of the Lord.\
He subdues peoples under us and nations under our feet. Our inheritance, our glory, is from him, given to Jacob out of love.\
The children of the Hebrews spread their garments on the road, crying out and saying: Hosanna to the Son of David; blest is he who comes in the name of the Lord.\
God goes up with shouts of joy; the Lord goes up with trumpet blast. Sing praise for God, sing praise, sing praise to our king, sing praise.\
The children of the Hebrews spread their garments on the road, crying out and saying: Hosanna to the Son of David; blest is he who comes in the name of the Lord.\
God is king of all the earth. Sing praise with all your skill. God is king over the nations. God reigns on his holy throne.\
The children of the Hebrews spread their garments on the road, crying out and saying: Hosanna to the Son of David; blest is he who comes in the name of the Lord.\
The princes of the peoples are assembled with the people of Abraham's God. The rulers of the earth belong to God, to God who reigns over all.\
The children of the Hebrews spread their garments on the road, crying out and saying: Hosanna to the Son of David; blest is he who comes in the name of the Lord.\
Hymn to Christ the King\
Israel's King are you, King David's magnificent offspring you are the ruler who come blest in the name of the Lord.\
R. Glory and honour and praise be to you, Christ, King and Redeemer to whom young children cried out loving Hosannas with joy.\
Heavenly hosts on high unite in singing your praises: men and women on earth and all creation join in.\
R. Glory and honour and praise be to you, Christ, King and Redeemer to whom young children cried out loving Hosannas with joy.\
Bearing branches of palm, Hebrews came crowding to greet you; see how with prayers and hymns we come to pay you our vows.\
R. Glory and honour and praise be to you, Christ, King and Redeemer to whom young children cried out loving Hosannas with joy.\
They offered gifts of praise to you, so near to your Passion; see how we sing this song now to you reigning on high.\
R. Glory and honour and praise be to you, Christ, King and Redeemer to whom young children cried out loving Hosannas with joy.\
Those you were pleased to accept: now accept our gifts of devotion, good and merciful King, lover of all that is good.\
R. Glory and honour and praise be to you, Christ, King and Redeemer to whom young children cried out loving Hosannas with joy.\
Entry into the Church

*As the procession enters the church, the following responsory or another song which refers to the Lord's entrance is sung.*

R. Ingrediénte Dómino in Sanctam civitátem Hebrórum púeri, resurrectiónem vit pronuntiántes, Cum ramis palmárun: Hosánna, clamábant, in excélsis.\
R. As the Lord entered the holy city, the children of the Hebrews proclaimed the resurrection of life. Waving their branches of palm, they cried: Hosanna in the Highest.\
V. When the people heard that Jesus was coming to Jerusalem, they went out to meet him. Waving their branches.

*When the priest comes to the altar he venerates it and may also incense it. Then he goes to his chair (removes the cope and puts on the chasuble) and begins immediately the COLLECT of Mass, which concludes the procession. Mass then continues in the usual way.*

THE SECOND FORM: THE SOLEMN ENTRANCE

*If the procession cannot be held outside the church, the commemoration of the Lord's entrance may be celebrated before the principal Mass with the solemn entrance, which takes place within the church.*

*The faithful, holding the branches, assemble either in front of the church door or inside the church. The priest and ministers, with a representative group of the faithful, go to a suitable place in the church outside the sanctuary, so that the most of the people will be able to see the rite.*

*While the priest goes to the appointed place, the antiphon Hosanna or other suitable song is sung. Then the blessing of branches and proclamation of the gospel about the Lord's entrance into Jerusalem take place, as above.*

*After the gospel the priest, with the ministers and the group of the faithful, moves solemnly through the church to the sanctuary, while the responsory or other appropriate song is sung.*

*When the priest comes to the altar he venerates it, goes to his chair, and immediately begins the COLLECT of Mass, which then continues in the usual way.*

THIRD FORM: THE SIMPLE ENTRANCE

*At all other Masses on this Sunday, if the solemn entrance is not held, the Lord's entrance is commemorated with the following simple entrance.*

*While the priest goes to the altar, the entrance antiphon with its psalm or another song with the same theme is sung. After the priest venerates the altar, he goes to his chair and greets the people. Mass continues in the usual way.*

##### Entrance Antiphon

Six days before the Passover, when the Lord came into the city of Jerusalem, the children ran to meet him; in their hands they carried palm branches and with a loud voice cried out: Hosanna in the highest! Blessed are you, who have come in your abundant mercy!\
O gates, lift high your heads; grow higher, ancient doors. Let him enter, the king of glory! Who is this king of glory? He, the Lord of hosts, he is the king of glory. Hosanna in the highest! Blessed are you, who have come in your abundant mercy!

*Where neither the procession nor the solemn entrance can be celebrated, there should be a bible service on the theme of the Lord's messianic entrance and passion, either on Saturday evening or on Sunday at a convenient time.*

*After the procession or solemn entrance the priest begins the Mass with the COLLECT.*

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-in-palmis-de-passione-domini.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who as an example of humility for the human race to follow caused our Saviour to take flesh and submit to the cross, graciously grant that we may heed his lesson of patient suffering and so merit a share in his Resurrection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-in-palmis-de-passione-domini.super-oblata}

Through the Passion of your Only Begotten Son, O Lord, may our reconciliation with you be near at hand, so that though we do not merit it by our own deeds, yet by this sacrifice made once for all we may feel already the effects of your mercy.

##### Preface

The redeeming work of Christ\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God, through Christ our Lord.\
For, though innocent, he suffered willingly for sinners and accepted unjust condemnation to save the guilty. His Death has washed away our sins, and his Resurrection has purchased our justification.\
And so, with all the Angels, we praise you, as in joyful celebration we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-in-palmis-de-passione-domini.ant-ad-communionem}

Father, if this chalice cannot pass without my drinking it, your will be done.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-in-palmis-de-passione-domini.post-communionem}

Nourished with these sacred gifts, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that just as through the death of your Son you have brought us to hope for what we believe, so by his Resurrection may you lead us to where you call.

##### Prayer Over The People {#misal.dominica-in-palmis-de-passione-domini.oratio-super-populum}

Look, we pray, O Lord, on this your family, for which our Lord Jesus Christ did not hesitate to be delivered into the hands of the wicked and submit to the agony of the Cross.

#### Monday of Holy Week {#misal.feria-ii-hebdomadae-sanctae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.feria-ii-hebdomadae-sanctae.ant-ad-introitum}

Contend, O Lord, with my contenders; fight those who fight me. Take up your buckler and shield; arise in my defence, Lord, my mighty help.

##### Collect {#misal.feria-ii-hebdomadae-sanctae.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that though in our weakness we fail, we may be revived through the Passion of your Only Begotten Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.feria-ii-hebdomadae-sanctae.super-oblata}

Look graciously, O Lord, upon the sacred mysteries we celebrate here: and may what you have mercifully provided to cancel the judgment we incurred bear for us fruit in eternal life.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.feria-ii-hebdomadae-sanctae.ant-ad-communionem}

Do not hide your face from me in the day of my distress. Turn your ear towards me; on the day when I call, speedily answer me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.feria-ii-hebdomadae-sanctae.post-communionem}

Visit your people, O Lord, we pray, and with ever-watchful love look upon the hearts dedicated to you by means of these sacred mysteries, so that under your protection we may keep safe this remedy of eternal salvation, which by your mercy we have received.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

May your protection, O Lord, we pray, defend the humble and keep ever safe those who trust in your mercy, that they may celebrate the paschal festivities not only with bodily observance but above all with purity of mind.

#### Tuesday of Holy Week {#misal.feria-iii-hebdomadae-sanctae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.feria-iii-hebdomadae-sanctae.ant-ad-introitum}

Do not leave me to the will of my foes, O Lord, for false witnesses rise up against me, and they breathe out violence.

##### Collect {#misal.feria-iii-hebdomadae-sanctae.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, grant us so to celebrate the mysteries of the Lord's Passion that we may merit to receive your pardon.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.feria-iii-hebdomadae-sanctae.super-oblata}

Look favourably, O Lord, we pray, on these offerings of your family, and to those you make partakers of these sacred gifts grant a share in their fullness.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.feria-iii-hebdomadae-sanctae.ant-ad-communionem}

God did not spare his own Son, but handed him over for us all.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.feria-iii-hebdomadae-sanctae.post-communionem}

Nourished by your saving gifts, we beseech your mercy, Lord, that by this same Sacrament, with which you have fed us in the present age you may make us partakers of life eternal.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

May your mercy, O God, cleanse the people that are subject to you from all seduction of former ways and make them capable of new holiness.

#### Wednesday of Holy Week {#misal.feria-iv-hebdomadae-sanctae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.feria-iv-hebdomadae-sanctae.ant-ad-introitum}

At the name of Jesus, every knee shall bend of those in heaven and on the earth and under the earth, for the Lord became obedient to death, death on a cross: therefore Jesus Christ is Lord, to the glory of God the Father.

##### Collect {#misal.feria-iv-hebdomadae-sanctae.collecta}

O God, who willed your Son to submit for our sake to the yoke of the Cross, so that you might drive from us the power of the enemy, grant us, your servants, to attain the grace of the resurrection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.feria-iv-hebdomadae-sanctae.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, we pray, the offerings made here, and graciously grant that, celebrating your Son's Passion in mystery, we may experience the grace of its effects.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.feria-iv-hebdomadae-sanctae.ant-ad-communionem}

The Son of Man did not come to be served but to serve and to give his life as a ransom for many.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.feria-iv-hebdomadae-sanctae.post-communionem}

Endow us, almighty God, with the firm conviction that, through your Son's Death in time, to which the revered mysteries bear witness, we may be assured of perpetual life.

*Prayer over the People (Optional)*

Grant your faithful, O Lord, we pray, to partake unceasingly of the paschal mysteries and to await with longing the gifts to come, that persevering in the Sacraments of their rebirth, they may be led by Lenten works to newness of life.

### HOLY THURSDAY

#### HOLY THURSDAY — CHRISM MASS {#misal.feria-v-hebdomadae-sanctae-ad-missam-chrismatis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.feria-v-hebdomadae-sanctae-ad-missam-chrismatis.ant-ad-introitum}

Jesus Christ has made us into a kingdom, priests for his God and Father. To him be glory and power for ever and ever. Amen.

##### Collect {#misal.feria-v-hebdomadae-sanctae-ad-missam-chrismatis.collecta}

O God, who anointed your Only Begotten Son with the Holy Spirit and made him Christ and Lord, graciously grant that being made sharers in his consecration, we may bear witness to your Redemption in the world.

*In this homily the bishop urges the priests to be faithful in fulfilling their office in the Church and should invite them to renew publicity their priestly promises.*

*Renewal of Commitment to Priestly Service*

*After the homily the bishop speaks to the priests in these or similar words:*

Beloved sons, on the anniversary of that day when Christ our Lord conferred his priesthood on his Apostles and on us, are you resolved to renew in the presence of your Bishop and God's holy people, the promises you once made?\
Priest: I am.\
Are you resolved to be more united with the Lord Jesus and more closely conformed to him, denying yourselves and confirming those promises about sacred duties towards Christ's Church which, prompted by love of him, you willingly and joyfully pledged on the day of your priestly ordination?\
Priest: I am.\
Are you resolved to be faithful stewards of the mysteries of God in the Holy Eucharist and the other liturgical rites and to discharge faithfully the sacred office of teaching, following Christ the Head and Shepherd, not seeking any gain, but moved only by zeal for souls?\
Priest: I am.

*Then the bishop addresses the people:*

As for you, dearest sons and daughters, pray for your Priests, that the Lord may pour out his gifts abundantly upon them, and keep them faithful as ministers of Christ, the High Priest, so that they may lead you to him, who is the source of salvation.\
People: Christ, hear us. Christ, graciously hear us.\
And pray also for me, that I may be faithful to the apostolic office entrusted to me in my lowliness and that in your midst I may be made day by day a living and more perfect image of Christ, the Priest, the Good Shepherd, the Teacher and the Servant of all.\
People: Christ, hear us. Christ, graciously hear us.\
May the Lord keep us all in his charity and lead all of us, shepherds and flock, to eternal life.\
All: Amen.

*The Profession of Faith (Creed) and General Intercessions are not said.*

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.feria-v-hebdomadae-sanctae-ad-missam-chrismatis.super-oblata}

May the power of this sacrifice, O Lord, we pray, mercifully wipe away what is old in us, and increase in us grace of salvation and newness of life.

##### Preface

The priesthood of Christ and the ministry of priests\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God.\
For by the anointing of the Holy Spirit you made your Only Begotten Son High Priest of the new and eternal covenant, and by your wondrous design were pleased to decree that his one Priesthood should continue in the Church.\
For Christ not only adorns with a royal priesthood the people he has made his own, but with a brother's kindness he also chooses men to become sharers in his sacred ministry through the laying on of hands. They are to renew in his name the sacrifice of human redemption, to set before your children the paschal banquet, to lead your holy people in charity, to nourish them with the word and strengthen them with the Sacraments.\
As they give up their lives for you and for the salvation of their brothers and sisters, they strive to be conformed to the image of Christ himself and offer you a constant witness of faith and love.\
And so, Lord, with all the Angels and Saints, we, too, give you thanks, as in exultation we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.feria-v-hebdomadae-sanctae-ad-missam-chrismatis.ant-ad-communionem}

I will sing forever of your mercies, O Lord; through all ages my mouth will proclaim your fidelity.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.feria-v-hebdomadae-sanctae-ad-missam-chrismatis.post-communionem}

We beseech you, almighty God, that those you renew by your Sacraments may merit to become the pleasing fragrance of Christ.

#### HOLY THURSDAY — EVENING MASS OF THE LORD'S SUPPER {#misal.feria-v-in-cena-domini-ad-missam-vespertinam}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.feria-v-in-cena-domini-ad-missam-vespertinam.ant-ad-introitum}

We should glory in the cross of our Lord Jesus Christ, in whom is our salvation, life and resurrection, through whom we are saved and delivered.

##### Collect {#misal.feria-v-in-cena-domini-ad-missam-vespertinam.collecta}

O God, who have called us to participate in this most sacred Supper, in which your Only Begotten Son, when about to hand himself over to death, entrusted to the Church a sacrifice new for all eternity, the banquet of his love, grant, we pray, that we may draw from so great a mystery, the fullness of charity and of life.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.feria-v-in-cena-domini-ad-missam-vespertinam.super-oblata}

Grant us, O Lord, we pray, that we may participate worthily in these mysteries, for whenever the memorial of this sacrifice is celebrated the work of our redemption is accomplished.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.feria-v-in-cena-domini-ad-missam-vespertinam.ant-ad-communionem}

This is the Body that will be given up for you; this is the chalice of the new covenant in my Blood, says the Lord; do this, whenever you receive it, in memory of me.

*After the distribution of communion, the ciborium with hosts for Good Friday is left on the altar.*

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.feria-v-in-cena-domini-ad-missam-vespertinam.post-communionem}

Grant, almighty God, that, just as we are renewed by the Supper of your Son in this present age, so may we enjoy his banquet for all eternity.\
Transfer of the Holy Eucharist

*After the prayer the priest stands before the altar and puts incense in the thurible. Kneeling, he incenses the Blessed Sacrament three times. Then he receives the humeral veil, takes the ciborium, and covers it with the ends of the veil.*

*The Blessed Sacrament is carried through the church in procession, led by a cross-bearer and accompanied by candles and incense, to the place of reposition prepared in a chapel suitably decorated for the occasion. During the procession the hymn Pange lingua (exclusive of the last two stanzas) or some other eucharistic song is sung.*

Hymn: Pange Lingua\
Nobis datus, nobis natus\
Verbum caro panem\
fitque sanguis Christi merum,\
et, si sensus deficit,\
Of the glorious Body telling,\
O my tongue, its mysteries sing,\
And the Blood, all price excelling,\
Which the world's eternal King,\
in a noble womb once dwelling,\
shed for the world's ransoming.\
Given for us, descending,\
Of a virgin to proceed,\
Man with man in converse blending,\
Scattered he the Gospel seed,\
Till his sojourn drew to ending,\
Which he closed in wondrous deed.\
At the last great Supper lying\
Circled by his brethren's band,\
Meekly with the law complying,\
First he finished its command,\
Then, immortal Food supplying,\
Gave himself with his own hand.\
Word made Flesh,\
by word he maketh very bread his Flesh to be;\
Man in wine Christ's Blood partaketh:\
And is senses fail to see,\
Faith alone the true heart waketh\
To behold the mystery.

*When the procession reaches the place of reposition, the priest sets the ciborium down. Then he puts incense in the thurible and kneeling, incenses the Blessed Sacrament, while Tantum ergo Sacramentum is sung.*

salus, honor, virtus,\
quoque sit et benedictio;\
compare sit laudatio.\
Therefore we, before him bending,\
This great Sacrament revere;\
Types and shadows have their ending,\
For the newer rite is here;\
Faith, our outward sense befriending,\
Makes the inward vision clear.\
Glory let us give, and blessing\
To the Father and the Son;\
Honour, might, and praise addressing,\
While eternal ages run;\
Ever too his love confessing,\
Who, from both, with both is one.\
Amen.

*The tabernacle or reposition is then closed.*

*After a period of silent adoration, the priest and ministers genuflect and return to the sacristy.*

#### GOOD FRIDAY — Celebration of the Lord's Passion {#misal.feria-vi-in-passione-domini}

*The celebration of the Lord's passion takes place in the afternoon, about three o'clock, unless pastoral reasons suggest a later hour. The celebration consists of three parts: liturgy of the word, veneration of the cross, and holy communion.*

*Then priest and deacon, wearing red Mass vestments, go to the altar. There they make a reverence and prostrate themselves, or they may kneel. All pray silently for a while.*

*Then the priest goes to the chair with the ministers. He faces the people and, with hands extended, sings or says one of the following prayers.*

*PRAYER*

*(Let us pray is not said.)*

Remember your mercies, O Lord, and with your eternal protection sanctify your servants, for whom Christ your Son, by the shedding of his Blood, established the Paschal Mystery.

*Or:*

O God, who by the Passion of Christ your Son, our Lord, abolished the death inherited from ancient sin by every succeeding generation; grant that just as, being conformed to him, we have borne by the law of nature the image of the man of earth, so by the sanctification of grace may we bear the image of the Man of heaven.

Part One: Liturgy of the Word

Part Two: Veneration of the Cross

First Form of Showing the Cross

*The veiled cross is carried to the altar, accompanied by two ministers with lighted candles. Standing at the altar the priest takes the cross, uncovers the upper part of it, then elevates it and begins the invitation. Behold the wood of the cross. He is assisted in the singing by the deacon or, if convenient, by the choir. All respond: Come, let us adore. (Veníte, adorémus) At the end of the singing all kneel and venerate the cross briefly in silence; the priest remains standing and holds the cross high.*

*Then the priest uncovers the right arm of the cross, lifts it up, and again begins the invitation Behold the wood of the cross, and the rite is repeated as before.*

*Finally he uncovers the entire cross, lifts it up, and again begins the invitation Behold the wood of the cross a third time, and the rite is repeated as before.*

*Accompanied by two ministers with lighted candles, the priest then carries the cross to the entrance of the sanctuary or to another suitable place. There he lays the cross down or hands it to the ministers to hold. Candles are placed on either side of the cross, and the veneration follows as below.*

Second form of Showing the cross

*The Priest or deacon, accompanied by the ministers or by another suitable minister, goes to the church door. There he takes the (uncovered) cross and the ministers take lighted candles. They go in procession through the church to the sanctuary. Near the entrance of the church in the middle of the church, and at the entrance to the sanctuary, the one carrying the cross stops, lifts it up, and sings the invitation: Behold the wood of the cross. All respond: Come, let us adore. (Veníte, adorémus) After each response all kneel and venerate the cross briefly in silence as above.*

*Then the cross and candles are placed at the entrance to the sanctuary.*

The Invitation

V. Behold the wood of the Cross, on which hung the salvation of the world.

R. Come, let us adore.

Veneration of the cross

*The priest, clergy, and faithful approach to venerate the cross in a kind of procession. They make a simple genuflection or perform some other appropriate sign of reverence according to local custom, for example, kissing the cross.*

*CHANTS TO BE SUNG DURING THE ADORATION OF THE HOLY CROSS*

Ant. We adore your Cross, O Lord, we praise and glorify your holy Resurrection, for behold, because of the wood of a tree joy has come to the whole world.

May God have mercy on us and bless us; may he let his face shed its light upon us, and have mercy on us

*And the antiphon is repeated: We adore your Cross...*

*THE REPROACHES*

*Parts belonging to one of the two choirs separately are indicated by the numbers 1 (first choir) and 2 (second choir); parts sung by both choirs together are numbered: 1 and 2. Some of the verses may also be sung by two cantors.*

*I*

l and 2 My people, what have I done to you?

Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

1 Because I led you out of the land of Egypt, you have prepared a Cross for your Saviour.

1 Hagios Theos,

2 Holy is God,

Sanctus Deus.

1 Hagios Ischyros,

2 Holy and Mighty,

Sanctus Fortis.

1 Hagios Athanatos, eleison himas.

2 Holy and Immortal One, have mercy on us.

l and 2 Because I led you out through the desert forty years and fed you with manna and brought you into a land of plenty, you have prepared a Cross for your Saviour.

1 Hagios Theos,

2 Holy is God,

Sanctus Deus.

1 Hagios Ischyros,

2 Holy and Mighty,

Sanctus Fortis.

1 Hagios Athanatos, eleison himas.

2 Holy and Immortal One, have mercy on us.

l and 2 What more should I have done for you and have not done? Indeed I planted you as my most beautiful chosen vine and you have turned very bitter for me, for in my thirst you gave me vinegar to drink and with a lance you pierced your Saviour's side.

1 Hagios Theos,

2 Holy is God,

Sanctus Deus.

1 Hagios Ischyros,

2 Holy and Mighty,

Sanctus Fortis.

1 Hagios Athanatos, eleison himas.

2 Holy and Immortal One, have mercy on us.

*II*

Cantors: I scourged Egypt for your sake with its firstborn sons, and you scourged me and handed me over.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

Cantors: I led you out from Egypt as Pharaoh lay sunk in the Red Sea, and you handed me over to the chief priests.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

Cantors: I opened up the sea before you and you opened my side with a lance.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

Cantors: I went before you in a pillar of cloud, and you led me into Pilate's palace.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

Cantors: I fed you with manna in the desert, and on me you rained blows and lashes.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

Cantors: I gave you saving water from the rock to drink, and for drink you gave me gall and vinegar.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

Cantors: I struck down for you the kings of the Canaanites, and you struck my head with a reed.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

Cantors: I put in your hand a royal scepter, and you put on my head a crown of thorns.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

Cantors: I exalted you with great power, and you hung me on the scaffold of the Cross.

1 and 2 repeat: My people, what have I done to you? Or how have I grieved you? Answer me!

*HYMN*

*All:*

Faithful Cross the Saints rely on,

Noble tree beyond compare!

Never was there such a scion,

Never leaf or flower so rare.

Sweet the timber, sweet the iron,

Sweet the burden that they bear!

*Cantors:*

Sing, my tongue, in exultation

Of our banner and device!

Make a solemn proclamation

Of a triumph and its price:

How the Saviour of creation

Conquered by his sacrifice!

*All:*

Faithful Cross the Saints rely on,

Noble tree beyond compare!

Never was there such a scion,

Never leaf or flower so rare.

*Cantors:*

For, when Adam first offended,

Eating that forbidden fruit,

Not all hopes of glory ended

With the serpent at the root:

Broken nature would be mended

By a second tree and shoot.

*All:*

Sweet the timber, sweet the iron,

Sweet the burden that they bear!

*Cantors:*

Thus the tempter was outwitted

By a wisdom deeper still:

Remedy and ailment fitted,

Means to cure and means to kill;

That the world might be acquitted,

Christ would do his Father's will.

*All:*

Faithful Cross the Saints rely on,

Noble tree beyond compare!

Never was there such a scion,

Never leaf or flower so rare.

*Cantors:*

So the Father, out of pity

For our self-inflicted doom,

Sent him from the heavenly city

When the holy time had come:

He, the Son and the Almighty,

Took our flesh in Mary's womb.

*All:*

Sweet the timber, sweet the iron,

Sweet the burden that they bear!

*Cantors:*

Hear a tiny baby crying,

Founder of the seas and strands:

See his virgin Mother tying

Cloth around his feet and hands:

Find him in a manger lying

Tightly wrapped in swaddling-bands!

*All:*

Faithful Cross the Saints rely on,

Noble tree beyond compare!

Never was there such a scion,

Never leaf or flower so rare.

*Cantors:*

So he came, the long-expected,

Not in glory, not to reign;

Only born to be rejected,

Choosing hunger, toil and pain,

Till the scaffold was erected

And the Paschal Lamb was slain.

*All:*

Sweet the timber, sweet the iron,

Sweet the burden that they bear!

*Cantors:*

No disgrace was too abhorrent:

Nailed and mocked and parched he died;

Blood and water, double warrant,

Issue from his wounded side,

Washing in a mighty torrent

Earth and stars and ocean tide.

*All:*
