##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

Jesus stood in the midst of his disciples and said to them: Peace be with you, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-ii.post-communionem}

Look with kindness upon your people, O Lord, and grant, we pray, that those you were pleased to renew by eternal mysteries may attain in their flesh the incorruptible glory of the resurrection.

#### Tuesday in the Sixth Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

Let us rejoice and be glad and give glory to God, for the Lord our God the Almighty reigns, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iii.collecta}

Grant, almighty and merciful God, that we may in truth receive a share in the Resurrection of Christ your Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that we may always find delight in these paschal mysteries, so that the renewal constantly at work within us may be the cause of our unending joy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

The Christ had to suffer and rise from the dead, and so enter into his glory, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iii.post-communionem}

Hear, O Lord, our prayers, that this most holy exchange, by which you have redeemed us, may bring your help in this present life and ensure for us eternal gladness.

#### Wednesday in the Sixth Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iv-ad-missam-matutinam}

*In regions where the Solemnity of the Ascension occurs on the following Sunday, this Mass is also used in the evening.*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iv-ad-missam-matutinam.ant-ad-introitum}

I will praise you, Lord, among the nations; I will tell of your name to my kin, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iv-ad-missam-matutinam.collecta}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that, as we celebrate in mystery the solemnities of your Son's Resurrection, so too we may be worthy to rejoice at his coming with all the Saints.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iv-ad-missam-matutinam.super-oblata}

O God, who by the wonderful exchange effected in this sacrifice have made us partakers of the one supreme Godhead, grant, we pray, that as we have come to know your truth, we may make it ours by a worthy way of life.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iv-ad-missam-matutinam.ant-ad-communionem}

I have chosen you from the world, says the Lord, and have appointed you to go out and bear fruit, fruit that will last, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-iv-ad-missam-matutinam.post-communionem}

Graciously be present to your people, we pray, O Lord, and lead those you have imbued with heavenly mysteries to pass from former ways to newness of life.

#### Thursday in the Sixth Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-v}

*This mass is celebrated in countries where the celebration of the Ascension is transferred to the Seventh Sunday of Easter.*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

O God, when you went forth before your people, marching with them and living among them, the earth trembled, heavens poured down rain, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-v.collecta}

O God, who made your people partakers in your redemption, grant us, we pray, that we may perpetually render thanks for the Resurrection of the Lord.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-v.super-oblata}

May our prayers rise up to you, O Lord, together with the sacrificial offerings, so that, purified by your graciousness, we may be conformed to the mysteries of your mighty love.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, I am with you always, even to the end of the age, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-v.post-communionem}

Almighty ever-living God, who restore us to eternal life in the Resurrection of Christ, increase in us, we pray, the fruits of this paschal Sacrament and pour into our hearts the strength of this saving food.

#### Friday in the Sixth Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

You have redeemed us, Lord, by your blood, from every tribe and tongue and people and nation, and have made us a kingdom, priests for our God, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-vi.collecta}

*When the Ascension is celebrated on Thursday:*

O God, who restore us to eternal life in the Resurrection of Christ, raise us up, we pray, to the author of our salvation, who is seated at your right hand, so that when our Saviour comes again in majesty those you have given new birth in Baptism may be clothed with blessed immortality.

*When the Ascension is celebrated on the following Sunday:*

Hear our prayers, O Lord, so that what was promised by the sanctifying power of your Word may everywhere be accomplished through the working of the Gospel and that all your adopted children may attain what the testimony of truth has foretold.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Accept in compassion, Lord, we pray, the offerings of your family, that under your protective care they may never lose what they have received, but attain the gifts that are eternal.

*or of the \]*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

Christ our Lord was handed over for our transgressions, and was raised again for our justification, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-feria-vi.post-communionem}

Keep safe, O Lord, we pray, those whom you have saved by your kindness, that, redeemed by the Passion of your Son, they may rejoice in his Resurrection.

#### Saturday in the Sixth Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-sabbato}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-sabbato.ant-ad-introitum}

O chosen people, proclaim the mighty works of him who called you out of darkness into his wonderful light, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-sabbato.collecta}

*When the Ascension is celebrated on Thursday:*

O God, whose Son at his Ascension to the heavens was pleased to promise the Holy Spirit to the Apostles, grant, we pray, that just as they received manifold gifts of heavenly teaching, so on us, too, you may bestow spiritual gifts.

*When the Ascension is celebrated on the following Sunday:*

Constantly shape our minds, we pray, O Lord, by the practice of good works, that trying always for what is better, we may strive to hold ever fast to the Paschal Mystery.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-sabbato.super-oblata}

Graciously sanctify these gifts, O Lord, we pray, and, accepting the oblation of this spiritual sacrifice, make of us an eternal offering to you.

*or of the*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-sabbato.ant-ad-communionem}

Father, I wish that where I am, those you gave me may also be with me, that they may see the glory that you gave me, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vi-paschae-sabbato.post-communionem}

We have partaken of the gifts of this sacred mystery, humbly imploring, O Lord, that what your Son commanded us to do in memory of him may bring us growth in charity.

### SOLEMNITY OF OUR LORD'S ASCENSION

#### SOLEMNITY OF OUR LORD'S ASCENSION — VIGIL MASS {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-introitum}

You kingdoms of the earth, sing to God; praise the Lord, who ascends above the highest heavens; his majesty and might are in the skies, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.collecta}

O God, whose Son today ascended to the heavens as the Apostles looked on, grant, we pray, that in accordance with his promise we may be worthy for him to live with us always on earth, and we with him in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.super-oblata}

O God, whose Only Begotten Son, our High Priest, is seated ever-living at your right hand to intercede for us, grant that we may approach with confidence the throne of grace, and there obtain your mercy.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-communionem}

Christ, offering a single sacrifice for sins, is seated for ever at God's right hand, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-vigilia.post-communionem}

May the gifts we have received from your altar, Lord, kindle in our hearts a longing for the heavenly homeland, and cause us to press forward following in the Saviour's footsteps to the place where for our sake he entered before us.

#### OUR LORD’S ASCENSION \[Mass during the Day\] {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-die}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-introitum}

Men of Galilee, why gaze in wonder at the heavens? This Jesus whom you saw ascending into heaven will return as you saw him go, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-die.collecta}

Gladden us with holy joys, almighty God, and make us rejoice with devout thanksgiving, for the Ascension of Christ your Son is our exaltation, and where the Head has gone before in glory, the Body is called to follow in hope.

*Or:*

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that we who believe that your Only Begotten Son, our Redeemer, ascended this day to the heavens, may in spirit dwell already in the heavenly realms.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-die.super-oblata}

We offer sacrifice now in supplication, O Lord, to honour the wondrous Ascension of your Son: grant, we pray, that through this most holy exchange we, too, may rise up to the heavenly realms.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, I am with you always even until the end of the age, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.in-ascensione-domini-ad-missam-in-die.post-communionem}

Almighty ever-living God, who allow those on earth to celebrate divine mysteries, grant, we pray, that Christian hope may draw us onward to where our nature is united with you.\
SEVENTH WEEK OF EASTER

#### Sunday in the Seventh Week of Easter {#misal.dominica-vii-paschae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-vii-paschae.ant-ad-introitum}

O Lord, hear my voice, for I have called to you; of you my heart has spoken: Seek his face; hide not your face from me, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-vii-paschae.collecta}

Graciously hear our supplications, O Lord, so that we, who believe that the Saviour of the human race is with you in your glory, may experience, as he promised, until the end of the world, his abiding presence among us.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-vii-paschae.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, the prayers of your faithful with the sacrificial offerings, that through these acts of devotedness we may pass over to the glory of heaven.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-vii-paschae.ant-ad-communionem}

Father, I pray that they may be one as we also are one, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-vii-paschae.post-communionem}

Hear us, O God our Saviour, and grant us confidence that through these sacred mysteries there will be accomplished in the body of the whole Church what has already come to pass in Christ her Head.

#### Monday in the Seventh Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

You will receive the power of the Holy Spirit coming upon you, and you will be my witnesses even to the ends of the earth, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-ii.collecta}

May the power of the Holy Spirit come to us, we pray, O Lord, that we may keep your will faithfully in mind and express it in a devout way of life.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-ii.super-oblata}

May this unblemished sacrifice purify us, O Lord, and impart to our minds the force of grace from on high.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

I will not leave you orphans, says the Lord; I will come to you again, and your heart will rejoice, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-ii.post-communionem}

Graciously be present to your people, we pray, O Lord, and lead those you have imbued with heavenly mysteries to pass from former ways to newness of life.

#### Tuesday in the Seventh Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iii.ant-ad-introitum}

I am the first and the last; I was dead and am now alive. Behold, I am alive for ever and ever, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iii.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty and merciful God, that the Holy Spirit, coming near and dwelling graciously within us, may make of us a perfect temple of his glory.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iii.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, the prayers of your faithful with the sacrificial offerings, that through these acts of devotedness we may pass over to the glory of heaven.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iii.ant-ad-communionem}

The Holy Spirit, whom the Father will send in my name, will teach you all things and remind you of all I have told you, says the Lord, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iii.post-communionem}

We have partaken of the gifts of this sacred mystery, humbly imploring, O Lord, that what your Son commanded us to do in memory of him may bring us growth in charity.

#### Wednesday in the Seventh Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iv}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iv.ant-ad-introitum}

All peoples, clap your hands. Cry to God with shouts of joy, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iv.collecta}

Graciously grant to your Church, O merciful God, that, gathered by the Holy Spirit, she may be devoted to you with all her heart and united in purity of intent.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iv.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, we pray, the sacrifices instituted by your commands, and through the sacred mysteries, which we celebrate as our dutiful service, graciously complete the sanctifying work by which you are pleased to redeem us.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iv.ant-ad-communionem}

When the Paraclete comes, whom I will send you, the Spirit of Truth who proceeds from the Father, he will bear witness to me, and you also will bear witness, says the Lord, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-iv.post-communionem}

May our partaking of this divine Sacrament, O Lord, constantly increase your grace within us, and, by cleansing us with its power, make us always ready to receive so great a gift.

#### Thursday in the Seventh Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-v}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-v.ant-ad-introitum}

With boldness let us approach the throne of grace, that we may receive mercy and find grace as a timely help, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-v.collecta}

May your Spirit, O Lord, we pray, imbue us powerfully with spiritual gifts, that he may give us a mind pleasing to you, and graciously conform us to your will.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-v.super-oblata}

Graciously sanctify these gifts, O Lord, we pray, and, accepting the oblation of this spiritual sacrifice, make of us an eternal offering to you.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-v.ant-ad-communionem}

I tell you the truth, it is for your good that I go; for if I do not go away, the Paraclete will not come to you, says the Lord, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-v.post-communionem}

May the mysteries we have received, O Lord, we pray, enlighten us by the instruction they bring and restore us through our participation in them, that we may merit the gifts of the Spirit.

#### Friday in the Seventh Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-vi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-vi.ant-ad-introitum}

Christ loved us and washed us clean of our sins by his Blood, and made us into a kingdom, priests for his God and Father, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-vi.collecta}

O God, who by the glorification of your Christ and the light of the Holy Spirit have unlocked for us the gates of eternity, grant, we pray, that partaking of so great a gift, our devotion may grow deeper and our faith be strengthened.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-vi.super-oblata}

Look mercifully, O Lord, we pray, upon the sacrificial gifts of your people, and that they may become acceptable to you, let the coming of the Holy Spirit cleanse our consciences.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-vi.ant-ad-communionem}

When the Spirit of truth comes, he will teach you all truth, says the Lord, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-feria-vi.post-communionem}

O God, by whose mysteries we are cleansed and nourished, grant, we pray, that this banquet which you give us may bring everlasting life.

#### Saturday in the Seventh Week of Easter {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-sabbato-ad-missam-matutinam}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-sabbato-ad-missam-matutinam.ant-ad-introitum}

The disciples devoted themselves with one accord to prayer with the women, and Mary the Mother of Jesus, and his brethren, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-sabbato-ad-missam-matutinam.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that we who have celebrated the paschal festivities may by your gift hold fast to them in the way that we live our lives.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-sabbato-ad-missam-matutinam.super-oblata}

May the Holy Spirit coming near, we pray, O Lord, prepare our minds for the divine Sacrament, since the Spirit himself is the remission of all sins.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-sabbato-ad-missam-matutinam.ant-ad-communionem}

The Holy Spirit will glorify me, for he will take from what is mine and declare it to you, says the Lord, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-vii-paschae-sabbato-ad-missam-matutinam.post-communionem}

Hear in your compassion our prayers, O Lord, that as we have been brought from things of the past to new mysteries, so, with former ways left behind, we may be made new in holiness of mind.

### PENTECOST SUNDAY

#### PENTECOST SUNDAY — VIGIL MASS {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-vigilia}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-introitum}

The love of God has been poured into our hearts through the Spirit of God dwelling within us, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-vigilia.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who willed the paschal mystery to be encompassed as a sign in fifty days, grant that from out of the scattered nations, the confusion of tongues may be gathered by heavenly grace into one confession of your name.

*Or:*

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that the splendour of your glory may shine forth upon us, and that, by the bright rays of the Holy Spirit, the light of your light may confirm the hearts of those born again by your grace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-vigilia.super-oblata}

Pour out upon these gifts the blessing of your Spirit, we pray, O Lord, so that through them your Church may be imbued with such love that the truth of your saving mystery may shine forth for the whole world.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-communionem}

On the last day of the festival, Jesus stood and cried out: If anyone is thirsty, let him come to me and drink, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-vigilia.post-communionem}

May these gifts we have consumed benefit us, O Lord, that we may always be aflame with the same Spirit whom you wondrously poured out on your Apostles.

#### PENTECOST SUNDAY — MASS DURING THE DAY {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-die}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-introitum}

The Spirit of the Lord has filled the whole world and that which contains all things understands what is said, alleluia.

*Or:*

The love of God has been poured into our hearts through the Spirit of God dwelling within us, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-die.collecta}

O God, who by the mystery of today's great feast sanctify your whole Church in every people and nation, pour out, we pray, the gifts of the Holy Spirit across the face of the earth and, with the divine grace that was at work when the Gospel was first proclaimed, fill now once more the hearts of believers.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-die.super-oblata}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that, in accordance with your Son's promise, the Holy Spirit may reveal to us more abundantly the hidden mystery of this sacrifice and graciously lead us into all truth.

##### Preface {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-die.praefatio}

The mystery of Pentecost\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God.\
For, bringing your paschal mystery to completion, you bestowed the Holy Spirit today on those you made your adopted children by uniting them to your Only Begotten Son. This same Spirit, as the Church came to birth, opened to all peoples the knowledge of God and brought together the many languages of the earth in profession of the one faith.\
Therefore, overcome with paschal joy, every land, every people exults in your praise and even the heavenly Powers, with the angelic hosts, sing together the unending hymn of your glory, as they acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-communionem}

They were all filled with the Holy Spirit, and spoke of the marvels of God, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-pentecostes-ad-missam-in-die.post-communionem}

O God, who bestow heavenly gifts upon your Church, safeguard, we pray, the grace you have given, that the gift of the Holy Sprit poured out upon her, may retain all its force and that this spiritual food may gain her abundance of eternal redemption.\
THE BLESSED VIRGIN MARY, MOTHER OF THE CHURCH \[Memorial\]

*(Monday after Pentecost)*

*Votive Mass of*

#### JESUS CHRIST, THE ETERNAL HIGH PRIEST \[National calendar, Feast\] {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-summi-et-aeterni-sacerdotis}

*(Thursday after Pentecost)*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-summi-et-aeterni-sacerdotis.ant-ad-introitum}

Christ, Mediator of the New Covenant, has an eternal priesthood because he remains for ever.

##### Collect {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-summi-et-aeterni-sacerdotis.collecta}

O God, who for the glory of your majesty and the salvation of the human race, made your Only Begotten Son the Eternal High Priest, grant that, through the outpouring of the Holy Spirit, those whom he has chosen as ministers and stewards of his mysteries may be found faithful in carrying out the ministry they have received.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-summi-et-aeterni-sacerdotis.super-oblata}

Lord, may our Mediator Jesus Christ render these gifts acceptable to you, and may he present us as sacrifices pleasing to you in union with him.

##### Preface {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-summi-et-aeterni-sacerdotis.praefatio}

The priesthood of Christ and of the Church\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God. For by the anointing of the Holy Spirit you made your Only Begotten Son High Priest of the new and eternal covenant, and by your wondrous design were pleased to decree that his one Priesthood should continue in the Church. For Christ not only adorns with a royal priesthood the people he has made his own, but with a brother’s kindness he also chooses men to become sharers in his sacred ministry through the laying on of hands. They are to renew in his name the sacrifice of human redemption, to set before your children the paschal banquet, to lead your holy people in charity, to nourish them with the word and strengthen them with the Sacraments. As they give up their lives for you and for the salvation of their brothers and sisters, they strive to be conformed to the image of Christ himself and offer you a constant witness of faith and love. And so, Lord, with all the Angels and Saints, we, too, give you thanks, as in exultation we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-summi-et-aeterni-sacerdotis.ant-ad-communionem}

And behold, I am with you always, even to the end of the age.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-summi-et-aeterni-sacerdotis.post-communionem}

May this divine sacrifice we have offered and received fill us with life, O Lord, we pray, so that, bound to you in lasting charity, we may bear fruit that lasts for ever.\
Ordinary Time ORDINARY TIME\
Solemnities and Feasts in Ordinary Time\
Other movable feastdays in Ordinary Time\
(Monday after Pentecost)\
(Thursday after Pentecost)\
Weeks in Ordinary Time

#### THE MOST HOLY TRINITY \[Solemnity\] {#misal.sanctissimae-trinitatis}

*(Sunday after Pentecost)*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.sanctissimae-trinitatis.ant-ad-introitum}

Blest be God the Father, and the Only Begotten Son of God, and also the Holy Spirit: for he has shown us his merciful love.

##### Collect {#misal.sanctissimae-trinitatis.collecta}

God our Father, who by sending into the world the Word of truth and the Spirit of sanctification made known to the human race your wondrous mystery; grant us, we pray, that in professing the true faith, we may acknowledge the Trinity of eternal glory, and adore your Unity, powerful in majesty.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.sanctissimae-trinitatis.super-oblata}

Sanctify by the invocation of your name, we pray, O Lord our God, this oblation of our service, and by it make of us an eternal offering to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.sanctissimae-trinitatis.ant-ad-communionem}

Since you are children of God, God has sent into your hearts the Spirit of his Son, the Spirit who cries out: Abba, Father.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.sanctissimae-trinitatis.post-communionem}

May receiving this Sacrament, O Lord our God, bring us health of body and soul, as we confess your eternal holy Trinity and undivided Unity.

#### THE BODY AND BLOOD OF CHRIST \[Solemnity\] {#misal.sanctissimi-corporis-et-sanguinis-christi}

*(Second Sunday after Pentecost, or Thursday after Trinity Sunday)*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.sanctissimi-corporis-et-sanguinis-christi.ant-ad-introitum}

He fed them with the finest wheat, and satisfied them with honey from the rock.

##### Collect {#misal.sanctissimi-corporis-et-sanguinis-christi.collecta}

O God, who in this wonderful Sacrament have left us a memorial of your Passion, grant us, we pray, so to revere the sacred mysteries of your Body and Blood that we may always experience in ourselves the fruits of your redemption.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.sanctissimi-corporis-et-sanguinis-christi.super-oblata}

Grant your Church, O Lord, we pray, the gifts of unity and peace, whose signs are to be seen in mystery in the offerings we here present.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.sanctissimi-corporis-et-sanguinis-christi.ant-ad-communionem}

Whoever eats my flesh and drinks my blood remains in me and I in him, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.sanctissimi-corporis-et-sanguinis-christi.post-communionem}

Grant, O Lord, we pray, that we may delight for all eternity in that share in your divine life, which is foreshadowed in the present age by our reception of your precious Body and Blood.

#### THE SACRED HEART OF JESUS \[Solemnity\] {#misal.sacratissimi-cordis-iesu}

*(Friday after the Second Sunday of Pentecost)*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.sacratissimi-cordis-iesu.ant-ad-introitum}

The designs of his heart are from age to age, to rescue their souls from death, and to keep them alive in famine.

##### Collect {#misal.sacratissimi-cordis-iesu.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that we who glory in the Heart of your beloved Son and recall the wonders of his love for us, may be made worthy to receive an overflowing measure of grace from that fount of heavenly gifts.

*Or:*

O God, who in the Heart of your Son, wounded by our sins, bestow on us in mercy the boundless treasures of your love, grant, we pray, that in paying him the homage of our devotion we may also offer worthy reparation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.sacratissimi-cordis-iesu.super-oblata}

Look, O Lord, we pray, on the surpassing charity in the Heart of your beloved Son, that what we offer may be a gift acceptable to you and an expiation of our offences.

##### Preface {#misal.sacratissimi-cordis-iesu.praefatio}

The boundless charity of Christ\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God, through Christ our Lord.\
For raised up high on the Cross, he gave himself up for us with a wonderful love and poured out blood and water from his pierced side, the well-spring of the Church's Sacraments, so that, won over to the open heart of the Saviour, all might draw water joyfully from the springs of salvation.\
And so, with all the Angels and Saints, we praise you as without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.sacratissimi-cordis-iesu.ant-ad-communionem}

Thus says the Lord: Let whoever is thirsty come to me and drink. Streams of living water will flow from within the one who believes in me.

*Or:*

One of the soldiers opened his side with a lance, and at once there came forth blood and water.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.sacratissimi-cordis-iesu.post-communionem}

May this, the sacrament of charity, O Lord, make us fervent with the fire of holy love, so that, drawn always to your Son, we may learn to see him in our neighbour.

#### THE IMMACULATE HEART OF MARY {#misal.immaculati-cordis-beatae-mariae-virginis}

*(Saturday following the Second Sunday after Pentecost)*

*\[Optional Memorial\]*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.immaculati-cordis-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-introitum}

My heart will rejoice in your salvation. I will sing to the Lord, who has been bountiful with me.

##### Collect {#misal.immaculati-cordis-beatae-mariae-virginis.collecta}

O God, who prepared a fit dwelling place for the Holy Spirit in the Heart of the Blessed Virgin Mary, graciously grant that, through her intercession, we may be a worthy temple of your glory.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.immaculati-cordis-beatae-mariae-virginis.super-oblata}

Look, O Lord, upon the prayers and offerings of your faithful, presented in commemoration of Blessed Mary, the Mother of God, that they may be pleasing to you and may confer on us your help and forgiveness.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.immaculati-cordis-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-communionem}

Mary treasured all these words, reflecting on them in her heart.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.immaculati-cordis-beatae-mariae-virginis.post-communionem}

Having been made partakers of eternal redemption, we pray, O Lord, that we who commemorate the Mother of your Son, may glory in the fullness of your grace and experience its continued increase for our salvation.

#### FIRST WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.hebdomada-i-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Upon a lofty throne, I saw a man seated, whom a host of angels adore, singing in unison: Behold him, the name of whose empire is eternal.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-i-per-annum.collecta}

Attend to the pleas of your people with heavenly care, O Lord, we pray, that they may see what must be done, and gain strength to do what they have seen.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-i-per-annum.super-oblata}

May your people's oblation, O Lord, find favour with you, we pray, that it may restore them to holiness and obtain what they devoutly entreat.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-i-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

With you, O Lord, is the fountain of life, and in your light we see light.

*Or:*

I have come that they may have life, and have it more abundantly, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-i-per-annum.post-communionem}

Humbly we ask you, almighty God: be graciously pleased to grant that those you renew with your Sacraments may also serve with lives pleasing to you.

#### SECOND WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-ii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

All the earth shall bow down before you, O God, and shall sing to you; shall sing to your name, O Most High!

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-ii-per-annum.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who govern all things, both in heaven and on earth, mercifully hear the pleading of your people and bestow your peace on our times.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-ii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Grant us, O Lord, we pray, that we may participate worthily in these mysteries, for whenever the memorial of this sacrifice is celebrated the work of our redemption is accomplished.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

You have prepared a table before me, and how precious is the chalice that quenches my thirst.

*Or:*

We have come to know and to believe in the love that God has for us.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-ii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Pour on us, O Lord, the Spirit of your love, and in your kindness make those you have nourished by this one heavenly Bread, one in mind and heart.

#### THIRD WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-iii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

O sing a new song to the Lord; sing to the Lord, all the earth. In his presence are majesty and splendour, strength and honour in his holy place.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-iii-per-annum.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, direct our actions according to your good pleasure, that in the name of your beloved Son we may abound in good works.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-iii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Accept our offerings, O Lord, we pray, and in sanctifying them grant that they may profit us for salvation.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Look toward the Lord and be radiant; let your faces not be abashed.

*Or:*

I am the light of the world, says the Lord; whoever follows me will not walk in darkness, but will have the light of life.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-iii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, receiving the grace by which you bring us to new life, we may always glory in your gift.

#### FOURTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-iv-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iv-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Save us, O Lord our God! And gather us from the nations, to give thanks to your holy name, and make it our glory to praise you.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-iv-per-annum.collecta}

Grant us, Lord our God, that we may honour you with all our mind, and love everyone in truth of heart.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-iv-per-annum.super-oblata}

O Lord, we bring to your altar these offerings of our service: be pleased to receive them, we pray, and transform them into the Sacrament of our redemption.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-iv-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Let your face shine on your servant. Save me in your merciful love. O Lord, let me never be put to shame, for I call on you.

*Or:*

Blessed are the poor in spirit, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven. Blessed are the meek, for they shall possess the land.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-iv-per-annum.post-communionem}

Nourished by these redeeming gifts, we pray, O Lord, that through this help to eternal salvation true faith may ever increase.

#### FIFTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-v-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-v-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

O come, let us worship God, and bow low before the God who made us, for he is the Lord our God.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-v-per-annum.collecta}

Keep your family safe, O Lord, with unfailing care, that, relying solely on the hope of heavenly grace, they may be defended always by your protection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-v-per-annum.super-oblata}

O Lord, our God, who once established these created things to sustain us in our frailty, grant, we pray, that they may become for us now the Sacrament of eternal life.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-v-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Let them thank the Lord for his mercy, his wonders for the children of men; for he satisfies the thirsty soul, and the hungry he fills with good things.

*Or:*

Blessed are those who mourn, for they shall be consoled. Blessed are those who hunger and thirst for righteousness, for they shall have their fill.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-v-per-annum.post-communionem}

O God, who have willed that we be partakers in the one Bread and the one Chalice, grant us, we pray, so to live that, made one in Christ, we may joyfully bear fruit for the salvation of the world.

#### SIXTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-vi-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-vi-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Be my protector, O God, a mighty stronghold to save me. For you are my rock, my stronghold! Lead me, guide me, for the sake of your name.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-vi-per-annum.collecta}

O God, who teach us that you abide in hearts that are just and true, grant that we may be so fashioned by your grace as to become a dwelling pleasing to you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-vi-per-annum.super-oblata}

May this oblation, O Lord, we pray, cleanse and renew us and may it become for those who do your will the source of eternal reward.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-vi-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

They ate and had their fill, and what they craved the Lord gave them; they were not disappointed in what they craved.

*Or:*

God so loved the world that he gave his only Begotten Son, that all who believe in him may not perish, but may have eternal life.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-vi-per-annum.post-communionem}

Having fed upon these heavenly delights, we pray, O Lord, that we may always long for that food by which we truly live

#### SEVENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-vii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-vii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

O Lord, I trust in your merciful love. Let my heart rejoice in your salvation. I will sing to the Lord who has been bountiful with me.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-vii-per-annum.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, always pondering spiritual things, we may carry out in both word and deed that which is pleasing to you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-vii-per-annum.super-oblata}

As we celebrate your mysteries, O Lord, with the observance that is your due, we humbly ask you, that what we offer to the honour of your majesty may profit us for salvation.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-vii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

I will recount all your wonders. I will rejoice in you and be glad, and sing psalms to your name, O Most High.

*Or:*

Lord, I have come to believe that you are the Christ, the Son of the living God, who is coming into this world.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-vii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that we may experience the effects of the salvation which is pledged to us through these mysteries.

#### EIGHTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-viii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-viii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord became my protector. He brought me out to a place of freedom; he saved me because he delighted in me.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-viii-per-annum.collecta}

Grant us, O Lord, we pray, that the course of our world may be directed by your peaceful rule and that your Church may rejoice untroubled in her devotion.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-viii-per-annum.super-oblata}

O God, who provides gifts to be offered to your name and count our oblations as signs of our desire to serve you with devotion, we ask of your mercy, that what you grant as the source of merit may also help us to attain merit's reward.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-viii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

I will sing to the Lord who has been bountiful with me, sing psalms to the name of the Lord Most High.

*Or:*

Behold, I am with you always, even to the end of the age, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-viii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Nourished by your saving gifts, we beseech your mercy, O Lord, that by this same Sacrament with which you feed us in the present age, you may make us partakers of life eternal.

#### NINTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-ix-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ix-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Turn to me and have mercy on me, for I am alone and poor. See my lowliness and suffering, and take away all my sins, my God.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-ix-per-annum.collecta}

O God, whose providence never fails in its design, keep from us, we humbly beseech you, all that might harm us and grant all that works for our good.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-ix-per-annum.super-oblata}

Trusting in your compassion, O Lord, we come eagerly with our offerings to your sacred altar, that, through the purifying action of your grace, we may be cleansed by the very mysteries we serve.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-ix-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

To you I call; for you will surely heed me, O God. Turn your ear to me; hear my words.

*Or:*

Amen, I say to you: Whatever you ask for in prayer, believe you will receive it, and it will be yours, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-ix-per-annum.post-communionem}

Govern by your Spirit, we pray, O Lord, those you feed with the Body and Blood of your Son, that, professing you not just in word or in speech, but also in works and in truth, we may merit to enter the Kingdom of Heaven.

#### TENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-x-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-x-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord is my light and my salvation; whom shall I fear? The Lord is the stronghold of my life; whom should I dread? When those who do evil draw near, they stumble and fall.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-x-per-annum.collecta}

O God, from whom all good things come, grant that we who call on you in our need may at your prompting discern what is right, and by your guidance do it.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-x-per-annum.super-oblata}

Look kindly upon our service, O Lord, we pray, that what we offer may be an acceptable oblation to you and lead us to grow in charity.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-x-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

The Lord is my rock, my fortress, and my deliverer; my God is my saving strength.

*Or:*

God is love, and whoever abides in love abides in God, and God in him.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-x-per-annum.post-communionem}

May your healing work, O Lord, free us, we pray, from doing evil and lead us to what is right.

#### ELEVENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xi-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xi-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

O Lord, hear my voice, for I have called to you; be my help. Do not abandon or forsake me, O God, my Saviour!

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xi-per-annum.collecta}

O God, strength of those who hope in you, graciously hear our pleas, and since without you mortal frailty can do nothing, grant us always the help of your grace, that in following your commands we may please you by our resolve and our deeds.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xi-per-annum.super-oblata}

O God, who in the offerings presented here provide for the twofold needs of human nature, nourishing us with food and renewing us with your Sacrament, grant, we pray, that the sustenance they provide may not fail us in body or in spirit.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xi-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

There is one thing I ask of the Lord, only this do I seek: to live in the house of the Lord all the days of my life.

*Or:*

Holy Father, keep in your name those you have given me, that they may be one as we are one, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xi-per-annum.post-communionem}

As this reception of your Holy Communion, O Lord, foreshadows the union of the faithful with you, so may it bring about unity in your Church.

#### TWELFTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord is the strength of his people, a saving refuge for the one he has anointed. Save your people, Lord, and bless your heritage, and govern them forever.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xii-per-annum.collecta}

Grant, O Lord, that we may always revere and love your holy Name, for you never deprive of your guidance those you set firm on the foundation of your love.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, the sacrifice of conciliation and praise and grant that, cleansed by its action, we may make offering of a heart pleasing to you.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

The eyes of all look to you, Lord, and you give them their food in due season.

*Or:*

I am the Good Shepherd, and I lay down my life for my sheep, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Renewed and nourished by the Sacred Body and Precious Blood of your Son, we ask of your mercy, O Lord, that what we celebrate with constant devotion may be our sure pledge of redemption.

#### THIRTEENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xiii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xiii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

All peoples, clap your hands. Cry to God with shouts of joy!

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xiii-per-annum.collecta}

O God, who through the grace of adoption chose us to be children of light, grant, we pray, that we may not be wrapped in the darkness of error but always be seen to stand in the bright light of truth.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xiii-per-annum.super-oblata}

O God, who graciously accomplish the effects of your mysteries, grant, we pray, that the deeds by which we serve you may be worthy of these sacred gifts.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xiii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Bless the Lord, O my soul, and all within me, his holy name.

*Or:*

O Father, I pray for them, that they may be one in us, that the world may believe that you have sent me, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xiii-per-annum.post-communionem}

May this divine sacrifice we have offered and received fill us with life, O Lord, we pray, so that, bound to you in lasting charity, we may bear fruit that lasts for ever.

#### FOURTEENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xiv-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xiv-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Your merciful love, O God, we have received in the midst of your temple. Your praise, O God, like your name, reaches the ends of the earth. Your right hand is filled with saving justice.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xiv-per-annum.collecta}

O God, who in the abasement of your Son have raised up a fallen world, fill your faithful with holy joy, for on those you have rescued from slavery to sin you bestow eternal gladness.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xiv-per-annum.super-oblata}

May this oblation dedicated to your Name purify us, O Lord, and day by day bring our conduct closer to the life of heaven.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xiv-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Taste and see that the Lord is good. Blessed the man who seeks refuge in him.

*Or:*

Come to me, all who labour and are burdened, and I will refresh you, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xiv-per-annum.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that having been replenished by such great gifts, we may gain the prize of salvation and never cease to praise you.

#### FIFTEENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xv-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xv-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

As for me, in justice I shall behold your face; I shall be filled with the vision of your glory.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xv-per-annum.collecta}

O God, who show the light of your truth to those who go stray so that they may return to the right path, give all who for the faith they profess are accounted Christians, the grace to reject whatever is contrary to the name of Christ and to strive after all that does it honour.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xv-per-annum.super-oblata}

Look upon the offerings of the Church, O Lord, as she makes her prayer to you, and grant that, when consumed by those who believe, they may bring ever greater holiness.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xv-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

The sparrow finds a home, and the swallow a nest for her young: by your altars, O Lord of hosts, my king and my God. Blessed are they who dwell in your house, forever singing your praise.

*Or:*

Whoever eats my flesh and drinks my blood remains in me and I in him, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xv-per-annum.post-communionem}

Having consumed these gifts, we pray, O Lord, that by our participation in this mystery, its saving effects upon us may grow.

#### SIXTEENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xvi-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xvi-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

See, I have God for my help. The Lord sustains my soul. I will sacrifice to you with willing heart, and praise your name, O Lord, for it is good.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xvi-per-annum.collecta}

Show favour, O Lord, to your servants, and mercifully increase the gifts of your grace, that made fervent in hope, faith and charity, they may be ever watchful in keeping your commands.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xvi-per-annum.super-oblata}

O God, who in the one perfect sacrifice brought to completion varied offerings of the law, accept, we pray, this sacrifice from your faithful servants and make it holy, as you blessed the gifts of Abel, so that what each has offered to the honour of your majesty may benefit the salvation of all.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xvi-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

The Lord, the gracious, the merciful, has made a memorial of his wonders; he gives food to those who fear him.\
Or:\
Behold, I stand at the door and knock, says the Lord. If anyone hears my voice and opens the door to me, I will enter his house and dine with him, and he with me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xvi-per-annum.post-communionem}

Graciously be present to your people, we pray, O Lord, and lead those you have imbued with heavenly mysteries to pass from former ways to newness of life.

#### SEVENTEENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xvii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xvii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

God is in his holy place, God who unites those who dwell in his house; he himself gives might and strength to his people.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xvii-per-annum.collecta}

O God, protector of those who hope in you, without whom nothing has firm foundation, nothing is holy, bestow in abundance your mercy upon us and grant that, with you as our ruler and guide, we may use the good things that pass in such a way as to hold fast even now to those that ever endure.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xvii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, we pray, the offerings which we bring from the abundance of your gifts, that through the powerful working of your grace these most sacred mysteries may sanctify our present way of life and lead us to eternal gladness.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xvii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Bless the Lord, O my soul, and never forget all his benefits.\
Or:\
Blessed are the merciful, for they shall receive mercy. Blessed are the clean of heart, for they shall see God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xvii-per-annum.post-communionem}

We have consumed, O Lord, this divine Sacrament, the perpetual memorial of the Passion of your Son; grant, we pray, that this gift, which he himself gave us with love beyond all telling, may profit us for salvation.

#### EIGHTEENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xviii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xviii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

O God, come to my assistance; O Lord, make haste to help me! You are my rescuer, my help; O Lord, do not delay.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xviii-per-annum.collecta}

Draw near to your servants, O Lord, and answer their prayers with unceasing kindness, that, for those who glory in you as their Creator and guide, you may restore what you have created and keep safe what you have restored.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xviii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Graciously sanctify these gifts, O Lord, we pray, and, accepting the oblation of this spiritual sacrifice, make of us an eternal offering to you.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xviii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

You have given us, O Lord, bread from heaven, endowed with all delights and sweetness in every taste.\
Or:\
I am the bread of life, says the Lord; whoever comes to me will not hunger, and whoever believes in me will not thirst.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xviii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Accompany with constant protection, O Lord, those you renew with these heavenly gifts, and in your never-failing care for them, make them worthy of eternal redemption.

#### NINETEENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xix-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xix-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Look to your covenant, O Lord; forget not the life of your poor ones forever. Arise, O God, and defend your cause; and forget not the cries of those who seek you.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xix-per-annum.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, whom, taught by the Holy Spirit, we dare to call our Father, bring, we pray, to perfection in our hearts the spirit of adoption as your sons and daughters, that we may merit to enter into the inheritance which you have promised.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xix-per-annum.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, to accept the offerings of your Church, for in your mercy you have given them to be offered, and by your power you transform them into the mystery of our salvation.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xix-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

O Jerusalem, glorify the Lord who gives you your fill of finest wheat.\
Or:\
The bread that I will give, says the Lord, is my flesh for the life of the world.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xix-per-annum.post-communionem}

May the communion of your Sacrament that we have consumed, save us, O Lord, and confirm us in the light of your truth.

#### TWENTIETH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xx-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xx-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Turn your eyes, O God, our shield; and look on the face of your anointed one; one day within your courts is better than a thousand elsewhere.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xx-per-annum.collecta}

O God, who have prepared for those who love you good things which no eye can see, fill our hearts, we pray, with the warmth of your love, so that, loving you in all things and above all things, we may attain your promises, which surpass every human desire.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xx-per-annum.super-oblata}

Receive our oblation, O Lord, by which is brought about a glorious exchange, that by offering what you have given, we may merit to receive your very self.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xx-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

With the Lord there is mercy; in him is plentiful redemption.\
Or:\
I am the living bread that came down from heaven, says the Lord. Whoever eats of this bread will live forever.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xx-per-annum.post-communionem}

Made partakers of Christ through these Sacraments, we humbly implore your mercy, Lord, that, conformed to his image on earth, we may merit also to be his coheirs in heaven.

#### TWENTY-FIRST WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxi-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxi-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Turn your ear, O Lord, and answer me; save the servant who trusts in you, my God. Have mercy on me, O Lord, for I cry to you all the day long.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxi-per-annum.collecta}

O God, who cause the minds of the faithful to unite in a single purpose, grant your people to love what you command and to desire what you promise, that, amid the uncertainties of this world, our hearts may be fixed on that place where true gladness is found.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxi-per-annum.super-oblata}

O Lord, who gained for yourself a people by adoption through the one sacrifice offered once for all, bestow on us graciously, we pray, the gifts of unity and peace in your Church.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxi-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

The earth is replete with the fruits of your work, O Lord; you bring forth bread from the earth, and wine to cheer the heart.\
Or:\
Whoever eats my flesh and drinks my blood has eternal life, says the Lord; and I will raise him up on the last day.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxi-per-annum.post-communionem}

Complete within us, O Lord, we pray, the healing work of your mercy, and graciously perfect and sustain us, so that in all things we may please you.

#### TWENTY-SECOND WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Have mercy on me, O Lord, for I cry to you all the day long. O Lord, you are good and forgiving, full of mercy to all who call to you.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxii-per-annum.collecta}

God of might, giver of every good gift, put into our hearts the love of your name, so that by deepening our sense of reverence, you may nurture in us what is good and, by your watchful care, keep safe what you have nurtured.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxii-per-annum.super-oblata}

May this sacred offering, O Lord, confer on us always the blessing of salvation, that what it celebrates in mystery it may accomplish in power.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

How great is the goodness, Lord, that you keep for those who fear you.\
Or:\
Blessed are the peacemakers, for they shall be called children of God. Blessed are those who are persecuted for the sake of righteousness, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Renewed by this bread from the heavenly table, we beseech you, Lord, that, being the food of charity, it may confirm our hearts and stir us to serve you in our neighbour.

#### TWENTY-THIRD WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxiii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxiii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

You are just, O Lord, and your judgment is right; treat your servant in accord with your merciful love.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxiii-per-annum.collecta}

O God, by whom we are redeemed and receive adoption, look graciously upon your beloved sons and daughters, that those who believe in Christ may receive true freedom and an everlasting inheritance.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxiii-per-annum.super-oblata}

O God, who give us the gift of true prayer and of peace, graciously grant that, through this offering, we may do fitting homage to your divine majesty and, by partaking of the sacred mystery, we may be faithfully united in mind and heart.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxiii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Like the deer that yearns for running streams, so my soul is yearning for you, my God: my soul is thirsting for God, the living God.\
Or:\
I am the light of the world, says the Lord; whoever follows me will not walk in darkness but will have the light of life.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxiii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Grant that your faithful, O Lord, whom you nourish and endow with life through the food of your Word and heavenly Sacrament, may so benefit from your beloved Son's great gifts that we merit an eternal share in his life.

#### TWENTY-FOURTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxiv-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxiv-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Give peace, O Lord, to those who wait for you, that your prophets be found true. Hear the prayers of your servant, and of your people Israel.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxiv-per-annum.collecta}

Look upon us, O God, Creator and ruler of all things, and that we may feel the working of your mercy, grant that we may serve you with all our heart.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxiv-per-annum.super-oblata}

Look with favour on our supplications, O Lord, and in your kindness accept these, your servants' offerings, that what each has offered to the honour of your name may serve the salvation of all.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxiv-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

How precious is your mercy, O God! The children of men seek shelter in the shadow of your wings.\
Or:\
The chalice of blessing that we bless is a communion in the Blood of Christ; and the bread that we break is a sharing in the Body of the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxiv-per-annum.post-communionem}

May the working of this heavenly gift, O Lord, we pray, take possession of our minds and bodies, so that its effects, and not our own desires, may always prevail in us.

#### TWENTY-FIFTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxv-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxv-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

I am the salvation of the people, says the Lord. Should they cry to me in any distress, I will hear them; and I will be their Lord forever.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxv-per-annum.collecta}

O God, who founded all the commands of your sacred Law upon love of you and of our neighbour, grant that, by keeping your precepts, we may merit to attain eternal life.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxv-per-annum.super-oblata}

Receive with favour, O Lord, we pray, the offerings of your people, that what they profess with devotion and faith may be theirs through these heavenly mysteries.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxv-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

You have laid down your precepts to be carefully kept. May my ways be firm in keeping your statutes.\
Or:\
I am the Good Shepherd, says the Lord; I know my sheep, and mine know me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxv-per-annum.post-communionem}

Graciously raise up, O Lord, those you renew with this Sacrament, that we may come to possess your redemption both in mystery and in the manner of our life.

#### TWENTY-SIXTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxvi-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxvi-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

All that you have done to us, O Lord, you have done with true judgment, for we have sinned against you and not obeyed your commandments. But give glory to your name and deal with us according to the bounty of your mercy.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxvi-per-annum.collecta}

O God, who manifest your almighty power above all by pardoning and showing mercy, bestow, we pray, your grace abundantly upon us, and make those hastening to attain your promises heirs to the treasures of heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxvi-per-annum.super-oblata}

Grant, us, O merciful God, that this our offering may find acceptance with you and that through it the wellspring of all blessing may be laid open before us.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxvi-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Remember your word to your servant, O Lord, by which you have given me hope. This is my comfort when I am brought low.\
Or:\
By this we came to know the love of God: that Christ laid down his life for us. So we ought to lay down our lives for one another.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxvi-per-annum.post-communionem}

May this heavenly mystery, O Lord, restore us in mind and body, that we may be coheirs in glory with Christ, to whose suffering we are united whenever we proclaim his Death.

#### TWENTY-SEVENTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxvii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxvii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Within your will, O Lord, all things are established and there is none that can resist your will. For you have made all things, the heaven and the earth, and all that is held within the circle of heaven; you are the Lord of all.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxvii-per-annum.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who in the abundance of your kindness surpass the merits and the desires of those who entreat you, pour out your mercy upon us: to pardon what conscience dreads, and give what prayer does not dare to ask.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxvii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, we pray, the sacrifices instituted by your commands, and through the sacred mysteries, which we celebrate with dutiful service, graciously complete the sanctifying work by which you are pleased to redeem us.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxvii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

The Lord is good to those who hope in him, to the soul that seeks him.\
Or:\
Though many, we are one bread, one body: we who share, all of us, the one bread and one chalice.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxvii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Grant us, almighty God, that we may be refreshed and nourished by the Sacrament which we have received, so as to be transformed into what we consume.

#### TWENTY-EIGHTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxviii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxviii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

If you, O Lord, should mark iniquities, Lord, who could stand? But with you is found forgiveness, O God of Israel.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxviii-per-annum.collecta}

May your grace, O Lord, we pray, at all times go before us and follow after, and make us always determined to carry out good works.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxviii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, the prayers of your faithful with the sacrificial offerings, that through these acts of devotedness, we may pass over to the glory of heaven.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxviii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

The rich suffer want and go hungry, but those who seek the Lord lack no blessing.\
Or:\
When the Lord appears, we shall be like him; for we shall see him as he is.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxviii-per-annum.post-communionem}

We entreat your majesty most humbly, O Lord, that, as you feed us with the nourishment which comes from the most holy Body and Blood of your Son, so you may make us sharers of his divine nature.

#### TWENTY-NINTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxix-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxix-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

To you I call; for you will surely heed me, O God. Turn your ear to me; hear my words. Guard me as the apple of your eye; in the shadow of your wings protect me.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxix-per-annum.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, grant that we may always conform our will to yours and serve your majesty in sincerity of heart.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxix-per-annum.super-oblata}

Grant us, Lord, we pray, a sincere respect for your gifts, that, through the purifying action of your grace, we may be cleansed by the very mysteries we serve.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxix-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, the eyes of the Lord are on those who fear him, who hope in his merciful love, to rescue their souls from death, to keep them alive in famine.\
Or:\
The Son of Man has come to give his life as a ransom for many.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxix-per-annum.post-communionem}

Grant us, O Lord, we pray, that benefiting from participation in heavenly things, we may be helped by what you give in this present age and prepared for the gifts that are eternal.

#### THIRTIETH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxx-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxx-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Let the hearts that seek the Lord rejoice. Turn to the Lord and his strength; constantly seek his face.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxx-per-annum.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, increase our faith, hope and charity, and make us love what you command, so that we may merit what you promise.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxx-per-annum.super-oblata}

Look, we pray, O Lord, on the offerings we make to your majesty, that whatever is done by us in your service may be directed above all to your glory.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxx-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

We will ring out our joy at your saving help, and exult in the name of our God.\
Or:\
Christ loved us and gave himself up for us, as a fragrant offering to God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxx-per-annum.post-communionem}

May your Sacraments, O Lord, we pray, perfect in us what lies within them, that the things we now celebrate in signs we may one day possess in truth.

#### THIRTY-FIRST WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxxi-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxxi-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Forsake me not, O Lord, my God; be not far from me! Make haste and come to my help, O Lord, my strong salvation!

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxxi-per-annum.collecta}

Almighty and merciful God, by whose gift your faithful offer you right and praiseworthy service, grant, we pray, that we may hasten without stumbling to receive the things you have promised.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxxi-per-annum.super-oblata}

May these sacrificial offerings, O Lord, become for you a pure oblation, and for us a holy outpouring of your mercy.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxxi-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

You will show me the path of life, the fullness of joy in your presence, O Lord.\
Or:\
Just as the living Father sent me and I have life because of the Father, so whoever feeds on me shall have life because of me, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxxi-per-annum.post-communionem}

May the working of your power, O Lord, increase in us, we pray, so that, renewed by these heavenly Sacraments, we may be prepared by your gift for receiving what they promise.

#### THIRTY-SECOND WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxxii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxxii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

Let my prayer come into your presence. Incline your ear to my cry for help, O Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxxii-per-annum.collecta}

Almighty and merciful God, graciously keep from us all adversity, so that, unhindered in mind and body alike, we may pursue in freedom of heart the things that are yours.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxxii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Look with favour, we pray, O Lord, upon the sacrificial gifts offered here, that celebrating in mystery the Passion of your Son, we may honour it with loving devotion.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxxii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

The Lord is my shepherd; there is nothing I shall want. Fresh and green are the pastures where he gives me repose. Near restful waters he leads me.\
Or:\
The disciples recognized the Lord Jesus in the breaking of bread.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxxii-per-annum.post-communionem}

Nourished by this sacred gift, O Lord, we give you thanks and beseech your mercy, that by the pouring forth of your Spirit the grace of integrity may endure in those your heavenly power has entered.

#### THIRTY-THIRD WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.dominica-xxxiii-per-annum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxxiii-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord said: I think thoughts of peace and not of affliction. You will call upon me, and I will answer you, and I will lead back your captives from every place.

##### Collect {#misal.dominica-xxxiii-per-annum.collecta}

Grant us, we pray, O Lord our God, the constant gladness of being devoted to you, for it is full and lasting happiness to serve with constancy the author of all that is good.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.dominica-xxxiii-per-annum.super-oblata}

Grant, O Lord, we pray, that what we offer in the sight of your majesty may obtain for us the grace of being devoted to you and gain us the prize of everlasting happiness.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.dominica-xxxiii-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

To be near God is my happiness, to place my hope in God the Lord.\
Or:\
Amen, I say to you, whatever you ask in prayer, believe that you will receive, and it shall be given to you, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.dominica-xxxiii-per-annum.post-communionem}

We have partaken of the gifts of this sacred mystery, humbly imploring, O Lord, that what your Son commanded us to do in memory of him may bring us growth in charity.

#### THIRTY-FOURTH WEEK IN ORDINARY TIME {#misal.hebdomada-xxxiv-per-annum}

*During weekdays:*

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-xxxiv-per-annum.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord speaks of peace to his people and his holy ones, and to those who turn to him.

##### Collect {#misal.hebdomada-xxxiv-per-annum.collecta}

Stir up the will of your faithful, we pray, O Lord, that, striving more eagerly to bring your divine work to fruitful completion, they may receive in greater measure the healing remedies your kindness bestows.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.hebdomada-xxxiv-per-annum.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, the sacred offerings which at your bidding we dedicate to your name and, in order that through these gifts we may become worthy of your love, grant us unfailing obedience to your commands.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.hebdomada-xxxiv-per-annum.ant-ad-communionem}

O praise the Lord, all you nations; for his merciful love towards us is great.\
Or:\
Behold, I am with you always, even to the end of the age, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.hebdomada-xxxiv-per-annum.post-communionem}

We pray, almighty God, that those to whom you give the joy of participating in divine mysteries may never be parted from you.

#### LAST SUNDAY IN ORDINARY TIME — OUR LORD JESUS CHRIST THE KING \[Solemnity\] {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-universorum-regis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-universorum-regis.ant-ad-introitum}

How worthy is the Lamb who was slain, to receive power and divinity, and wisdom and strength and honour. To him belong glory and power for ever and ever.

##### Collect {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-universorum-regis.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, whose will is to restore all things in your beloved Son, the King of the universe; grant, we pray, that the whole creation, set free from slavery, may render your majesty service and ceaselessly proclaim your praise.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-universorum-regis.super-oblata}

As we offer you, O Lord, the sacrifice by which the human race is reconciled to you, we humbly pray, that your Son himself may bestow on all nations the gifts of unity and peace.

##### Preface {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-universorum-regis.praefatio}

Christ, King of the universe\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God.\
