For you anointed your Only Begotten Son, our Lord Jesus Christ, with the oil of gladness as eternal Priest and King of all creation, so that, by offering himself on the altar of the Cross as a spotless sacrifice to bring us peace, he might accomplish the mysteries of human redemption and making all created things subject to his rule, he might present to the immensity of your majesty an eternal and universal kingdom, a kingdom of truth and life, a kingdom of holiness and grace, a kingdom of justice, love and peace.\
And so, with Angels and Archangels, with Thrones and Dominions, and with all the hosts and Powers of heaven, as we sing the hymn of your glory without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-universorum-regis.ant-ad-communionem}

The Lord sits as King forever. The Lord will bless his people with peace.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.domini-nostri-iesu-christi-universorum-regis.post-communionem}

Having received the food of immortality, we ask, O Lord, that glorying in obedience to the commands of Christ, the King of the universe, we may live with him eternally in his heavenly kingdom.

## Proper of Saints

### JANUARY

#### January 1 — MARY, MOTHER OF GOD \[Solemnity\] — Octave of Christmas

*Octave of Christmas*

#### January 2 — Saints Basil the Great and Gregory Nazianzen, bishops and doctors of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-2-ianuarii-ss-basilii-magni-et-gregorii-nazianzeni}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-2-ianuarii-ss-basilii-magni-et-gregorii-nazianzeni.ant-ad-introitum}

Let the peoples recount the wisdom of the Saints, and let the Church proclaim their praise. Their names will live on and on.

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-ianuarii-ss-basilii-magni-et-gregorii-nazianzeni.collecta}

O God, who were pleased to give light to your Church by the example and teaching of the Bishops Saints Basil and Gregory, grant, we pray, that in humility we may learn your truth and practice it faithfully in charity.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-2-ianuarii-ss-basilii-magni-et-gregorii-nazianzeni.super-oblata}

Accept this sacrifice from your people, we pray, O Lord, and make what is offered for your glory in honour of Saints Basil and Gregory a means to our eternal salvation.

*or \]*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-2-ianuarii-ss-basilii-magni-et-gregorii-nazianzeni.ant-ad-communionem}

We proclaim Christ crucified; Christ, the power of God and the wisdom of God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-2-ianuarii-ss-basilii-magni-et-gregorii-nazianzeni.post-communionem}

May partaking at the heavenly table, almighty God, confirm and increase strength from on high in all who celebrate the feast day of Saints Basil and Gregory, that we may preserve in integrity the gift of faith and walk in the path of salvation you trace for us.

#### January 3 — Holy Name of Jesus \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-3-ianuarii-sanctissimi-nominis-iesu}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-3-ianuarii-sanctissimi-nominis-iesu.ant-ad-introitum}

At the name of Jesus, every knee should bend, of those in heaven and on earth and under the earth, and every tongue confess that Jesus Christ is Lord, to the glory of God the Father.

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-ianuarii-sanctissimi-nominis-iesu.collecta}

O God, who founded the salvation of the human race on the Incarnation of your Word, give your peoples the mercy they implore, so that all may know there is no other name to be invoked but the Name of your Only Begotten Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-3-ianuarii-sanctissimi-nominis-iesu.super-oblata}

Bringing you these offerings from what your bounty bestows on us, we pray, O Lord, that just as you have given to Christ, obedient even until death, the Name that saves, so you may grant us protection by its power.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-3-ianuarii-sanctissimi-nominis-iesu.ant-ad-communionem}

O Lord, our Lord, how majestic is your name through all the earth!

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-3-ianuarii-sanctissimi-nominis-iesu.post-communionem}

May the sacrificial gifts offered to your majesty, O Lord, to honour the Name of Christ, and which we have now received, fill us, we pray, with your abundant grace, so that we may come to rejoice that our names, too, are written in heaven.

#### January 7 — Saint Raymond of Peñafort, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-7-ianuarii-s-raimundi-de-penyafort}

\[Common of , for one pastor\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-7-ianuarii-s-raimundi-de-penyafort.collecta}

O God, who adorned the Priest Saint Raymond with the virtue of outstanding mercy and compassion for sinners and for captives, grant us, through his intercession, that, released from slavery to sin, we may carry out in freedom of spirit what is pleasing to you.

#### January 12 — Saint Aelred of Rievaulx, Abbot \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-12-ianuarii-s-aelredi-rievallensis-angliae}

\[Common of for Abbots\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-ianuarii-s-aelredi-rievallensis-angliae.collecta}

O God, who endowed Saint Aelred, Abbot of Rievaulx, with the gift of fostering Christian friendship and the wisdom to lead others in the way of holiness, grant to your people, we pray, that same spirit of fraternal affection, so that in loving one another we may know the love of Christ and rejoice in the eternal possession of your supreme goodness.

#### January 13 — Saint Hilary, bishop, doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-13-ianuarii-s-hilarii}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-ianuarii-s-hilarii.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that we may rightly understand and truthfully profess the divinity of your Son, which the Bishop Saint Hilary taught with such constancy.

#### January 17 — Saint Anthony, abbot \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-17-ianuarii-s-antonii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-17-ianuarii-s-antonii.ant-ad-introitum}

The just will flourish like the palm tree, and grow like a Lebanon cedar, planted in the house of the Lord, in the courts of the house of our God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-ianuarii-s-antonii.collecta}

O God, who brought the Abbot Saint Anthony to serve you by a wondrous way of life in the desert, grant, through his intercession, that, denying ourselves, we may ever love you above all things.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-17-ianuarii-s-antonii.super-oblata}

May these offerings of our service, placed on your altar in commemoration of Saint Anthony, be acceptable to you, O Lord, we pray, and grant that, released from earthly attachments, we may have our riches in you alone.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-17-ianuarii-s-antonii.ant-ad-communionem}

If you would be perfect, go, sell what you have, give to the poor, and follow me, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-17-ianuarii-s-antonii.post-communionem}

Nourished for our healing by your Sacraments, O Lord, may we escape every snare of the enemy unharmed, just as by your grace Saint Anthony won glorious victories over the powers of darkness.

#### January 19 — Saint Wulstan, bishop, \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-19-ianuarii-s-wulstani-angliae}

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-ianuarii-s-wulstani-angliae.collecta}

Fill us, we pray you, Lord our God, with the spirit of charity which burned in the heart of Saint Wulstan your Bishop, that, as he honoured you by faithful service of your poor, so by the help of his prayers we may live lives of service and of charity, and attain to the blessed life of your kingdom.

#### January 20 — Saint Fabian {#misal.die-20-ianuarii-s-fabiani}

Saint Fabian, pope and martyr \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for popes, or of , for one martyr\]

*Or:*

Saint Sebastian, martyr \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one martyr\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-20-ianuarii-s-fabiani.collecta}

O God, glory of your Priests, grant, we pray, that, helped by the intercession of your Martyr Saint Fabian, we may advance in our sharing of the same faith as his, and in worthy service.

#### Saint Sebastian {#misal.die-20-ianuarii-s-sebastiani}

##### Collect {#misal.die-20-ianuarii-s-sebastiani.collecta}

Grant us, we pray, O Lord, a spirit of fortitude, so that, taught by the glorious example of your Martyr Saint Sebastian, we may learn to obey you rather than men.

#### January 21 — Saint Agnes, virgin and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-21-ianuarii-s-agnetis}

\[Common of or \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-ianuarii-s-agnetis.collecta}

Almighty everlasting God, who choose what is weak in the world to confound the strong, mercifully grant that we who celebrate the heavenly birthday of your Martyr Saint Agnes may follow her constancy in the faith.

#### January 22 — Saint Vincent, deacon and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-22-ianuarii-s-vincentii}

\[Common of , for one martyr\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-ianuarii-s-vincentii.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, mercifully pour out your Spirit upon us, so that our hearts may possess that strong love by which the Martyr Saint Vincent triumphed over all bodily torments.

#### January 24 — Saint Francis de Sales, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-24-ianuarii-s-francisci-de-sales}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-ianuarii-s-francisci-de-sales.collecta}

O God, who for the salvation of souls willed that the Bishop Saint Francis de Sales become all things to all, grant, graciously that, following his example, we may always display the gentleness of your charity in the service of our neighbour.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-24-ianuarii-s-francisci-de-sales.super-oblata}

Through this saving sacrifice which we offer you, O Lord, kindle in our hearts that divine fire of the Holy Spirit with which you wonderfully inflamed the most gentle soul of Saint Francis de Sales.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-24-ianuarii-s-francisci-de-sales.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that through the Sacrament we have received, we might imitate on earth the charity and meekness of Saint Francis de Sales and so attain the glory of heaven.

#### January 25 — Conversion of Saint Paul, apostle \[Feast\] {#misal.die-25-ianuarii-in-conversione-s-pauli}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-25-ianuarii-in-conversione-s-pauli.ant-ad-introitum}

I know the one in whom I have believed and I am sure that he, the just judge, the mighty, will keep safe what is my due until that day.

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-ianuarii-in-conversione-s-pauli.collecta}

O God, who taught the whole world through the preaching of the blessed Apostle Paul, draw us, we pray, nearer to you through the example of him whose conversion we celebrate today, and so make us witnesses to your truth in the world.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-25-ianuarii-in-conversione-s-pauli.super-oblata}

As we celebrate the divine mysteries, O Lord, we pray, may the Spirit fill us with that light of faith with which he constantly enlightened the blessed Apostle Paul for the spreading of your glory.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-25-ianuarii-in-conversione-s-pauli.ant-ad-communionem}

I live by faith in the Son of God, who has loved me and given himself up for me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-25-ianuarii-in-conversione-s-pauli.post-communionem}

May the Sacrament we have received, O Lord our God, stir up in us that fire of charity with which the blessed Apostle Paul burned ardently as he bore his concern for all the Churches.

#### January 26 — Saints Timothy and Titus, bishops \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-26-ianuarii-ss-timothei-et-titi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-26-ianuarii-ss-timothei-et-titi.ant-ad-introitum}

Tell among the nations his glory, and his wonders among all the peoples: the Lord is great and highly to be praised.

##### Collect {#misal.die-26-ianuarii-ss-timothei-et-titi.collecta}

O God, who adorned Saints Timothy and Titus with apostolic virtues, grant, through the intercession of them both, that, living justly and devoutly in this present age, we may merit to reach our heavenly homeland.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-26-ianuarii-ss-timothei-et-titi.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, we pray, the offerings of your people, which we bring in celebration of Saints Timothy and Titus, and in your kindness render us fully acceptable by giving us sincerity of heart.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-26-ianuarii-ss-timothei-et-titi.ant-ad-communionem}

Go into all the world, and proclaim the Gospel. I am with you always, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-26-ianuarii-ss-timothei-et-titi.post-communionem}

May the Sacrament we have received, O Lord our God, nourish in us that faith taught by the preaching of the Apostles and kept safe by the labours of Saints Timothy and Titus.

#### January 27 — Saint Angela Merici, virgin, founder \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-27-ianuarii-s-angelae-merici}

\[Common of for one virgin or for educators\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-27-ianuarii-s-angelae-merici.collecta}

May the Virgin Saint Angela never fail to commend us to your compassion O Lord, we pray, that, following the lessons of her charity and prudence, we may hold fast to your teaching and express it in what we do.

#### January 28 — Saint Thomas Aquinas, priest, religious, doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-28-ianuarii-s-thomae-de-aquino}

\[Common of , or of , for one pastor\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-ianuarii-s-thomae-de-aquino.collecta}

O God, who made Saint Thomas Aquinas outstanding in his zeal for holiness and his study of sacred doctrine, grant us, we pray, that we may understand what he taught, and imitate what he accomplished.

#### January 31 — Saint John Bosco, priest and founder \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-31-ianuarii-s-ioannis-bosco}

\[Common of , for one pastor or of , for educators\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-31-ianuarii-s-ioannis-bosco.collecta}

O God, who raised up the Priest Saint John Bosco as a father and teacher of the young, grant, we pray, that, aflame with the same fire of love, we may seek out souls and serve you alone.

### FEBRUARY

#### February 2 — The Presentation of the Lord \[Feast\] {#misal.die-2-februarii-in-praesentatione-domini}

*THE BLESSING OF CANDLES AND THE PROCESSION*

First Form: The Precession

*1\. At an appropriate hour, a gathering takes place at a smaller church or other suitable place outside the church to which the procession will go. The faithful hold in their hand unlit candles.*

*2\. The Priest, wearing white vestments as for Mass, approaches with the ministers. Instead of the chasuble the Priest may wear a cope, which he leaves aside after the procession is over.*

*3\. While the candles are being lit, the following antiphon or another appropriate chant is sung:*

Behold, our Lord will come with power, to enlighten the eyes of his servants, alleluia.

*4\. When the chant is concluded, the Priest, facing the people, says:*

In the name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit.

*Then the Priest greets the people in the usual way, and next he gives an introductory address, encouraging the faithful to celebrate the rite of this feastday actively and consciously. He may use these or similar words:*

Dear brethren (brothers and sisters), forty days have passed since we celebrated the joyful feast of the Nativity of the Lord. Today is the blessed day when Jesus was presented in the Temple by Mary and Joseph. Outwardly he was fulfilling the Law, but in reality he was coming to meet his believing people. Prompted by the Holy Spirit, Simeon and Anna came to the Temple. Enlightened by the same Spirit, they recognized the Lord and confessed him with exultation. So let us also, gathered together by the Holy Spirit, proceed to the house of God to encounter Christ. There we shall find him and recognize him in the breaking of the bread until he comes again, revealed in glory.

*5\. After the address the Priest blesses the candles, saying, with hands extended:*

Let us pray.

O God, source and origin of all light, who on this day showed to the just man Simeon the Light for revelation to the Gentiles, we humbly ask that, in answer to your people's prayers, you may be pleased to sanctify with your blessing † these candles, which we are eager to carry in praise of your Name, so that treading the path of virtue, we may reach that light which never fails. Through Christ our Lord.

R. Amen.

*Or:*

O God, true light, who create light eternal, spreading it far and wide, pour into the hearts of the faithful the brilliance of perpetual light, so that all who are brightened in your holy temple by the splendour of these candles, may happily reach the light of your glory. Through Christ our Lord.

R. Amen.

*He sprinkles the candles with holy water without saying anything, and puts incense into the thurible for the procession.*

*6\. Then the Priest receives from the Deacon or a minister the lighted candle prepared for him and the procession begins, with the Deacon announcing (or, if there is no Deacon, the Priest himself):*

Let us go in peace to meet the Lord.

*Or:*

Let us go forth in peace.

*In this case all respond:*

In the name of Christ. Amen.

*7\. All carry lighted candles. As the procession moves forward, one or other of the antiphons that follow is sung, namely the antiphon with the canticle, or the antiphon or another appropriate chant.*

*I.*

Ant. A light for revelation to the Gentiles and the glory of your people Israel. Lord, now you let your servant go in peace, in accordance with your word.

Ant. A light for revelation to the Gentiles and the glory of your people Israel. For my eyes have seen your salvation.

Ant. A light for revelation to the Gentiles and the glory of your people Israel. Which you have prepared in the sight of all peoples.

Ant. A light for revelation to the Gentiles and the glory of your people Israel.

*II*

Ant. Sion, adorn your bridal chamber and welcome Christ the King: take Mary in your arms, who is the gate of heaven, for she herself is carrying the King of glory and new light. As Virgin she remains, though bringing in her hands the Son before the morning star begotten, whom Simeon, taking in his arms announced to the peoples as Lord of life and death and Saviour of the world.

*8\. As the procession enters the church, the of the Mass is sung. When the Priest has arrived at the altar, he venerates it and, if appropriate, incenses it. Then he goes to the chair, where he takes off the cope, if he used it in the procession, and puts on a chasuble. Then, the singing of the hymn Glória in excélsis (Glory to God in the highest), he says the COLLECT as usual. The Mass continues in the usual manner.*

Second Form: The Solemn Entrance

*1\. Whenever a procession cannot take place, the faithful gather in church, holding candles in their hands. The Priest, wearing white sacred vestments as for Mass, together with the ministers and a representative group of the faithful, goes to a suitable place, either in front of the church door or inside the church itself, where at least a large part of the faithful can conveniently participate in the rite.*

*2\. When the Priest reaches the place appointed for the blessing of the candles, candles are lit while the antiphon or another appropriate chant is sung.*

*3\. Then, after the greeting and address, the Priest blesses the candles, as above ; and then the procession to the altar takes place, with singing (nos. 6-7). For Mass, what is indicated in above is observed.*

At the Mass

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-2-februarii-in-praesentatione-domini.ant-ad-introitum}

Your merciful love, O God, we have received in the midst of your temple. Your praise, O God, like your name, reaches the ends of the earth; your right hand is filled with saving justice.

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-februarii-in-praesentatione-domini.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, we humbly implore your majesty that, just as your Only Begotten Son was presented on this day in the Temple in the substance of our flesh, so, by your grace, may we be presented to you with minds made pure.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-2-februarii-in-praesentatione-domini.super-oblata}

May the offering made with exultation by your Church be pleasing to you, O Lord, we pray, for you willed that your Only Begotten Son be offered to you for the life of the world as the Lamb without blemish.

##### Preface {#misal.die-2-februarii-in-praesentatione-domini.praefatio}

The mystery of the presentation of the Lord\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God.\
For your co-eternal Son was presented on this day in the Temple and revealed by the Spirit as the glory of Israel and Light of the nations.\
And so we, too, go forth, rejoicing to encounter your Salvation, and with the Angels and Saints praise you as without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-2-februarii-in-praesentatione-domini.ant-ad-communionem}

My eyes have seen your salvation, which you prepared in the sight of all the peoples.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-2-februarii-in-praesentatione-domini.post-communionem}

By these holy gifts which we have received, O Lord, bring your grace to perfection within us, and, as you fulfilled Simeon's expectation that he would not see death until he had been privileged to welcome the Christ, so may we, going forth to meet the Lord, obtain the gift of eternal life.

#### February 3 — Saint Blaise {#misal.die-3-februarii-s-blasii}

Saint Blaise, bishop and martyr \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one martyr, or of , for bishops\]

*Or:*

Saint Ansgar, bishop \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for missionaries or of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-februarii-s-blasii.collecta}

Hear, O Lord, the supplications your people make under the patronage of the Martyr Saint Blaise, and grant that they may rejoice in peace in this present life and find help for life eternal.

#### Saint Ansgar {#misal.die-3-februarii-s-ansgarii}

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-februarii-s-ansgarii.collecta}

O God, who willed to send the Bishop Saint Ansgar to enlighten many peoples, grant us, through his intercession, that we may always walk in the light of your truth.

#### February 5 — Saint Agatha, virgin and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-5-februarii-s-agathae}

\[Common of or of , for one virgin\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-5-februarii-s-agathae.collecta}

May the Virgin Martyr Saint Agatha implore your compassion for us, O Lord, we pray, for she found favour with you by the courage of her martyrdom and the merit of her chastity.

#### February 6 — Saint Paul Miki, martyr, and his companions, martyrs \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-6-februarii-ss-pauli-miki-et-sociorum}

\[Common of , for several martyrs\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-6-februarii-ss-pauli-miki-et-sociorum.collecta}

O God, strength of all the Saints, who through the Cross were pleased to call the Martyrs Saint Paul Miki and companions to life, grant, we pray, that by their intercession we may hold with courage even until death to the faith that we profess.

#### February 8 — Saint Jerome Emiliani {#misal.die-8-februarii-s-hieronymi-emiliani}

Saint Jerome Emiliani \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for educators\]

*Or:*

Saint Josephine Bakhita, virgin

\[Common of , for one virgin\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-8-februarii-s-hieronymi-emiliani.collecta}

O God, Father of mercies, who sent Saint Jerome Emiliani as a helper and father to orphans, grant, through his intercession, that we may preserve faithfully the spirit of adoption, by which we are called, and truly are, your children.

#### Saint Josephine Bakhita {#misal.die-8-februarii-s-iosephinae-bakhita}

##### Collect {#misal.die-8-februarii-s-iosephinae-bakhita.collecta}

O God, who led Saint Josephine Bakhita from abject slavery to the dignity of being your daughter and a bride of Christ, grant, we pray, that by her example we may show constant love for the Lord Jesus crucified, remaining steadfast in charity and prompt to show compassion.

#### February 10 — Saint Scholastica, virgin \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-10-februarii-s-scholasticae}

\[Common of , for one virgin or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-10-februarii-s-scholasticae.collecta}

As we celebrate anew the Memorial of the Virgin Saint Scholastica, we pray, O Lord, that, following her example, we may serve you with unfailing love and happily receive what comes from loving you.

#### February 11 — Our Lady of Lourdes \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-11-februarii-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-lourdes}

\[Common of the \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-11-februarii-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-lourdes.collecta}

Grant us, O merciful God, protection in our weakness, that we who keep the Memorial of the Immaculate Mother of God, may, with the help of her intercession, rise up from our iniquities.

#### February 14 — Saint Cyril, monk, and Saint Methodius, bishop \[Memorial or Feast in Europe\] {#misal.die-14-februarii-ss-cyrilli-et-methodii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-14-februarii-ss-cyrilli-et-methodii.ant-ad-introitum}

These are holy men who became friends of God, glorious heralds of divine truth.

##### Collect {#misal.die-14-februarii-ss-cyrilli-et-methodii.collecta}

O God, who enlightened the Slavic peoples through the brothers Saints Cyril and Methodius, grant that our hearts may grasp the words of your teaching, and perfect us as a people of one accord in true faith and right confession.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-14-februarii-ss-cyrilli-et-methodii.super-oblata}

Look, O Lord, upon the offerings which we bring before your majesty in commemoration of Saints Cyril and Methodius, and grant that these gifts may become the sign of a new humanity, reconciled to you in loving charity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-14-februarii-ss-cyrilli-et-methodii.ant-ad-communionem}

The disciples went forth and preached the gospel while the Lord worked with them, confirming the word through accompanying signs.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-14-februarii-ss-cyrilli-et-methodii.post-communionem}

O God, Father of all nations, who make us sharers in the one Bread and the one Spirit and heirs of the eternal banquet; grant in your kindness, on this feast day of Saints Cyril and Methodius, that the multitude of your children, persevering in the same faith, may be united in building up the Kingdom of justice and peace.

#### February 17 — Seven Founders of the Servite Order \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-17-februarii-ss-septem-fundatorum-ordinis-servorum-b-m-v}

\[Common of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-februarii-ss-septem-fundatorum-ordinis-servorum-b-m-v.collecta}

Impart to us, O Lord, in kindness the filial devotion with which the holy brothers venerated so devoutly the Mother of God and led your people to yourself.

#### February 21 — Saint Peter Damian, bishop and doctor \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-21-februarii-s-petri-damiani}

\[Common of , for a bishop, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-februarii-s-petri-damiani.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that we may so follow the teaching and example of the Bishop Saint Peter Damian, that, putting nothing before Christ and always ardent in the service of your Church, we may be led to the joys of eternal light.

#### February 22 — Chair of Saint Peter, Apostle \[Feast\] {#misal.die-22-februarii-cathedrae-s-petri}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-22-februarii-cathedrae-s-petri.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord says to Simon Peter: I have prayed for you that your faith may not fail, and once you have turned back, strengthen your brothers.

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-februarii-cathedrae-s-petri.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that no tempests may disturb us, for you have set us fast on the rock of the Apostle Peter's confession of faith.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-22-februarii-cathedrae-s-petri.super-oblata}

Accept with favour, O Lord, we pray, the prayers and offerings of your Church, that, with Saint Peter as her shepherd, she may come to an eternal inheritance, for it is through his teaching that she holds the faith in its integrity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-22-februarii-cathedrae-s-petri.ant-ad-communionem}

Peter said to Jesus: You are the Christ, the Son of the living God. And Jesus replied: You are Peter, and upon this rock I will build my Church.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-22-februarii-cathedrae-s-petri.post-communionem}

O God, who at our celebration of the feast day of the blessed Apostle Peter have nourished us by communion in the Body and Blood of Christ, grant, we pray, that this redeeming exchange may be for us a Sacrament of unity and peace.

#### February 23 — Saint Polycarp, bishop and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-23-februarii-s-polycarpi}

\[Common of , for one martyr, or of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-februarii-s-polycarpi.collecta}

God of all creation, who were pleased to give the Bishop Saint Polycarp a place in the company of the Martyrs, grant, through his intercession, that sharing with him in the chalice of Christ, we may rise through the Holy Spirit to eternal life.

#### February 27 — Saint Gregory of Narek, abbot and doctor of the Church \[Optional memorial\] {#misal.die-27-februarii-s-gregorii-narecensis}

\[Common of , or of , for one abbot\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-27-februarii-s-gregorii-narecensis.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who were pleased to imbue with mystical doctrine Saint Gregory of Narek, the teacher and glory of the Armenian people, grant us, by his teaching, to learn the art of speaking with you and constantly to fortify our life with the Sacraments of the Church.

### MARCH

#### March 1 — Saint David, Bishop, Patron of Wales \[English Calendar, Feast\] {#misal.die-1-martii-s-davidis-angliae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-1-martii-s-davidis-angliae.ant-ad-introitum}

How beautiful upon the mountains are the feet of him who brings glad tidings of peace, bearing good news, announcing salvation!

##### Collect {#misal.die-1-martii-s-davidis-angliae.collecta}

O God, who graciously bestowed on your Bishop Saint David of Wales the virtue of wisdom and the gift of eloquence and made him an example of prayer and pastoral zeal, grant that, through his intercession, your Church may ever prosper and render you joyful praise.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-1-martii-s-davidis-angliae.super-oblata}

Look with favour, O Lord, we pray, on the offerings we set upon this sacred altar on the feast of the Bishop Saint David, that, bestowing on us your pardon, our oblations may give honour to your name.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-1-martii-s-davidis-angliae.ant-ad-communionem}

We proclaim Christ crucified; Christ, the power of God and the wisdom of God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-1-martii-s-davidis-angliae.post-communionem}

We pray, almighty God, that we, who are fortified by the power of this Sacrament, may learn through the example of your Bishop Saint David to seek you always above all things and to bear in this world the likeness of New Man.

#### March 4 — Saint Casimir \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-4-martii-s-casimiri}

\[Common of , for one saint\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-martii-s-casimiri.collecta}

Almighty God, to serve you is to reign; grant that, with the help of Saint Casimir's intercession, we may constantly serve you in holiness and justice.

#### March 7 — Saints Felicity and Perpetua, martyrs \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-7-martii-ss-perpetuae-et-felicitatis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-7-martii-ss-perpetuae-et-felicitatis.ant-ad-introitum}

The souls of the Saints are rejoicing in heaven, the Saints who followed the footsteps of Christ, and since for love of him they shed their blood, they now exult with Christ for ever.

##### Collect {#misal.die-7-martii-ss-perpetuae-et-felicitatis.collecta}

O God, at the urging of whose love the Martyrs Saints Perpetua and Felicity defied their persecutors and overcame the torment of death, grant, we ask, by their prayers that we may ever grow in your love.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-7-martii-ss-perpetuae-et-felicitatis.super-oblata}

As we joyfully offer, O Lord, this day's sacrifice, recalling the heaven-sent victory of Saints Perpetua and Felicity, we proclaim by it your mighty deeds and rejoice at having gained their glorious intercession.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-7-martii-ss-perpetuae-et-felicitatis.ant-ad-communionem}

For the sake of Jesus we are given up to death, that the life of Jesus also may be manifested in our mortal flesh.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-7-martii-ss-perpetuae-et-felicitatis.post-communionem}

As we draw everlasting joys, O Lord, from our participation in this Sacrament and from the Memorial of Saints Perpetua and Felicity, we humbly implore, that by your gift we may truly understand what you grant us to enact in diligent service.

#### March 8 — Saint John of God, religious \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-8-martii-s-ioannis-a-deo}

\[Common of , for religious or those who practiced works of mercy\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-8-martii-s-ioannis-a-deo.collecta}

O God, who filled Saint John of God with a spirit of compassion, grant, we pray, that, giving ourselves to works of charity, we may merit to be found among the elect in your Kingdom.

#### March 9 — Saint Frances of Rome, religious \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-9-martii-s-franciscae-romanae}

\[Common of a or , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-martii-s-franciscae-romanae.collecta}

O God, who have given us in Saint Frances of Rome a singular model of both married and monastic life, grant us perseverance in your service, that in every circumstance of life we may see and follow you.

#### March 17 — Saint Patrick, bishop, Patron of Ireland \[Feast in England\] {#misal.die-17-martii-s-patricii}

\[Common of , for bishops or for or \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-martii-s-patricii.collecta}

O God, who chose the Bishop Saint Patrick to preach your glory to the peoples of Ireland, grant through his merits and intercession, that those who glory in the name of Christian may never cease to proclaim your wondrous deeds to all.

#### March 17 — Saint Patrick, bishop, Patron of Ireland \[Feast in England\] — Proper Mass

##### Entrance Antiphon

Proclaim the salvation of God day by day; tell among the nations his glory.

##### Collect

O God, who chose the Bishop Saint Patrick to preach your glory to the peoples of Ireland, grant through his merits and intercession, that those who glory in the name of Christian may never cease to proclaim your wondrous deeds to all.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings

Lord, accept this pure sacrifice which, through the labours of Saint Patrick, your grateful people make to the glory of your name.

##### Communion Antiphon

I confer a kingdom on you, just as my Father has conferred one on me, that you may eat and drink at my table in my Kingdom, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion

Strengthen us, O Lord, by this sacrament, so that we may profess the faith taught by Saint Patrick and proclaim it by our way of living.

#### March 18 — Saint Cyril of Jerusalem, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-18-martii-s-cyrilli-hierosolymitani}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-18-martii-s-cyrilli-hierosolymitani.collecta}

O God, who through the Bishop Saint Cyril of Jerusalem led your Church in a wonderful way to a deeper sense of the mysteries of salvation, grant us, through his intercession, that we may so acknowledge your Son as to have life ever more abundantly.

#### March 19 — St Joseph, Husband of Mary \[Solemnity\] {#misal.die-19-martii-s-ioseph-sponsi-beatae-mariae-virginis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-19-martii-s-ioseph-sponsi-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold a faithful and prudent steward whom the Lord set over his household.

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-martii-s-ioseph-sponsi-beatae-mariae-virginis.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, by Saint Joseph's intercession, your Church may constantly watch over the unfolding of the mysteries of human salvation, whose beginnings you entrusted to his faithful care.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-19-martii-s-ioseph-sponsi-beatae-mariae-virginis.super-oblata}

We pray, O Lord, that just as Saint Joseph served with loving care your Only Begotten Son, born of the Virgin Mary, so may we be worthy to minister with a pure heart at your altar.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-19-martii-s-ioseph-sponsi-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-communionem}

Well done, good and faithful servant: Come, share your master's joy.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-19-martii-s-ioseph-sponsi-beatae-mariae-virginis.post-communionem}

Defend with unfailing protection, O Lord, we pray, the family you have nourished with food from this altar while they rejoice at the Solemnity of Saint Joseph, and graciously keep safe your gifts among them.

#### March 23 — Saint Turibius of Mogrovejo, bishop {#misal.die-23-martii-s-turibii-de-mogrovejo}

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-martii-s-turibii-de-mogrovejo.collecta}

O God, who gave increase to your Church through the apostolic labours and zeal for truth of the Bishop Saint Turibius, grant that the people consecrated to you may always receive new growth in faith and holiness.

#### March 25 — Annunciation of the Lord \[Solemnity\] {#misal.die-25-martii-in-annuntiatione-domini}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-25-martii-in-annuntiatione-domini.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord said, as he entered the world: Behold, I come to do your will, O God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-martii-in-annuntiatione-domini.collecta}

O God, who willed that your Word should take on the reality of human flesh in the womb of the Virgin Mary, grant, we pray, that we, who confess our Redeemer to be God and man, may merit to become partakers even in his divine nature.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-25-martii-in-annuntiatione-domini.super-oblata}

Be pleased, almighty God, to accept your Church’s offering, so that she, who is aware that her beginnings lie in the Incarnation of your Only Begotten Son, may rejoice to celebrate his mysteries on this Solemnity. Who lives and reigns for ever and ever.

##### Preface {#misal.die-25-martii-in-annuntiatione-domini.praefatio}

The mystery of the incarnation\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God, through Christ our Lord.\
For the Virgin Mary heard with faith that the Christ was to be born among men and for men's sake by the overshadowing power of the Holy Spirit. Lovingly she bore him in her immaculate womb, that the promises to the children of Israel might come about and the hope of nations be accomplished beyond all telling.\
Through him the host of Angels adores your majesty and rejoices in your presence for ever. May our voices, we pray, join with theirs in one chorus of exultant praise, as we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-25-martii-in-annuntiatione-domini.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, a Virgin shall conceive and bear a son; and his name will be called Emmanuel.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-25-martii-in-annuntiatione-domini.post-communionem}

Confirm in our minds the mysteries of the true faith, we pray, O Lord, so that confessing that he who was conceived of the Virgin Mary is true God and true man, we may, through the saving power of his Resurrection, merit to attain eternal joy.

### APRIL

#### April 2 — Saint Francis of Paola, hermit \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-2-aprilis-s-francisci-de-paola}

\[Common of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-aprilis-s-francisci-de-paola.collecta}

O God, exaltation of the lowly, who raised Saint Francis of Paola to the glory of your Saints, grant, we pray, that, by his merits and example, we may happily attain the rewards promised to the humble.

#### April 4 — Saint Isidore of Seville, bishop and doctor of the church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-4-aprilis-s-isidori}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-aprilis-s-isidori.collecta}

Hear graciously the prayers, O Lord, which we make in commemoration of Saint Isidore, that your Church may be aided by his intercession, just as she has been instructed by his heavenly teaching.

#### April 5 — Saint Vincent Ferrer, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-5-aprilis-s-vincentii-ferrer}

\[Common of , for missionaries\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-5-aprilis-s-vincentii-ferrer.collecta}

O God, who raised up the Priest Saint Vincent Ferrer to minister by the preaching of the Gospel, grant, we pray, that when the Judge comes whom Saint Vincent proclaimed on earth, we may be blessed to behold him ascend his heavenly throne.

#### April 7 — Saint John Baptist de la Salle, priest \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-7-aprilis-s-ioannis-baptistae-de-la-salle}

\[Common of , for one pastor or of , for educators\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-7-aprilis-s-ioannis-baptistae-de-la-salle.collecta}

O God, who chose Saint John Baptist de la Salle to educate young Christians, raise up, we pray, teachers in your Church who will devote themselves wholeheartedly to the human and Christian formation of the young.

#### April 11 — Saint Stanislaus, bishop \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-11-aprilis-s-stanislai}

\[Common of , for one martyr, or for , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-11-aprilis-s-stanislai.collecta}

O God, for whose honour the Bishop Saint Stanislaus fell beneath the swords of his persecutors, grant, we pray, that we may persevere strong in faith even until death.

#### April 13 — Saint Martin I, pope and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-13-aprilis-s-martini-i}

\[Common of , for one martyr, or for , for popes\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-aprilis-s-martini-i.collecta}

Grant, almighty God, that we may withstand the trials of this world with invincible firmness of purpose, just as you did not allow your Martyr Pope Saint Martin the First to be daunted by threats or broken by suffering.

#### April 21 — Saint Anselm, bishop and doctor of the church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-21-aprilis-s-anselmi}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-aprilis-s-anselmi.collecta}

O God, who led the Bishop Saint Anselm to seek out and teach the depths of your wisdom, grant, we pray, that our faith in you may so aid our understanding that what we believe by your command may give delight to our hearts.

#### April 23 — Saint George, martyr, Patron of England \[Optional Memorial or Solemnity in England\] {#misal.die-23-aprilis-s-georgii}

\[ or Common of , for one martyr\]

Saint George

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-aprilis-s-georgii.collecta}

Extolling your power, O Lord, we humbly implore you, that, as Saint George imitated the Passion of the Lord, so he may lend us ready help in our weakness.

#### Saint George

##### Entrance Antiphon

Rejoice, you Saints, in the presence of the Lamb; a kingdom has been prepared for you from the foundation of the world, alleluia.

*Or:*

On the asp and the viper you will tread, and trample the young lion and the dragon, alleluia.

##### Collect

God of hosts, who so kindled the fire of charity in the heart of Saint George your martyr that he bore witness to the risen Lord both by his life and by his death, grant us through his intercession, we pray, the same faith and power of love, that we who rejoice in his triumph may be led to share with him in the fullness of the resurrection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings

Receive, we pray, O Lord, the sacrifice of conciliation and praise, which we offer to your majesty in commemoration of the blessed Martyr Saint George, that it may lead us to forgiveness and confirm us in constant thanksgiving.

##### Communion Antiphon

If we have died with Christ, we shall also live with him; if we persevere, we shall also reign with him, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion

Rejoicing on this festival day, O Lord, we have received your heavenly gifts; grant, we pray, that we who in this divine banquet proclaim the death of your Son may merit with Saint George to be partakers in his resurrection and glory.

#### April 24 — Saint Adalbert {#misal.die-23-aprilis-s-adalberti}

Saint Adalbert, bishop and martyr \[English Calendar\]

\[Common of , for one martyr, or for , for bishops\]

*Or:*

Saint Fidelis of Sigmaringen, priest and martyr \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for priests, or of , for one martyr\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-aprilis-s-adalberti.collecta}

O God, who bestowed the crown of martyrdom on the Bishop Saint Adalbert, as he burned with zeal for souls, grant, we pray, by his prayers that the obedience of the flock may never fail the shepherds, nor the care of the shepherds be ever lacking to the flock.

#### Saint Fidelis of Sigmaringen {#misal.die-24-aprilis-s-fidelis-de-sigmaringen}

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-aprilis-s-fidelis-de-sigmaringen.collecta}

O God, who were pleased to award the palm of martyrdom to Saint Fidelis as, burning with love for you, he propagated the faith, grant, we pray, through his intercession, that, grounded in charity, we may merit to know with him the power of the Resurrection of Christ.

#### April 25 — Saint Mark, evangelist \[Feast\] {#misal.die-25-aprilis-s-marci}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-25-aprilis-s-marci.ant-ad-introitum}

Go into all the world, and proclaim the Gospel to every creature, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-aprilis-s-marci.collecta}

O God, who raised up Saint Mark, your Evangelist, and endowed him with the grace to preach the Gospel, grant, we pray, that we may so profit from his teaching as to follow faithfully in the footsteps of Christ.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-25-aprilis-s-marci.super-oblata}

As we venerate the glory of Saint Mark, we offer you, Lord, the sacrifice of praise and humbly beseech you, that your Church may always persevere in the preaching of the Gospel.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-25-aprilis-s-marci.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, I am with you always, even to the end of the age, says the Lord, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-25-aprilis-s-marci.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that what we have received from your holy altar may sanctify us and make us strong in the faith of the Gospel which Saint Mark proclaimed.

#### April 28 — Saint Peter Chanel {#misal.die-28-aprilis-s-petri-chanel}

Saint Peter Chanel, priest and martyr \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one martyr, or for , for missionaries\]

*Or:*

Saint Louis Mary de Montfort, priest \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one pastor\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-aprilis-s-petri-chanel.collecta}

O God, who for the spreading of your Church crowned Saint Peter Chanel with martyrdom, grant that, in these days of paschal joy, we may so celebrate the mysteries of Christ's Death and Resurrection, as to bear worthy witness to newness of life.

#### Saint Louis Mary de Montfort {#misal.die-28-aprilis-s-ludovici-mariae-grignion-de-montfort}

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-aprilis-s-ludovici-mariae-grignion-de-montfort.collecta}

O God, who willed to direct the steps of the Priest Saint Louis along the way of salvation and of the love of Christ in the company of the Blessed Virgin, grant us, by his example, that meditating on the mysteries of your love, we may strive tirelessly for the building up of your Church.

*Or:*

Almighty and eternal God, who made the Priest Saint Louis an outstanding witness and teacher of total devotion to Christ your Son through the hands of his Blessed Mother, grant us that, following the same spiritual path, we may constantly spread your Kingdom.

#### April 29 — Saint Catherine of Siena, Virgin and Doctor of the Church \[Feast in Europe\] {#misal.die-29-aprilis-s-catharinae-senensis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-29-aprilis-s-catharinae-senensis.ant-ad-introitum}

Here is a wise virgin, from among the number of the prudent, who went forth with lighted lamp to meet Christ, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-aprilis-s-catharinae-senensis.collecta}

O God, who set Saint Catherine of Siena on fire with divine love in her contemplation of the Lord's Passion and her service of your Church, grant, through her intercession, that your people, participating in the mystery of Christ, may ever exult in the revelation of his glory.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-29-aprilis-s-catharinae-senensis.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, the saving sacrifice we offer in commemoration of Saint Catherine, so that instructed by her teaching, we may give ever more fervent thanks to you, the one true God.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-29-aprilis-s-catharinae-senensis.ant-ad-communionem}

If we walk in the light, as God is in the light, then we have fellowship with one another, and the blood of his Son Jesus Christ cleanses us from all sin, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-29-aprilis-s-catharinae-senensis.post-communionem}

May the heavenly table at which we have been fed, O Lord, confer eternal life upon us, as even in this world it nourished the life of Saint Catherine.

#### April 30 — Saint Pius V, pope \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-30-aprilis-s-pii-v}

\[Common of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-30-aprilis-s-pii-v.collecta}

O God, who in your providence raised up Pope Saint Pius the Fifth in your Church that the faith might be safeguarded and more fitting worship be offered to you, grant, through his intercession, that we may participate in your mysteries with lively faith and fruitful charity.

### MAY

#### May 1 — Saint Joseph the Worker \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-1-maii-s-ioseph-opificis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-1-maii-s-ioseph-opificis.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed are all who fear the Lord, and walk in his ways! By the labour of your hands you shall eat; blessed are you, and blessed will you be, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.die-1-maii-s-ioseph-opificis.collecta}

O God, Creator of all things, who laid down for the human race the law of work, grant graciously that by the example of Saint Joseph and under his patronage, we may complete the works you set us to do and attain the rewards you promise.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-1-maii-s-ioseph-opificis.super-oblata}

O God, fount of all mercy, look upon our offerings, which we bring before your majesty in commemoration of Saint Joseph, and mercifully grant that the gifts we offer may become the means of protection for those who call upon you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-1-maii-s-ioseph-opificis.ant-ad-communionem}

Whatever you do in word or deed, do everything in the Name of the Lord, giving thanks to God through him, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-1-maii-s-ioseph-opificis.post-communionem}

Having fed upon heavenly delights, we humbly ask you, O Lord, that, by Saint Joseph's example, cherishing in our hearts the signs of your love, we may ever enjoy the fruit of perpetual peace.

#### May 2 — Saint Athanasius, bishop and doctor \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-2-maii-s-athanasii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-2-maii-s-athanasii.ant-ad-introitum}

In the midst of the Church he opened his mouth, and the Lord filled him with the spirit of wisdom and understanding and clothed him in a robe of glory, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-maii-s-athanasii.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who raised up the Bishop Saint Athanasius as an outstanding champion of your Son's divinity, mercifully grant, that rejoicing in his teaching and his protection, we may never cease to grow in knowledge and love of you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-2-maii-s-athanasii.super-oblata}

Look, O Lord, upon the offerings we present to you in commemoration of Saint Athanasius, and may witnessing to your truth bring salvation to those who profess the same unblemished faith as his.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-2-maii-s-athanasii.ant-ad-communionem}

No one can lay a foundation other than the one that is there, namely, Jesus Christ, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-2-maii-s-athanasii.post-communionem}

Grant us, we pray, almighty God, that the true divinity of your Only Begotten Son, which we firmly profess with Saint Athanasius, may through this Sacrament ever give us life and protection.

#### May 3 — Saints Philip and James, Apostles \[Feast\] {#misal.die-3-maii-ss-philippi-et-iacobi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-3-maii-ss-philippi-et-iacobi.ant-ad-introitum}

These are the holy men whom the Lord chose in his own perfect love; to them he gave eternal glory, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-maii-ss-philippi-et-iacobi.collecta}

O God, who gladden us each year with the feast day of the Apostles Philip and James, grant us through their prayers a share in the Passion and Resurrection of your Only Begotten Son, so that we may merit to behold you for eternity.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-3-maii-ss-philippi-et-iacobi.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, the offerings we bring for the feast day of the Apostles Philip and James and bestow on us religion pure and undefiled.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-3-maii-ss-philippi-et-iacobi.ant-ad-communionem}

Lord, show us the Father, and that will be enough for us. Whoever has seen me, Philip, has seen the Father also, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-3-maii-ss-philippi-et-iacobi.post-communionem}

Purify our minds, we pray, O Lord, by these holy gifts we have received, so that, contemplating you in your Son together with the Apostles Philip and James, we may be worthy to possess eternal life.

#### May 4 — The English Martyrs \[English Calendar, Feast\] {#misal.die-4-maii-ss-martyrum-anglorum-angliae}

\[Common of , for several martyrs\] or Proper:

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-4-maii-ss-martyrum-anglorum-angliae.ant-ad-introitum}

These who are clothed in white robes are they who have survived the time of great distress and have washed their robes in the blood of the Lamb, alleluia.

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-maii-ss-martyrum-anglorum-angliae.collecta}

Almighty God, who in our country raised up martyrs from every walk of life to vindicate the authority of your Church in teaching and worship, grant through their intercession, we pray, that all our people may be gathered once again to celebrate the same sacraments under the one Shepherd, Jesus Christ your Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-4-maii-ss-martyrum-anglorum-angliae.super-oblata}

Look with such serenity and kindness, we pray, O Lord, upon these present offerings, that they may be filled with the blessing of the Holy Spirit and may stir in our hearts that powerful love through which the holy Martyrs overcame every bodily torment.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-4-maii-ss-martyrum-anglorum-angliae.ant-ad-communionem}

To the victor I will give the right to eat from the tree of life which is in the paradise of my God, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-4-maii-ss-martyrum-anglorum-angliae.post-communionem}

Restored by the sustenance of the one Bread, O Lord, on the commemoration of the blessed Martyrs we humbly pray, that you may confirm us ever in your charity and make us walk in newness of life.

#### May 10 — Saint John of Avila, priest and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-10-maii-s-ioannis-de-avila}

\[Common of , for priests, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-10-maii-s-ioannis-de-avila.collecta}

O God, who gave to your clergy and people Saint John De Avila, a teacher outstanding for the holiness and zeal of his life, grant, we pray, that in our own time the Church may increase in holiness by the exceptional devotion of your ministers.

#### May 12 — Saints Nereus and Achilleus, martyrs \[Optional Memorial\] — Saint Pancras, martyr \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for several martyrs\]

*Or:*

\[Common of , for one martyr\]

#### Saints Nereus and Achilleus {#misal.die-12-maii-ss-nerei-et-achillei}

*Or:*

Blessed Alvaro del Portillo, bishop \[In particular places\]

\[Common for , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-maii-ss-nerei-et-achillei.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that we who know how courageous the glorious Martyrs Nereus and Achilleus were in confessing you, may experience their loving intercession for us in your presence.

#### Saint Pancras {#misal.die-12-maii-s-pancratii}

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-maii-s-pancratii.collecta}

May your Church rejoice, O God, confident in the intercession of the Martyr Saint Pancras, and by his glorious prayers may she persevere in devotion to you and stand ever firm.

#### May 12 — Blessed Alvaro del Portillo {#misal.die-12-maii-b-alvari-del-portillo-operis-dei}

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-maii-b-alvari-del-portillo-operis-dei.collecta}

God the Father of mercies, who filled the bishop Blessed Alvaro with a spirit of truth and love, grant, we pray, that, following in his footsteps, we may humbly devote ourselves to the saving mission of the Church.

#### May 13 — Our Lady of Fatima \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-13-maii-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-fatima}

\[Common of the \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-maii-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-fatima.collecta}

O God, who chose the Mother of your Son to be our Mother also, grant us that, persevering in penance and prayer for the salvation of the world, we may further more effectively each day the reign of Christ.

#### May 14 — Saint Matthias, Apostle \[Feast\] {#misal.die-14-maii-s-matthiae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-14-maii-s-matthiae.ant-ad-introitum}

It was not you who chose me, says the Lord, but I who chose you and appointed you to go and bear fruit, fruit that will last (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.die-14-maii-s-matthiae.collecta}

O God, who assigned Saint Matthias a place in the college of the Apostles, grant us, through his intercession, that rejoicing at how your love has been allotted to us, we may merit to be numbered among the elect.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-14-maii-s-matthiae.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, the offerings of your Church, reverently presented for the Feast of Saint Matthias, and through them strengthen us by the power of your grace.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-14-maii-s-matthiae.ant-ad-communionem}

This is my commandment: love one another as I love you, says the Lord, alleluia.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-14-maii-s-matthiae.post-communionem}

Never cease, O Lord, we pray, to fill your family with divine gifts, and, through blessed Matthias's intercession for us, graciously admit us to a share in the lot of the Saints in light.

#### May 18 — Saint John I, pope and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-18-maii-s-ioannis-i}

\[Common of , for popes, or of Martyrs, for one martyr: or \]

*Or:*

Blessed Guadalupe Ortiz, \[In particular places\]

\[Common of \]

St John I

##### Collect {#misal.die-18-maii-s-ioannis-i.collecta}

O God, who reward faithful souls and who have consecrated this day by the martyrdom of Pope Saint John the First, graciously hear the prayers of your people and grant that we, who venerate his merits, may imitate his constancy in the faith.

#### May 18 — Blessed Guadalupe Ortiz {#misal.die-18-maii-b-guadalupae-ortiz-de-landazuri-operis-dei}

##### Collect {#misal.die-18-maii-b-guadalupae-ortiz-de-landazuri-operis-dei.collecta}

O God, who have given Blessed Guadalupe the grace to transform her daily work in a place of encounter with Christ and service to others, grant us, following her example, to transmit faith and joy to our brothers.

#### May 19 — Saint Dunstan, bishop \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-19-maii-s-dunstani-angliae}

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-maii-s-dunstani-angliae.collecta}

O God, source of all gifts, who raised up the Bishop Saint Dunstan to be a true shepherd of the flock, a restorer of monastic life and a trusted counsellor of kings, grant at his intercession, we pray, an abundance of your Spirit to all pastors, that with wisdom and truth they may offer worthy service to Christ and to his people.

#### May 20 — Saint Bernardine of Siena, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-20-maii-s-bernardini-senensis}

\[Common of , for missionaries or for \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-20-maii-s-bernardini-senensis.collecta}

O God, who gave the Priest Saint Bernardine a great love for the holy Name of Jesus, grant, through his merits and prayers, that we may ever be set aflame with the spirit of your love.

#### May 21 — Saint Christopher Magallanes, priest and martyr, and his companions, martyrs \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-21-maii-ss-christophori-magallanes-et-sociorum}

\[Common of Martyrs for several martyrs: or \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-maii-ss-christophori-magallanes-et-sociorum.collecta}

Almighty and eternal God, who made the Priest Saint Christopher Magallanes and his companions faithful to Christ the King even to the point of martyrdom, grant us, through their intercession that, persevering in confession of the true faith, we may always hold fast to the commandments of your love.

#### May 22 — Saint Rita of Cascia, religious \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-22-maii-s-ritae-de-cascia}

\[Common of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-maii-s-ritae-de-cascia.collecta}

Bestow on us, we pray, O Lord, the wisdom and strength of the Cross, with which you were pleased to endow Saint Rita, so that, suffering in every tribulation with Christ, we may participate ever more deeply in his Paschal Mystery.

#### May 25 — Saint Bede the Venerable {#misal.die-25-maii-s-bedae-venerabilis}

Saint Bede the Venerable, priest and doctor \[Memorial in England\]

\[Common of or of \]

*Or:*

Saint Gregory VII, pope \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for popes\]

*Or:*

Saint Mary Magdalene of Pazzi, virgin \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of for one virgin\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-maii-s-bedae-venerabilis.collecta}

O God, who bring light to your Church through the learning of the Priest Saint Bede, mercifully grant that your servants may always be enlightened by his wisdom and helped by his merits.

*In England:*

O God, who bring light to your Church through the learning of the Venerable Bede, your holy Priest, mercifully grant that your servants may always be enlightened by his wisdom and helped by his merits.

#### Saint Gregory VII {#misal.die-25-maii-s-gregorii-vii}

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-maii-s-gregorii-vii.collecta}

Give to your Church, we pray, O Lord, that spirit of fortitude and zeal for justice which you made to shine forth in Pope Saint Gregory the Seventh, so that, rejecting evil, she may be free to carry out in charity whatever is right.

#### Saint Mary Magdalene of Pazzi {#misal.die-25-maii-s-mariae-magdalenae-de-pazzi}

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-maii-s-mariae-magdalenae-de-pazzi.collecta}

O God, lover of virginity, who adorned with heavenly gifts the Virgin Saint Mary Magdalene de Pazzi, setting her on fire with your love; grant, we pray, that we who honour her today may imitate her example of purity and love.

#### May 26 — Saint Philip Neri, priest \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-26-maii-s-philippi-neri}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-26-maii-s-philippi-neri.ant-ad-introitum}

The love of God has been poured into our hearts through the Spirit of God dwelling within us (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.die-26-maii-s-philippi-neri.collecta}

O God, who never cease to bestow the glory of holiness on the faithful servants you raise up for yourself, graciously grant that the Holy Spirit may kindle in us that fire with which he wonderfully filled the heart of Saint Philip Neri.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-26-maii-s-philippi-neri.super-oblata}

As we offer you the sacrifice of praise, O Lord, we ask that, by the example of Saint Philip, we may always give ourselves cheerfully for the glory of your name and the service of our neighbour.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-26-maii-s-philippi-neri.ant-ad-communionem}

As the Father loves me, so I also love you; remain in my love (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-26-maii-s-philippi-neri.post-communionem}

Having fed upon heavenly delights, we pray, O Lord, that in imitation of Saint Philip we may always long for the things by which we truly live.

#### May 27 — Saint Augustine of Canterbury, bishop \[Optional Memorial or Feast in England\]

Saint Augustine of Canterbury, bishop \[Optional Memorial or Feast in England\]

\[Common of for missionaries, or for a or \]

*COLLECT \[or in \]*

O God, who by the preaching of the Bishop Saint Augustine of Canterbury led the English peoples to the Gospel, grant, we pray, that the fruits of his labours may remain ever abundant in your Church.

#### May 27 — Saint Augustine of Canterbury, bishop \[Optional Memorial or Feast in England\] {#misal.die-27-maii-s-augustini-cantuariensis}

##### Entrance Antiphon

I will praise you, Lord, among the nations, and I will tell of your name to my kin (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.die-27-maii-s-augustini-cantuariensis.collecta}

Almighty God, who by the mission of the Bishop Saint Augustine of Canterbury called the English people into the wondrous light of the Gospel, grant through his intercession, we pray, that, faithful to that same Gospel proclaimed, we may strive to make known your truth and build up your Church on the foundations he laid.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings

Grant our supplication, we pray, almighty God, that with these sacrificial offerings of your people, which we bring on the Feast of Saint Augustine, you will graciously mingle the gifts of heaven.

##### Communion Antiphon

O chosen people, proclaim the mighty works of him who called you out of darkness, into his wonderful light (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion

As we rejoice at the feast of Saint Augustine, we have received the pledge of eternal redemption, O Lord, and we pray that it may be of help to us both now and for the life to come.

#### May 29 — Saint Paul VI, pope \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-29-maii-s-pauli-vi}

\[Common of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-maii-s-pauli-vi.collecta}

Lord, who have entrusted the care of your Church to Pope St Paul, courageous apostle of the Gospel of your Son, grant that, enlightened by his teachings, we can cooperate with you to spread the civilisation of love in the world.

#### May 31 — The Visitation of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Feast\] {#misal.die-31-maii-in-visitatione-beatae-mariae-virginis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-31-maii-in-visitatione-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-introitum}

Come and hear, all who fear God; I will tell what the Lord did for my soul (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.die-31-maii-in-visitatione-beatae-mariae-virginis.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who while the Blessed Virgin Mary was carrying your Son in her womb, inspired her to visit Elizabeth; grant us, we pray, that, faithful to the promptings of the Spirit, we may magnify your greatness with the Virgin Mary at all times.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-31-maii-in-visitatione-beatae-mariae-virginis.super-oblata}

May our offering of this saving sacrifice be acceptable to your majesty, O Lord, as you were pleased to accept the charity of the most Blessed Mother of your Only Begotten Son.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-31-maii-in-visitatione-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-communionem}

All generations will call me blessed, for he who is mighty has done great things for me, and holy is his name (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-31-maii-in-visitatione-beatae-mariae-virginis.post-communionem}

May your Church proclaim your greatness, O God, for you have done great things for your faithful, and, as Saint John the Baptist leapt with joy when he first sensed the hidden presence of Christ, so may your Church rejoice to receive in this Sacrament the same ever-living Lord.\
Monday after Pentecost\
Thursday after Pentecost (Feast in England and Wales)\
Saturday after the Second Sunday after Pentecost

### JUNE

#### June 1 — Saint Justin, martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-1-iunii-s-iustini}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-1-iunii-s-iustini.ant-ad-introitum}

The wicked have told me lies, but not so is your law: I spoke of your decrees before kings, and was not confounded (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.die-1-iunii-s-iustini.collecta}

O God, who through the folly of the Cross wondrously taught Saint Justin the Martyr the surpassing knowledge of Jesus Christ, grant us, through his intercession, that, having rejected deception and error, we may become steadfast in the faith.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-1-iunii-s-iustini.super-oblata}

Grant us, we pray, O Lord, that we may celebrate worthily these mysteries, which Saint Justin strenuously defended.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-1-iunii-s-iustini.ant-ad-communionem}

I resolved to know nothing while I was with you except Jesus Christ, and him crucified (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-1-iunii-s-iustini.post-communionem}

Refreshed by heavenly food, we humbly implore you, O Lord, that, attentive to the teaching of Saint Justin the Martyr, we may abide at all times in thanksgiving for the gifts we have received.

#### June 2 — Saints Marcellinus and Peter, martyrs \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-2-iunii-ss-marcellini-et-petri}

\[Common of Martyrs, for several martyrs: or \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-iunii-ss-marcellini-et-petri.collecta}

O God, who surround us with protection through the glorious confession of the Martyrs Saints Marcellinus and Peter, grant that we may profit by imitating them and be upheld by their prayer.

#### June 3 — Saints Charles Lwanga and companions, martyrs \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-3-iunii-ss-caroli-lwanga-et-sociorum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-3-iunii-ss-caroli-lwanga-et-sociorum.ant-ad-introitum}

As gold in the furnace, the Lord put his chosen to the test; as sacrificial offerings he took them to himself; and in due time they will be honoured, and grace and peace will be with the elect of God (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-iunii-ss-caroli-lwanga-et-sociorum.collecta}

O God, who have made the blood of Martyrs the seed of Christians, mercifully grant that the field which is your Church, watered by the blood shed by Saint Charles Lwanga and his companions, may be fertile and ever yield you an abundant harvest.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-3-iunii-ss-caroli-lwanga-et-sociorum.super-oblata}

We offer you sacrifice, O Lord, humbly praying that, as you granted the blessed Martyrs grace to die rather than sin, so you may bring us to minister at your altar in dedication to you alone.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-3-iunii-ss-caroli-lwanga-et-sociorum.ant-ad-communionem}

How precious in the eyes of the Lord is the death of his holy ones (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-3-iunii-ss-caroli-lwanga-et-sociorum.post-communionem}

We have received this divine Sacrament, O Lord, as we celebrate the victory of your holy Martyrs; may what helped them to endure torment, we pray, make us, in the face of trials, steadfast in faith and in charity.

#### June 5 — Saint Boniface, bishop and martyr \[Memorial\]

\[Common of , for missionaries, or of Martyrs, for one martyr: or \]

*COLLECT \[ or in \]*

May the Martyr Saint Boniface be our advocate, O Lord, that we may firmly hold and confidently profess by our deeds the faith he taught with his lips and sealed in his blood and confidently profess it by our deeds.

*Or in England:*

O God, who raised up the holy Bishop and Martyr Saint Boniface from the English nation to enlighten many peoples with the gospel of Christ, grant, we pray, that we may firmly hold and confidently profess in action the faith he taught with his lips and sealed with his blood.

#### June 6 — Saint Norbert, bishop \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-6-iunii-s-norberti}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-6-iunii-s-norberti.collecta}

O God, who made the Bishop Saint Norbert a servant of your Church outstanding in his prayer and pastoral zeal, grant, we ask, that by the help of his intercession the flock of the faithful may always find shepherds after your own heart and be fed in the pastures of salvation.

#### June 9 — Saint Columba, Abbot \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-9-iunii-s-columbae-angliae}

\[Common of or of , for missionaries\]

*Or:*

Saint Ephrem of Syria, deacon and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of \]

Saint Columba

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-iunii-s-columbae-angliae.collecta}

Almighty God, who filled the heart of your Abbot Saint Columba with the joy of the Holy Spirit and with deep love for those in his care, grant, by his intercession, that your pilgrim people may follow him, strong in faith, sustained by hope and one in the love that binds us to you.

#### June 9 — Saint Ephrem of Syria {#misal.die-9-iunii-s-ephraem}

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-iunii-s-ephraem.collecta}

Pour into our hearts O Lord, we pray, the Holy Spirit, at whose prompting the Deacon Saint Ephrem exulted in singing of your mysteries and from whom he received the strength to serve you alone.

#### June 11 — Saint Barnabas, Apostle \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-11-iunii-s-barnabae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-11-iunii-s-barnabae.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed is this holy man who was worthy to be numbered among the Apostles, for he was a good man, filled with the Holy Spirit and with faith (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.die-11-iunii-s-barnabae.collecta}

O God, who decreed that Saint Barnabas, a man filled with faith and the Holy Spirit, should be set apart to convert the nations, grant that the Gospel of Christ, which he strenuously preached, may be faithfully proclaimed by word and by deed.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-11-iunii-s-barnabae.super-oblata}

Sanctify with your blessing, we pray, O Lord, the offerings presented here, so that by your grace they may set us on fire with the flame of your love, by which Saint Barnabas brought the light of the Gospel to the nations.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-11-iunii-s-barnabae.ant-ad-communionem}

I no longer call you slaves, because a slave does not know what his master is doing. But I have called you friends, because I have told you everything I have heard from my Father (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-11-iunii-s-barnabae.post-communionem}

As we receive the pledge of eternal life, we humbly implore you, Lord, that what we celebrate in sacramental signs on the Memorial of the blessed Apostle Barnabas we may one day behold unveiled.

#### June 13 — Saint Anthony of Padua, priest and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-13-iunii-s-antonii-de-padova}

\[Common of , for one pastor, or of or of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-iunii-s-antonii-de-padova.collecta}

Almighty everlasting God, who gave Saint Anthony of Padua to your people as an outstanding preacher and an intercessor in their need, grant that, with his assistance, as we follow the teachings of the Christian life, we may know your help in every trial.

#### June 16 — Saint Richard of Chichester, Bishop \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-16-iunii-s-richardi-cicestrensis-angliae}

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-iunii-s-richardi-cicestrensis-angliae.collecta}

Lord God, who bestowed on the Bishop Saint Richard of Chichester a love of learning, a zeal for souls and a devotion to the poor, in your kindness grant to us, we pray, the assistance of his intercession that we may be led to deeper knowledge and love of your Only Begotten Son so that we may follow him more closely.

#### June 19 — Saint Romuald, abbot \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-19-iunii-s-romualdi}

\[Common of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-iunii-s-romualdi.collecta}

O God, who through Saint Romuald renewed the manner of life of hermits in your Church, grant that, denying ourselves and following Christ, we may merit to reach the heavenly realms on high.

#### June 20 — Saint Alban, Protomartyr of Britain \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-20-iunii-s-albani-angliae}

\[Common of , for one martyr\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-20-iunii-s-albani-angliae.collecta}

O God, by whose grace Saint Alban gave himself up for his friend and received the martyr's crown as the first in this land to shed his blood for Christ, grant, we pray, that, following his example and supported by his prayers, we may worship you, the living God, and give true witness to Christ our Lord.

#### June 21 — Saint Aloysius Gonzaga, religious \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-21-iunii-s-aloisii-gonzaga}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-21-iunii-s-aloisii-gonzaga.ant-ad-introitum}

The clean of hands and pure of heart shall climb the mountain of the Lord, and stand in his holy place.

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-iunii-s-aloisii-gonzaga.collecta}

O God, giver of heavenly gifts, who in Saint Aloysius Gonzaga joined penitence to a wonderful innocence of life, grant, through his merits and intercession that, though we have failed to follow him in innocence, we may imitate him in penitence.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-21-iunii-s-aloisii-gonzaga.super-oblata}

Grant us, O Lord, that by the example of Saint Aloysius, we may take our place at the heavenly banquet clothed always in our wedding garment, so that by participation in this mystery, we may possess the riches of your grace.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-21-iunii-s-aloisii-gonzaga.ant-ad-communionem}

God gave them bread from heaven; man ate the bread of Angels.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-21-iunii-s-aloisii-gonzaga.post-communionem}

Bring us who have been fed with the food of Angels, O Lord, to serve you in purity of life and, following the example of Saint Aloysius, whom we honour today, may we persevere in constant thanksgiving.

#### June 22 — Saints John Fisher and Thomas More {#misal.die-22-iunii-ss-ioannis-fisher-et-thomae-more}

Saints John Fisher and Thomas More, martyrs \[Optional Memorial or Feast in England\]

\[ or Common of , for several martyrs\]

*Or:*

Saint Paulinus of Nola, bishop \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-iunii-ss-ioannis-fisher-et-thomae-more.collecta}
