O God, who in martyrdom have brought true faith to its highest expression, grant graciously that, strengthened through the intercession of Saints John Fisher and Thomas More, we may confirm by the witness of our life the faith we profess with our lips.

#### Saint Paulinus of Nola {#misal.die-22-iunii-s-paulini-nolani}

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-iunii-s-paulini-nolani.collecta}

O God, who made the Bishop Saint Paulinus of Nola outstanding for love of poverty and for pastoral care, graciously grant that, as we celebrate his merits, we may imitate the example of his charity.

#### Proper Mass Ss John Fisher and Thomas More

##### Entrance Antiphon

Isti sunt viri qui contempsérunt decrétum regis, et dixérunt ei: Non opórtet nos de hac re respóndere tibi.\
These are the brave who resisted the king’s command, saying: in this matter it is our duty to disobey.

##### Collect

O God, who in martyrdom have brought true faith to its highest expression, grant graciously that, strengthened through the intercession of Saints John Fisher and Thomas More, we may confirm by the witness of our life the faith we profess with our lips.

*Or:*

God of truth and justice, by whose grace your servants Saints John Fisher and Thomas More stood forth as champions of your Church in this land, grant us, we pray, by their example and prayers, that, following always a good conscience and holding fast to the truth, we may at the last meet merrily with them and be partakers for ever in the inheritance of your saints in light.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings

Receive, holy Father, the offerings we bring on the Feast of the holy Martyrs Saints John Fisher and Thomas More, and grant that we, your servants, may be found steadfast in confessing your name.

##### Communion Antiphon

Holy men shed their glorious blood for the Lord; they loved Christ in their life, they imitated him in their death, and therefore were crowned in triumph.

##### Prayer After Communion

Renew us, Lord, we pray, with this food from heaven, and strengthen us by the example and prayers of your martyrs Saints John Fisher and Thomas More, so that, always following the voice of conscience, we may ever be your good servants.

#### June 23 — Saint Etheldreda (Audrey), Virgin \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-23-iunii-s-etheldredae-angliae}

\[Common of or \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-iunii-s-etheldredae-angliae.collecta}

O God, by whose grace the Virgin Saint Etheldreda chose rather to be a servant of Christ than the queen of an earthly realm, grant we pray, through her intercession, that we may be freed from earthly toils and be found worthy of a heavenly crown.

#### June 24 — Nativity of Saint John the Baptist \[Solemnity\]

#### June 24 — Nativity of Saint John the Baptist \[Solemnity\] — VIGIL MASS {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-vigilia}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-introitum}

He will be great in the sight of the Lord and will be filled with the Holy Spirit, even from his mother's womb; and many will rejoice at his birth.

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-vigilia.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that your family may walk in the way of salvation, and, attentive to what Saint John the Precursor urged, may come safely to the One he foretold, our Lord Jesus Christ.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-vigilia.super-oblata}

Look with favour, O Lord, upon the offerings made by your people on the Solemnity of Saint John the Baptist, and grant that what we celebrate in mystery we may follow with deeds of devoted service.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed be the Lord, the God of Israel! He has visited his people and redeemed them.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-vigilia.post-communionem}

May the marvellous prayer of Saint John the Baptist accompany us who have eaten our fill at this sacrificial feast, O Lord, and since Saint John proclaimed your Son to be the Lamb who would take away our sins, may he implore now your favour towards us.

#### June 24 — Nativity of Saint John the Baptist \[Solemnity\] — MASS DURING THE DAY {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-die}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-introitum}

A man was sent from God, whose name was John. He came to testify to the light, to prepare a people fit for the Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-die.collecta}

O God, who raised up Saint John the Baptist to make ready a nation fit for Christ the Lord, give your people, we pray, the grace of spiritual joys and direct the hearts of all the faithful into the way of salvation and peace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-die.super-oblata}

We place these offerings on your altar, O Lord, to celebrate with fitting honour the nativity of him who both foretold the coming of the world's Saviour and pointed him out when he came.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-communionem}

Through the tender mercy of our God, the Dawn from on high will visit us.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-24-iunii-in-nativitate-s-ioannis-baptistae-ad-missam-in-die.post-communionem}

Having feasted at the banquet of the heavenly Lamb, we pray, O Lord, that, finding joy in the nativity of Saint John the Baptist, your Church may know as the author of her rebirth the Christ whose coming John foretold.

#### June 26 — St. Josemaría Escrivá, priest

#### June 27 — Saint Cyril of Alexandria, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-27-iunii-s-cyrilli-alexandrini}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-27-iunii-s-cyrilli-alexandrini.collecta}

O God, who made the Bishop Saint Cyril of Alexandria an invincible champion of the divine motherhood of the most Blessed Virgin Mary, grant, we pray, that we who believe she is truly the Mother of God may be saved through the Incarnation of Christ your Son.

#### June 28 — Saint Irenæus, bishop and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-28-iunii-s-irenaei}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-28-iunii-s-irenaei.ant-ad-introitum}

The law of truth was in his mouth; no dishonesty was found on his lips. He walked with me in integrity and peace, and turned many away from evil.

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-iunii-s-irenaei.collecta}

O God, who called the Bishop Saint Irenaeus to confirm true doctrine and the peace of the Church, grant, we pray, through his intercession, that, being renewed in faith and charity, we may always be intent on fostering unity and concord.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-28-iunii-s-irenaei.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice we offer you with joy on the heavenly birthday of Saint Irenaeus bring you glory, O Lord, and instil in us a love of the truth, so that we may keep the Church's faith inviolate and her unity secure.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-28-iunii-s-irenaei.ant-ad-communionem}

Remain in me, as I remain in you, says the Lord. Whoever remains in me, and I in him, bears fruit in plenty.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-28-iunii-s-irenaei.post-communionem}

Through these sacred mysteries, we pray, O Lord, give us in your compassion an increase of that faith which brought glory to the Bishop Saint Irenaeus as he maintained it even until death, and may the same faith bring to us who truly follow it, justification in your sight.

#### June 29 — Saints Peter and Paul, apostles \[Solemnity\]

#### June 29 — Saints Peter and Paul, apostles \[Solemnity\] — VIGIL MASS {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-vigilia}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-introitum}

Peter the Apostle, and Paul the teacher of the Gentiles: these have taught us your law, O Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-vigilia.collecta}

Grant, we pray, O Lord our God, that we may be sustained by the intercession of the blessed Apostles Peter and Paul, that, as through them you gave your Church the foundations of her heavenly office, so through them you may help her to eternal salvation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-vigilia.super-oblata}

We bring offerings to your altar, O Lord, as we glory in the solemn feast of the blessed Apostles Peter and Paul, so that the more we doubt our own merits, the more we may rejoice that we are to be saved by your loving kindness.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-communionem}

Simon, Son of John, do you love me more than these? Lord, you know everything; you know that I love you.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-vigilia.post-communionem}

By this heavenly Sacrament, O Lord, we pray, strengthen your faithful, whom you have enlightened with the teaching of the Apostles.

#### June 29 — Saints Peter and Paul, apostles \[Solemnity\] — MASS DURING THE DAY {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-die}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-introitum}

These are the ones who, living in the flesh, planted the Church with their blood; they drank the chalice of the Lord and became the friends of God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-die.collecta}

O God, who on the solemnity of the Apostles Peter and Paul, give us the noble and holy joy of this day, grant, we pray, that your Church may in all things follow the teaching of those through whom she received the beginnings of right religion.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-die.super-oblata}

May the prayer of the Apostles, O Lord, accompany the sacrificial gift that we offer to your name for consecration, and may their intercession make us devoted to you in celebration of the sacrifice.

##### Preface {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-die.praefatio}

The twofold mission of Peter and Paul in the Church\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God.\
For by your providence the blessed Apostles Peter and Paul bring us joy: Peter, foremost in confessing the faith, Paul, its outstanding preacher, Peter, who established the early Church from the remnant of Israel, Paul, master and teacher of the Gentiles that you call.\
And so each, in a different way, gathered together the one family of Christ: and revered together throughout the world, they share one Martyr's crown.\
And therefore, with all the Angels and Saints, we praise you, as without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-communionem}

Peter said to Jesus: You are the Christ, the Son of the living God. And Jesus replied: You are Peter, and upon this rock I will build my Church.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-29-iunii-ss-petri-et-pauli-ad-missam-in-die.post-communionem}

Grant us, O Lord, who have been renewed by this Sacrament, so to live in the Church that, persevering in the breaking of the Bread and in the teaching of the Apostles, we may be one heart and one soul, made steadfast in your love.

#### June 30 — The First Holy Martyrs of the Holy Roman Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-30-iunii-ss-protomartyrum-sanctae-romanae-ecclesiae}

\[Common of , for several martyrs\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-30-iunii-ss-protomartyrum-sanctae-romanae-ecclesiae.collecta}

O God, who consecrated the abundant first fruits of the Roman Church by the blood of the Martyrs, grant, we pray, that with firm courage we may together draw strength from so great a struggle and ever rejoice at the triumph of faithful love.\
JULY

### JULY

#### July 1 — Saint Oliver Plunkett, bishop and martyr \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-1-iulii-s-oliverii-plunkett-angliae}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-1-iulii-s-oliverii-plunkett-angliae.collecta}

God our Father, you filled Saint Oliver with the spirit of fortitude, enabling him to feed your people with his word and to lay down his life for the faith; at his intercession keep us strong in that same faith and help us to proclaim it everywhere.

#### July 3 — Saints Thomas, apostle \[Feast\] {#misal.die-3-iulii-s-thomae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-3-iulii-s-thomae.ant-ad-introitum}

You are my God, and I confess you: you are my God, and I exalt you; I will thank you, for you became my saviour.

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-iulii-s-thomae.collecta}

Grant, almighty God, that we may glory in the feast of the blessed Apostle Thomas, so that we may always be sustained by his intercession and, believing, may have life in the Name of Jesus Christ your Son, whom Thomas acknowledged as the Lord.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-3-iulii-s-thomae.super-oblata}

We render you, O Lord, the service that is your due, humbly imploring you to keep safe your gifts in us, as we honour the confession of the Apostle Saint Thomas and offer you a sacrifice of praise.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-3-iulii-s-thomae.ant-ad-communionem}

Bring your hand and feel the place of the nails, and do not be unbelieving but believing.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-3-iulii-s-thomae.post-communionem}

O God, as we truly receive in this Sacrament the Body of your Only Begotten Son, grant, we pray, that we may recognize him with the Apostle Thomas by faith as our Lord and our God and proclaim him by our deeds and by our life.

#### July 4 — Saint Elizabeth of Portugal \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-4-iulii-s-elisabeth-lusitaniae}

\[Common of , those who practiced works of mercy\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-iulii-s-elisabeth-lusitaniae.collecta}

O God, author of peace and lover of charity, who adorned Saint Elizabeth of Portugal with a marvellous grace for reconciling those in conflict, grant, through her intercession, that we may become peacemakers, and so be called children of God.

#### July 5 — Saint Anthony Maria Zaccaria, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-5-iulii-s-antonii-mariae-zaccaria}

\[Common of , for one pastor, or of , for educators, or of for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-5-iulii-s-antonii-mariae-zaccaria.collecta}

Grant, O Lord, that in the spirit of the Apostle Paul we may pursue the surpassing knowledge of Jesus Christ, for having learned it, Saint Anthony Zaccaria constantly preached your saving word in the Church.

#### July 6 — Saint Maria Goretti, virgin and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-6-iulii-s-mariae-goretti}

\[Common of for a virgin martyr or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-6-iulii-s-mariae-goretti.collecta}

O God, author of innocence and lover of chastity, who bestowed the grace of martyrdom on your handmaid, the Virgin Saint Maria Goretti, in her youth, grant, we pray, through her intercession, that as you gave her a crown for her steadfastness, so we, too, may be firm in obeying your commandments.

#### July 9 — Saint Augustine Zhao Rong, priest, & companions, martyrs \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-9-iulii-ss-augustini-zhao-rong-et-sociorum}

\[Common of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-iulii-ss-augustini-zhao-rong-et-sociorum.collecta}

O God, who in your wonderful providence have strengthened your Church through the confession of the Martyrs Saints Augustine Zhao and companions, grant that your people, faithful to the mission entrusted to it, may enjoy ever greater freedom and witness to the truth before the world.

#### July 11 — Saint Benedict, abbot \[Memorial or Feast in Europe\] {#misal.die-11-iulii-s-benedicti}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-11-iulii-s-benedicti.ant-ad-introitum}

There was a man of venerable life, Benedict, blessed by grace and by name, who, leaving home and patrimony and desiring to please God alone, sought out the habit of holy living.

##### Collect {#misal.die-11-iulii-s-benedicti.collecta}

O God, who made the Abbot Saint Benedict an outstanding master in the school of divine service, grant, we pray, that putting nothing before love of you, we may hasten with a loving heart in the way of your commands.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-11-iulii-s-benedicti.super-oblata}

Look kindly, Lord, upon these holy offerings, which we make in honour of Saint Benedict, and grant that, by following his example in seeking you, we may merit the gifts of unity in your service and of peace.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-11-iulii-s-benedicti.ant-ad-communionem}

This is the steward, faithful and prudent, whom the Lord set over his household to give them their allowance of food at the proper time.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-11-iulii-s-benedicti.post-communionem}

Having received this pledge of eternal life, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, attentive to the teaching of Saint Benedict, we may faithfully serve your designs and love one another with fervent charity.

#### July 13 — Saint Henry, king \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-13-iulii-s-henrici}

\[Common of for one saint\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-iulii-s-henrici.collecta}

O God, whose abundant grace prepared Saint Henry to be raised by you in a wonderful way from the cares of earthly rule to heavenly realms, grant, we pray, through his intercession, that amid the uncertainties of this world we may hasten towards you with minds made pure.

#### July 14 — Saint Camillus de Lellis, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-14-iulii-s-camilli-de-lellis}

\[Common of , those who practiced works of mercy\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-14-iulii-s-camilli-de-lellis.collecta}

O God, who adorned the Priest Saint Camillus with a singular grace of charity towards the sick, pour out upon us, by his merits, a spirit of love for you so that, serving you in our neighbour, at the hour of our death we may pass safely over to you.

#### July 15 — Saint Bonaventure, bishop and doctor \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-15-iulii-s-bonaventurae}

\[Common of or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-15-iulii-s-bonaventurae.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, just as we celebrate the heavenly birthday of the Bishop Saint Bonaventure, we may benefit from his great learning and constantly imitate the ardour of his charity.

#### July 16 — Our Lady of Mount Carmel \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-16-iulii-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-monte-carmelo}

\[Common of the \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-iulii-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-monte-carmelo.collecta}

May the venerable intercession of the glorious Virgin Mary come to our aid, we pray, O Lord, so that, fortified by her protection, we may reach the mountain which is Christ.

#### July 20 — Saint Apollinaris, bishop and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-20-iulii-s-apollinaris}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-20-iulii-s-apollinaris.collecta}

Direct your faithful, Lord, in the way of eternal salvation which the Bishop Saint Apollinaris showed by his teaching and martyrdom, and grant, through his intercession, that we may so persevere in keeping your commandments as to merit being crowned with him.

#### July 21 — Saint Lawrence of Brindisi, priest and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-21-iulii-s-laurentii-de-brindisi}

\[Common of , for priests, or of or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-iulii-s-laurentii-de-brindisi.collecta}

O God, who for the glory of your Name and the salvation of souls bestowed on the Priest Saint Lawrence of Brindisi a spirit of counsel and fortitude, grant, we pray, that in the same spirit we may know what must be done and, through his intercession, bring it to completion.

#### July 22 — Saint Mary Magdalene \[Feast\] {#misal.die-22-iulii-s-mariae-magdalenae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-22-iulii-s-mariae-magdalenae.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord said to Mary Magdalene: Go to my brothers and tell them: I am going to my Father and your Father, to my God and your God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-iulii-s-mariae-magdalenae.collecta}

O God, whose Only Begotten Son entrusted Mary Magdalene before all others with announcing the great joy of the Resurrection, grant, we pray, that, through her intercession and example, we may proclaim the living Christ and come to see him reigning in your glory.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-22-iulii-s-mariae-magdalenae.super-oblata}

Accept, O Lord, the offerings presented in commemoration of Saint Mary Magdalene, whose homage of charity was graciously accepted by your Only Begotten Son.

##### Preface

Apostle of Apostles\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God,\
whose mercy is no less than His power, to preach the Gospel to everyone, through Christ, our Lord.\
In the garden He appeared to Mary Magdalen, who loved him in life, who witnessed his death on the cross, who sought him as he lay in the tomb, who was the first to adore Him when He rose from the dead, and whose apostolic duty was honoured by the apostles that the good news of life might reach the ends of the earth.\
And so, Lord, with the Angels and Saints we, too, give you thanks as in exultation we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-22-iulii-s-mariae-magdalenae.ant-ad-communionem}

The love of Christ impels us, so that those who live may live no longer for themselves but for him who died for them and was raised.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-22-iulii-s-mariae-magdalenae.post-communionem}

May the holy reception of your mysteries, Lord, instil in us that persevering love with which Saint Mary Magdalene clung resolutely to Christ her Master.

#### July 23 — Saint Bridget of Sweden, religious \[Feast in Europe\] {#misal.die-23-iulii-s-birgittae}

\[Common of for holy women or of \] or proper:

##### Entrance Antiphon

Let us all rejoice in the Lord, as we honour Saint Bridget, at whose feast the Angels rejoice and praise the Son of God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-iulii-s-birgittae.collecta}

O God, who guided Saint Bridget of Sweden along different paths of life, and wondrously taught her the wisdom of the Cross as she contemplated the Passion of your Son; grant us, we pray, that walking worthily in our vocation, we may seek you in all things.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings

Most merciful God, who were pleased to create in Saint Bridget the New Man in your image, the old having passed away; graciously grant, we pray, that, renewed like her, we may offer you the acceptable sacrifice of conciliation.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon

Your love is for justice; your hatred for evil. Therefore God, your God, has anointed you with the oil of gladness above kings.

##### Prayer After Communion

We pray, almighty God, that we, who are fortified by the power of this Sacrament, may learn through the example of Saint Bridget to seek you always above all things and to bear in this world the likeness of the New Man.

#### July 24 — Saint Sharbel Makluf, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-24-iulii-s-sarbelii-makhlu-f}

\[Common of for one pastor or for \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-iulii-s-sarbelii-makhlu-f.collecta}

O God, who called the Priest Saint Sharbel to the solitary combat of the desert and imbued him with all manner of devotion, grant us, we pray, that, being made imitators of the Lord's Passion, we may merit to be co-heirs of his Kingdom.

#### July 25 — Saint James, apostle \[Feast\] {#misal.die-25-iulii-s-iacobi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-25-iulii-s-iacobi.ant-ad-introitum}

As he walked by the Sea of Galilee, Jesus saw James the son of Zebedee and John his brother mending their nets and he called them.

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-iulii-s-iacobi.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who consecrated the first fruits of your Apostles by the blood of Saint James, grant, we pray, that your Church may be strengthened by his confession of faith and constantly sustained by his protection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-25-iulii-s-iacobi.super-oblata}

Cleanse us, Lord, by the saving baptism of your Son's Passion, so that on the Feast of Saint James, whom you willed to be the first among the Apostles to drink of Christ's chalice of suffering, we may offer a sacrifice pleasing to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-25-iulii-s-iacobi.ant-ad-communionem}

They drank the chalice of the Lord, and became the friends of God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-25-iulii-s-iacobi.post-communionem}

Help us, O Lord, we pray, through the intercession of the blessed Apostle James, on whose feast day we have received with joy your holy gifts.

#### July 26 — Saints Joachim and Anne, parents of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-26-iulii-ss-ioachim-et-annae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-26-iulii-ss-ioachim-et-annae.ant-ad-introitum}

Let us praise Joachim and Anne, to whom, in their generation, the Lord gave him who was a blessing for all the nations.

##### Collect {#misal.die-26-iulii-ss-ioachim-et-annae.collecta}

O Lord, God of our Fathers, who bestowed on Saints Joachim and Anne this grace, that of them should be born the Mother of your incarnate Son, grant, through the prayers of both, that we may attain the salvation you have promised to your people.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-26-iulii-ss-ioachim-et-annae.super-oblata}

Receive, we pray, O Lord, these offerings of our homage, and grant that we may merit a share in the same blessing which you promised to Abraham and his descendants.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-26-iulii-ss-ioachim-et-annae.ant-ad-communionem}

They received blessings from the Lord and mercy from God their saviour.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-26-iulii-ss-ioachim-et-annae.post-communionem}

O God, who willed that your Only Begotten Son should be born from among humanity so that by a wonderful mystery humanity might be born again from you; we pray that in your kindness you may sanctify by the spirit of adoption those you have fed with the Bread you give your children.

#### July 29 — Saints Martha, Mary and Lazarus \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-29-iulii-s-marthae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-29-iulii-s-marthae.ant-ad-introitum}

Jesus entered a village, where a woman named Martha welcomed him into her home.

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-iulii-s-marthae.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that the example of your Saints may spur us on to a better life, so that we, who celebrate the memory of Saints Martha, Mary and Lazarus, may also imitate without ceasing their deeds.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-29-iulii-s-marthae.super-oblata}

As we proclaim your wonders in your Saints, O Lord, we humbly implore your majesty, that, as their homage of love was pleasing to you, so, too, our dutiful service may find favour in your sight.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-29-iulii-s-marthae.ant-ad-communionem}

Martha said to Jesus: You are the Christ, the Son of God, who is coming into the world.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-29-iulii-s-marthae.post-communionem}

May the holy reception of the Body and Blood of your Only Begotten Son, O Lord, turn us away from the cares of this fallen world, so that, following the example of Saints Martha, Mary and Lazarus, we may grow in sincere love for you on earth and rejoice to behold you for eternity in heaven.

#### July 30 — Saint Peter Chrysologus, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-30-iulii-s-petri-chrysologi}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-30-iulii-s-petri-chrysologi.collecta}

O God, who made the Bishop Saint Peter Chrysologus an outstanding preacher of your Incarnate Word, grant, through his intercession, that we may constantly ponder in our hearts the mysteries of your salvation and faithfully express them in what we do.

#### July 31 — Saint Ignatius of Loyola, priest \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-31-iulii-s-ignatii-de-loyola}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-31-iulii-s-ignatii-de-loyola.ant-ad-introitum}

At the name of Jesus every knee should bend of those in heaven and on earth and under the earth, and every tongue confess that Jesus Christ is Lord, to the glory of God the Father.

##### Collect {#misal.die-31-iulii-s-ignatii-de-loyola.collecta}

O God, who raised up Saint Ignatius of Loyola in your Church to further the greater glory of your Name, grant that, by his help, we may imitate him in fighting the good fight on earth and merit to receive with him a crown in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-31-iulii-s-ignatii-de-loyola.super-oblata}

May these offerings we make to you as we celebrate Saint Ignatius be pleasing, Lord God, and grant that the sacred mysteries which you have made the fount of all holiness, may sanctify us, too, in the truth.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-31-iulii-s-ignatii-de-loyola.ant-ad-communionem}

Thus says the Lord: I have come to cast fire on the earth, and how I wish that it were kindled!

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-31-iulii-s-ignatii-de-loyola.post-communionem}

May the sacrifice of praise that we have offered with thanksgiving in honour of Saint Ignatius, O Lord, bring us to exalt your majesty without end.

### AUGUST

#### August 1 — Saint Alphonsus Liguori, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-1-augusti-s-alfonsi-mariae-de-liguori}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-1-augusti-s-alfonsi-mariae-de-liguori.collecta}

O God, who constantly raise up in your Church new examples of virtue, grant that we may follow so closely in the footsteps of the Bishop Saint Alphonsus in his zeal for souls as to attain the same rewards that are his in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-1-augusti-s-alfonsi-mariae-de-liguori.super-oblata}

Be pleased, O Lord, to consume our hearts in the celestial fire of your Spirit, just as you granted that Saint Alphonsus should celebrate these mysteries and through them offer himself to you as a holy sacrifice.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-1-augusti-s-alfonsi-mariae-de-liguori.post-communionem}

O God, who gave us Saint Alphonsus to be a faithful steward and preacher of this great mystery, grant that your faithful may receive it often and, receiving it, praise you without end.

#### August 2 — Saint Eusebius of Vercelli {#misal.die-2-augusti-s-eusebii-vercellensis}

Saint Eusebius of Vercelli, bishop \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for bishops\]

*Or:*

Saint Peter Julian Eymard, priest \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one pastor, or of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-augusti-s-eusebii-vercellensis.collecta}

Lead us, Lord God, to imitate the constancy of Saint Eusebius in affirming the divinity your Son, so that, by preserving the faith he taught as your Bishop, we may merit a share in the very life of your Son.

#### Saint Peter Julian Eymard, priest {#misal.die-2-augusti-s-petri-iuliani-eymard}

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-augusti-s-petri-iuliani-eymard.collecta}

O God, who adorned Saint Peter Julian Eymard with a wonderful love for the sacred mysteries of the Body and Blood of your Son, graciously grant that we, too, may be worthy to receive the delights he drew from this divine banquet.

#### August 4 — Saint John Vianney, priest \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-4-augusti-s-ioannis-mariae-vianney}

\[Common of , for one pastor\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-augusti-s-ioannis-mariae-vianney.collecta}

Almighty and merciful God, who made the Priest Saint John Vianney wonderful in his pastoral zeal, grant, we pray, that through his intercession and example we may win brothers and sisters in charity for Christ and attain with them eternal glory.

#### August 5 — Dedication of the Basilica of Saint Mary Major \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-5-augusti-in-dedicatione-basilicae-s-mariae}

\[Common of the \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-5-augusti-in-dedicatione-basilicae-s-mariae.collecta}

Pardon the faults of your servants, we pray, O Lord, that we who cannot please you by our own deeds, may be saved through the intercession of the Mother of your Son and our Lord.

#### August 6 — The Transfiguration of the Lord \[Feast\] {#misal.die-6-augusti-in-transfiguratione-domini}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-6-augusti-in-transfiguratione-domini.ant-ad-introitum}

In a resplendent cloud the Holy Spirit appeared. The Father's voice was heard: This is my beloved Son, with whom I am well pleased. Listen to him.

##### Collect {#misal.die-6-augusti-in-transfiguratione-domini.collecta}

O God, who in the glorious Transfiguration of your Only Begotten Son confirmed the mysteries of faith by the witness of the Fathers and wonderfully prefigured our full adoption to sonship, grant, we pray, to your servants, that, listening to the voice of your beloved Son, we may merit to become co-heirs with him.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-6-augusti-in-transfiguratione-domini.super-oblata}

Sanctify, O Lord, we pray, these offerings here made to celebrate the glorious Transfiguration of your Only Begotten Son, and by his radiant splendour cleanse us from the stains of sin.

##### Preface {#misal.die-6-augusti-in-transfiguratione-domini.praefatio}

The mystery of the transfiguration\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God, through Christ our Lord.\
For he revealed his glory in the presence of chosen witnesses and filled with the greatest splendour that bodily form which he shares with all humanity, that the scandal of the Cross might be removed from the hearts of his disciples and that he might show how in the body of the whole Church is to be fulfilled what so wonderfully shone forth first in its Head.\
And so, with the Powers of heaven, we worship you constantly on earth, and before your majesty without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-6-augusti-in-transfiguratione-domini.ant-ad-communionem}

When Christ appears, we shall be like him, for we shall see him as he is.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-6-augusti-in-transfiguratione-domini.post-communionem}

May the heavenly nourishment we have received, O Lord, we pray, transform us into the likeness of your Son whose radiant splendour you willed to make manifest in his glorious Transfiguration.

#### August 7 — Saints Sixtus II {#misal.die-7-augusti-ss-xysti-ii-et-sociorum}

Saints Sixtus II, pope and martyr, and companions, martyrs \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of \]

*Or:*

Saint Cajetan, priest \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one pastor, or of , for religious}

##### Collect {#misal.die-7-augusti-ss-xysti-ii-et-sociorum.collecta}

By the power of the Holy Spirit make us, we pray, Almighty God, docile in believing the faith and courageous in confessing it, just as you granted Saint Sixtus and his companions that they might lay down their lives for the sake of your word and in witness to Jesus.

#### Saint Cajetan, priest {#misal.die-7-augusti-s-caietani}

##### Collect {#misal.die-7-augusti-s-caietani.collecta}

O God, who endowed the Priest Saint Cajetan with the grace of imitating the apostolic way of life, grant us, through his example and intercession, to trust in you at all times and to seek your Kingdom without ceasing.

#### August 8 — Saint Dominic, priest \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-8-augusti-s-dominici}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-8-augusti-s-dominici.ant-ad-introitum}

In the midst of the Church he opened his mouth, and the Lord filled him with the spirit of wisdom and understanding and clothed him in a robe of glory.

##### Collect {#misal.die-8-augusti-s-dominici.collecta}

May Saint Dominic come to the help of your Church by his merits and teaching, O Lord, and may he who was an outstanding preacher of your truth be a devoted intercessor on our behalf.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-8-augusti-s-dominici.super-oblata}

Attend mercifully to the prayers we offer you, O Lord, by the intercession of Saint Dominic, and through the great power of this sacrifice strengthen by the protection of your grace those who champion the faith.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-8-augusti-s-dominici.ant-ad-communionem}

This is the steward, faithful and prudent, whom the Lord set over his household to give them their allowance of food at the proper time.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-8-augusti-s-dominici.post-communionem}

May your Church, O Lord, receive with wholehearted reverence the power of this heavenly Sacrament, by which we have been nourished on the commemoration of Saint Dominic, and may your Church, having nourished by means of his preaching, be helped through his intercession.

#### August 9 — Saint Teresa Benedicta of the Cross, virgin and martyr \[Optional Memorial or Feast in Europe\] {#misal.die-9-augusti-s-teresiae-benedictae-a-cruce}

\[Common of , or of \] or proper:

##### Entrance Antiphon

May I never boast except in the cross of our Lord Jesus Christ, through which the world has been crucified to me, and I to the world.

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-augusti-s-teresiae-benedictae-a-cruce.collecta}

God of our Fathers, who brought the Martyr Saint Teresa Benedicta of the Cross to know your crucified Son and to imitate him even until death, grant, through her intercession, that the whole human race may acknowledge Christ as its Saviour and through him come to behold you for eternity.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings

Graciously accept and perfect, O Lord, the oblations we dedicate to you in commemoration of your martyr, Saint Teresa Benedicta, for you brought to completion the many offerings of the old covenant by that one perfect sacrifice which your Son offered in his blood.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon

Though I should walk in the valley of the shadow of death, no evil will I fear, for you are with me.

##### Prayer After Communion

Grant, most merciful God, that, as we venerate Saint Teresa Benedicta, these heavenly fruits of the tree of the cross may give strength to our hearts, so that, by clinging faithfully to Christ on earth, we may merit to eat in paradise of the tree of life.

#### August 10 — Saint Lawrence, deacon and martyr \[Feast\] {#misal.die-10-augusti-s-laurentii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-10-augusti-s-laurentii.ant-ad-introitum}

This is the blessed Lawrence who gave himself up for the treasure of the Church: for this he earned the suffering of martyrdom to ascend with joy to the Lord Jesus Christ.

##### Collect {#misal.die-10-augusti-s-laurentii.collecta}

O God, giver of that ardour of love for you, by which Saint Lawrence was outstandingly faithful in service and glorious in martyrdom, grant that we may love what he loved and put into practice what he taught.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-10-augusti-s-laurentii.super-oblata}

Receive with favour, O Lord, the offerings we joyfully make on the feast day of Saint Lawrence and grant that they become a help to our salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-10-augusti-s-laurentii.ant-ad-communionem}

Whoever serves me must follow me and where I am there also will my servant be, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-10-augusti-s-laurentii.post-communionem}

Nourished by these sacred gifts, we humbly implore you, Lord, that the homage of dutiful service which we render on the feast day of Saint Lawrence, may bring us an increase of your saving grace.

#### August 11 \[Memorial\] — Saint Clare, virgin {#misal.die-11-augusti-s-clarae}

\[Common of , or for \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-11-augusti-s-clarae.collecta}

O God, who in your mercy led Saint Clare to a love of poverty, grant, through her intercession, that following Christ in poverty of spirit, we may merit to contemplate you one day in the heavenly Kingdom.

#### August 12 — Saint Jane Frances de Chantal, religious \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-12-augusti-s-ioannae-franciscae-de-chantal}

\[Common of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-augusti-s-ioannae-franciscae-de-chantal.collecta}

O God, who made Saint Jane Frances radiant with outstanding merits in different walks of life, grant us, through her intercession, that walking faithfully in our vocation we may constantly be examples of shining light.

#### August 13 — Saint Pontian, pope and martyr, and Saint Hippolytus, priest and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-13-augusti-ss-pontiani-et-hippolyti}

\[Common of , or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-augusti-ss-pontiani-et-hippolyti.collecta}

May the precious long-suffering of the just, O Lord, we pray, bring us a great increase of love for you, and always prompt in our hearts constancy in the holy faith.

#### August 14 — Saint Maximillian Kolbe, priest and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-14-augusti-s-maximiliani-mariae-kolbe}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-14-augusti-s-maximiliani-mariae-kolbe.ant-ad-introitum}

Come, you blessed of my Father, says the Lord. Amen I say to you: whatever you did for one of the least of my brethren, you did it for me.

##### Collect {#misal.die-14-augusti-s-maximiliani-mariae-kolbe.collecta}

O God, who filled the Priest and Martyr Saint Maximilian Kolbe with a burning love for the Immaculate Virgin Mary and with zeal for souls and love of neighbour, graciously grant, through his intercession, that striving for your glory by eager service of others, we may be conformed, even until death, to your Son.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-14-augusti-s-maximiliani-mariae-kolbe.super-oblata}

We present our oblations to you, O Lord, humbly praying that we may learn from the example of Saint Maximilian to offer our very lives to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-14-augusti-s-maximiliani-mariae-kolbe.ant-ad-communionem}

Greater love has no one than to lay down his life for his friends, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-14-augusti-s-maximiliani-mariae-kolbe.post-communionem}

We pray, O Lord, that, renewed by the Body and Blood of your Son, we may be inflamed with the same fire of charity that Saint Maximilian received from this holy banquet.

#### August 15 — The Assumption of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Solemnity\]

#### August 15 — The Assumption of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Solemnity\] — VIGIL MASS {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-vigilia}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-introitum}

Glorious things are spoken of you, O Mary, who today were exalted above the choirs of Angels into eternal triumph with Christ.

##### Collect {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-vigilia.collecta}

O God, who looking on the lowliness of the Blessed Virgin Mary, raised her to such grace, that your Only Begotten Son was born of her according to the flesh, and that she was crowned this day with surpassing glory; grant through her prayers, that saved by the mystery of your redemption, we may merit to be exalted by you on high.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-vigilia.super-oblata}

Receive, we pray, O Lord, the sacrifice of conciliation and praise which we celebrate on the Assumption of the holy Mother of God, that it may lead us to your pardon and confirm us in perpetual thanksgiving.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-vigilia.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed is the womb of the Virgin Mary, which bore the Son of the eternal Father.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-vigilia.post-communionem}

Having partaken of this heavenly table, we beseech your mercy, Lord our God, that we who honour the Assumption of the Mother of God may be freed from every threat of harm.

#### August 15 — The Assumption of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Solemnity\] — MASS DURING THE DAY {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-die}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-introitum}

A great sign appeared in heaven: a woman clothed with the sun, and the moon beneath her feet, and on her head a crown of twelve stars.

*Or:*

Let us all rejoice in the Lord, as we celebrate the feast day in honour of the Virgin Mary, at whose Assumption the Angels rejoice and praise the Son of God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-die.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who assumed the Immaculate Virgin Mary, the Mother of your Son, body and soul into heavenly glory, grant, we pray, that, always attentive to the things that are above, we may merit to be sharers of her glory.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-die.super-oblata}

May this oblation, our tribute of homage, rise up to you, O Lord, and, through the intercession of the Most Blessed Virgin Mary, whom you assumed into heaven, may our hearts, aflame with the fire of love, constantly long for you.

##### Preface {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-die.praefatio}

Mary assumed into glory\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God, through Christ our Lord.\
For today the Virgin Mother of God was assumed into heaven, as the beginning and image of your Church's coming to perfection and a sign of sure hope and comfort to your pilgrim people; rightly you would not allow her to see the corruption of the tomb, since from her own body she marvellously brought forth your incarnate Son, the Author of all life.\
And so, in company with the choirs of Angels, we praise you, and with joy we proclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-die.ant-ad-communionem}

All generations will call me blessed, for he who is mighty has done great things for me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-15-augusti-in-assumptione-beatae-mariae-virginis-ad-missam-in-die.post-communionem}

Having received the Sacrament of salvation, we ask you to grant, O Lord, that, through the intercession of the Blessed Virgin Mary, whom you assumed into heaven, we may be brought to the glory of the resurrection.

#### August 16 — Saint Stephen of Hungary \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-16-augusti-s-stephani-hungariae}

\[Common of for one saint\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-augusti-s-stephani-hungariae.collecta}

Grant your Church, we pray, almighty God, that she may have Saint Stephen of Hungary, who fostered her growth while a king on earth, as her glorious defender in heaven.

#### August 19 — Saint John Eudes, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-19-augusti-s-ioannis-eudes}

\[Common of , for one pastor or of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-augusti-s-ioannis-eudes.collecta}

O God, who wonderfully chose the Priest Saint John Eudes to proclaim the unfathomable riches of Christ, grant us by his example and teachings that growing in knowledge of you, we may live faithfully by the light of the Gospel.

#### August 20 — Saint Bernard, abbot and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-20-augusti-s-bernardi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-20-augusti-s-bernardi.ant-ad-introitum}

Filled by the Lord with a spirit of understanding, blessed Bernard ministered streams of clear teaching to the people of God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-20-augusti-s-bernardi.collecta}

O God, who made of the Abbot Saint Bernard a man consumed with zeal for your house and a light shining and burning in your Church, grant, through his intercession, that we may be on fire with the same spirit and walk always as children of light.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-20-augusti-s-bernardi.super-oblata}

We offer to your majesty, O Lord, the Sacrament of unity and peace, as we celebrate the Memorial of the Abbot Saint Bernard, a man outstanding in word and deed, who strove to bring order and concord to your Church.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-20-augusti-s-bernardi.ant-ad-communionem}

As the Father loves me, so I also love you; remain in my love, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-20-augusti-s-bernardi.post-communionem}

May the food we have received, O Lord, as we honour Saint Bernard, work its effect in us, so that, strengthened by his example and instructed by his teaching, we may be caught up in love of your incarnate Word.

#### August 21 — Saint Pius X, pope \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-21-augusti-s-pii-x}

\[Common of , for popes\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-augusti-s-pii-x.collecta}

O God, who to safeguard the Catholic faith and to restore all things in Christ, filled Pope Saint Pius the Tenth with heavenly wisdom and apostolic fortitude, graciously grant that, following his teaching and example, we may gain an eternal prize.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-21-augusti-s-pii-x.super-oblata}

Receive with kindness our oblations and grant, O Lord, we pray, that, following the teachings of Pope Saint Pius, we may celebrate these divine mysteries with sincere reverence and receive them in a spirit of faith.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-21-augusti-s-pii-x.post-communionem}

Celebrating the Memorial of Pope Saint Pius, we pray, O Lord our God, that by the power of this heavenly table we may be made constant in the faith and be of one accord in your love.

#### August 22 — Queenship of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-22-augusti-beatae-mariae-virginis-reginae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-22-augusti-beatae-mariae-virginis-reginae.ant-ad-introitum}

At your right stands the queen in robes of gold, finely arrayed.

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-augusti-beatae-mariae-virginis-reginae.collecta}

O God, who made the Mother of your Son to be our Mother and our Queen, graciously grant that, sustained by her intercession, we may attain in the heavenly Kingdom the glory promised to your children.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-22-augusti-beatae-mariae-virginis-reginae.super-oblata}

As we observe this Memorial of the Blessed Virgin Mary, we bring you our offerings, O Lord, praying to be given strength by the humanity of Christ, who offered himself to you on the Cross as the unblemished oblation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-22-augusti-beatae-mariae-virginis-reginae.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are you who have believed that what was spoken to you by the Lord will be fulfilled.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-22-augusti-beatae-mariae-virginis-reginae.post-communionem}

Having received this heavenly Sacrament, we humbly pray, O Lord, that we who reverently celebrate the Memorial of the Blessed Virgin Mary may merit to be partakers at your eternal banquet.

#### August 23 — Saint Rose of Lima, virgin \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-23-augusti-s-rosae-de-lima}

\[Common of , or of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-augusti-s-rosae-de-lima.collecta}

O God, you set Saint Rose of Lima on fire with your love, so that, secluded from the world in the austerity of a life of penance, she might give herself to you alone; grant, we pray, that through her intercession we may tread the paths of life on earth and drink at the stream of your delights in heaven.

#### August 24 — Saint Bartholomew, apostle \[Feast\] {#misal.die-24-augusti-s-bartholomaei}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-24-augusti-s-bartholomaei.ant-ad-introitum}

Proclaim the salvation of God day by day; tell among the nations his glory.

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-augusti-s-bartholomaei.collecta}

Strengthen in us, O Lord, the faith by which the blessed Apostle Bartholomew clung wholeheartedly to your Son, and grant that through the help of his prayers your Church may become for all the nations the sacrament of salvation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-24-augusti-s-bartholomaei.super-oblata}

As we celebrate anew the feast day of Saint Bartholomew, O Lord, we pray that we may obtain your help through the intercession of the Apostle in whose honour we bring you this sacrifice of praise.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-24-augusti-s-bartholomaei.ant-ad-communionem}

I confer a kingdom on you, just as my Father has conferred one on me, that you may eat and drink at my table in my Kingdom, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-24-augusti-s-bartholomaei.post-communionem}

As we celebrate the feast day of the blessed Apostle Bartholomew, we have received the pledge of eternal salvation, O Lord, and we pray that it may be of help to us both now and for the life to come.

#### August 25 — Saint Louis of France {#misal.die-25-augusti-s-ludovici}

Saint Louis of France \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of for one saint\]

*Or:*

Saint Joseph of Calasanz, priest \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , or of , for educators\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-augusti-s-ludovici.collecta}

O God, who brought Saint Louis from the cares of earthly rule to the glory of your heavenly realm, grant, we pray, through his intercession, that by fulfilling our duties on earth, we may seek out your eternal Kingdom.

#### Saint Joseph of Calasanz {#misal.die-25-augusti-s-iosephi-de-calasanz}

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-augusti-s-iosephi-de-calasanz.collecta}

O God, who adorned the priest Saint Joseph Calasanz with such charity and patience that he laboured tirelessly to educate children and endow them with every virtue, grant, we pray, that we who venerate him as a teacher of wisdom may constantly imitate him, for he worked in harmony with your truth.

#### August 26 — Blessed Dominic of the Mother of God Barberi, priest \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-26-augusti-b-dominici-barberi-angliae}

\[Common of , for one pastor or of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-26-augusti-b-dominici-barberi-angliae.collecta}

O God, who chose the Priest Blessed Dominic Barberi to be a minister of your salvation, so that by his teaching and example many might find peace and reconciliation in your Church, mercifully guide our steps, we humbly pray, along the same way of love and truth until by your grace we gain its eternal reward.

#### August 27 — Saint Monica \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-27-augusti-s-monicae}

\[Common of for Holy Women\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-27-augusti-s-monicae.collecta}

O God, who console the sorrowful and who mercifully accepted the motherly tears of Saint Monica for the conversion of her son Augustine, grant us, through the intercession of both, that we may bitterly regret our sins and find the grace of your pardon.

#### August 28 — Saint Augustine, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-28-augusti-s-augustini}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-28-augusti-s-augustini.ant-ad-introitum}

In the midst of the Church he opened his mouth, and the Lord filled him with the spirit of wisdom and understanding and clothed him in a robe of glory.

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-augusti-s-augustini.collecta}

Renew in your Church, we pray, O Lord, the spirit with which you endowed your Bishop Saint Augustine that, filled with the same spirit, we may thirst for you, the sole fount of true wisdom, and seek you, the author of heavenly love.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-28-augusti-s-augustini.super-oblata}

Celebrating the memorial of our salvation, we humbly beseech your mercy, O Lord, that this Sacrament of your loving kindness may be for us the sign of unity and the bond of charity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-28-augusti-s-augustini.ant-ad-communionem}

Thus says the Lord: You have but one teacher, the Christ, and you are all brothers.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-28-augusti-s-augustini.post-communionem}

May partaking of Christ's table sanctify us, we pray, O Lord, that, being made members of his Body, we may become what we have received.

#### August 29 — The Passion of Saint John the Baptist \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-29-augusti-in-passione-s-ioannis-baptistae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-29-augusti-in-passione-s-ioannis-baptistae.ant-ad-introitum}

I spoke, O Lord, of your decrees before kings, and was not confounded; I pondered your commands and loved them greatly.

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-augusti-in-passione-s-ioannis-baptistae.collecta}

O God, who willed that Saint John the Baptist should go ahead of your Son both in birth and in death, grant that as he died a Martyr for truth and justice, we, too, may fight hard for the confession of what you teach.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-29-augusti-in-passione-s-ioannis-baptistae.super-oblata}

Through these offerings which we bring you, O Lord, grant that we may make straight your paths, as taught by the voice crying in the desert of Saint John the Baptist, who powerfully sealed his teaching by the shedding of his blood.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-29-augusti-in-passione-s-ioannis-baptistae.ant-ad-communionem}

John answered and said: He must increase, but I must decrease.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-29-augusti-in-passione-s-ioannis-baptistae.post-communionem}

Grant, O Lord, as we celebrate the heavenly birth of Saint John the Baptist, that we may revere, for what it signifies, the saving Sacrament we have received and, even more, may rejoice at its clear effects in us.

#### August 30 — SS Margaret Clitherow, Anne Line & Margaret Ward, Virgin, Martyrs \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-30-augusti-ss-margaritae-clitherow-annae-line-et-margaritae-ward-angliae}

\[Common of for several martyrs or for \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-30-augusti-ss-margaritae-clitherow-annae-line-et-margaritae-ward-angliae.collecta}

Grant, almighty God, to us who honour the fidelity in life and constancy in death of your holy Martyrs, Saints Margaret Clitherow, Anne Line and Margaret Ward, that they, who drew from you the strength to triumph, may likewise always obtain from you the grace of victory for us.

#### August 31 — Saint Aidan, Bishop and the Saints of Lindisfarne \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-31-augusti-s-aidani-et-ss-lindisfarnensium-angliae}

\[Common of , for a bishop or \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-31-augusti-s-aidani-et-ss-lindisfarnensium-angliae.collecta}

Lord God, you sent the gentle Bishop Saint Aidan to proclaim the Gospel of Jesus Christ; grant us, by his prayers, to live as he taught in simplicity, humility and love for the poor.

### SEPTEMBER

#### September 3 — Saint Gregory the Great, Pope and doctor of the Church \[Memorial or Feast in England\] {#misal.die-3-septembris-s-gregorii-magni}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-3-septembris-s-gregorii-magni.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed Gregory, raised upon the throne of Peter, sought always the beauty of the Lord, and lived in celebration of that love.

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-septembris-s-gregorii-magni.collecta}

O God, who care for your people with gentleness and rule them in love, through the intercession of Pope Saint Gregory, endow, we pray, with a spirit of wisdom those to whom you have given authority to govern, that the flourishing of a holy flock may become the eternal joy of the shepherds.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-3-septembris-s-gregorii-magni.super-oblata}

Grant our supplication, we pray, O Lord, that this sacrifice we present in celebration of Saint Gregory may be for our good, since through its offering you have loosed the offences of all the world.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-3-septembris-s-gregorii-magni.ant-ad-communionem}

This is the steward, faithful and prudent, whom the Lord set over his household to give them their allowance of food at the proper time.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-3-septembris-s-gregorii-magni.post-communionem}

Through Christ the teacher, O Lord, instruct those you feed with Christ, the living Bread, that on the feast day of Saint Gregory they may learn your truth and express it in works of charity.

#### September 4 — Saint Cuthbert, Bishop \[English calendar\] {#misal.die-4-septembris-s-cuthberti-angliae}

\[Common of for a bishop\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-septembris-s-cuthberti-angliae.collecta}

O God, who called your servant the Bishop Saint Cuthbert from tending sheep to serving Christ as a shepherd of your people, grant in your kindness, we pray, that your Church may imitate his example by seeking out the lost and bearing them home to your fold.

#### September 5 — Saint Teresa of Calcutta \[In particular places\] {#misal.die-5-septembris-s-teresiae-de-calcutta-angliae}

\[Common of , those who practiced works of mercy or \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-5-septembris-s-teresiae-de-calcutta-angliae.collecta}

God, who called Blessed Teresa, virgin, to respond to the love of your Son thirsting on the cross with outstanding charity to the poorest of the poor, grant us, we beseech you, by her intercession, to minister to Christ in his suffering brothers.

#### September 8 — Nativity of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Feast\] {#misal.die-8-septembris-in-nativitate-beatae-mariae-virginis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-8-septembris-in-nativitate-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-introitum}

Let us celebrate with joy the Nativity of the Blessed Virgin Mary, for from her arose the sun of justice, Christ our God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-8-septembris-in-nativitate-beatae-mariae-virginis.collecta}

Impart to your servants, we pray, O Lord, the gift of heavenly grace, that the feast of the Nativity of the Blessed Virgin may bring deeper peace to those for whom the birth of her Son was the dawning of salvation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-8-septembris-in-nativitate-beatae-mariae-virginis.super-oblata}

May the humanity of your Only Begotten Son, come, O Lord, to our aid, and may he, who at his birth from the Blessed Virgin did not diminish but consecrated her integrity, by taking from us now our wicked deeds, make our oblation acceptable to you.

*Or:*

As we celebrate with joy he Nativity of the Blessed Virgin Mary, we bring you our offerings, O Lord, and we humbly pray to be given strength by the humanity of your Son, who from her was pleased to take flesh.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-8-septembris-in-nativitate-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, the Virgin will bear a son who will save his people from their sins.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-8-septembris-in-nativitate-beatae-mariae-virginis.post-communionem}

May your Church exult, O Lord, for you have renewed her with these sacred mysteries, as she rejoices in the Nativity of the Blessed Virgin Mary, which was the hope and the daybreak of salvation for all the world.

#### September 9 — Saint Peter Claver, Priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-9-septembris-s-petri-claver}

\[Common of , for one pastor, or , for those who practiced works of mercy\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-septembris-s-petri-claver.collecta}

O God, who made Saint Peter Claver a slave of slaves and strengthened him with wonderful charity and patience as he came to their help, grant, through his intercession, that, seeking the things of Jesus Christ, we may love our neighbour in deeds and in truth.

#### September 12 — Holy Name of Mary \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-12-septembris-sanctissimi-nominis-mariae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-12-septembris-sanctissimi-nominis-mariae.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed are you, O Virgin Mary, by the Lord God Most High, above all women on the earth; for he has so exalted your name, that your praise shall be undying on our lips.

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-septembris-sanctissimi-nominis-mariae.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, for all who celebrate the glorious Name of the Blessed Virgin Mary, she may obtain your merciful favour.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-12-septembris-sanctissimi-nominis-mariae.super-oblata}

May the intercession of Blessed Mary ever-Virgin commend our offerings, we pray, O Lord, and may it make us acceptable to your majesty as we revere and venerate her Name.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-12-septembris-sanctissimi-nominis-mariae.ant-ad-communionem}

All generations will call me blessed, for God has looked on his lowly handmaid.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-12-septembris-sanctissimi-nominis-mariae.post-communionem}

May we obtain the grace of your blessing, O Lord, through the intercession of Mary, the Mother of God, that from her whose holy Name we venerate, we may obtain help in our every need.

#### September 13 — Saint John Chrysostom, Bishop and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-13-septembris-s-ioannis-chrysostomi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-13-septembris-s-ioannis-chrysostomi.ant-ad-introitum}

Those who are wise will shine brightly like the splendour of the firmament and those who lead the many to justice shall be like the stars forever.

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-septembris-s-ioannis-chrysostomi.collecta}

O God, strength of those who hope in you, who willed that the Bishop Saint John Chrysostom should be illustrious by his wonderful eloquence and his experience of suffering, grant us, we pray, that, instructed by his teachings, we may be strengthened through the example of his invincible patience.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-13-septembris-s-ioannis-chrysostomi.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice which we gladly present in commemoration of Saint John Chrysostom be pleasing to you, O God, for taught by him, we, too, give ourselves entirely to you in praise.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-13-septembris-s-ioannis-chrysostomi.ant-ad-communionem}

We proclaim Christ crucified; Christ, the power of God and the wisdom of God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-13-septembris-s-ioannis-chrysostomi.post-communionem}

Grant, O merciful God, that these mysteries we have received as we commemorate Saint John Chrysostom may confirm us in your love and enable us to be faithful in confessing your truth.

#### September 14 — Exaltation of the Holy Cross \[Feast\] {#misal.die-14-septembris-in-exaltatione-sanctae-crucis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-14-septembris-in-exaltatione-sanctae-crucis.ant-ad-introitum}

We should glory in the cross of our Lord Jesus Christ, in whom is our salvation, life and resurrection, through whom we are saved and delivered.

##### Collect {#misal.die-14-septembris-in-exaltatione-sanctae-crucis.collecta}

O God, who willed that your Only Begotten Son should undergo the Cross to save the human race, grant, we pray, that we who have known his mystery on earth may merit the grace of his redemption in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-14-septembris-in-exaltatione-sanctae-crucis.super-oblata}

May this oblation, O Lord, which on the altar of the Cross cancelled the offence of the whole world, cleanse us, we pray, of all our sins.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-14-septembris-in-exaltatione-sanctae-crucis.ant-ad-communionem}

When I am lifted up from the earth, I will draw everyone to myself, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-14-septembris-in-exaltatione-sanctae-crucis.post-communionem}

Having been nourished by your holy banquet, we beseech you, Lord Jesus Christ, to bring those you have redeemed by the wood of your life-giving Cross to the glory of the resurrection.

#### September 15 — Our Lady of Sorrows \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-15-septembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-perdolentis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-15-septembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-perdolentis.ant-ad-introitum}

Simeon said to Mary: Behold, this child is destined for the ruin and rising of many in Israel, and to be a sign of contradiction; and your own soul a sword will pierce.

##### Collect {#misal.die-15-septembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-perdolentis.collecta}

O God, who willed that when your Son was lifted high on the Cross his Mother should stand close by and share his suffering, grant that your Church, participating with the Virgin Mary in the Passion of Christ, may merit a share in his Resurrection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-15-septembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-perdolentis.super-oblata}

Receive, O merciful God, to the praise of your Name the prayers and sacrificial offerings which we bring to you as we venerate the Blessed Virgin Mary, whom you graciously gave to us as a most devoted Mother when she stood by the Cross of Jesus.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-15-septembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-perdolentis.ant-ad-communionem}

Rejoice when you share in the sufferings of Christ, that you may also rejoice exultantly when his glory is revealed.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-15-septembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-perdolentis.post-communionem}

Having received the Sacrament of eternal redemption, we humbly ask, O Lord, that, honouring how the Blessed Virgin Mary suffered with her Son, we may complete in ourselves for the Church's sake what is lacking in the sufferings of Christ.

#### September 16 — Saints Cornelius, pope, and Cyprian, bishop, martyrs \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-16-septembris-ss-cornelii-et-cypriani}

\[Common of or of for a bishop\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-septembris-ss-cornelii-et-cypriani.collecta}

O God, who gave Saints Cornelius and Cyprian to your people as diligent shepherds and valiant Martyrs, grant that, through their intercession, we may be strengthened in faith and constancy and spend ourselves without reserve for the unity of the Church.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-16-septembris-ss-cornelii-et-cypriani.super-oblata}

Receive, we pray, O Lord, the offerings of your people in honour of the passion of your holy Martyrs Saints Cornelius and Cyprian, and may the gifts that gave them courage under persecution make us, too, steadfast in all trials.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-16-septembris-ss-cornelii-et-cypriani.post-communionem}

Through these mysteries which we have received, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, by the example of the Martyrs Saints Cornelius and Cyprian, we may be strengthened with the fortitude of your Spirit to bear witness to the truth of the Gospel.

#### September 17 — Saint Robert Bellarmine, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-17-septembris-s-roberti-bellarmino}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

Saint Hildegard of Bingen, virgin and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of or of \]

St Robert Bellarmine

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-septembris-s-roberti-bellarmino.collecta}

O God, who adorned the Bishop Saint Robert Bellarmine with wonderful learning and virtue to vindicate the faith of your Church, grant, through his intercession, that in the integrity of that same faith, your people may always find joy.

#### September 17 — St Hildegard of Bingen {#misal.die-17-septembris-s-hildegardis-bingensis}

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-septembris-s-hildegardis-bingensis.collecta}

O God, fount of life, who imbued the Virgin Saint Hildegard with a spirit of prophecy, grant us, we pray, by her example and intercession, to know your ways, and in the darkness of this age to perceive the splendour of your light.

#### September 19 — St Januarius, bishop and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-19-septembris-s-ianuarii}

\[Common of , for one martyr, or of , for bishops\]

*Or:*

Saint Theodore of Canterbury, bishop \[English Calendar\]

\[Common of , for bishops\]

St Januarius

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-septembris-s-ianuarii.collecta}

O God, who grant us to venerate the memory of the Martyr Saint Januarius, give us, we pray, the joy of his company in blessed happiness for all eternity.

#### September 19 — St Theodore of Canterbury {#misal.die-19-septembris-s-theodori-cantuariensis-angliae}

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-septembris-s-theodori-cantuariensis-angliae.collecta}

O God, who called your servant Saint Theodore to be a Bishop among us and to set the Church in a firm foundation, grant, we pray, through his intercession, that we may always remain steadfast on the rock which is Christ and be obedient to the calling we have received.

#### September 20 — Saint Andrew Kim Tae-gon, priest and martyr, and Saint Paul Chong Ha-sang and their companions, martyrs \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-20-septembris-ss-andreae-kim-tae-go-n-et-pauli-cho-ng-ha-sang-et-sociorum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-20-septembris-ss-andreae-kim-tae-go-n-et-pauli-cho-ng-ha-sang-et-sociorum.ant-ad-introitum}

The blood of the holy Martyrs was poured out for Christ upon the earth; therefore they have gained everlasting rewards.

##### Collect {#misal.die-20-septembris-ss-andreae-kim-tae-go-n-et-pauli-cho-ng-ha-sang-et-sociorum.collecta}

O God, who have been pleased to increase your adopted children in all the world, and who made the blood of the Martyrs Saint Andrew Kim Tae-gon and his companions a most fruitful seed of Christians, grant that we may be defended by their help and profit always from their example.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-20-septembris-ss-andreae-kim-tae-go-n-et-pauli-cho-ng-ha-sang-et-sociorum.super-oblata}

Look with favour, almighty God, on the offerings of your people and, through the intercession of the blessed Martyrs, grant that we ourselves may become a sacrifice acceptable to you for the salvation of all the world.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-20-septembris-ss-andreae-kim-tae-go-n-et-pauli-cho-ng-ha-sang-et-sociorum.ant-ad-communionem}

Everyone who acknowledges me before others I will acknowledge before my heavenly Father, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-20-septembris-ss-andreae-kim-tae-go-n-et-pauli-cho-ng-ha-sang-et-sociorum.post-communionem}

Nourished with the food of the valiant as we celebrate the blessed Martyrs, we humbly ask you, O Lord, that, clinging faithfully to Christ, we may labour in the Church for the salvation of all.

#### September 21 — Saint Matthew, apostle and evangelist \[Feast\] {#misal.die-21-septembris-s-matthaei}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-21-septembris-s-matthaei.ant-ad-introitum}

Go and make disciples of all nations, baptizing them and teaching them to observe all that I have commanded you, says the Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-septembris-s-matthaei.collecta}

O God, who with untold mercy were pleased to choose as an Apostle Saint Matthew, the tax collector, grant that, sustained by his example and intercession we may merit to hold firm in following you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-21-septembris-s-matthaei.super-oblata}

As we celebrate anew the memory of Saint Matthew, we bring you sacrifices and prayers, O Lord, humbly imploring you to look kindly on your Church, whose faith you have nourished by the preaching of the Apostles.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-21-septembris-s-matthaei.ant-ad-communionem}

I did not come to call the just, but sinners, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-21-septembris-s-matthaei.post-communionem}

Sharing in that saving joy, O Lord, with which Saint Matthew welcomed the Saviour as a guest in his home, we pray: grant that we may always be renewed by the food we receive from Christ, who came to call not the just, but sinners to salvation.

#### September 23 — Saint Pius of Pietrelcina, priest \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-23-septembris-s-pii-de-pietrelcina}

\[Common of , for one pastor or for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-septembris-s-pii-de-pietrelcina.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who, by a singular grace, gave the Priest Saint Pius a share in the Cross of your Son and by means of his ministry renewed the wonders of your mercy, grant that, through his intercession, we may be united constantly to the sufferings of Christ, and so brought happily to the glory of the resurrection.

#### September 24 — Our Lady of Walsingham \[English Calendar, Memorial\] — or Our Lady of Ransom {#misal.die-24-septembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-walsingham-angliae}

\[Common of the \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-septembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-walsingham-angliae.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that as in the mystery of the Incarnation the Blessed and ever Virgin Mary conceived your Son in her heart before she conceived him in the womb, so we, your pilgrim people, rejoicing in her motherly care, may welcome him into our hearts and become a holy house fit for his eternal dwelling.

#### September 26 — Saints Cosmas and Damian, martyrs \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-26-septembris-ss-cosmae-et-damiani}

\[Common of , for several martyrs\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-26-septembris-ss-cosmae-et-damiani.collecta}

May you be magnified, O Lord, by the revered memory of your Saints Cosmas and Damian, for with providence beyond words you have conferred on them everlasting glory, and on us your unfailing help.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-26-septembris-ss-cosmae-et-damiani.super-oblata}

In honour of the precious death of your just ones, O Lord, we come to offer that sacrifice from which all martyrdom draws its origin.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-26-septembris-ss-cosmae-et-damiani.post-communionem}

Preserve in us your gift, O Lord, and may what we have received at your hands as we commemorate the Martyrs Saints Cosmas and Damian, bring us healing, salvation and peace.

#### September 27 — St Vincent de Paul, priest \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-27-septembris-s-vincentii-de-paul}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-27-septembris-s-vincentii-de-paul.ant-ad-introitum}

The spirit of God is upon me, for he has anointed me and sent me to preach the good news to the poor, to heal the broken-hearted.

##### Collect {#misal.die-27-septembris-s-vincentii-de-paul.collecta}

O God, who for the relief of the poor and the formation of the clergy endowed the Priest Saint Vincent de Paul with apostolic virtues, grant, we pray, that afire with that same spirit, we may love what he loved and put into practice what he taught.
