##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-27-septembris-s-vincentii-de-paul.super-oblata}

O God, who enabled Saint Vincent to imitate what he celebrated in the divine mysteries, grant that by the power of this sacrifice we, too, may be transformed into an oblation acceptable to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-27-septembris-s-vincentii-de-paul.ant-ad-communionem}

Let them thank the Lord for his mercy, his wonders for the children of men; for he satisfies the thirsty soul, and the hungry he fills with good things.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-27-septembris-s-vincentii-de-paul.post-communionem}

Renewed by this heavenly Sacrament, O Lord, we humbly pray, that just as we are prompted by Saint Vincent's example to imitate your Son in his preaching of the Gospel to the poor, so, too, we may be sustained by his patronage.

#### September 28 — St Wenceslaus, martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-28-septembris-s-venceslai}

\[Common of , for one martyr\]

*Or:*

Saint Lawrence Ruiz and companions, martyrs \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for several martyrs\]

St Wenceslaus

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-septembris-s-venceslai.collecta}

O God, who taught the Martyr Saint Wenceslaus to place the heavenly Kingdom before an earthly one, grant through his prayers that, denying ourselves, we may hold fast to you with all our heart.

#### September 28 — St Lawrence Ruiz and companions {#misal.die-28-septembris-ss-laurentii-ruiz-et-sociorum}

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-septembris-ss-laurentii-ruiz-et-sociorum.collecta}

Grant us, we pray, Lord God, the same perseverance in serving you and our neighbour as your Martyrs Saint Lawrence Ruiz and his companions, since those persecuted for the sake of righteousness are blessed in your Kingdom.

#### September 29 — Saints Michael, Gabriel, and Raphael, archangels \[Feast\] {#misal.die-29-septembris-ss-michaelis-gabrielis-et-raphaelis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-29-septembris-ss-michaelis-gabrielis-et-raphaelis.ant-ad-introitum}

Bless the Lord, all you his angels, mighty in power, fulfilling his word, and heeding his voice.

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-septembris-ss-michaelis-gabrielis-et-raphaelis.collecta}

O God, who dispose in marvellous order ministries both angelic and human, graciously grant that our life on earth may be defended by those who watch over us as they minister perpetually to you in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-29-septembris-ss-michaelis-gabrielis-et-raphaelis.super-oblata}

We offer you a sacrifice of praise, O Lord, humbly entreating, that as these gifts are borne by the ministry of Angels into the presence of your majesty, you may receive them favourably and make them profitable for our salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-29-septembris-ss-michaelis-gabrielis-et-raphaelis.ant-ad-communionem}

I will thank you, Lord, with all my heart; in the presence of the angels I will praise you.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-29-septembris-ss-michaelis-gabrielis-et-raphaelis.post-communionem}

Having been nourished upon heavenly Bread, we beseech you humbly, O Lord, that, drawing from it new strength, under the faithful protection of your Angels, we may advance boldly along the way of salvation.

#### September 30 — St Jerome, priest and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-30-septembris-s-hieronymi}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-30-septembris-s-hieronymi.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed indeed is he who ponders the law of the Lord day and night: he will yield his fruit in due season.

##### Collect {#misal.die-30-septembris-s-hieronymi.collecta}

O God, who gave the Priest Saint Jerome a living and tender love for Sacred Scripture, grant that your people may be ever more fruitfully nourished by your Word and find in it the fount of life.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-30-septembris-s-hieronymi.super-oblata}

Grant us, O Lord, that having meditated on your Word following the example of Saint Jerome, we may more eagerly draw near to offer your majesty the sacrifice of salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-30-septembris-s-hieronymi.ant-ad-communionem}

Lord God, your words were found and I consumed them; your word became the joy and the happiness of my heart.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-30-septembris-s-hieronymi.post-communionem}

May these holy gifts we have received, O Lord, as we rejoice in celebrating Saint Jerome, stir up the hearts of your faithful so that, attentive to sacred teachings, they may understand the path they are to follow and, by following it, obtain life everlasting.

### OCTOBER

#### October 1 — Saint Thérèse of the Child Jesus, virgin and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-1-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu-infante}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-1-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu-infante.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord led her and taught her, and kept her as the apple of his eye. Like an eagle spreading its wings he took her up and bore her on his shoulders. The Lord alone was her guide.

##### Collect {#misal.die-1-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu-infante.collecta}

O God, who open your Kingdom to those who are humble and to little ones, lead us to follow trustingly in the little way of Saint Thérèse, so that, through her intercession, we may see your eternal glory revealed.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-1-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu-infante.super-oblata}

As we proclaim your wonders in Saint Thérèse, O Lord, we humbly implore your majesty, that, as her merits are pleasing to you, so, too, our dutiful service may find favour in your sight.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-1-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu-infante.ant-ad-communionem}

Thus says the Lord: unless you turn and become like children, you will not enter the kingdom of heaven.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-1-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu-infante.post-communionem}

May the Sacrament we have received, O Lord, kindle in us the force of that love with which Saint Thérèse dedicated herself to you and longed to obtain your mercy for all.

#### October 2 — The Guardian Angels \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-2-octobris-ss-angelorum-custodum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-2-octobris-ss-angelorum-custodum.ant-ad-introitum}

Angels of the Lord, bless the Lord, praise and exalt him above all forever.

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-octobris-ss-angelorum-custodum.collecta}

O God, who in your unfathomable providence are pleased to send your holy Angels to guard us, hear our supplication as we cry to you, that we may always be defended by their protection and rejoice eternally in their company.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-2-octobris-ss-angelorum-custodum.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, the offerings we bring before you as we venerate your holy Angels, and graciously grant that, under their constant protection, we may be delivered from present dangers and brought happily to life eternal.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-2-octobris-ss-angelorum-custodum.ant-ad-communionem}

In the presence of the Angels I will praise you, my God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-2-octobris-ss-angelorum-custodum.post-communionem}

As you are pleased to nourish us for eternal life with so great a Sacrament, O Lord, direct us by the ministry of Angels into the way of salvation and peace.

#### October 4 — Saint Francis of Assisi, religious \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-4-octobris-s-francisci-assisiensis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-4-octobris-s-francisci-assisiensis.ant-ad-introitum}

Francis, the man of God, left his home behind, abandoned his inheritance and became poor and penniless, but the Lord raised him up.

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-octobris-s-francisci-assisiensis.collecta}

O God, by whose gift Saint Francis was conformed to Christ in poverty and humility, grant that by walking in Francis's footsteps, we may follow your Son, and through joyful charity come to be united with you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-4-octobris-s-francisci-assisiensis.super-oblata}

As we bring you these offerings, O Lord, we pray that we may be rightly disposed for the celebration of the mystery of the Cross, which Saint Francis so ardently embraced.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-4-octobris-s-francisci-assisiensis.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are the poor in spirit, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-4-octobris-s-francisci-assisiensis.post-communionem}

Grant us, we pray, O Lord, through these holy gifts which we have received, that, imitating the charity and apostolic zeal of Saint Francis, we may experience the effects of your love and spread them everywhere for the salvation of all.

#### October 5 — Saint Faustina Kowalska, virgin \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-5-octobris-s-faustinae-kowalska}

\[Common of , or of , for a nun\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-5-octobris-s-faustinae-kowalska.collecta}

O God, who entrusted Saint Faustina with the task of proclaiming the immense riches of your infinite mercy, grant us through her intercession, that like her we may have complete trust in your goodness and carry out works of charity with a generous heart.

#### October 6 — Saint Bruno, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-6-octobris-s-brunonis}

\[Common of or of , for one pastor\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-6-octobris-s-brunonis.collecta}

O God, who called Saint Bruno to serve you in solitude, grant, through his intercession, that amid the changes of this world we may constantly have eyes for you alone.

#### October 7 — Our Lady of the Rosary \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-7-octobris-beatae-mariae-virginis-a-rosario}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-7-octobris-beatae-mariae-virginis-a-rosario.ant-ad-introitum}

Hail Mary, full of grace, the Lord is with you. Blessed are you among women and blessed is the fruit of your womb.

##### Collect {#misal.die-7-octobris-beatae-mariae-virginis-a-rosario.collecta}

Pour forth, we beseech you, O Lord, your grace into our hearts, that we, to whom the Incarnation of Christ your Son was made known by the message of an Angel, may, through the intercession of the Blessed Virgin Mary, by his Passion and Cross be brought to the glory of his Resurrection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-7-octobris-beatae-mariae-virginis-a-rosario.super-oblata}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that we may be rightly conformed to these offerings we bring, and so honour the mysteries of your Only Begotten Son, as to be made worthy of his promises.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-7-octobris-beatae-mariae-virginis-a-rosario.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, you will conceive in your womb and bear a son, and you shall name him Jesus.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-7-octobris-beatae-mariae-virginis-a-rosario.post-communionem}

We pray, O Lord our God, that just as we proclaim in this Sacrament the Death and Resurrection of your Son, so, being made partakers in his suffering, may we also merit a share in his consolation and his glory.

#### October 9 — Saint Denis {#misal.die-9-octobris-ss-dionysii-et-sociorum}

St John Henry Newman, priest \[English Calendar\]

\[ or Common of , for one pastor\]

*Or:*

Saint Denis, bishop and martyr, and Companions, martyrs \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for several martyrs\]

*Or:*

Saint John Leonardi, priest \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one pastor, or of for those who practiced works of mercy\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-octobris-ss-dionysii-et-sociorum.collecta}

O God, who sent Saint Denis and his companions to preach your glory to the nations and strengthened them for their mission with the virtue of constancy in suffering, grant, we pray, that we may imitate them in disdaining prosperity in this world and in being undaunted by any trial.

#### Saint John Leonardi {#misal.die-9-octobris-s-ioannis-leonardi}

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-octobris-s-ioannis-leonardi.collecta}

O God, giver of all good things, who through the Priest Saint John Leonardi caused the Gospel to be announced to the nations, grant, through his intercession, that the true faith may always and everywhere prosper.

#### Saint John Henry Newman {#misal.die-9-octobris-s-ioannis-henrici-newman-angliae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-9-octobris-s-ioannis-henrici-newman-angliae.ant-ad-introitum}

Bénedic, ánima mea, Dómino. Dómine Deus meus, magnificátus es veheménter! Exit homo ad opus suum et ad operatiónem suam usque ad vesprum.\
Bless the Lord, O my soul O Lord my God, you are good. Man goes forth to his work and to his labour until the evening.\
JULY \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-octobris-s-ioannis-henrici-newman-angliae.collecta}

O God, who bestowed on the Priest Saint John Henry Newman the grace to follow your kindly light and find peace in your Church, graciously grant that, through his intercession and example, we may be led out of shadows and images into the fullness of your truth.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-9-octobris-s-ioannis-henrici-newman-angliae.super-oblata}

Accept, O heavenly Father, this holy sacrifice that we offer in commemoration of Saint John Henry Newman for your praise and greater glory and for our salvation. Through Christ our Lord.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-9-octobris-s-ioannis-henrici-newman-angliae.ant-ad-communionem}

Hic est panis de cælo descéndens, dicit Dóminus; si quis ex ipso manducáverit, non moriétur in ætérnum.\
This is the bread that comes down from heaven, says the Lord; so that one may eat it and not die.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-9-octobris-s-ioannis-henrici-newman-angliae.post-communionem}

O Lord, as we rejoice to receive Christ, the living Bread, on this feast day of Saint John Henry Newman, so may our witness be made more real, never moving minds without touching hearts. Through Christ our Lord.

#### October 10 — St. Paulinus of York, Bishop \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-10-octobris-s-paulini-eboracensis-angliae}

\[Common of , for a bishop\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-10-octobris-s-paulini-eboracensis-angliae.collecta}

God, who sent the Bishop Saint Paulinus of York to build up your Church, grant, we beseech you, by his prayers and example that we may make known your wonders and tell your truth, that with him we may receive the reward you prepare for all your faithful.

#### October 11 — Saint John XXIII, pope \[In particular places\] {#misal.die-11-octobris-s-ioannis-xxiii}

\[Common of , for popes\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-11-octobris-s-ioannis-xxiii.collecta}

Almighty and eternal God, who in the Pope Saint John XXIII, gave the whole world a shining example of a good shepherd, grant that, through his intercession, we may with joy spread abroad the fullness of Christian charity.

#### October 12 — St. Wilfrid, Bishop \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-12-octobris-s-wilfridi-angliae}

\[Common of , for a bishop\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-octobris-s-wilfridi-angliae.collecta}

O God, who endowed the Bishop Saint Wilfrid with outstanding graces for the building of your Church, grant that by the help of his prayers we may, like him, weather every storm and be brought at the last into the haven of your peace.

#### October 13 — St. Edward the Confessor, King \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-13-octobris-s-eduardi-confessoris-angliae}

\[Common of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-octobris-s-eduardi-confessoris-angliae.collecta}

Lord God, who set your servant Saint Edward upon the throne of an earthly realm and inspired him with zeal for the Kingdom of Heaven, grant, we pray, that we may faithfully serve you in this life and with him inherit your eternal glory.

#### October 14 — St. Callistus I, pope and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-14-octobris-s-callisti-i}

\[Common of , for one martyr, or of , for popes\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-14-octobris-s-callisti-i.collecta}

O God, who raised up Pope Saint Callistus the First to serve the Church and attend devoutly to Christ's faithful departed, strengthen us, we pray, by his witness to the faith, so that, rescued from the slavery of corruption, we may merit an incorruptible inheritance.

#### October 15 — Saint Teresa of Jesus, virgin and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-15-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-15-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu.ant-ad-introitum}

Like the deer that yearns for running streams, so my soul is yearning for you, my God: my soul is thirsting for God, the living God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-15-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu.collecta}

O God, who through your Spirit raised up Saint Teresa of Jesus to show the Church the way to seek perfection, grant that we may always be nourished upon the food of her heavenly teaching and fired with longing for true holiness.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-15-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu.super-oblata}

May our offerings, O Lord, be acceptable to your majesty, to whom the devoted service of Saint Teresa was pleasing in such great measure.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-15-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu.ant-ad-communionem}

I will sing forever of your mercies, O Lord; through all ages my mouth will proclaim your fidelity.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-15-octobris-s-teresiae-a-iesu.post-communionem}

Grant, O Lord our God, that your obedient family, whom you have fed with the Bread of heaven, may follow the example of Saint Teresa and rejoice to sing of your mercies for all eternity.

#### October 16 — Saint Hedwig {#misal.die-16-octobris-s-hedvigis}

Saint Hedwig, religious \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for religious or for \]

*Or:*

Saint Margaret Mary Alacoque, virgin \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one virgin\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-octobris-s-hedvigis.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that the revered intercession of Saint Hedwig may bring us heavenly aid, just as her wonderful life is an example of humility for all.

#### Saint Margaret Mary Alacoque {#misal.die-16-octobris-s-margaritae-mariae-alacoque}

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-octobris-s-margaritae-mariae-alacoque.collecta}

Pour out on us, we pray, O Lord, the spirit with which you so remarkably endowed Saint Margaret Mary, so that we may come to know that love of Christ which surpasses all understanding and be utterly filled with your fullness.

#### October 17 — Saint Ignatius of Antioch, bishop and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-17-octobris-s-ignatii-antiocheni}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-17-octobris-s-ignatii-antiocheni.ant-ad-introitum}

I am crucified with Christ, yet I live; no longer I, but Christ lives in me. I live by faith in the Son of God, who has loved me and given himself up for me.

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-octobris-s-ignatii-antiocheni.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who adorn the sacred body of your Church with the confessions of holy Martyrs, grant, we pray, that, just as the glorious passion of Saint Ignatius of Antioch which we celebrate today, brought him eternal splendour, so may it be for us unending protection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-17-octobris-s-ignatii-antiocheni.super-oblata}

May this oblation and our homage be pleasing to you, O Lord, just as you accepted Saint Ignatius, the wheat of Christ, made pure bread through his martyrdom and passion.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-17-octobris-s-ignatii-antiocheni.ant-ad-communionem}

I am the wheat of Christ to be ground by the teeth of beasts, that I may be found to be pure bread.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-17-octobris-s-ignatii-antiocheni.post-communionem}

May the heavenly Bread we have received on Saint Ignatius's day, O Lord, renew us, we pray, and make us Christians in name and in deed.

#### October 18 — Saint Luke, evangelist \[Feast\] {#misal.die-18-octobris-s-lucae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-18-octobris-s-lucae.ant-ad-introitum}

How beautiful upon the mountains are the feet of him who brings glad tidings of peace, bearing good news, announcing salvation.

##### Collect {#misal.die-18-octobris-s-lucae.collecta}

Lord God, who chose Saint Luke to reveal by his preaching and writings the mystery of your love for the poor, grant that those who already glory in your Name may persevere as one heart and one soul, and that all nations may merit to see your salvation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-18-octobris-s-lucae.super-oblata}

Grant through your heavenly gifts that we may serve you in freedom of heart, we pray, O Lord, so that the offerings we make on the feast day of Saint Luke may bring us healing and give us glory.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-18-octobris-s-lucae.ant-ad-communionem}

The Lord sent out disciples to proclaim throughout the towns: The kingdom of God is at hand for you.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-18-octobris-s-lucae.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that what we have received from your holy altar may sanctify us and make us strong in the faith of the Gospel that Saint Luke proclaimed.

#### October 19 — Saint John de Brébeuf and Saint Isaac Jogues, priests and martyrs \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-19-octobris-ss-ioannis-de-brebeuf-et-isaac-jogues-et-sociorum}

\[Common of \]

*Or:*

Saint Paul of the Cross, priest \[Optional Memorial\]

\[ Mass \]

Saint John de Brébeuf and Saint Isaac Jogues

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-octobris-ss-ioannis-de-brebeuf-et-isaac-jogues-et-sociorum.collecta}

O God, who chose to manifest the blessed hope of your eternal kingdom by the toil of Saints John de Brebeuf, Isaac Jogues and their companions and by the shedding of their blood, grant graciously that, through their intercession, the faith of Christians may be strengthened day by day.

#### Saint Paul of the Cross {#misal.die-19-octobris-s-pauli-a-cruce}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-19-octobris-s-pauli-a-cruce.ant-ad-introitum}

I resolved to know nothing while I was with you except for Jesus Christ, and him crucified.

##### Collect {#misal.die-19-octobris-s-pauli-a-cruce.collecta}

May the Priest Saint Paul whose only love was the Cross, obtain for us your grace, O Lord, so that, urged on more strongly by his example, we may each embrace our own cross with courage.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-19-octobris-s-pauli-a-cruce.super-oblata}

Look, upon the sacrificial gifts we offer, almighty God, in commemoration of Saint Paul of the Cross and grant that we who celebrate the mysteries of the Lord's Passion may imitate what we now do.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-19-octobris-s-pauli-a-cruce.ant-ad-communionem}

We proclaim Christ crucified; Christ, the power of God and the wisdom of God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-19-octobris-s-pauli-a-cruce.post-communionem}

O God, who in Saint Paul have wonderfully made known the mystery of the Cross, graciously grant that, drawing strength from this sacrifice, we may cling faithfully to Christ and labour in the Church for the salvation of all.

#### October 22 — Saint John Paul II, pope \[English calendar\] {#misal.die-22-octobris-s-ioannis-pauli-ii}

\[Common of , for popes\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-octobris-s-ioannis-pauli-ii.collecta}

O God, who are rich in mercy and who willed that the blessed John Paul the Second should preside as Pope over your universal Church, grant, we pray, that instructed by his teaching, we may open our hearts to the saving grace of Christ, the sole Redeemer of mankind.

#### October 23 — St. John Capistrano, priest \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-23-octobris-s-ioannis-de-capestrano}

\[Common of , for missionaries, or of , for religious\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-octobris-s-ioannis-de-capestrano.collecta}

O God, who raised up Saint John of Capistrano to comfort your faithful people in tribulation, place us, we pray, under your safe protection and keep your Church in everlasting peace.

#### October 24 — St. Anthony Claret, bishop \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-24-octobris-s-antonii-mariae-claret}

\[Common of , for a bishop or of , for missionaries\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-octobris-s-antonii-mariae-claret.collecta}

O God, who for the evangelization of peoples strengthened the Bishop Saint Anthony Mary Claret with admirable charity and long-suffering, grant, through his intercession, that, seeking the things that are yours, we may earnestly devote ourselves to winning our brothers and sisters for Christ.

#### October 26 — Saint Chad and Saint Cedd, bishops \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-26-octobris-ss-ceaddae-et-ceddi-angliae}

\[Common of , for several pastors\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-26-octobris-ss-ceaddae-et-ceddi-angliae.collecta}

O God, who called your servants the Bishops Saints Chad and Cedd from among the English who first turned to Christ your Son and made them ministers of grace to their own people, grant, we pray, at their intercession, that we may imitate them in a peaceful and humble spirit and a prayerful life, and so commend to others the faith which we ourselves profess.

#### October 28 — Saints Simon and Jude, Apostles \[Feast\] {#misal.die-28-octobris-ss-simonis-et-iudae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-28-octobris-ss-simonis-et-iudae.ant-ad-introitum}

These are the holy men whom the Lord chose in his own perfect love; to them he gave eternal glory.

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-octobris-ss-simonis-et-iudae.collecta}

O God, who by the blessed Apostles have brought us to acknowledge your Name, grant graciously, through the intercession of Saints Simon and Jude, that the Church may constantly grow by increase of the people who believe in you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-28-octobris-ss-simonis-et-iudae.super-oblata}

As we venerate the perpetual glory of the holy Apostles Simon and Jude, O Lord, we ask that you receive our prayers and lead us to worthy celebration of the sacred mysteries.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-28-octobris-ss-simonis-et-iudae.ant-ad-communionem}

Whoever loves me will keep my word, says the Lord; and my Father will love him, and we will come to him, and make our home with him.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-28-octobris-ss-simonis-et-iudae.post-communionem}

Having received this Sacrament, O Lord, we humbly implore you in the Holy Spirit, that what we do to honour the glorious passion of the Apostles Simon and Jude, may keep us ever in your love.

### NOVEMBER

#### November 1 — All Saint's Day \[Solemnity\] {#misal.die-1-novembris-omnium-sanctorum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-1-novembris-omnium-sanctorum.ant-ad-introitum}

Let us all rejoice in the Lord, as we celebrate the feast day in honour of all the Saints, at whose festival the Angels rejoice and praise the Son of God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-1-novembris-omnium-sanctorum.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, by whose gift we venerate in one celebration the merits of all the Saints, bestow on us, we pray, through the prayers of so many intercessors, an abundance of the reconciliation with you for which we earnestly long.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-1-novembris-omnium-sanctorum.super-oblata}

May these offerings we bring in honour of all the Saints be pleasing to you, O Lord, and grant that just as we believe the Saints to be already assured of immortality, so we may experience their concern for our salvation.

##### Preface {#misal.die-1-novembris-omnium-sanctorum.praefatio}

Jerusalem, our mother\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God.\
For today by your gift we celebrate the festival of your city, the heavenly Jerusalem, our mother, where the great array of our brothers and sisters already gives you eternal praise. Towards her, we eagerly hasten, as pilgrims advancing by faith, rejoicing in the glory endowed upon those exalted members of the Church through whom you give us in our frailty, strength and good example.\
And so, we glorify you with the multitude of Saints and Angels, as with one voice of praise we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-1-novembris-omnium-sanctorum.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are the clean of heart, for they shall see God. Blessed are the peacemakers, for they shall be called children of God. Blessed are they who are persecuted for the sake of righteousness, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-1-novembris-omnium-sanctorum.post-communionem}

As we adore you, O God, who alone are holy and wonderful in all your Saints, we implore your grace, so that coming to perfect holiness in the fullness of your love, we may pass from this pilgrim table to the banquet of our heavenly homeland.

#### November 2 — Commemoration of All the Faithful Departed

#### November 2 — Commemoration of All the Faithful Departed — FIRST MASS {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-1}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-1.ant-ad-introitum}

Just as Jesus died and has risen again, so through Jesus God will bring with him those who have fallen asleep; and as in Adam all die, so also in Christ will all be brought to life.

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-1.collecta}

Listen kindly to our prayers, O Lord, and as our faith in your Son, raised from the dead, is deepened, so may our hope of resurrection for your departed servants also find new strength.

*or readings for*

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-1.super-oblata}

Look favourably on our offerings, O Lord, so that your departed servants may be taken up into glory with your Son, in whose great mystery of love we are all united.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-1.ant-ad-communionem}

I am the resurrection and the life, says the Lord. Whoever believes in me, even though he dies, will live, and everyone who lives and believes in me will not die for ever.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-1.post-communionem}

Grant we pray, O Lord, that your departed servants, for whom we have celebrated this paschal Sacrament, may pass over to a dwelling place of light and peace.\
\[Responsorium for the dead\]

#### November 2 — Commemoration of All the Faithful Departed — SECOND MASS {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-2}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-2.ant-ad-introitum}

Eternal rest grant unto them, O Lord, and let perpetual light shine upon them.

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-2.collecta}

O God, glory of the faithful and life of the just, by the Death and Resurrection of whose Son we have been redeemed, look mercifully on your departed servants, that just as they professed the mystery of our resurrection, they may merit the joys of eternal happiness.

*or readings for*

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-2.super-oblata}

Almighty and merciful God, by means of these sacrificial gifts wash away, we pray, in the Blood of Christ, the sins of your departed servants, so that you may purify unceasingly by your merciful forgiveness those you once cleansed in the waters of Baptism.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-2.ant-ad-communionem}

Let perpetual light shine upon them, O Lord, with your Saints for ever, for you are merciful.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-2.post-communionem}

Having received the Sacrament of your Only Begotten Son, who was sacrificed for us and rose in glory, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, for your departed servants, that cleansed by the paschal mysteries, they may glory in the gift of the resurrection to come.\
\[Responsorium for the dead\]

#### November 2 — Commemoration of All the Faithful Departed — THIRD MASS {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-3}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-3.ant-ad-introitum}

God, who raised Jesus from the dead, will give life also to your mortal bodies, through his Spirit that dwells in you.

##### Collect {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-3.collecta}

O God, who willed that your Only Begotten Son, having conquered death, should pass over into the realm of heaven, grant, we pray, to your departed servants that, with the mortality of this life overcome, they may gaze eternally on you, their Creator and Redeemer.

*or readings for*

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-3.super-oblata}

Receive, Lord, in your kindness, the sacrificial offering we make for all your servants who sleep in Christ, that, set free from the bonds of death by this singular sacrifice, they may merit eternal life.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-3.ant-ad-communionem}

We await a saviour, the Lord Jesus Christ, who will change our mortal bodies, to conform with his glorified body.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-2-novembris-in-commemoratione-omnium-fidelium-defunctorum-3.post-communionem}

Through these sacrificial gifts which we have received, bestow on your departed servants your great mercy and to those you endowed with the grace of Baptism, grant also the fullness of eternal joy.\
\[Responsorium for the dead\]

#### November 3 — Saint Martin de Porres {#misal.die-3-novembris-s-martini-de-porres}

Saint Martin de Porres, religious \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for religious\]

*Or:*

Saint Winifrede, Virgin \[English Calendar\]

\[Common of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-novembris-s-martini-de-porres.collecta}

O God, who led Saint Martin de Porres by the path of humility to heavenly glory, grant that we may so follow his radiant example in this life as to merit to be exalted with him in heaven.

#### Saint Winifrede {#misal.die-3-novembris-s-winefridae-angliae}

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-novembris-s-winefridae-angliae.collecta}

God of steadfast love, who graciously revealed the beauty of virginity when you willed that your Son should be born of the Blessed Virgin Mary, grant, we pray, that, as we honour the memory of your holy virgin Winifrede, we may, at her intercession, enjoy your gifts of healing.

#### November 4 — Saint Charles Borromeo, bishop \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-4-novembris-s-caroli-borromeo}

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-novembris-s-caroli-borromeo.collecta}

Preserve in the midst of your people, we ask, O Lord, the spirit with which you filled the Bishop Saint Charles Borromeo, that your Church may be constantly renewed and, by conforming herself to the likeness of Christ, may show his face to the world.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-4-novembris-s-caroli-borromeo.super-oblata}

Look, O Lord, upon the offering placed on your altar for the commemoration of Saint Charles, and grant by the power of this sacrifice that as you made him an attentive pastor, outstanding in the merit of his virtues, so may you make us abound in good fruit by our works.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-4-novembris-s-caroli-borromeo.post-communionem}

May the sacred mysteries of which we have partaken, O Lord, we pray, give us that determination which made Saint Charles faithful in ministry and fervent in charity.

#### November 7 — Saint Willibrord, bishop \[English Calendar\] {#misal.die-7-novembris-s-willibrordi-angliae}

\[Common of , for a bishop\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-7-novembris-s-willibrordi-angliae.collecta}

God, the saviour of all nations, you sent the Bishop Saint Willibrord from this land to proclaim the Gospel to many peoples and to confirm them in the faith; grant us, at his intercession, to witness to your steadfast love by word and deed, so that your Church may increase and grow strong in holiness.

#### November 8 — Saint Severinus, Martyr

November 8

Saint Severinus, Martyr

\[Common of \]

#### November 9 — The Dedication of the Lateran Basilica \[Feast\] {#misal.die-9-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicae-lateranensis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-9-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicae-lateranensis.ant-ad-introitum}

I saw the holy city, a new Jerusalem, coming down out of heaven from God, prepared like a bride adorned for her husband.

*Or:*

Behold God's dwelling with the human race. He will dwell with them and they will be his people, and God himself with them will be their God.

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicae-lateranensis.collecta}

O God, who from living and chosen stones prepare an eternal dwelling for your majesty, increase in your Church the grace you have bestowed, so that by unceasing growth your faithful people may build up the heavenly Jerusalem.

*Or:*

O God, who were pleased to call your Church the Bride, grant that the people that serves your name may revere you, love you and follow you, and may be led by you to attain your promises in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-9-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicae-lateranensis.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, the gift that is offered here, and grant that by it those who seek your favour may receive in this place the strength of the Sacraments and an answer to their prayers.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-9-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicae-lateranensis.ant-ad-communionem}

Be built up like living stones, into a spiritual house, a holy priesthood.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-9-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicae-lateranensis.post-communionem}

O God, who chose to foreshadow for us the heavenly Jerusalem through the sign of your Church on earth, grant, we pray, that by our partaking of this Sacrament we may be made the temple of your grace and we may enter the dwelling place of your glory.

#### November 10 — Saint Leo the Great, pope and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-10-novembris-s-leonis-magni}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-10-novembris-s-leonis-magni.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord established for him a covenant of peace, and made him the prince, that he might have the dignity of the priesthood forever.

##### Collect {#misal.die-10-novembris-s-leonis-magni.collecta}

O God, who never allow the gates of hell to prevail against your Church, firmly founded on the apostolic rock, grant her, we pray, that through the intercession of Pope Saint Leo, she may stand firm in your truth, and know the protection of lasting peace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-10-novembris-s-leonis-magni.super-oblata}

Through the offerings made here, we pray, O Lord, graciously shed light on your Church, so that your flock may everywhere prosper and that, under your governance, the shepherds may become pleasing to your Name.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-10-novembris-s-leonis-magni.ant-ad-communionem}

Peter said to Jesus: You are the Christ, the Son of the living God. And Jesus replied: You are Peter, and upon this rock I will build my Church.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-10-novembris-s-leonis-magni.post-communionem}

Be pleased, O Lord, we pray, to govern your Church whom you have nourished by this holy meal, so that, firmly directed, she may enjoy ever greater freedom and persevere in integrity of religion.

#### November 11 — Saint Martin of Tours, bishop \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-11-novembris-s-martini-turonensis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-11-novembris-s-martini-turonensis.ant-ad-introitum}

I shall raise up for myself a faithful priest who will act in accord with my heart and my mind, says the Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.die-11-novembris-s-martini-turonensis.collecta}

O God, who are glorified in the Bishop Saint Martin, by both by his life and his death, make new, we pray, the wonders of your grace in our hearts, that neither death nor life may separate us from your love.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-11-novembris-s-martini-turonensis.super-oblata}

Sanctify these offerings, we pray, Lord God, which we joyfully present in honour of Saint Martin, so that through them our life may always be directed whether in tribulation or in prosperity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-11-novembris-s-martini-turonensis.ant-ad-communionem}

Amen, I say to you: Whatever you did for one of the least of my brethren, you did for me, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-11-novembris-s-martini-turonensis.post-communionem}

Grant to us who have been restored by this Sacrament of unity, O Lord, perfect harmony in all things with your will, that, just as Saint Martin submitted himself entirely to you, so we, too, may glory in being truly yours.

#### November 12 — Saint Josaphat, bishop and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-12-novembris-s-iosaphat}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-12-novembris-s-iosaphat.ant-ad-introitum}

Because of the Lord's covenant and the ancestral laws, the Saints of God persevered in loving brotherhood, for there was always one spirit in them, and one faith.

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-novembris-s-iosaphat.collecta}

Stir up in your Church, we pray, O Lord, the Spirit that filled Saint Josaphat as he laid down his life for the sheep, so that through his intercession we, too, may be strengthened by the same Spirit and not be afraid to lay down our life for others.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-12-novembris-s-iosaphat.super-oblata}

Most merciful God, pour out your blessing upon these offerings and confirm us in the faith that Saint Josaphat professed by the shedding of his blood.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-12-novembris-s-iosaphat.ant-ad-communionem}

Whoever loses his life for my sake, will find it in eternity, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-12-novembris-s-iosaphat.post-communionem}

May this heavenly table, O Lord, bestow on us a spirit of fortitude and peace, so that, following Saint Josaphat's example, we may willingly spend our lives working for the honour and unity of the Church.

#### November 15 — Saint Albert the Great, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-15-novembris-s-alberti-magni}

\[Common of , for bishops, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-15-novembris-s-alberti-magni.collecta}

O God, who made the Bishop Saint Albert great by his joining of human wisdom to divine faith, grant, we pray, that we may so adhere to the truths he taught, that through progress in learning we may come to a deeper knowledge and love of you.

#### November 16 — Saint Margaret of Scotland {#misal.die-16-novembris-s-margaritae-scotiae}

Saint Margaret of Scotland \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for those who practised works of mercy\]

*Or:*

Saint Gertrude, virgin \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , or of for a nun\]

*Or:*

Saint Edmund of Abingdon, bishop \[English Calendar\]

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-novembris-s-margaritae-scotiae.collecta}

O God, who made Saint Margaret of Scotland wonderful in her outstanding charity towards the poor, grant that, through her intercession and example, we may reflect among all humanity the image of your divine goodness.

#### Saint Gertrude {#misal.die-16-novembris-s-gertrudis}

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-novembris-s-gertrudis.collecta}

O God, who prepared a delightful dwelling for yourself in the heart of the Virgin Saint Gertrude, graciously bring light, through her intercession, to the darkness of our hearts that we may joyfully experience you present and at work within us.

#### Saint Edmund of Abingdon {#misal.die-16-novembris-s-edmundi-de-abingdon-angliae}

##### Collect {#misal.die-16-novembris-s-edmundi-de-abingdon-angliae.collecta}

O God, by whose grace the Bishop Saint Edmund of Abingdon was vigilant over integrity in public office and discipline in religious life, grant, we pray, through his intercession, that same spirit of constancy to your Church, that she may be fearless in promoting justice.

#### November 17 — Saint Elizabeth of Hungary {#misal.die-17-novembris-s-elisabeth-hungariae}

Saint Elizabeth of Hungary, religious \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for those who practised works of mercy\]

*Or:*

Saint Hilda, Abbess \[English Calendar\]

\[Common of for a nun\]

*Or:*

Saint Hugh of Lincoln, Bishop \[English Calendar\]

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-novembris-s-elisabeth-hungariae.collecta}

O God, by whose gift Saint Elizabeth of Hungary recognized and revered Christ in the poor, grant, through her intercession, that we may serve with unfailing charity the needy and those afflicted.

#### Saint Hilda {#misal.die-17-novembris-s-hildae-angliae}

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-novembris-s-hildae-angliae.collecta}

O God, who made Saint Hilda radiant with holiness and by her guidance gave learning, growth and unity to your Church, grant, by her example and prayers, the grace to proclaim your Gospel in our day and to sing your unceasing praise.

#### Saint Hugh of Lincoln {#misal.die-17-novembris-s-hugonis-lincolniensis-angliae}

##### Collect {#misal.die-17-novembris-s-hugonis-lincolniensis-angliae.collecta}

O God, who gave the Bishop Saint Hugh of Lincoln the grace of relying on you alone and endowed him with wise and cheerful boldness to commend to earthly rulers the merits of a holy life, grant us, we pray, that, with him as our advocate, we may have good cause for the hope that is in us.

#### November 18 — Dedication of the Basilicas of Saints Peter and Paul \[Optional memorial\] {#misal.die-18-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicarum-ss-petri-et-pauli}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-18-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicarum-ss-petri-et-pauli.ant-ad-introitum}

You will make them princes over all the earth; they will remember your name through all generations. Thus the peoples will praise you forever, from age to age.

##### Collect {#misal.die-18-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicarum-ss-petri-et-pauli.collecta}

Defend your Church, O Lord, by the protection of the holy Apostles, that as she received from them the beginnings of her divine knowledge of things so through them she may receive, even to the end of the ages, an increase in heavenly grace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-18-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicarum-ss-petri-et-pauli.super-oblata}

As we bring you this offering of our service, we beg your mercy, Lord, that the truth handed down to us by the ministry of the Apostles Peter and Paul may endure undefiled in our hearts.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-18-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicarum-ss-petri-et-pauli.ant-ad-communionem}

O Lord, you have the words of eternal life, and we have come to believe that you are the Christ, the Son of God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-18-novembris-in-dedicatione-basilicarum-ss-petri-et-pauli.post-communionem}

May your people, we pray, O Lord, nourished by the Bread of heaven, rejoice in commemorating the Apostles Peter and Paul, for it is through your gift that we are governed under their patronage.

#### November 21 — The Presentation of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-21-novembris-in-praesentatione-beatae-mariae-virginis}

\[Common of the \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-novembris-in-praesentatione-beatae-mariae-virginis.collecta}

As we venerate the glorious memory of the most holy Virgin Mary, grant, we pray, O Lord, through her intercession, that we, too, may merit to receive of the fullness of your grace.

#### November 22 — Saint Cecilia, virgin and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-22-novembris-s-caeciliae}

\[Common of , for a virgin martyr, or of , for one virgin\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-22-novembris-s-caeciliae.collecta}

O God, who gladden us each year with the feast day of Saint Cecilia, grant, we pray, that what has been devoutly handed down concerning this handmaid of yours may offer us examples to imitate and proclaim the wonders worked in his servants by Christ your Son.

#### November 23 — Saint Clement I {#misal.die-23-novembris-s-clementis-i}

Saint Clement I, pope and martyr \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for one martyr, or of , for popes\]

*Or:*

Saint Columban, abbot \[Optional Memorial\]

\[Common of , for missionaries, or of an \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-novembris-s-clementis-i.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who are wonderful in the virtue of all your Saints, grant us joy in the yearly commemoration of Saint Clement, who, as a Martyr and High Priest of your Son, bore out by his witness what he celebrated in mystery, and confirmed by example what he preached with his lips.

#### Saint Columban {#misal.die-23-novembris-s-columbani}

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-novembris-s-columbani.collecta}

O God, who in Saint Columban wonderfully joined the work of evangelization to zeal for the monastic life, grant, we pray, that, through his intercession and example, we may strive to seek you above all things and bring increase to your faithful people.

#### November 24 — Saint Andrew Dung-Lac, priest and martyr, and his companions, martyrs \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-24-novembris-ss-andreae-dung-la-c-et-sociorum}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-24-novembris-ss-andreae-dung-la-c-et-sociorum.ant-ad-introitum}

May we never boast, except in the Cross of our Lord Jesus Christ. For the word of the Cross is the power of God to us who have been saved.

##### Collect {#misal.die-24-novembris-ss-andreae-dung-la-c-et-sociorum.collecta}

O God, source and origin of all fatherhood, who kept the Martyrs Saint Andrew Dang-Lac and his companions faithful to the Cross of your Son even to the shedding of their blood, grant, through their intercession that, spreading your love among our brothers and sisters, we may be your children both in name and in truth.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-24-novembris-ss-andreae-dung-la-c-et-sociorum.super-oblata}

Receive, O holy Father, the offerings we bring as we venerate the passion of the holy Martyrs, so that amid the trials of this life we may always be found faithful and may offer ourselves to you as an acceptable sacrifice.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-24-novembris-ss-andreae-dung-la-c-et-sociorum.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are they who are persecuted for the sake of righteousness, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-24-novembris-ss-andreae-dung-la-c-et-sociorum.post-communionem}

Renewed by the one Bread as we commemorate the holy Martyrs, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, abiding as one in your love, we may merit by endurance an eternal prize.

#### November 25 — Saint Catherine of Alexandria, virgin and martyr \[Optional Memorial\] {#misal.die-25-novembris-s-catharinae-alexandrinae}

\[Common of , for a virgin martyr, or of , for one virgin\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-25-novembris-s-catharinae-alexandrinae.collecta}

Almighty everlasting God, who gave Saint Catherine of Alexandria to your people as a Virgin and an invincible Martyr, grant that, through her intercession, we may be strengthened in faith and constancy and spend ourselves without reserve for the unity of the Church.

#### November 30 — Saint Andrew, apostle \[Feast\] {#misal.die-30-novembris-s-andreae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-30-novembris-s-andreae.ant-ad-introitum}

Beside the Sea of Galilee, the Lord saw two brothers, Peter and Andrew, and he said to them: Come after me and I will make you fishers of men.

##### Collect {#misal.die-30-novembris-s-andreae.collecta}

We humbly implore your majesty, O Lord, that just as the blessed Apostle Andrew was for your Church a preacher and pastor, so he may be for us a constant intercessor before you.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-30-novembris-s-andreae.super-oblata}

Grant us, almighty God, that through these offerings, which we bring on the feast day of Saint Andrew, we may please you by what we have brought and be given life by what you have accepted.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-30-novembris-s-andreae.ant-ad-communionem}

Andrew told his brother Simon: We have found the Messiah, the Christ, and he brought him to Jesus.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-30-novembris-s-andreae.post-communionem}

May communion in your Sacrament strengthen us, O Lord, so that, by the example of the blessed Apostle Andrew, we who carry in our body the Death of Christ may merit to live with him in glory.

### DECEMBER

#### December 3 — Saint Francis Xavier, priest \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-3-decembris-s-francisci-xavier}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-3-decembris-s-francisci-xavier.ant-ad-introitum}

I will praise you, Lord, among the nations; I will tell of your name to my kin.

##### Collect {#misal.die-3-decembris-s-francisci-xavier.collecta}

O God, who through the preaching of Saint Francis Xavier won many peoples to yourself, grant that the hearts of the faithful may burn with the same zeal for the faith, and that Holy Church may everywhere rejoice in an abundance of offspring.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-3-decembris-s-francisci-xavier.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, these offerings we bring you in commemoration of Saint Francis Xavier, and grant that, as he journeyed to distant lands out of longing for the salvation of souls, so we, too, bearing effective witness to the Gospel, may with our brothers and sisters eagerly hasten towards you.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-3-decembris-s-francisci-xavier.ant-ad-communionem}

What I say to you in the darkness speak in the light, says the Lord; what you hear whispered, proclaim on the housetops.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-3-decembris-s-francisci-xavier.post-communionem}

May your mysteries, O God, kindle in us that fire of charity with which Saint Francis Xavier burned for the salvation of souls, so that, walking ever more worthily in our vocation, we may obtain with him the reward you promise to those who labour well in your harvest.

#### December 4 — Saint John Damascene, priest and doctor of the Church \[Optional memorial\] {#misal.die-4-decembris-s-ioannis-damasceni}

\[Common of , for one pastor, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-4-decembris-s-ioannis-damasceni.collecta}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that we may be helped by the prayers of the Priest Saint John Damascene, so that the true faith, which he excelled in teaching, may always be our light and our strength.

#### December 6 — Saint Nicholas, bishop \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-6-decembris-s-nicolai}

\[Common of , for bishops\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-6-decembris-s-nicolai.collecta}

We humbly implore your mercy, Lord: protect us in all dangers through the prayers of the Bishop Saint Nicholas, that the way of salvation may lie open before us.

#### December 7 — Saint Ambrose, bishop and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-7-decembris-s-ambrosii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-7-decembris-s-ambrosii.ant-ad-introitum}

In the midst of the Church he opened his mouth, and the Lord filled him with the spirit of wisdom and understanding and clothed him in a robe of glory.

##### Collect {#misal.die-7-decembris-s-ambrosii.collecta}

O God, who made the Bishop Saint Ambrose a teacher of the Catholic faith and a model of apostolic courage, raise up in your Church men after your own heart to govern her with courage and wisdom.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-7-decembris-s-ambrosii.super-oblata}

As we celebrate the divine mysteries, O Lord, we pray, may the Holy Spirit fill us with that light of faith by which he constantly enlightened Saint Ambrose for the spreading of your glory.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-7-decembris-s-ambrosii.ant-ad-communionem}

He who ponders the law of the Lord day and night will yield fruit in due season.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-7-decembris-s-ambrosii.post-communionem}

Lead those who have been strengthened by the power of this Sacrament, O Lord, so to profit from the teaching of Saint Ambrose that, hastening fearlessly along your paths, we may be prepared for the delights of the eternal banquet.

#### December 8 — The Immaculate Conception of the Blessed Virgin Mary \[Solemnity\] {#misal.die-8-decembris-in-conceptione-immaculata-beatae-mariae-virginis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-8-decembris-in-conceptione-immaculata-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-introitum}

I rejoice heartily in the Lord, in my God is the joy of my soul; for he has clothed me with a robe of salvation, and wrapped me in a mantle of justice, like a bride adorned with her jewels.

##### Collect {#misal.die-8-decembris-in-conceptione-immaculata-beatae-mariae-virginis.collecta}

O God, who by the Immaculate Conception of the Blessed Virgin prepared a worthy dwelling for your Son, grant, we pray, that, as you preserved her from every stain by virtue of the Death of your Son, which you foresaw, so, through her intercession, we, too, may be cleansed and admitted to your presence.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-8-decembris-in-conceptione-immaculata-beatae-mariae-virginis.super-oblata}

Graciously accept the saving sacrifice which we offer you, O Lord, on the Solemnity of the Immaculate Conception of the Blessed Virgin Mary, and grant that, as we profess her on account of your prevenient grace to be untouched by any stain of sin, so, through her intercession, we may be delivered from all our faults.

##### Preface {#misal.die-8-decembris-in-conceptione-immaculata-beatae-mariae-virginis.praefatio}

The mystery of Mary and the Church\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God.\
For you preserved the Most Blessed Virgin Mary from all stain of original sin, so that in her, endowed with the rich fullness of your grace, you might prepare a worthy Mother for your Son, and signify the beginning of the Church, his beautiful Bride without spot or wrinkle.\
She, the most pure Virgin, was to bring forth a Son, the innocent Lamb who would wipe away our offences; you placed her above all others to be for your people an advocate of grace and a model of holiness.\
And so, in company with the choirs of Angels, we praise you, and with joy we proclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-8-decembris-in-conceptione-immaculata-beatae-mariae-virginis.ant-ad-communionem}

Glorious things are spoken of you, O Mary, because from you arose the sun of justice, Christ our God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-8-decembris-in-conceptione-immaculata-beatae-mariae-virginis.post-communionem}

May the Sacrament we have received, O Lord our God, heal in us the wounds of that fault from which in a singular way you preserved Blessed Mary in her Immaculate Conception.

#### December 9 — Saint John Diego Cuauhtlatoatzin, hermit \[Optional memorial\] {#misal.die-9-decembris-s-ioannis-didaci-cuauhtlatoatzin}

\[Common of , for one saint\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-9-decembris-s-ioannis-didaci-cuauhtlatoatzin.collecta}

O God, who by means of Saint John Diego showed the love of the most holy Virgin Mary for your people, grant, through his intercession, that, by following the counsels our Mother gave at Guadalupe, we may be ever constant in fulfilling your will.

#### December 10 — Our Lady of Loreto \[Optional memorial\] {#misal.die-10-decembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-loreto}

\[Common of the , Advent season\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-10-decembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-loreto.collecta}

O God, who, fulfilling the promise made to our Fathers, chose the Blessed Virgin Mary to become the mother of the Saviour, grant that we may follow her example, for her humility was pleasing to you and her obedience profitable to us.

#### December 11 — Saint Damasus I, pope \[Optional memorial\] {#misal.die-11-decembris-s-damasi-i}

\[Common of , for popes\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-11-decembris-s-damasi-i.collecta}

Grant, we pray, O Lord, that we may constantly exalt the merits of your Martyrs, whom Pope Saint Damasus so venerated and loved.

#### December 12 — Our Lady of Guadalupe {#misal.die-12-decembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-guadalupe}

\[Common of the , Advent season\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-12-decembris-beatae-mariae-virginis-de-guadalupe.collecta}

O God, Father of mercies, who placed your people under the singular protection of your Son's most holy Mother, grant that all who invoke the Blessed Virgin of Guadalupe may seek with ever more lively faith the progress of people in the ways of justice and of peace.

#### December 13 — Saint Lucy, virgin and martyr \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-13-decembris-s-luciae}

\[Common of for a virgin martyr or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-13-decembris-s-luciae.collecta}

May the glorious intercession of the Virgin and Martyr Saint Lucy give us new heart, we pray, O Lord, so that we may celebrate her heavenly birthday in this present age and so behold things eternal.

#### December 14 — Saint John of the Cross, priest and doctor of the Church \[Memorial\] {#misal.die-14-decembris-s-ioannis-a-cruce}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-14-decembris-s-ioannis-a-cruce.ant-ad-introitum}

May I never boast, except in the cross of our Lord Jesus Christ, through which the world has been crucified to me, and I to the world.

##### Collect {#misal.die-14-decembris-s-ioannis-a-cruce.collecta}

O God, who gave the Priest Saint John an outstanding love of perfect self-denial and of the Cross, grant that by imitating him closely at all times we may come to contemplate for eternity your glory.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-14-decembris-s-ioannis-a-cruce.super-oblata}

Look upon the sacrificial gifts we offer, almighty God, in commemoration of Saint John of the Cross and grant that we who celebrate the mysteries of the Lord's Passion may imitate what we now do.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-14-decembris-s-ioannis-a-cruce.ant-ad-communionem}

Whoever wishes to come after me must deny himself, take up his cross, and follow me, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-14-decembris-s-ioannis-a-cruce.post-communionem}

O God, who in Saint John have wonderfully made known the mystery of the Cross, graciously grant that, drawing strength from this sacrifice, we may cling faithfully to Christ and labour in the Church for the salvation of all.

### December 17

### December 18

### December 19

### December 20

#### December 21 — Saint Peter Canisius, priest and doctor of the Church {#misal.die-21-decembris-s-petri-canisii}

*Or:*

\[Common of , for one pastor, or of \]

##### Collect {#misal.die-21-decembris-s-petri-canisii.collecta}

O God, who for the defence of the Catholic faith made the Priest Saint Peter Canisius strong in virtue and learning, grant, through his intercession, that those who seek the truth may joyfully find you, their God, and that your faithful people may persevere in confessing you.

### December 22

#### December 23 — Saint John of Kanty, priest {#misal.die-23-decembris-s-ioannis-de-keo-ty}

*Or:*

\[Common of , or of for those who practiced works of mercy\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-23-decembris-s-ioannis-de-keo-ty.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, by the example of the Priest Saint John of Kanty, we may advance in the knowledge of holy things and, by showing compassion to all, may gain forgiveness in your sight.

### December 24

#### December 25 — The Nativity of the Lord (Christmas) \[Solemnity\]

*\[Holy day of obligation\]*

#### December 26 — Saint Stephen, protomartyr \[Feast\] {#misal.die-26-decembris-s-stephani}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-26-decembris-s-stephani.ant-ad-introitum}

The gates of heaven were opened for blessed Stephen, who was found to be first among the number of the Martyrs and therefore is crowned triumphant in heaven.

##### Collect {#misal.die-26-decembris-s-stephani.collecta}

Grant, Lord, we pray, that we may imitate what we worship, and so learn to love even our enemies, for we celebrate the heavenly birthday of a man who knew how to pray even for his persecutors.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-26-decembris-s-stephani.super-oblata}

May these offerings of our devotion today, be acceptable to you, we pray, O Lord, for they are prompted by the glorious commemoration of Saint Stephen.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-26-decembris-s-stephani.ant-ad-communionem}

As they were stoning Stephen, he called out: Lord Jesus, receive my spirit.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-26-decembris-s-stephani.post-communionem}

For the many mercies which surround us we give thanks to you, O Lord, who save us through the Nativity of your Son and gladden us with the celebration of the blessed Martyr Stephen.

#### December 27 — Saint John, apostle and evangelist \[Feast\] {#misal.die-27-decembris-s-ioannis}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-27-decembris-s-ioannis.ant-ad-introitum}

This is John, who reclined on the Lord's breast at supper, the blessed Apostle to whom celestial secrets were revealed and who spread the words of life through all the world.

*Or:*

In the midst of the Church he opened his mouth, and the Lord filled him with the spirit of wisdom and understanding, and clothed him in a robe of glory.

##### Collect {#misal.die-27-decembris-s-ioannis.collecta}

O God, who through the blessed Apostle John have unlocked for us the secrets of your Word, grant, we pray, that we may grasp with proper understanding what he has so marvellously brought to our ear.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-27-decembris-s-ioannis.super-oblata}

Sanctify the offerings we have made, O Lord, we pray, and grant that from the banquet of this supper we may draw forth the hidden wisdom of the eternal Word, which, from this same source, you revealed to your Apostle John.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-27-decembris-s-ioannis.ant-ad-communionem}

The Word became flesh and made his dwelling among us, and from his fullness we have all received.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-27-decembris-s-ioannis.post-communionem}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that, the Word made flesh proclaimed by the blessed Apostle John may, through this mystery which we have celebrated, ever dwell among us.

#### December 28 — The Holy Innocents, martyrs \[Feast\] {#misal.die-28-decembris-ss-innocentium}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-28-decembris-ss-innocentium.ant-ad-introitum}

The innocents were slaughtered as infants for Christ; spotless, they follow the Lamb and sing for ever: Glory to you, O Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.die-28-decembris-ss-innocentium.collecta}

O God, whom the Holy Innocents confessed and proclaimed on this day, not by speaking but by dying, grant, we pray, that the faith in you which we confess with our lips may also speak through our manner of life.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-28-decembris-ss-innocentium.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, we pray, the offerings of your devoted servants, and purify us as we faithfully serve these, your mysteries, by which you grant justification even to those who lack understanding.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-28-decembris-ss-innocentium.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold those redeemed as the first fruits of the human race for God and the Lamb, and who follow the Lamb wherever he goes.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-28-decembris-ss-innocentium.post-communionem}

Grant, O Lord, abundant salvation to your faithful as they receive your holy gifts on the feast day of these your Saints, who though still unable to profess your Son in speech, were crowned with heavenly grace on account of his birth.

#### December 29 — Saint Thomas Becket, bishop and martyr \[English Calendar, Feast\] {#misal.die-29-decembris-s-thomae-becket}

*Or:*

\[Common of , for one martyr, or of , for bishops\] or proper:

##### Entrance Antiphon

In the presence of kings, O Lord, I will declare your will, for your commands have been my delight.

##### Collect {#misal.die-29-decembris-s-thomae-becket.collecta}

O God, who gave the Martyr Saint Thomas Becket the courage to give up his life for the sake of justice, grant, through his intercession, that renouncing our life for the sake of Christ in this world, we may find it in heaven.

*Or:*

O God, who gave to your Bishop Saint Thomas the grace to lay aside all earthly fear and be faithful even unto death, grant, we pray, at his intercession, that your people disregarding worldly esteem may resist what is wrong, uphold your rule and serve you to their life's end.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings

May the offerings we bring in celebration of Saint Thomas be acceptable to you, we pray, O Lord, so that they may be pleasing to your majesty just as the shedding of this Martyr's blood was precious in your sight.

##### Communion Antiphon

Whoever loses his life for my sake, will find it in eternity, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion

May the sacred mysteries of which we have partaken, O Lord, we pray, give us that determination which made your Martyr Saint Thomas faithful in your service and victorious in suffering.

### December 30

#### December 31 — Saint Sylvester I, pope {#misal.die-31-decembris-s-silvestri-i}

*Or:*

\[Common of , for popes\]

##### Collect {#misal.die-31-decembris-s-silvestri-i.collecta}

Come, O Lord, to the help of your people, sustained by the intercession of Pope Saint Sylvester, so that, running the course of this present life under your guidance, we may happily attain life without end.

## Commons

#### COMMON OF THE DEDICATION OF A CHURCH — On the Anniversary of the Dedication \[In the Dedicated Church\] {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-i}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-i.ant-ad-introitum}

Wonderful are you, O God in your holy place. The God of Israel himself gives his people strength and courage. Blessed be God! (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-i.collecta}

O God, who year by year renew for us the day when this your holy temple was consecrated, hear the prayers of your people and grant that in this place for you there may always be pure worship and for us, fullness of redemption.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-i.super-oblata}

Recalling the day when you were pleased to fill your house with glory and holiness, O Lord, we pray that you may make of us a sacrificial offering always acceptable to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-i.ant-ad-communionem}

You are the Temple of God, and the Spirit of God dwells in you. The temple of God, which you are, is holy (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-i.post-communionem}

May the people consecrated to you, O Lord, we pray, receive the fruits and joy of your blessing, that the festive homage they have offered you today in the body, may redound upon them as a spiritual gift.

*SOLEMN BLESSING*

May God, the Lord of heaven and earth, who has gathered you today in memory of the dedication of this church, make you abound in heavenly blessings.\
Amen.\
And may he who has willed that all his scattered children be gathered together in his Son, grant that you may become his temple and the dwelling place of the Holy Spirit.\
Amen.\
Thus, may you be made thoroughly clean, so that God may dwell within you and you may possess with all the Saints the inheritance of eternal happiness.\
Amen.\
And may the blessing of almighty God, the Father, and the Son, + and the Holy Spirit, descend upon you and remain with you for ever.\
Amen.

#### COMMON OF THE DEDICATION OF A CHURCH — On the Anniversary of the Dedication \[Outside the Dedicated Church\] {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-ii}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-ii.ant-ad-introitum}

I saw the holy city, a new Jerusalem, coming down out of heaven from God prepared like a bride adorned for her husband (E.T. alleluia).

*Or:*

Behold God's dwelling with the human race. He will dwell with them and they will be his people, and God himself with them will be their God (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-ii.collecta}

O God, who from living and chosen stones prepare an eternal dwelling for your majesty, increase in your Church the grace you have bestowed, so that by unceasing growth your faithful people may build up the heavenly Jerusalem.

*Or:*

O God, who were pleased to call your Church the Bride, grant that the people that serves your name may revere you, love you and follow you, and may be led by you to attain your promises in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-ii.super-oblata}

Accept, we pray, O Lord, the gift that is offered here, and grant that by it those who seek your favour may receive in this place the strength of the Sacraments and an answer to their prayers.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-ii.ant-ad-communionem}

Be built up like living stones, into a spiritual house, a holy priesthood (E.T. alleluia).

*Or:*

My house shall be a house of prayer, says the Lord: in that house, everyone who asks receives, and the one who seeks finds, and to the one who knocks, the door will be opened (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.commune-dedicationis-ecclesiae-ii.post-communionem}

O God, who chose to foreshadow for us the heavenly Jerusalem through the sign of your Church on earth, grant, we pray, that by our partaking of this Sacrament we may be made the temple of your grace and we may enter the dwelling place of your glory.

#### COMMON OF THE BLESSED VIRGIN MARY — Ordinary Time \[Option 1\] {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-1}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-1.ant-ad-introitum}

Hail, Holy Mother, who gave birth to the King, who rules heaven and earth for ever.

##### Collect {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-1.collecta}

Grant, Lord God, that we, your servants, may rejoice in unfailing health of mind and body and through the glorious intercession of Blessed Mary ever-Virgin, may we be set free from present sorrow and come to enjoy eternal happiness.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-1.super-oblata}

Receive, O Lord, we ask, the prayers of your people with the sacrificial offerings, that, through the intercession of Blessed Mary, the Mother of your Son, no petition may go unanswered, no request be made in vain.

*Or:*

May the humanity of your Only Begotten Son, come, O Lord, to our aid, and may he, who at his birth from the Blessed Virgin did not diminish but consecrated her integrity, by taking from us now our wicked deeds, make our oblation acceptable to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-1.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed is the womb of the Virgin Mary, which bore the Son of the eternal Father.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-1.post-communionem}

As we receive this heavenly Sacrament, we beseech, O Lord, your mercy, that we who rejoice in commemorating the Blessed Virgin Mary may, by imitating her, serve worthily the mystery of our redemption.

#### COMMON OF THE BLESSED VIRGIN MARY — Ordinary Time \[Option 2\] {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-2}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-2.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed are you, O Virgin Mary, who bore the Creator of all things. You became the Mother of your Maker, and you remain for ever Virgin.

##### Collect {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-2.collecta}

Grant us, O merciful God, protection in our weakness, that we who keep the Memorial of the holy Mother of God may, with the help of her intercession, rise up from our iniquities.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-2.super-oblata}

As we honour the memory of the Mother of your Son, we pray, O Lord, that the oblation of this sacrifice may by your grace make of us an eternal offering to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-2.ant-ad-communionem}

Fecit mihi magna qui potens est, et sanctum nomen eius.\
He who is mighty has done great things for me, and holy is his name.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-2.post-communionem}

Having been made partakers of eternal redemption, we pray, O Lord, that we who commemorate the Mother may glory in the fullness of your grace and experience its continued increase for our salvation.

#### COMMON OF THE BLESSED VIRGIN MARY — Ordinary Time \[Option 3\] {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-3}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-3.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed are you, O Virgin Mary, by the Lord God Most High, above all women on the earth; for he has so exalted your name, that your praise shall be undying on our lips.

##### Collect {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-3.collecta}

As we venerate the glorious memory of the most holy Virgin Mary, grant, we pray, O Lord, through her intercession, that we, too, may merit to receive of the fullness of your grace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-3.super-oblata}

We offer you the sacrifice of praise, O Lord, as we rejoice in commemorating the Mother of your Son; grant, we pray, that through this most holy exchange we may advance towards eternal redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-3.ant-ad-communionem}

All generations will call me blessed, for God has looked on his lowly handmaid.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-3.post-communionem}

Renewed with this heavenly food, we humbly implore you, Lord, that we who, under sacramental signs, have received your Son, born of the tender Virgin, may profess him in words and hold fast to him in deeds.

#### COMMON OF THE BLESSED VIRGIN MARY — Ordinary Time \[Option 4\] {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-4}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-4.ant-ad-introitum}

All the richest of the people long to see your face: behind her, her maiden companions are escorted to the King: her attendants are escorted to you amid gladness and joy.

##### Collect {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-4.collecta}

Pardon the faults of your servants, we pray, O Lord, that we who cannot please you by our own deeds, may be saved through the intercession of the Mother of your Son and our Lord.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.commune-beatae-mariae-virginis-i-4.super-oblata}
