Behold a wise woman who has built her house. She feared the Lord and walked in the right path (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.commune-sanctorum-et-sanctarum-v-2.collecta}

O God, the exaltation of the lowly, who willed that blessed N. should excel in the beauty of her charity and patience, grant, through her merits and intercession, that carrying our cross each day, we may always persevere in love for you.

*Or:*

Pour out upon us, Lord, the spirit of knowledge and love of you with which you filled your handmaid blessed N., so that, serving you sincerely in imitation of her, we may be pleasing to you by our faith and our works.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.commune-sanctorum-et-sanctarum-v-2.super-oblata}

Look with favour, O Lord, on the sacrificial offering of your people, and what they devoutly celebrate in honour of blessed N., may they experience in its power to save.

*or*

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.commune-sanctorum-et-sanctarum-v-2.ant-ad-communionem}

Whoever does the will of my heavenly Father is my brother, and sister, and mother, says the Lord (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.commune-sanctorum-et-sanctarum-v-2.post-communionem}

We are replenished, O Lord, with the gifts we have received on the feast day of blessed N.; grant, we pray, that we may be purified by their effects and strengthened by the help they bring.

## Votive Masses

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE BLESSED TRINITY {#misal.missa-votiva-1}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-1.ant-ad-introitum}

Blest be God the Father, and the Only Begotten Son of God, and also the Holy Spirit: for he has shown us his merciful love.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-1.collecta}

God our Father, who by sending into the world the Word of truth and the Spirit of sanctification made known to the human race your wondrous mystery; grant us, we pray, that in professing the true faith, we may acknowledge the Trinity of eternal glory, and adore your Unity, powerful in majesty.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-1.super-oblata}

Sanctify by the invocation of your Name, we pray, O Lord our God, this oblation of our service, and by it make of us an eternal offering to you.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-1.ant-ad-communionem}

Since you are children of God, God has sent into your hearts the Spirit of his Son, the Spirit who cries out: Abba, Father.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-1.post-communionem}

May receiving this Sacrament, O Lord our God, bring us health of body and soul, as we confess your eternal holy Trinity and undivided Unity.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE MERCY OF GOD {#misal.missa-votiva-2}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-2.ant-ad-introitum}

God has loved us with an everlasting love; he sent his Only Begotten Son as an expiation for our sins and not for our sins alone, but for those of all the world.

*or:*

I will sing for ever of your mercies, O Lord; through all ages my mouth will proclaim your fidelity.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-2.collecta}

O God, whose mercies are without number and whose treasure of goodness is infinite, graciously increase the faith of the people consecrated to you, that all may grasp and rightly understand by whose love they have been created, through whose Blood they have been redeemed, and by whose Spirit they have been reborn.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-2.super-oblata}

Receive our oblations in your mercy, O Lord, and transform them into the Sacrament of redemption, the memorial of your Son's Death and Resurrection, so that, by the power of this sacrifice and with constant trust in Christ, we may come to eternal life.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-2.ant-ad-communionem}

The mercy of the Lord is everlasting upon those who hold him in fear.

*or:*

One of the soldiers opened his side with a lance, and at once there came forth blood and water.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-2.post-communionem}

Grant to us, O merciful God, that, nourished by the Body and Blood of your Son, we may draw confidently from the wellsprings of mercy and show ourselves ever more compassionate towards our brothers and sisters.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF JESUS CHRIST, ETERNAL HIGH PRIEST {#misal.missa-votiva-3}

\[For National calendar feast, go to \]

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-3.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord has sworn an oath he will not change: You are a priest forever, according to the order of Melchizedek.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-3.collecta}

O God, who for your glory and the salvation of the human race willed to establish Christ as the eternal High Priest, grant that the people he has gained for you by his Blood, through their participation in his memorial, may experience the power of his Cross and Resurrection.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-3.super-oblata}

Grant us, O Lord, we pray, that we may participate worthily in these mysteries, for whenever the memorial of this sacrifice is celebrated the work of our redemption is accomplished.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-3.ant-ad-communionem}

This is the Body that will be given up for you; this is the Chalice of the new covenant in my Blood, says the Lord; do this, whenever you receive it in memory of me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-3.post-communionem}

We pray, O Lord, that through our partaking in this sacrifice, which your Son commanded to be offered in his memory, you may make us together with him an everlasting oblation to you.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE HOLY CROSS {#misal.missa-votiva-4}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-4.ant-ad-introitum}

We should glory in the cross of our Lord Jesus Christ, in whom is our salvation, life and resurrection, through whom we are saved and delivered.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-4.collecta}

O God, who willed that your Only Begotten Son should undergo the Cross to save the human race, grant, we pray, that we who have known his mystery on earth may merit the grace of his redemption in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-4.super-oblata}

May this oblation, O Lord, which on the altar of the Cross cancelled the offence of the whole world, cleanse us, we pray, of all our sins.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-4.ant-ad-communionem}

When I am lifted up from the earth, I will draw everyone to myself, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-4.post-communionem}

Having been nourished by your holy banquet, we beseech you, Lord Jesus Christ, to bring those you have redeemed by the wood of your life-giving Cross to the glory of the resurrection.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE HOLY EUCHARIST {#misal.missa-votiva-5}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-5.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord opened the gates of heaven, and rained down manna upon them to eat, and gave them bread from heaven: man ate the bread of angels.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-5.collecta}

O God, who have accomplished the work of human redemption through the Paschal Mystery of your Only Begotten Son, graciously grant, that we who confidently proclaim under sacramental signs the Death and Resurrection of Christ may experience continued increase of your saving grace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-5.super-oblata}

Celebrating the memorial of our salvation, we humbly beseech your mercy, O Lord, that this Sacrament you give us in your compassion may be for us the sign of unity and the bond of charity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-5.ant-ad-communionem}

I am the living bread, that came down from heaven, says the Lord. Whoever eats of this bread will live for ever, and the bread that I will give is my flesh for the life of the world.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-5.post-communionem}

May sharing at the heavenly table sanctify us, Lord, we pray, so that through the Body and Blood of Christ, the whole family of believers may be bound together.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE HOLY NAME OF JESUS {#misal.missa-votiva-6}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-6.ant-ad-introitum}

At the name of Jesus every knee should bend of those in heaven and on earth and under the earth, and every tongue confess that Jesus Christ is Lord, to the glory of God the Father.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-6.collecta}

As we venerate the Most Holy Name of Jesus, mercifully grant us, Lord, that savouring its sweetness in this life we may be filled with everlasting joy in our heavenly homeland.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-6.super-oblata}

Be pleased, almighty Father, to accept our offerings in the Name of Jesus, for we are confident that we shall receive whatever we ask in your Son's Name, as he himself, with such kindness, promises.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-6.ant-ad-communionem}

There is no other name under heaven given among the human race by which we must be saved.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-6.post-communionem}

Grant us in your mercy, O Lord, we pray, that in these sacred mysteries we may do worthy homage to the Lord Jesus, for it is your will that at his Name every knee should bend and in him all people find salvation.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE PRECIOUS BLOOD {#misal.missa-votiva-7}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-7.ant-ad-introitum}

You have redeemed us, Lord, by your Blood, from every tribe and tongue and people and nation, and made us a kingdom for our God.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-7.collecta}

O God, who by the Precious Blood of your Only Begotten Son have redeemed the whole world, preserve in us the work of your mercy, so that ever honouring the mystery of our salvation, we may merit to obtain its fruits.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-7.super-oblata}

As we offer our oblation to your majesty, O Lord, may we draw near in these mysteries to Jesus, the Mediator of the New Covenant, and celebrate anew the sprinkling of his Blood, in which lies all our salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-7.ant-ad-communionem}

The chalice of blessing that we bless is a communion in the Blood of Christ; and the bread that we break is a sharing in the Body of the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-7.post-communionem}

Refreshed, O Lord, by this saving food and drink, we pray that we may always be bathed in the Blood of our Saviour, so that it may become for us a spring of water welling up to eternal life.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE SACRED HEART {#misal.missa-votiva-8}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-8.ant-ad-introitum}

The designs of his Heart are from age to age, to rescue their souls from death, and to keep them alive in famine.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-8.collecta}

Clothe us, Lord God, with the virtues of the Heart of your Son and set us aflame with his love, that, conformed to his image, we may merit a share in eternal redemption.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-8.super-oblata}

O God, Father of mercies, who, because of the great love with which you loved us, with untold goodness gave us your Only Begotten Son, grant, we pray, that, being perfectly united with him, we may offer you worthy homage.

##### Preface {#misal.missa-votiva-8.praefatio}

The boundless charity of Christ\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, almighty and eternal God, through Christ our Lord.\
For raised up high on the Cross, he gave himself up for us with a wonderful love and poured out blood and water from his pierced side, the well-spring of the Church's Sacraments, so that, won over to the open heart of the Saviour, all might draw water joyfully from the springs of salvation.\
And so, with all the Angels and Saints, we praise you as without end we acclaim:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-8.ant-ad-communionem}

Thus says the Lord: Let whoever is thirsty come to me and drink. Streams of living water will flow from within the one who believes in me.

*Or:*

One of the soldiers opened his side with a lance, and at once there came forth blood and water.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-8.post-communionem}

Made partakers in your Sacrament of charity, we humbly implore your mercy, Lord, that we may be conformed to Christ on earth and merit to be co-heirs of his glory in heaven.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE HOLY SPIRIT I {#misal.missa-votiva-9-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-9-a.ant-ad-introitum}

The love of God has been poured into our hearts through the Spirit of God dwelling within us.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-9-a.collecta}

O God, who have taught the hearts of the faithful by the light of the Holy Spirit, grant that in the same Spirit we may be truly wise and ever rejoice in his consolation.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-9-a.super-oblata}

Sanctify, we pray, O Lord, the offerings made here, and cleanse our hearts by the light of the Holy Spirit.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-9-a.ant-ad-communionem}

Confirm, O God, what you have brought about in us, from your holy temple, which is in Jerusalem.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-9-a.post-communionem}

May the outpouring of the Holy Spirit cleanse our hearts, O Lord, and make them fruitful by the inner sprinkling of his dew.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE HOLY SPIRIT II {#misal.missa-votiva-9-b}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-9-b.ant-ad-introitum}

When the Spirit of truth comes, he will teach you all truth, says the Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-9-b.collecta}

May the Paraclete who proceeds from you, we pray, O Lord, enlighten our minds, and lead us into all truth, just as your Son has promised.

*Or:*

O God, to whom every heart lies open, every desire speaks plainly, and from whom no secret is hidden, cleanse, we pray, the thoughts of our heart by the outpouring of the Holy Spirit, that we may merit to love you perfectly and offer you worthy praise.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-9-b.super-oblata}

Look, we pray, O Lord, on the spiritual sacrifice placed on your altar with loving devotion, and give your servants a right spirit, so that their faith may make these gifts pleasing to you and their humility commend them.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-9-b.ant-ad-communionem}

The Spirit, whom I will send you from the Father, will glorify me, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-9-b.post-communionem}

Lord our God, who have been pleased to nourish us with heavenly food, pour, we pray, the delights of your Spirit into the recesses of our heart, that what we have devoutly received in time we may possess as a gift for eternity.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE HOLY SPIRIT III {#misal.missa-votiva-9-c}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-9-c.ant-ad-introitum}

The Spirit of the Lord is upon me; he has sent me to bring glad tidings to the poor, says the Lord.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-9-c.collecta}

O God, who sanctify your universal Church in every people and nation; pour out, we pray, the gifts of your Spirit across the face of the earth so that your divine grace, which was at work when the Gospel was first proclaimed, may now spread through the hearts of those who believe in you.

*Or:*

O God, by whose Spirit we are governed and by whose protection we are kept safe, extend over us your mercy and give ear to our supplications, that the faith of those who believe in you may always be sustained by your gifts.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-9-c.super-oblata}

May the fire of the Spirit, O Lord, sanctify the sacrificial gifts offered in your sight, just as it enkindled the hearts of the disciples of your Son.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-9-c.ant-ad-communionem}

You send forth your spirit, and they are created, and you renew the face of the earth.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-9-c.post-communionem}

May these gifts we have consumed benefit us, O Lord, that we may always be aflame with the same Spirit whom you wondrously poured out on your Apostles.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF MARY, MOTHER OF THE CHURCH {#misal.missa-votiva-10-b-de-beata-maria-ecclesiae-matre}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-10-b-de-beata-maria-ecclesiae-matre.ant-ad-introitum}

The disciples devoted themselves with one accord to prayer with Mary, the mother of Jesus.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-10-b-de-beata-maria-ecclesiae-matre.collecta}

O God, Father of mercies, whose Only Begotten Son, as he hung upon the Cross, chose the Blessed Virgin Mary, his Mother, to be our Mother also; grant, we pray, that with her loving help, your Church may be more fruitful day by day, and exulting in the holiness of her children, may draw to her embrace all the families of the peoples.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-10-b-de-beata-maria-ecclesiae-matre.super-oblata}

Receive our offerings, O Lord, and transform them into the mystery of salvation, so that by its power we may be set aflame with the charity of the Virgin Mary, Mother of the Church, and with her may be united more closely to the work of redemption.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-10-b-de-beata-maria-ecclesiae-matre.ant-ad-communionem}

A wedding was held in Cana of Galilee, and the mother of Jesus was there; then Jesus performed the first of his signs and manifested his glory and his disciples believed in him.

*Or:*

As he hung upon the cross, Jesus said to the disciple whom he loved: Behold your mother.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-10-b-de-beata-maria-ecclesiae-matre.post-communionem}

Having received the pledge of redemption and of life we humbly pray, O Lord, that, with the Blessed Virgin's motherly help, your Church may teach all nations by proclaiming the Gospel and, thanks to the outpouring of the Spirit, may fill the whole earth.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE MOST HOLY NAME OF MARY {#misal.missa-votiva-10-c-de-sanctissimo-nomine-mariae}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-10-c-de-sanctissimo-nomine-mariae.ant-ad-introitum}

Blessed are you, O Virgin Mary, by the Lord God Most High, above all women on the earth; for he has so exalted your Name, that your praise shall be undying on our lips.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-10-c-de-sanctissimo-nomine-mariae.collecta}

O God, who chose the Blessed Virgin Mary, full of your grace, from among women to become the Mother of your Son, our Redeemer, mercifully grant that venerating her holy name, we may escape the dangers of this present age and obtain with her life eternal.

*Or:*

O God, whose Son, dying on the altar of the Cross, willed that the Most Blessed Virgin Mary, whom he had chosen as his Mother, should be our Mother also, graciously grant, we pray, that we who fly to her protection may find comfort by invoking our Mother's name.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-10-c-de-sanctissimo-nomine-mariae.super-oblata}

Look favourably on the offerings made here, O Lord, that, through the intercession of Blessed Mary ever-Virgin, our hearts, filled with the light of the Holy Spirit, may constantly strive to cling to Christ, your Son.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-10-c-de-sanctissimo-nomine-mariae.ant-ad-communionem}

The angel Gabriel was sent from God to a virgin, and the virgin's name was Mary.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-10-c-de-sanctissimo-nomine-mariae.post-communionem}

Grant us, we pray, O Lord, whom you have strengthened at the table of word and Sacrament, that, with Blessed Mary as our patron and guide, we may reject whatever is contrary to the name of Christian and follow whatever accords with it.

#### OUR LADY QUEEN OF APOSTLES {#misal.missa-votiva-10-d-de-sancta-maria-regina-apostolorum}

The disciples were persevering with one accord in prayer with Mary, the mother of Jesus.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-10-d-de-sancta-maria-regina-apostolorum.collecta}

O God, who gave the Holy Spirit to your Apostles as they prayed with Mary the Mother of Jesus, grant that, through her intercession, we may faithfully serve your majesty and extend, by word and example, the glory of your Name.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-10-d-de-sancta-maria-regina-apostolorum.super-oblata}

By your favour, O Lord, and through the intercession of Blessed Mary ever-Virgin, may our offering obtain for your Church this grace, that she may grow in the number of the faithful and be ever radiant with an abundance of virtues.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-10-d-de-sancta-maria-regina-apostolorum.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed is the womb that bore Christ the Lord; blessed, rather, are those who hear God’s word and keep it.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-10-d-de-sancta-maria-regina-apostolorum.post-communionem}

Having partaken, O Lord, of these helps to our salvation in our commemoration of the Blessed Virgin Mary, Queen of Apostles, we humbly beseech you, that, persevering in your will and in service of the human family, your people may draw ever closer to salvation. Through Christ our Lord.

#### MARY, MOTHER OF FAIREST LOVE {#misal.die-14-februarii-beatae-mariae-virginis-matris-pulchrae-dilectionis-operis-dei}

Daughter of Zion, full of loveliness and grace, you are fair as the moon, glorious as the sun, most favoured of all women.

*Or:*

Go forth, daughters of Zion, and see your queen; the morning stars sing her praises, sun and moon bow before her beauty, and all God's children rejoice in her presence.

##### Collect {#misal.die-14-februarii-beatae-mariae-virginis-matris-pulchrae-dilectionis-operis-dei.collecta}

Lord, let the Virgin Mary in her glory intercede for us; adorned with the gifts of the Holy Spirit, she won your favour and brought forth for us your only Son, fair beyond all your children on earth. Grant that we may turn away from the ugliness of sin and love only the beauty that comes from your Spirit.

*Or:*

Lord our God, by the wonder of your wisdom Jesus Christ, the Bridegroom of the Church and fairest of all your children on earth, came forth from a virgin's womb; through the intercession of his Mother in her glory give joy and peace to all peoples and fill our hearts with the light of your holiness.

*Or:*

Lord God, your lowly handmaid, the Virgin Mary, is resplendent in your presence with the glory of her Son and the beauty of holiness; grant that like her we may seek only what is true and just and so come before you, the origin of all beauty and the author of purest love.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-14-februarii-beatae-mariae-virginis-matris-pulchrae-dilectionis-operis-dei.super-oblata}

May we receive, O Lord, we pray, the effects of this offering dedicated to you, so that, walking with the Blessed Virgin Mary in the way of beauty, we may be renewed by growth in heavenly life and come at last to the vision of your glory.

##### Preface {#misal.die-14-februarii-beatae-mariae-virginis-matris-pulchrae-dilectionis-operis-dei.praefatio}

Mary most pure\
V. The Lord be with you.\
R. And with your spirit.\
V. Lift up your hearts.\
R. We lift them up to the Lord.\
V. Let us give thanks to the Lord our God.\
R. It is right and just.\
It is truly right and just, our duty and our salvation, always and everywhere to give you thanks, Lord, holy Father, and, as we celebrate the memory of the Blessed Virgin Mary, to proclaim with fitting praise the greatness of your name.\
Beauty was hers at her conception: free from all stain of sin, she is resplendent in the glory of grace. Beauty was hers in her virginal motherhood: she brought forth her Son, the radiance of your glory, as the Saviour and brother of us all. Beauty was hers in the Passion of her Son: marked by his Blood, in her meekness she shared the suffering of the Lamb of God, her Son, silent before his executioners, and won for herself a new title of motherhood. Beauty was hers in the Resurrection of Christ: she reigns with him in glory, the sharer now in his triumph.\
Through him the Angels of heaven offer their prayer of adoration as they rejoice in your presence for ever. May our voices be one with theirs in their triumphant hymn of praise:

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-14-februarii-beatae-mariae-virginis-matris-pulchrae-dilectionis-operis-dei.ant-ad-communionem}

No other woman can compare with her, in the beauty of her face, in the wisdom of her words.

*Or:*

*Diffúsa est grátia in lábiis tuis, proptérea benedíxit te Deus in ætérnum.*

Graciousness is poured out upon your lips, for God has blessed you for ever more.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-14-februarii-beatae-mariae-virginis-matris-pulchrae-dilectionis-operis-dei.post-communionem}

Lord God, continue to bless with your unending favour those whom you renew in strength by this holy Sacrament; and let those to whom you have presented the Blessed Virgin Mary in all the beauty of her virtue walk always in the ways of your justice.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF THE HOLY ANGELS {#misal.missa-votiva-11}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-11.ant-ad-introitum}

Bless the Lord, all you his angels, mighty in power, fulfilling his word and heeding his voice.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-11.collecta}

O God, who dispose in marvellous order ministries both angelic and human, graciously grant that our life on earth may be defended by those who watch over us as they minister perpetually to you in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-11.super-oblata}

We offer you a sacrifice of praise, O Lord, humbly entreating, that as these gifts are borne by the ministry of Angels into the presence of your majesty, you may receive them favourably and make them profitable for our salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-11.ant-ad-communionem}

*In conspéctu Angelórum psallam tibi, Deus meus.*

In the presence of the angels I will praise you, my God.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-11.post-communionem}

Having been nourished upon heavenly bread we beseech you humbly, O Lord, that, drawing from it new strength, under the faithful protection of your Angels, we may advance boldly along the way of salvation.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF SAINT JOSEPH {#misal.missa-votiva-13}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-13.ant-ad-introitum}

Behold a faithful and prudent steward whom the Lord set over his household.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-13.collecta}

O God, who in your inexpressible providence were pleased to choose Saint Joseph as spouse of the most holy Mother of your Son, grant, we pray, that we who revere him as our protector on earth may be worthy of his heavenly intercession.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-13.super-oblata}

As we prepare to offer the sacrifice of praise, O holy Father, we humbly ask to be sustained in our service by the prayers of Saint Joseph, whom you called to watch like a father on earth over your Only Begotten Son.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-13.ant-ad-communionem}

Well done, good and faithful servant; come, share your master's joy.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-13.post-communionem}

Restored by these life-giving Sacraments, Lord, may we live for you always in justice and holiness, helped by the example and intercession of Saint Joseph, who in carrying out your great mysteries served you as a man just and obedient.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF SAINT PETER {#misal.missa-votiva-16}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-16.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord says to Simon Peter: I have prayed for you that your faith may not fail, and once you have turned back, strengthen your brothers.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-16.collecta}

O God, who gave the keys of the Kingdom of Heaven to your blessed Apostle Peter and handed over to him the pontifical office of binding and loosing, grant, we pray, that through the help of his intercession, we may be set free from the bonds of our sins.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-16.super-oblata}

Receive with favour your people's oblation, O Lord, we pray, as we commemorate the blessed Apostle Peter, for you taught him by a hidden revelation to confess you, the living God, and the Christ your Son, and led him to bear witness to his Master by means of a glorious passion.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-16.ant-ad-communionem}

Peter said to Jesus: You are the Christ, the Son of the living God. And Jesus replied: You are Peter, and upon this rock I will build my Church.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-16.post-communionem}

Having been admitted, O Lord, to the banquet of salvation, as we venerate the memory of the blessed Apostle Peter, we ask with gladness that we may always hold fast to your Son, who alone has the words of life, so that, as faithful sheep of your flock, we may be happily led to eternal pastures.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF SAINT PAUL {#misal.missa-votiva-17}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-17.ant-ad-introitum}

I know the one in whom I have believed and I am sure that he, the just judge, the mighty, will keep safe what is my due until that day.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-17.collecta}

Lord God, who in a wondrous way chose the blessed Apostle Paul to preach the Gospel, grant, we pray, that the whole world may be imbued with that faith which he brought before rulers and nations, for the unceasing growth of your Church.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-17.super-oblata}

As we celebrate the divine mysteries, O Lord, we pray, may the Spirit fill us with that light of faith by which he constantly enlightened the blessed Apostle Paul for the spreading of your glory.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-17.ant-ad-communionem}

I live by faith in the Son of God, who has loved me and given himself up for me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-17.post-communionem}

Grant Lord, to those refreshed by communion in the Body and Blood of your Son, that Christ himself may be life to us, that nothing may separate us from his love, and that, attentive to the teaching of the blessed Apostle Paul, we may walk in charity with our neighbour.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF ALL THE HOLY APOSTLES {#misal.missa-votiva-14}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-14.ant-ad-introitum}

It was not you who chose me, says the Lord, but I who chose you and appointed you to go and bear fruit, fruit that will last.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-14.collecta}

May your Church always exult, O Lord, in the honour given through the ages to the blessed Apostles, that she may be guided by these protectors in whose teaching and merits she finds joy.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-14.super-oblata}

Pour out on us, Lord, your Holy Spirit, whom you poured in abundance upon the Apostles, that we may recognize what you have given us through them, and duly offer the sacrifice of praise to your glory.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-14.ant-ad-communionem}

You who have followed me will sit on twelve thrones, judging the twelve tribes of Israel, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-14.post-communionem}

Grant that we may persevere, O God, with rejoicing and simplicity of heart in the teaching of the Apostles, in the breaking of the bread, and in the prayers.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF ALL SAINTS {#misal.missa-votiva-19}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-19.ant-ad-introitum}

The souls of the Saints are rejoicing in heaven, the Saints who followed the footsteps of Christ; and so now exult with Christ for ever.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-votiva-19.collecta}

O God, fount of all holiness, make us each walk worthily in our vocation, through the intercession of your Saints, on whom you bestowed a great variety of graces on earth and a single glorious reward in heaven.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-votiva-19.super-oblata}

May these offerings we bring in honour of all the Saints be pleasing to you, O Lord, and grant that, just as we believe the Saints to be already assured of immortality, so we may experience their concern for our salvation.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-votiva-19.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are the clean of heart, for they shall see God. Blessed are the peacemakers, for they shall be called children of God. Blessed are they who are persecuted for the sake of righteousness, for theirs is the kingdom of heaven.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-votiva-19.post-communionem}

O God, who refresh us with the one Bread and sustain us with one hope, strengthen us likewise, we pray, by your grace, that as we are one with your Saints, one body and one spirit in Christ, we may rise with him to glory.

#### VOTIVE MASS OF SAINT JOSEMARIA ESCRIVA {#misal.die-26-iunii-s-iosephmariae-escriva-de-balaguer-operis-dei}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.die-26-iunii-s-iosephmariae-escriva-de-balaguer-operis-dei.ant-ad-introitum}

I will appoint over you shepherds after my own heart, who will shepherd you wisely and prudently (E.T. alleluia).

##### Collect {#misal.die-26-iunii-s-iosephmariae-escriva-de-balaguer-operis-dei.collecta}

O God, who raised up your priest Saint Josemaría in the Church to proclaim the universal call to holiness and the apostolate, grant that by his intercession and example we may, through our daily work, be formed in the likeness of Jesus your Son and serve the work of redemption with burning love. Through our Lord Jesus Christ, your Son, who lives and reigns with you in the unity of the Holy Spirit, one God, for ever and ever.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.die-26-iunii-s-iosephmariae-escriva-de-balaguer-operis-dei.super-oblata}

Holy Father, accept these gifts that we offer in memory of Saint Josemaría, so that, through the sacrifice offered by Christ on the altar of the Cross and made present here in this sacrament, you may graciously sanctify all that we do. Through Christ our Lord.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.die-26-iunii-s-iosephmariae-escriva-de-balaguer-operis-dei.ant-ad-communionem}

The Son of Man did not come to be served but to serve, and to give his life as a ransom for many (E.T. alleluia).

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.die-26-iunii-s-iosephmariae-escriva-de-balaguer-operis-dei.post-communionem}

O Lord our God, may the mysteries we have received in this celebration of Saint Josemaría, strengthen in us the spirit of adoption as your children, so that, in faithful adherence to your will, we may advance joyfully along the path of holiness. Through Christ our Lord.

## Masses and Prayers for Various Needs and Occasions

#### FOR THE UNIVERSAL CHURCH (Option A) {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-a.ant-ad-introitum}

God has made known to us the mystery of his will, to bring together all things in Christ, all things in heaven and on earth in him.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-a.collecta}

O God, who in your wonderful providence decreed that Christ's Kingdom should be extended throughout the earth and that all should become partakers of his saving redemption; grant, we pray, that your Church may be the universal sacrament of salvation, and that Christ may be revealed to all as the hope of the nations and their Saviour.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-a.super-oblata}

Look upon the offerings of the people consecrated to you, O merciful God, and through the power of this Sacrament, grant that the multitude of those who believe in you may constantly be made a chosen race, a royal priesthood, a holy nation, a people of your own.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-a.ant-ad-communionem}

The Spirit and the bride say, Come. Amen. Come, Lord Jesus.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-a.post-communionem}

O God, who constantly feed and strengthen the Church with your Sacraments, grant to us, who have been nourished at the heavenly table, that, by obeying your teachings of love, we may become a life-giving leaven and a means of salvation for the human family.

#### For the Universal Church (Option B) {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-b}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-b.ant-ad-introitum}

I had a vision of a great multitude, which no one could count, from every nation, race, people, and tongue.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-b.collecta}

O God, in the covenant of your Christ you never cease to gather to yourself from all nations a people growing together in unity through the Spirit; grant, we pray, that your Church, faithful to the mission entrusted to her, may continually go forward with the human family and always be the leaven and the soul of human society, to renew it in Christ and transform it into the family of God.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-b.super-oblata}

Receive with kindness the offerings we bring you, O Lord, and grant that your Church, which came forth from the side of Christ as he slept on the Cross, may ever draw her holiness from participation in this mystery, living by it always and responding worthily to her founder, Jesus Christ our Lord. Who lives and reigns for ever and ever.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-b.ant-ad-communionem}

One of the soldiers opened his side with a lance, and at once there came forth blood and water.

*Or:*

Blessing and glory and wisdom and thanksgiving, honour and power and might be to our God forever and ever. Amen.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-b.post-communionem}

Nourished by the Sacrament of your Son, we implore you, Lord, to make fruitful the work of your Church, for by it you constantly reveal the fullness of the mystery of salvation to the poor, whom you have called to an honoured place in your eternal Kingdom.

#### For the Universal Church (Option C) {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-c}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-c.ant-ad-introitum}

Where two or three are gathered together in my name, there am I in the midst of them.

*Or:*

Though many, we are one body in Christ and individually parts of one another.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-c.collecta}

Grant, we pray, almighty God, that your Church may always remain a holy people formed as one by the unity of Father, Son and Holy Spirit, so that she may manifest to the world the Sacrament of your holiness and unity and may lead it to the perfection of your charity.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-c.super-oblata}

Celebrating the memorial of your Son's boundless love, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that, through the ministry of your Church, the fruits of his saving work may advance the salvation of all the world.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-c.ant-ad-communionem}

Though many, we are one bread, one body, for we all partake of the one bread and one chalice.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-c.post-communionem}

O God, who by this wonderful Sacrament give courage and comfort to the Church, grant that, through these holy gifts, your people may hold fast to Christ, so that by the tasks they carry out in this present age they may in freedom build up your eternal Kingdom.

#### For the Universal Church (Option D) {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-d}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-d.ant-ad-introitum}

Father, I pray for those who will believe in me, that they may be one in us, so that the world may believe it was you who sent me.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-d.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who in Christ revealed your glory to all the nations, watch over the works of your mercy that holy Church, spread throughout the whole world, may persevere with steadfast faith in confessing your Name.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-d.super-oblata}

O God, who constantly sanctify your Church through the same sacrifice by which you have made her clean, grant that, united to Christ her Head, she may offer herself to you with him, and be united with you in purity of will.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-d.ant-ad-communionem}

Whoever remains in me and I in him will bear much fruit, because without me you can do nothing, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-d.post-communionem}

Be pleased, O Lord, we pray, to govern the Church you have nourished by this holy meal, so that, firmly directed, she may grow in freedom and persevere in integrity of religion.

#### For the Particular Church (Option E) {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-e-pro-ecclesia-particulari}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-e-pro-ecclesia-particulari.ant-ad-introitum}

To Jesus Christ who loves us and has freed us from our sins by his blood, who has made us into a kingdom, priests for his God. and Father, to him be glory and power forever and ever. Amen.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-e-pro-ecclesia-particulari.collecta}

O God, who in each pilgrim Church throughout the world make visible the one, holy, catholic and apostolic Church, graciously grant that your faithful may be so united to their shepherd and gathered together in the Holy Spirit through the Gospel and the Eucharist, as to embody worthily the universality of your people and become a sign and instrument in the world of the presence of Christ.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-e-pro-ecclesia-particulari.super-oblata}

Celebrating the memorial of your Son's boundless love, we humbly beseech you, O Lord, that through the ministry of your Church, the fruits of his saving work may advance the salvation of all the world.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-e-pro-ecclesia-particulari.ant-ad-communionem}

Behold, I stand at the door and knock. If anyone hears my voice and opens the door to me, I will come in to him and dine with him, and he with me.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-1-e-pro-ecclesia-particulari.post-communionem}

In this your Church, O Lord, may integrity of faith, holiness of life, fraternal charity and pure religion flourish and abide till the end, and, as you do not fail to feed her with the Body of your Son and with your word, so also never cease, we pray, to guide her under your protection.

#### FOR THE POPE {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-2}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-2.ant-ad-introitum}

You are Peter, and upon this rock I will build my Church, and the gates of the netherworld shall not prevail against it. To you I will give the keys to the kingdom of heaven.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-2.collecta}

O God, who in your providential design willed that your Church be built upon blessed Peter, whom you set over the other Apostles, look with favour, we pray, on N. our Pope and grant that he whom you have made Peter's successor, may be for your people a visible source and foundation of unity in faith and of communion.

*Or:*

O God, shepherd and ruler of all the faithful, look favourably on your servant N., whom you have set at the head of your Church as her shepherd; grant, we pray, that by word and example he may be of service to those over whom he presides so that, together with the flock entrusted to his care, he may come to everlasting life.

*Or:*

O God, who chose your servant N. in succession to the Apostle Peter as shepherd of the whole flock, look favourably on the supplications of your people and grant that, as Vicar of Christ on earth, he may confirm his brethren, and that the whole Church may be in communion with him in the bond of unity, love and peace, so that in you, the shepherd of souls, all may know the truth and attain life eternal.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-2.super-oblata}

Be pleased, we pray, O Lord, with the offerings presented here, and govern with unfailing protection your holy Church, together with N. our Pope, whom you have chosen to be her shepherd.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-2.ant-ad-communionem}

Simon, Son of John, do you love me more than these? Lord, you know all things; you know that I love you.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-2.post-communionem}

Having been made sharers at the heavenly table, we humbly entreat you, Lord: by the power of this mystery, strengthen your Church in unity and charity and, as you have entrusted your servant N. with the office of shepherd, grant him always salvation and protection, together with the flock entrusted to his care.

#### FOR A BISHOP {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-3}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-3.ant-ad-introitum}

I will look after my sheep, says the Lord, and I will appoint a shepherd to pasture them, and I, the Lord, will be their God.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-3.collecta}

O God, eternal shepherd of the faithful, who tend your Church in countless ways and rule over her in love; grant, we pray, that N., your servant, whom you have set over your people, may preside in the place of Christ over the flock whose shepherd he is, and be faithful as a teacher of doctrine, a Priest of sacred worship and as one who serves them by governing.

*Or:*

O God, shepherd and ruler of all the faithful, look favourably on your servant N., whom you have set at the head of your Church of N. as her shepherd; grant, we pray, that by word and example he may be of service to those over whom he presides so that, together with the flock entrusted to his care, he may come to everlasting life.

*Or:*

O Lord, who for the feeding of your flock have set your servant N. over it as a successor to the Apostles; grant him, we pray, a spirit of counsel and fortitude, a spirit of knowledge and piety, so that, by faithfully governing the people entrusted to him, he may build up in the world the sacrament of the Church.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-3.super-oblata}

May this oblation, O Lord, which we have presented for your servant N., become an offering acceptable to you and, for the good of the flock, may he whom you have raised up among your people to be High Priest, be endowed, by your gift, with apostolic virtues.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-3.ant-ad-communionem}

The Son of Man did not come to be served but to serve, and to give his life as a ransom for many.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-3.post-communionem}

By the power of this Sacrament, O Lord, increase the gifts of your grace in N., your servant and our Bishop, that he may serve you worthily in the pastoral ministry and receive the eternal rewards of a faithful steward.

#### FOR PRIESTS {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-6}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-6.ant-ad-introitum}

The Spirit of the Lord is upon me, because he has anointed me and sent me to preach good news to the poor, to heal the broken-hearted and to let the oppressed go free.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-6.collecta}

O God, who made your Only Begotten Son eternal High Priest, grant that those he has chosen as ministers and stewards of your mysteries may be found faithful in carrying out the ministry they have received.

*Or:*

Lord our God, who in governing your people make use of the ministry of Priests, grant to these men a persevering obedience to your will so that by their ministry and life they may gain glory for you in Christ.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-6.super-oblata}

O God, who have willed that your Priests should minister at the holy altar and serve your people, grant by the power of this sacrifice, we pray, that their labours may constantly please you and in your Church bear that fruit which lasts for ever.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-6.ant-ad-communionem}

Holy Father, consecrate them in the truth; your word is truth. As you sent me into the world, so I sent them into the world, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-6.post-communionem}

May the divine sacrifice we have offered and received, O Lord, give new life to your Priests and to all your servants, that, united to you in unfailing love, they may receive the grace of giving worthy service to your majesty.

#### ELECTION OF POPE OR BISHOP {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-4}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-4.ant-ad-introitum}

I will raise up for myself a faithful priest, who shall do according to my heart and mind. I will establish a lasting house for him and he shall walk before me all his days.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-4.collecta}

O God, eternal shepherd, who govern your flock with unfailing care, grant in your boundless fatherly love a pastor for your Church who will please you by his holiness and to us show watchful care.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-4.super-oblata}

May your abundant kindness favour us, O Lord, that through the sacred offerings we reverently bring to you, we may come to rejoice that a pastor pleasing to your majesty presides over your holy Church.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-4.ant-ad-communionem}

I chose you and appointed you to go and bear fruit, says the Lord, fruit that will last.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-4.post-communionem}

As we have been renewed, O Lord, with the supreme Sacrament of salvation, the Body and Blood of your Only Begotten Son, may the wondrous grace of your majesty gladden us with the gift of a shepherd who will instruct your people by his virtues and imbue the minds of the faithful with the truth of the Gospel.

#### FOR THE UNITY OF CHRISTIANS \[Option A\] {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-a.ant-ad-introitum}

I am the good shepherd, and I know my sheep and mine know me, says the Lord. Just as the Father knows me and I know the Father; and I lay down my life for the sheep.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-a.collecta}

Almighty ever-living God, who gather what is scattered and keep together what you have gathered, look kindly on the flock of your Son, that those whom one Baptism has consecrated may be joined together by integrity of faith and united in the bond of charity.

*Or:*

We humbly ask you, Lord, lover of the human family, to pour out more fully upon us the grace of your Spirit, and grant that, walking worthily in the vocation to which you have called us, we may bear witness to the truth before others and seek with confidence the unity of all believers in the bond of peace.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-a.super-oblata}

O Lord, who gained for yourself a people by adoption through the one sacrifice offered once for all, bestow graciously on us, we pray, the gifts of unity and peace in your Church.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-a.ant-ad-communionem}

Though many, we are one bread, one body, for we all partake of the one bread and one chalice.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-a.post-communionem}

Just as this reception of your Holy Communion, O Lord, foreshadows the union of the faithful in you, so may it bring about unity in your Church.

#### FOR THE UNITY OF CHRISTIANS \[Option B\] {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-b}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-b.ant-ad-introitum}

Save us, O Lord our God, and gather us from the nations, that we may give thanks to your holy name, and make it our glory to praise you.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-b.collecta}

O God, who have united many nations in confessing your Name, grant that those reborn in the font of Baptism may be one in the faith of their hearts and the homage of their deeds.

*Or:*

Attend with favour to the prayers of your people, we ask, O Lord, and grant that the hearts of believers may be united in your praise and in repentance together, so that, with division among Christians overcome, we may hasten with joy to your eternal Kingdom in the perfect communion of the Church.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-b.super-oblata}

Celebrating the memorial of our salvation, we humbly beseech your mercy, O Lord, that this Sacrament you give us in your compassion may be for us the sign of unity and the bond of charity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-b.ant-ad-communionem}

And over all these put on love, which is the bond of perfection. And let the peace of Christ rule in your hearts, the peace into which you were also called in one body.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-b.post-communionem}

Pour on us, O Lord, the Spirit of your love, and in your kindness make those you have nourished by this one heavenly bread, one in mind and heart.

#### FOR THE UNITY OF CHRISTIANS \[Option C\] {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-c}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-c.ant-ad-introitum}

One body and one Spirit, as you were also called to the one hope of your call; one Lord, one faith, one baptism; one God and Father of all, who is over all and through all and in all.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-c.collecta}

Look with favour on your people, Lord, we pray, and pour out upon them the gifts of your Spirit, that they may grow constantly in love of the truth and devote themselves with zeal to perfect unity among Christians.

*Or:*

Make known in us, O Lord, the abundance of your mercy, and in the power of your Spirit remove the divisions between Christians, that your Church may appear more clearly as a sign raised high among the nations and that the world, enlightened by your Spirit, may believe in the Christ whom you have sent.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-c.super-oblata}

May the sacrifice we offer you purify us, O Lord, and make all who are joined in one Baptism partakers at last of one and the same celebration of these mysteries.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-c.ant-ad-communionem}

May all be one, as you, Father, are in me and I in you, that they also may be in us, I in them and you in me, that they may be brought to perfection as one.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-17-c.post-communionem}

Receiving the Sacrament of your Christ, we pray, O Lord, that you may renew in your Church the sanctifying grace you have given and that all who glory in the name of Christian may come to serve you in unity of faith.

#### FOR PERSECUTED CHRISTIANS {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-19}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-19.ant-ad-introitum}

Look to your covenant, O Lord, and do not forget the life of your poor ones forever. Arise, O Lord, and defend your cause; and do not forget the voices of those who seek you!

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-19.collecta}

O God, who in your inscrutable providence will that the Church be united to the sufferings of your Son, grant, we pray, to your faithful who suffer for your Name's sake a spirit of patience and charity, that they may be found true and faithful witnesses to the promises you have made.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-19.super-oblata}

Receive, we ask, O Lord, the prayers and sacrificial gifts we offer in humility, and grant that those who suffer persecution for their faithful service to you may rejoice to be united to the sacrifice of Christ your Son and may know that their names are written in heaven among the company of the elect.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-19.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are you when they insult you and persecute you because of me, says the Lord. Rejoice and be glad, for your reward will be great in heaven.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-19.post-communionem}

By the power of this Sacrament, O Lord, confirm your servants in the truth and grant to your faithful who suffer tribulation that, as they follow your Son in bearing their cross, they may, in every trial, glory in the name of Christian.

#### FOR THE EVANGELISATION OF PEOPLES (Option A) {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-a.ant-ad-introitum}

O God, be gracious and bless us, and let your face shed its light upon us, and have mercy. So will your ways be known upon earth and all nations learn your salvation.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-a.collecta}

O God, whose will it is that all should be saved and come to the knowledge of the truth, look upon your abundant harvest and be pleased to send workers to gather it, that the Gospel may be preached to all creation and that your people, gathered by the word of life and sustained by the power of the Sacraments, may advance in the path of salvation and love.

*Or:*

O God, who sent your Son into the world as the true light pour out, we pray, the Spirit he promised to sow seeds of truth constantly in people's hearts and to awaken in them obedience to the faith, so that, being born to new life through Baptism, all may become part of your one people.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-a.super-oblata}

Look, O Lord, upon the face of your Christ, who handed himself over as a ransom for all, so that through him, from the rising of the sun to its setting, your Name may be exalted among the nations and in every place a single offering may be presented to your majesty.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-a.ant-ad-communionem}

Teach all nations to keep whatever I have commanded you, says the Lord. And behold, I am with you always, even to the end of the age.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-a.post-communionem}

Nourished by these redeeming gifts, we pray, O Lord, that through this help to eternal salvation true faith may ever increase.

#### FOR THE EVANGELISATION OF PEOPLES (Option B) {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-b}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-b.ant-ad-introitum}

Tell among the nations his glory, and his wonders among all the peoples. For the Lord is great and highly to be praised.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-b.collecta}

O God, you have willed that your Church be the sacrament of salvation for all nations so that Christ's saving work may continue to the end of the ages; stir up, we pray, the hearts of your faithful and grant that they may feel a more urgent call to work for the salvation of every creature, so that from all the peoples on earth one family and one people of your own may arise and increase.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-b.super-oblata}

May the offerings and prayers of your Church, O Lord, rise up in the sight of your majesty and gain acceptance, just as the glorious Passion of your Son was pleasing to you for the salvation of the whole world.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-b.ant-ad-communionem}

O praise the Lord, all you nations; acclaim him, all you peoples! For his merciful love has prevailed over us, and the Lord's faithfulness endures forever.

*Or:*

Go into all the world, and proclaim the gospel, says the Lord. I am with you always.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-18-b.post-communionem}

May our participation at your table sanctify us, O Lord, we pray, and grant that through the Sacrament of your Church all nations may receive in rejoicing the salvation accomplished on the Cross by your Only Begotten Son.

#### FOR THE NEW EVANGELISATION {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-pro-nova-evangelizatione}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-pro-nova-evangelizatione.ant-ad-introitum}

Glory in his holy name; let the hearts that seek the Lord rejoice. Turn to the Lord and his strength; remember the wonders he has done.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-pro-nova-evangelizatione.collecta}

O God, who in the power of the Holy Spirit have sent your Word to announce good news to the poor, grant that, with eyes fixed upon him, we may ever live in sincere charity, made heralds and witnesses of his Gospel in all the world.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-pro-nova-evangelizatione.super-oblata}

Graciously sanctify these gifts, we pray, O Lord, and kindly receive our humble offering, that our bodies may become a living sacrifice, holy and pleasing to you, and grant that we may serve you in newness of life, not according to the old humanity but according to your Spirit. Through Christ our Lord.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-pro-nova-evangelizatione.ant-ad-communionem}

The Spirit of the Lord is upon me, for he has anointed me to announce the good news, to proclaim a year acceptable to the Lord, and a day of redemption.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-pro-nova-evangelizatione.post-communionem}

Having renewed us in mind by the nourishment of the precious Body and Blood of your Son, graciously grant us, O Lord, a changed heart and a new spirit that we may walk with perseverance in newness of life. Through Christ our Lord.

#### FOR THE NATION, STATE, OR CITY {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-21}

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-21.collecta}

O God, who arrange all things according to a wonderful design, graciously receive the prayers we pour out to you for our country (state), that through the wisdom of its leaders and the integrity of its citizens harmony and justice may be assured and lasting prosperity come with peace.

#### FOR PEACE AND JUSTICE {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-30-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-30-a.ant-ad-introitum}

Give peace, O Lord, to those who wait for you; hear the prayers of your servants and guide us in the way of justice.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-30-a.collecta}

O God, who have revealed that peacemakers are to be called your children, grant, we pray, that we may work without ceasing to establish that justice which alone ensures true and lasting peace.

*Or:*

O God, who show a father's care for all, grant, in your mercy, that the members of the human race, to whom you have given a single origin, may form in peace a single family and always be united by a fraternal spirit.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-30-a.super-oblata}

May the saving sacrifice of your Son, the King of peace, offered under sacramental signs that signify peace and unity, strengthen, we pray, O Lord, concord among all your children.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-30-a.ant-ad-communionem}

Blessed are the peacemakers, for they will be called children of God.

*Or:*

Peace I leave with you, my peace I give you, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-30-a.post-communionem}

Bestow on us, we pray, O Lord, the spirit of charity, so that, sustained by the Body and Blood of your Only Begotten Son, we may be effective in nurturing among all the peace that he has left us.

#### IN TIME OF WAR OR CIVIL DISTURBANCE {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-31}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-31.ant-ad-introitum}

The Lord said: I think thoughts of peace and not of affliction. You will call upon me, and I will answer you, and I will lead back your captives from every place.

*Or:*

The waves of death rose about me; the pains of the netherworld surrounded me. In my anguish I called to the Lord, and from his holy temple he heard my voice.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-31.collecta}

O God, merciful and strong, who crush wars and cast down the proud, be pleased to banish violence swiftly from our midst, and to wipe away all tears, so that we may all truly deserve to be called your children.

*Or:*

O God, author and lover of peace, to know you is to live, to serve you is to reign, defend against every attack those who cry to you, so that we, who trust in your protection, may not fear the weapons of any foe.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-31.super-oblata}

Be mindful, Lord, that your Son, who himself is peace, has destroyed our hatreds by his Blood: look in mercy on our evil deeds, and grant that to those whom you love this sacrifice may restore peace and tranquility.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-31.ant-ad-communionem}

Peace I leave with you; my peace I give to you, says the Lord. Not as the world gives do I give it to you. Do not let your hearts be troubled or afraid.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-31.post-communionem}

Grant to us, O Lord, that, nourished with the delights of the one Bread that fortifies the human heart, we may successfully overcome the fury of war and resolutely keep your law of love and justice.

#### FOR REFUGEES AND EXILES {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-32}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-32.ant-ad-introitum}

For you has God commanded his angels to keep you in all your ways.

*Or:*

The Lord said, I think thoughts of peace and not of affliction. You will call upon me, and I will answer you, and I will lead back your captives from every place.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-32.collecta}

O Lord, to whom no one is a stranger, and from whose help no one is ever distant, look with compassion on refugees and exiles, on segregated persons and on lost children; restore them, we pray, to a homeland, and give us a kind heart for the needy and for strangers.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-32.super-oblata}

O Lord, who willed that your Son should lay down his life to gather into one your scattered children, grant that this sacrifice of your peace may bring about a communion of minds and hearts and an increase in fraternal charity.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-32.ant-ad-communionem}

My refuge and my stronghold, my God, in whom I trust.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-32.post-communionem}

O Lord, who have renewed us with the one Bread and the one Chalice, grant that in sincerity of heart we may show true compassion towards strangers and the abandoned, and that all of us may deserve to be gathered together at last in the land of the living.

#### IN TIME OF FAMINE AND THOSE SUFFERING HUNGER {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-33-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-33-a.ant-ad-introitum}

Look to your covenant, O Lord, and forget not the life of your poor ones forever.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-33-a.collecta}

O God, who being both good and almighty, provide for all creatures, give us, we pray, an effective love for our brothers and sisters who suffer hunger, so that famine may be banished and that they may have strength to serve you with free and untroubled hearts.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-33-a.super-oblata}

Look, O Lord, on the oblation we present to you from among your wonderful gifts, so that the offering which signifies abundance of divine life and unity in charity may impel us to share all things justly and to care for one another as brothers and sisters.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-33-a.ant-ad-communionem}

Come to me, all who labor and are burdened, and I will refresh you, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-33-a.post-communionem}

O God, almighty Father, we humbly ask you that the living Bread, which has come down from heaven, may give us strength to relieve our brothers and sisters in their need.

#### IN THANKSGIVING {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-49-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-49-a.ant-ad-introitum}

Sing and make music to the Lord in your hearts, always thanking God the Father for all things in the name of our Lord Jesus Christ.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-49-a.collecta}

O God, who always listen mercifully to your servants in distress, we beseech you humbly, as we give thanks for your kindness, that, free from all evil, we may constantly serve you in gladness.

*Or:*

O God, the Father of every gift, we confess that all we have and are comes down from you; teach us to recognize the effects of your boundless care and to love you with a sincere heart and with all our strength.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-49-a.super-oblata}

O Lord, who gave us your Son to rescue us graciously from death and from every evil, accept, we pray, in mercy this sacrifice, which we offer you in thanksgiving for our deliverance from distress.

*Or:*

For the gifts you have bestowed, O Lord, we offer you the sacrifice of praise, humbly begging that what you have conferred upon us in our unworthiness we may give back to the glory of your Name.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-49-a.ant-ad-communionem}

I will thank you, Lord, with all my heart, for you have heard the words of my mouth.

*Or:*

How can I repay the Lord for all his goodness to me? The chalice of salvation I will raise, and I will call on the name of the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-49-a.post-communionem}

Almighty God, who through this Bread of Life are pleased to free your servants from the bond of sin and in your compassion to restore their strength, grant us to advance without hindrance towards the hope of glory.

*Or:*

O God, who have given to us as spiritual food the saving Sacrament of your Son which we have offered you in thanksgiving, grant that, being strengthened by the gifts of courage and joy, we may serve you more devotedly and be worthy of still further blessings.

#### FOR THE SANCTIFICATION OF HUMAN LABOUR {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-26-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-26-a.ant-ad-introitum}

In the beginning, when God created the heavens and the earth, God created man in his image; God looked at everything he had made, and he found it very good.

*Or:*

May your favour, O Lord, be upon us, and may you give success to the work of our hands.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-26-a.collecta}

O God, Creator of all things, who have commanded the human race to bear the burden of labour, grant that the work we are beginning may bring progress in this life and, by your favour, advance the spread of Christ's Kingdom. Who lives and reigns with you.

*Or:*

O God, who through human labour never cease to perfect and govern the vast work of creation, listen to the supplications of your people and grant that all men and women may find work that befits their dignity, joins them more closely to one another and enables them to serve their neighbour.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-26-a.super-oblata}

O God, who in the gifts presented here nourish the human race with food and renew it with your Sacrament, grant, we pray, that the sustenance they impart may not fail us in body or in mind.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-26-a.ant-ad-communionem}

Whatever you do in word or in deed, do everything in the name of the Lord Jesus, giving thanks to God the Father through him.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-26-a.post-communionem}

Having been made partakers of this table of unity and charity, we beseech your mercy, Lord, that through the work you have given us to do we may sustain our life on earth and trustingly build up your Kingdom.

#### FOR THE FAMILY {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-12}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-12.ant-ad-introitum}

Honour your father and mother. This is the first commandment with a promise, that it may go well with you and that you may have a long life on earth.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-12.collecta}

O God, in whose eternal design family life has its firm foundation, look with compassion on the prayers of your servants and grant that, following the example of the Holy Family of your Only Begotten Son in practicing the virtues of family life and in the bonds of charity, we may, in the joy of your house, delight one day in eternal rewards.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-12.super-oblata}

We offer you, Lord, the sacrifice of conciliation, humbly asking that you may establish our families firmly in your grace and your peace.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-12.ant-ad-communionem}

Can a mother forget her infant? Even should she forget, I will never forget you, says the Lord.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-12.post-communionem}

Bring those you refresh with this heavenly Sacrament, most merciful Father, to imitate constantly the example of the Holy Family of your Only Begotten Son, so that, after the trials of this world, they may share their company for ever.

#### FOR THE SICK {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-45}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-45.ant-ad-introitum}

Have mercy on me, Lord, for I languish; Lord, heal me; my bones are trembling, and my soul is greatly shaken.

*Or:*

Truly the Lord has borne our infirmities, and he has carried our sorrows.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-45.collecta}

O God, who willed that our infirmities be borne by your Only Begotten Son to show the value of human suffering, listen in kindness to our prayers for our brothers and sisters who are sick; grant that all who are oppressed by pain, distress or other afflictions may know that they are chosen among those proclaimed blessed, and are united to Christ in his suffering for the salvation of the world.

*Or:*

Almighty ever-living God, eternal health of believers, hear our prayers for your servants who are sick: grant them, we implore you, your merciful help, so that, with their health restored, they may give you thanks in the midst of your Church.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-45.super-oblata}

Since the moments of our life unfold, O God, according to your good pleasure, receive the prayers and sacrificial offerings by which we implore your mercy for our brothers and sisters who are ill, that having been anxious for them in their danger, we may rejoice at their recovery of health.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-45.ant-ad-communionem}

In my flesh I am completing what is lacking in the afflictions of Christ on behalf of his body, which is the Church.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-45.post-communionem}

O God, only support of our human weakness, show the power of your protection over your servants who are sick, that, sustained by your merciful help, they may be restored to your holy Church in good health.

#### FOR THE DYING {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-46}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-46.ant-ad-introitum}

No one lives for himself, and no one dies for himself. For if we live, we live for the Lord, and if we die, we die for the Lord; so then, whether we live or die, we are the Lord's.

*Or:*

Truly the Lord has borne our infirmities, and he has carried our sorrows.

##### Collect {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-46.collecta}

Almighty and merciful God, who through the fact of death itself have mercifully unlocked for the human race the gate to eternal life, look kindly on your servant in his (her) final struggle so that, united to your Son's Passion and sealed with his he (she) may come without blemish into your presence.

*Or, for those that will die today:*

Almighty and merciful God, who show your love to all creatures everywhere, hear graciously the prayers we make for those who will die today, that, redeemed by your Son's Precious Blood, they may leave this world without stain of sin and find eternal rest in the embrace of your mercy.

##### Prayer Over The Offerings {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-46.super-oblata}

Receive, O God, the sacrificial offering we bring you with confidence for your servant who has reached this life's end, and grant through this Sacrament the cleansing of all his (her) offences, that though beset by the trials you ordain for us here he (she) may in the life to come know eternal rest.

##### Communion Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-46.ant-ad-communionem}

In my flesh I am completing what is lacking in the afflictions of Christ on behalf of his body, which is the Church.

*Or:*

Whoever eats my flesh and drinks my blood has eternal life, says the Lord, and I will raise him on the last day.

##### Prayer After Communion {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-46.post-communionem}

By the power of this Sacrament, O Lord, be pleased to sustain your servant with your merciful grace, that at the hour of death he (she) may not see the enemy prevail against him (her), but may be worthy to pass over to life eternal in the company of your Angels.

#### FOR FORGIVENESS OF SINS (Option A) {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-38-a}

##### Entrance Antiphon {#misal.missa-pro-variis-necessitatibus-38-a.ant-ad-introitum}
